[6/11, 12:55 PM] My Zahrah: 🌹🥀🌷
*K'WARK'WARAH*
                  🌼🏵️🌺
     '''{ITAMA MATAR SARKI CE}'''

         _LOVE TRIANGLE♥️_

1️⃣

1987-October- 23
      Sintiri yake  hannun  goye a bayan shi, tsakanin zauren gidan shi sanye yake da janfa! Shigowa da sauri wata dattijuwa tayi ta zub'e akan gwiwarta cikin hakki, sai da ta dai-daita nutsuwarta sannan tace.
"Allah Ya taimaki me martaba! Ubangiji ya kare takawa lafiya! Allah ya bawa takawa Abinda yake nima! A gafarce Ni da kitsen  da nayi!"

       Shiru zauren ya dauka! Kafin yayi magana cikin sansanyar murya yace..
"Ina jinki Jakadiya!"

Sake risinawa tayi sosai sannan tace.
"Allah taimaki Sarki Jalaludeen! Adalin Sarkin mu! Me kaunar Al'ummar shi!"

      Shiru tayi sannan ta had'iye yawu kafin ta cigaba da cewa.
"Allah ya karama nisan kwana! K'wark'waranka Ummu Rumana tana kan gwiwa! Ita da Fulani Hasiyah"

       Shiru yayi kamar bayi zauren, sannan ya d'aga mata hannu!
   Mik'ewa tayi cikin sauri tace.
"Na Barka lafiya! Allah ya kawo maganin Izza lafiya"

              Tunda ta bashi labarin Rumana tana kan gwiwa yaji duk wata nutsuwar shi ya kwace,
        Ajiyar zuciya ya sauke Kafin ya koma kan shimfid'ar shi da yasha kwalliya da dardumai tare da kilisai, ya zauna a yanzun a halin da yake ciki mutum d'aya yake bukatar gani a cikin wannan yanayin! Wato tsohon amininshi Magaji!

       Mik'ewa yayi tare da nufar cikin d'akin shi, ya sauya kayan shi ya b'adda kama, sannan ya nufi kofar dake cikin Zauren shi wanda zai fidda ka wajen masarautar Daura.

         ------
  A hanyar fita Jakadiya suka yi karo da Shamaki! 
"A'ah Jigal kazamin tsuntsu,"

"A'ah me abu da abinsu kura da kalabin kitse daga ina haka"

   Murmushi tayi da alamun abinda ya gaya mata yayi mata dad'i tace.
"Hmm! Maraba da gasheshe kura taga bakin jaki"

   Gyara tsayuwar yayi sosai dan ya fahimci akwai magana a bakinta.
    Juyawa yayi hagu da dama yace.
"Da kukan kura, da bacewar akuya duk d'aya ce! Meke tafe dake"

    Mika hannunta tayi cikin muzurai tace.
"Da babbar rowa gara karamar kyauta"

         Zuba mata ido yayi aikuwa ta kauda kanta, kamar ba ita tayi maganar ba. Jinjina kai yayi sannan ya sanya hannu a cikin aljuhun shi ya ciro kud'i ya mika mata.

Murmushi tayi sannan ta kirga kuɗin kafin tace.
"Cinikin duniya, diban nono!"

   "Hmm me tsegumi bashi rabuwa da waiwaya! Meke faru"

    Juyar da kai tayi sannan tace.
"Bikin farar kaza! Su balbala gayyar sod'i ce, Fulani Hasiyah da K'wark'warar Sarki suna bisa gwiwarsu! Amma wannan karon zakarar dawaki ne kukanshi bana Alkhairin bane, zan koma ciki."

     "Kina nufin!"

"Gyaran hanci, yafi gyaran gona! Koma me nake nufi kasan baza a nunawa ringing wata ba, dan da alamu cikin su dole asami d'aya ta"

     Shiru yayi bai tanka mata ba ya kad'e saka tarar shi yayi gaba, yana magana.
"Tab bashi yuwa, an san da wuyar biri ake d'aura shi a k'ugu! Dole mu hana haihuwar d'a namiji a cikin wannan masarautar!"

            "Assalamu alaikum!"
     Da sauri ta zub'e akan gwiwarta, tace.
"Allah Ya taimaki! Fulani Sakonki ya isa ga sarki Jalaludeen!"

      Juya bayanta tayi tana kallon yan tsuntsayen da suke shawagi a cikin kejinsu! Sannan ta mai da idanunta kan jaririyar da take nad'e cikin kunshi zani.

        "Kije ki duba min Rumana! Ki nemi amintacciyar baiwa ki bata gadin Rumana, idan ta haihu kar a kai ga fad'a takawa"

        Gyad'a kai kawai Jakadiya tayi, sannan ta mik'e da sauri tare da barin d'akin. Tana murmushi sannan tace.
"Mutanen duniya kudan doki ne, dole abisu a hankali! Muje zuwa"

          Da sauri ta wuce zuwa hanyar da zai kaita shashin k'wark'wara Rumana, tana shiga kamar wacce yazo da haihuwar.  Korar unguwar zoman tayi, takira wata cikin bayin ta turata Katanga bayi, sai gasu tare da wata baiwa dattijuwa itama.
"Larai ki gyara abinda aka haifa, naga kamar ta sume kar labarin haihuwar ya isa ga takawa! Duba min ma tukun me ta haifa"

     Dake k'wark'wara Rumana a durkushe take, d'aga zanin Baiwa Larai tayi, ta janyo abinda aka samu. A d'imauce ta d'ago kanta tana kallon Jakadiya, bakinta na rawa tace.
"Ranki shi dad'e! Ai magajin masarauta aka samu."

 Wata irin zabura Jakadiya tayi cikin mamaki tare da lekawa, taga abinda aka samu d'a namiji ne kyakyawa dashi, kaman shi da sarki Jamaluddin ya b'aci, hasken fatar uwar shi.
         Takowa tayi ta d'ago kan Rumana wacce take sume bayan haihuwar ta, tace.
"Ki nad'e jaririn ki tabbatar kin yanke cibiya"

          Tana gama fad'ar haka tabar cikin dakin da sauri, kallon unguwar zomar tayi, sannan tace.
" Zaku iya tafiya, dan ta sauka lafiya kuma."

 Sai tayi shiru sannan ta wuce bayan taga barin su gurin, b'ata nufi shashin Fulani ba, fita tayi daga masarautar,

        Wani gida ta nuna sannan ta wuce zauren gidan, babu sallama ta akfa musu.
"Toh tauraruwa mai wutsiya! Meke tafe dake!"
 
Murmushi tayi sannan tace.
"An kawo kare dan haushi, ya buge da kukan akuya!"

     Zubur ya mik'e jikin shi na rawa,
     "Karya ne Jakadiya! Masallacin kura kare bashi limancin"

    "Allah sarki! Harbi ga wutsiya kuskure ne" Inji Jakadiya.
     
   "Me haka ke nufi?"
Ya wurgata mata tambaya,
  

      "Eh hakan yana nufin duk matan sarki babu wacce tayi nasarar da Rumana tayi,"

"Aikin banza kiba a kunne! Sani ake zance."
      "Na Barka lafiya"
 Ta ficce da sauri, daga cikin gidan ta nufi masarautar tana juyawa murmushi tayi sannan tace.

"Komi gaggawar hasara dole ta jira samu"

          Shashin Fulani ta nufa, cikin sauri ko sallama ba tayi ba ta zub'e a gaban fulani, cikin hali ta sunkuyar da kai zuciyarta cike da fargaba, dan duk wata munakisa da kutungwailarta bata kamo kafar Fulani Hasiyah ba.
     "Me Rumana ta samu?!"
     Tambayar da Fulani ta wurgo mata ya katse mata tunanin da take.
     "Ranki shi dad'e! Ina niman afuwarki, Allah ya huci zuciyarki! Hmm ta sami abinda Takawa yake mafarkin samu ne"

        Murmushi Fulani Hasiyah ta sake, har sai da Jakadiya ta d'ago kanta tana mamakinta sannan tace.
"Allah ya huci zuciyarki! Farin ciki kike na gani!"

     "Ki dauki y'ar ki kaita inda Rumana take! Sannan wacce kika bari a gurin! Ki sanya Bak'o ya aikata inda bazata dawo ba! Gawar sai ku mata sutura a tabbatar da Rumana ce ta haihuwa Y'a mace! sauran bayanin kin fahimta, ina yaso Jaririyar ki san hikimar da zaki kara!"
   Takowa tayi gaban Jakadiya tana kallon cikin idanunta, ta cigaba da cewa.
"Abinda wancan yace, shi nace kirarin me tsoro kin fahimta"

    Gyad'a kai Jakadiya tayi cikin tsantsar tsoro tace.
"Uwar d'akina aka mutu ma balle lalacewa! Ai zancenki kamar an binne Rumana da ranta ne, balle Larai"
   
   "Muguwar aniya bata karewa tukunya," Inji Fulani.

   Sunkuyar da kai Jakadiya tayi dan ta fahimci sakon Fulani, a hankali tace.
"Allah kad'ai ke kaunar kura"
       
      Fita Jakadiya tayi bayan ta dauki jaririyar sannan tace.
" Duk inda jirgi yaje, inda hakuri wata rana kunkuru zashi sai dai ya jima bai isa ba! Fulani dani kike zancen"

         Tana shiga inda Rumana take ta kori larai sannan ta dauki d'an Rumana ta ajiye mata yar da tazo da ita, sannan ta fita da Yaron da Rumana ta haifa.


               Bayan Sa'a d'aya aka fidda sanarwar mutuwar Rumana bayan ta haihu, sai mutuwar Baiwar da ta amshi haihuwar sakamakon gubar da tasha.
                   ------

  Tun bayan fitar Mai martaba Sarki Jalaludeen, ya nufi gidan abokin shi, sun jima suna tattaunawa kafin ya bar gidan, zuwa masarautar a cikin shigar shi na tsofi tukuf ya isa shashinsa, yana cire kayan. Ya tsinkayo Muryar Jakadiya.
     Gaban shine yayi mugun Fad'uwa ya fito daga d'akin yana kallonta.

      "Ina me niman afuwarka! Ya shugabana! Fulani Hasiyah ta sauka lafiya an sami magajinka! Sai Kuma abin alhinin da ya kuma faruwa shine, Uwar d'akina ta haifi Y'a mace Amma kuma rai yayi halin sa."

            Ji yayi jiri ya nemi ka dashi, zama yayi a kujeran dake zauren shi tare da dage kan shi, yana jin wani irin ciwon kai tare da zafin da kan shi yayi.

    "Jeki!"
    "Allah ya baka hakuri da danganar rashinta"

           Tana fita ya kira fuskarshi akan ta tafin hannunshi kwalla na zuba mishi. Tuno ranar da Jakadiya ta kawo mishi ita yayi, cikin shigar alkyaba tare da wata riga me santsi,  a cikin kuruciyarta dan lokacin bazata wuce sha uku sha huɗu ba, shi yayi nasarar koya mata sanin waye shi, a daren ya tabbatar da ita ma matar shi ce. Abubuwa sun tafi kuma sun dawo mishi.

         Dakyar ya shirya sannan ya fito.
        Aka yi jana'izar ta aka kaita gidanta na gaskiya, dake ba a fita da duk wanda yake cikin masarautar misali sarki ko matansa, ko yaran shi a a masarautar akwai makabarta.
 Tunda ya duka kan kabarinta yake zubda kwalla da sosai sai Da Waziri da Sarkin malamai suka d'ago shi.

        Bayan kwana bakwai aka rad'awa Yaran suna, Namijin aka sanya mishi Jamaludeen ita kuma mace. Aka sanya mata Jamalah, dukda anyi rasuwa haka ya takaita shagalin da aka shirya
           ------
1990-March-09
"Bazan bari haka ta kuma faruwa ba,dole nayi amfani da damar da masani."

     
      "A'ah Shamaki! Idan junarka samakone, toh wani a tafe ya kwana! Kabi a sannu sai mu kai labari idan kuma kayi, fitar burtu tabbas zamu iya fad'a holoko hadarin ƙaƙa"

     "Lallai Jakadiya kin zama ciki, me manta kyautar juya. Tsaf na shirya."

     "Hmmmm! Shamaki kamar yadda Gara takan kalli dutse sai gani sai hange haka zamu cigaba da zuba idanun mu dan kana kuskura, zaka yi borin tukunya idan tayi borin kanta zata b'ata."

          "Jakadiya jeki yau babu alkhairi a tattare dake sai zallar sharri."

 Fita tayi da sauri, tabar gidan.
         -----
1997-july-29
A babbar Asibitin garin katsani aka kawo sarki Jalaludeen wanda ya dad'e daga kan dokinshi zai tafi hawan sallah, hankalin kowa a tashe.

Sai da ya shafe wata shida kafin aka sallame shi akan keken guragun,  inda baya iya ko magana sai dai nuni da hannu, kuma bai cika nunawa kowa ba, sai Jamal wanda lokacin yake shekaru goma sha daya. Shine yakewa mahaifin shi komai, dan yaki yarda da kowa sai Jamal din, wani lokaci haka zai sanya shi a gaba yana kallon shi.
             -----
 2003-5-17.
"Don Allah! Jakadiya na rokeki ki barni naga Mai Martaba! Ko sau d'aya ne! Kinji don Allah."

       "Kinga Rumana ki fitar min tun ina ganin girman ki! In ba haka ba zan sanya a wulakantanki."

     Haka Rumana ta zuba mata ido, kwalla na zubo mata tace.
"Jakadiya! Kina b'oye min wani abu, amma ba komai! Me hankali shi yake gane dingishin kwad'o."

   Tana gama fad'ar haka ta bar dakin Jakadiya, ta nufi hanyar gidansu. Da gudu aka bangajeta, tsayawa tayi, tana kallon yaran mace da namiji, a zuciye namijin ya biyo macen, ita kuma ta koma bayan Rumana ta b'oye tana dariya tana cewa..
"An gaya maka d'in me idanun mage."
      
    Cikin sansanyar murya yace.
"Jamalah"
   D'ago kai Rumana tayi tana kallon shi, sannan ta juya ga ita Yarinyar tace.
"Ki mishi magana kar ya tab'a Ni dan Umma Fulani bata mishi fad'a sai Abba!"

         Kura mishi ido tayi tun daga kanshi har zuwa kafar shi, kai karewa har da girar shi da gashin idanunshi, wanda suka yi wani kama da Sarkin Jalaludeen, hasken fatar shi da kwayar idanunshi, zuciyarta ce tayi wani irin bugawa, lokacin d'aya ta shiga kokwanto da zuciyarta, zazzakar muryan yarinyar ta katse mata kallon shi tace.
"Don Allah! Ki mishi fad'a"
      
Zuciyarta na bugawa cikin sauri da tace.
"Yarima! Karka tab'a kaji"
"Yawwa! Ki gaya mishi nice Babba bashi ba, dan Umma Fulani tace na bashi awa biyu"

              Wani irin bugu zuciyarta yayi kamar zai faso kirjinta, sai da ta dage kirjin.
   Cikin wani irin murya tace.
"Don Allah! Naci Arziki karka tab'a ta"

        Gyad'a mata kai yayi sannan yace.
"Shi kenan! Umma bazan tab'a ba"
           Kallon juna suka yi, ya kauda akanshi da sauri, sabida cika mishi ido da tayi, cizon lips dinshi yayi sannan ya bar gurin, da ido ta bishi.

     Tana mamakin yaron, tasamu labarin abinda ta haifa ya mutu, shine sarki yasaka aka dawo da ita cikin bayi, sannan kuma taji labarin Fulani Hasiyah ta haifi yan biyu.

[6/11, 12:55 PM] My Zahrah: 🌹🥀🌷
*ƘWARƘWARAH*
                  🌼🏵️🌺
     '''{ITAMA MATAR SARKI CE}'''

Mai_Dambu
       Oum-Muwaddah


    _Allah Jiya kun bani dariya daga masu rudewa a sunan book sai Mrs Umar da tace bata fahimtar Hausar ga shafinku nan daku aka fara💋_
2️⃣
     Juyawa tayi ta koma shashin Jakadiya, ta kura mata ido! Sannan tace.
"Kina b'oye min wani sirri"

      "Kinga ki bar gidan nan ko nace ki fita ki koma inda kika fito! Barinki da nayi a raye shine kuskure na! Da na kasheki ko sa aka kashe ki, da baki kawo labari ba."
    
Wani irin kallon Rumana take bin Jakadiya dashi, sannan tace.
"Tabbas duk wanda ya ce garinsu da nisa, ba a kama shi ya kuɓu ce ba."
       Murmushi Jakadiya tayi sannan tace.
"Idan rakuminki ya b'ata.."
  "Ba sai kin juya min ba na ganshi kuma yana tsakanin ku! Sai dai wannan raƙumin ba taciwa! Kuma bazata isa ga tanadu ba, na rantse miki dole ne cin kasuwa da makiyi! Ko kuma so ko baku so sai na sai na baku mamaki"

      Dariya Jakadiya tayi sannan tace.
" Idan farauta taki kyau, ko kunkuru guduwa yake, balle kuma ke da kike a matsayin baiwa k'wark'wara ma mara kima"

       Dariya sosai Rumana tayi sannan tace..
"Aiko da girgizar kunar tafi magarya! Balle aje ga lissafi ai biyu tafi d'aya koda rabi ne, k'wark'wara da ai tafi baiwar mara daraja da kima, Jakadiya."
   Takawa tayi gaban Jakadiya ta sake murmushi musamman da taga idanun Jakadiya a waje cike da tsoro ta sake dariya sannan tace.
" Kura kenan ga tsoro, ga baka tsoro a dana tsoronki dan yanzun bani da ikon miki komai, amma tabbas lokaci zai zo kare sai ya fiki daraja."

      
  Tana gama fad'ar haka ta juya tayi ficcewarta, tana fita Jakadiya taja tsaki tana cewa.
*Hhhh! Wai bishi zatawa kunkuru gori, wato ita dan ta zauna da sarki shine tafimu kima, ayi dai mugani ina tusa zata hura wuta*

      Koda Jakadiya taje shashin Fulani ba gaya mata ba, dan akwai nufi a ranta.

    Dan haka tayi tunanin Amfani da wata damar dan cimma wata damar.

    Sosa Kai tayi sannan tace.
"Uwar d'akina! Baya goya marayu! Uwar mara uwa! Gatan marayu! Uwar d'akina daga ke babu wani kari! Duk sauran bayanai suka biyo."

       D'ago kai tayi tana kallon tana kallon Jakadiya. Gyara zama Jakadiya tayi sannan tace..
"Dama wata yar abu na gani nake son tsegumta miki! Uwar d'akina akan Jamal ne ko za a dauka mishi baiwar data manyanta"

   Wani irin kallon ta mata sannan ta kauda kanta, kafin tace.
"Wa kike ga zamu tura?!"

     "Rumanah!!!"
   A hargitse ta wurga mata kallo sannan tace.
"Baki da hankali! Rumana kuma"

   Gyad'a kai tayi sannan tace.
"Allah ya huci zuciyarki! Takawa baida lafiya! Kuma Shamaki shine akabawa rikon kwarya! Sannan Galadima yana da buri akan mulkin! Shine nake ganin idan da hali a fara koyawa Yarima Jamal sha'anin mulki, kafin lokacin da zai karbi kujerar shi, amma idan bai miki ba shi kenan! Allah ya huci zuciyarki na barki lafiya"

            Har zata mike Fulani tace mata.
"Dakata! Me yasa kika zab'i Rumana"

           Murmushi tayi a ranta tace.
*Ganin gida kare, yan zagi kura kin fad'a tarkonsu*

A zahirance kuwa tace.
"Sabida itace bazata iya cutar da jinin mai martaba ba, sannan zata kaunaci Yarima Jamal kar ya!"

       Gyara Zama Fulani tayi sannan tace.
"Wannan shawararki ta hauka ce! Taya ina Mahaifiyarshi ya kaunaci wata"

       "Allah ya kara miki nisan kwana! Domin cimma wata nasarar dole sai an sadaukar da wasu nasarorin! Idan kika amince wannan me sauki ne! Idan kika ki kuma tabbas duk wacce za'a kawo za a saye ta domin cimma kudirin su akanmu"

    Jinjina kai tayi sannan tace.
"Kije zan nime ki"

   "Ki huta lafiya! Allah ya tsare gabanki da bayanki! Hutawarki lafiya yar sarki jikar sarki, Uwar sarki matar sarki."

    Tafita Jakadiya tayi sannan ta sake murmushi tace.
"Fulani Hasiyah! Sai na lalata miki rayuwarki! Kamar yadda kika tab'a wulakantani, idan da rabon kura a takobi, sai a sayar a sayi akuya!" 

Bayan kwana takwas Fulani Hasiyah takira Jakadiya ta faɗa mata ta amince, Amma bisa sharad'i kar wata doguwar alaka ta shiga tsakanin Rumana da Jamal.

   Tun daga  ranar Aka kai Rumana Shashin Jamal take kula da duk wani abinsa, cikin so da kauna. Dan tana kewar Mai Martaba.
    
       -----
       _Bayan shekara sha shida(16Yrs)_

   Abubuwa sun faru a cikin masarautar Daura! Wasu ma bazai iya fahimtar komai ba, tun bayan faduwar Sarki Jalaludeen, sai aka nad'a Kamin shi Shamaki wato Abdu
Tsugunne yayi gaban mai martaba yana mishi alola, yana idarwa ya kalli Mai Martaba cikin murmushi yace.
"Abba na gama maka ko zamu tafi"

    Lumshe idanunshi yayi cikin jin dadi sannan ya motsa hannun shi, wanda akala kusan shekaru wani abu kenan yana zaune a gurin.
           Dan lokacin da Jamal ya gama Secondry school ya bada shawarar a fita dashi waje, kuma akayi Na'am da shawarar inda aka fita da shi, sai dai babu nasara dan an tabbatar da cewa lafiyan shi lau.

              Sabida lalura Mai Martaba Jamal yaki tafiya wata makaranta nesa da gida, yayi ƙoƙarin koya mishi abubuwan kamar idan yana son abu ya nuna da ido, ko da hannu. Na hannun ne ya gaggara. Tura shi yayi suka nufi masallaci, duk inda suka ratsa sai an zub'e domin mika musu gaisuwa, koda suka isa masallaci ya.

    Bayan sun dawo gida Ya gyarawa Mai Martaba gurin zaman shi, har zai fita "Jamal!"
Ya kira sunan shi. Cike da farin ciki ya koma gaban shi ya durkusa dakyar ya iya d'aga harshen shi yace.
   
   "Idan muguwar kaza ta shiga cikin akurki, kowacce kaza sai ta sare. Ban gaya maka haka dan karya maka gwiwa ba sai dai Sa'a tafi gata, ka zama me jin kai da tausayi. Sannan kaje gurin Magaji."

      Tunda Jamal ya fita yake bin bayan shi da ido.
_Rumanah! Kin tafi kin barni, da ace ki kika haifi Jamal da bazan zubda kwalla me yawa ba_

    Gyad'a kai yayi sannan ya mik'e tare da barin d'akin jikin s
......

    Duk abinda Mai Martaba da Jamal suka tattauna sai da Jakadiya ta kwaso tass sannan ta nufi gidan Galadima.
      Tana shiga gidan ta fara kirari.
    "Gafara dai nazo ganin Fari me farin aniya! B'auna saniyar sake! Turnuku kake, namijin zaki uban  namun daji!  Garnakaki kake kafin namijin zaki hatsabibanci! Gaba salama bayan salama"

   Gyara zama yayi cikin izza ya kalleta, sannan ya d'aga mata hannu.
"Ina jinki"
     "Kowa ya keta rigar tasa, yasan inda zare yake!"

         Kauda kai yayi sannan yace.
"A bakin  tsoho fito keda daraja, ina jinki"

            Sosai kanta tayi cikin munafunci tace.
"Ya tafi gidan Magaji"

Murmushi yayi sannan yace.
"Maganin biri, karen maguzawa! Kyaleshi Yaje"

       "Gyara zama tayi sannan tace.
"Biri a hannun malam yake guɗa, a hannun bamaguje kuka yake"

        "Hmmmm! Naji ance ana ganinki da Shamaki? Indai bazaki tsaya iya Ni ba toh bana bukatar duk wata tsegumi"

           Karya wuya tayi cikin damuwa sannan tace.
"Ina ruga basu zauna lafiya ba, ai runji zama lafiya! Duk yadda kace shi kenan."

Tana gama fad'ar haka ta bar zauren tana me bankawa kofar harara tace.
"Harbi ga dan jaki gado ne, dani kuke zance."

-----
Tunda nayi wayo muke kara kaina da mahaifiyata! Daga nan sai nan, kuma munfi ratsa dazuzzuka! Yau ma a hannun wani mutum muka fad'a wanda yake janye damu ƙamar wasu dabobi, Fad'uwa Mahaifiyata tayi na kira sunanta da Karfi.
"Annah!"Dole ya tsaya zama nayi na  daura kanta akan cinyata, ina kallon shi, kwalla cike a idanuna nace.
"Taimaka mana da ruwan sha mana baka ga yadda bakinta ya bushe bane."

   Wurga min gorar yayi sannan ya koma gefe, sai da tasha sosai sannan ya kuma wurgo mana gurasa, jikina na rawa na bata, ta dan ci sannan nima naci. 

  "Zaku iya tashi dan saura kad'an mu isa masarautar Daura"

   Haka na d'agata muka cigaba da tafiya.

        Sai da muka yi tafiya sosai sannan muka shigo garin. Ta baya muka bi, bayan ya sunce mana daurin muka cigaba da tafi, dukkan dukkan shekaruna bazasu wucce sha biyar ba,
     
                Kofar masarautar ya zauna ya kuma umarce mu  da zama, kwanciya Annah tayi a jikina tace.
"Junnuh karki tab'a cin amanar kowa ko a had'a kai dake aci amanar wani, Junnuh ki kare kimarki da darajar ki, Junnuh karki ɗaura idanunki akan abin wani! Junnuh duniya da abin cikin ta duk sha'anin ne na Ubangiji, Junnuh kiji tsoron Allah a cikin alamarinki. Banda karya indai ba dan ki wanke kanki bane, banda mugunta koda ke akawa kiyi ƙoƙari ki rama da Alkhairin! Junnuh abinda ya baki tsoro wata rana shi zai baki tausayi, abinda ya saki kuka wata rana shi zai sanyaki dariya! Junnuh Ban san duniyar da muke ba, amma karki manta ke Y'ace  mai daraja."

        Kuka nake sosai dan abinda Annah take fada min kamar wasiyya take bani,  taya zan rayu idan babu mahaifiyata.

     Tawowa suke a cikin wata mota wacce lokacin babu ita sosai a kasar Hausa, sai gidajen sarakuna, tarin da Annah take yasani mik'ewa ina cewa mutumin da ya kawo mu magana, sai wayam na gani babu shi babu labarin shi, Ma'ana yayi tafiyar shi. Bakina na rawa nace.
"Annah! Ya tafi"
   Tarin da take har dani, durkusawa nayi ina me rike da ita, nace.
"Annah karki tafi! Don Allah ki tsaya dani zan noma miki lafiya"
   Daga nesa ya hango mu, musamman Ni wacce na birkice da kuka, ina girgiza Annah.
   "Aswad! Shin me ka fahimtar da Abinda Baba Magaji yace?!"

       "Yarima Jamal! Ni kaina Hausar ta min tsauri, Wai kar Ingarma ya rena gudun kura! Abin nufi karka rena abinda yake faruwa a cikin masarautar"

"Tsaya a can da alamu, ana niman taimako ne."

     Har sun wucce suka kuma dawowa, buɗe motar yayi.
   Sautin kuka na ya sauka a kunnen shi, lumshe idanunshi yayi sannan ya buɗe a hankali ya sauko daga cikin motar ya iso inda muke kallona yayi sannan yace.
"Aswad! Zoka taimaka mu kaita cikin masarautar"

    Da sauri na mike, tare da kare Annah, na d'ago kaina na sauke akan kyakyawar fuskar shi, shima Ni yake kallo.
         Zuciyata ce tayi wani irin bugawa, lokaci guda. Sauke idanuna nayi daga fuskar shi na sunkuyar da kai na kasa dan ya cika min ido.

      Har lokacin zuciya bata bar bugawa ba, tab'a gefena yayi shida abokin shi suka dauki Annah suka sakata a cikin motar so me kama da kunkuru.

                Juyawa yayi ya kalle Ni, Sannan yace.
"Hmm! Muje"
    Wani irin abu nake ji yana yawo akaina, lokaci guda na rasa nutsuwa ta, uwa uba jin muryan shi da kayan shi ya jefani cikin wata duniya na daban, me haka ke nufi dani, halin da Mahaifiyata take ciki zanji dashi ko da halin da na fad'a sakamakon ganin wannan me kyan, na shiga tara Ni Junnuh.
 Lallai bahaushe yayi gaskiya da yace.
"Duk wanda ya koma rumbun sa, shi zai fad'i tsadar Toka"
            
   Duk da kan Annah yana cinyata bai hana idan na d'ago kaina sai mun had'a idanun mu, da sauri nake....

3️⃣

       Tsigar jikina ne ke mik'ewa, a hankali na fara ambaton Allah, har muka shiga cikin masarautar  can wani guri na daban suka kai mu sannan suka tsaya.
    Fita d'aya yayi shi yana cikin motar, hannun shi na d'an buga jikin motar. Can sai gasu tare da wata babbar mata, tana zuwa ta kalle Ni cikin sakin fuska.
      "Ke zaki iya fitowa!"

  A hankali na fito matar da me tuka motar ya taimaka mata suka fitar da Annah, can suka tafi da ita, ina tsaye kamar doluwa.
    Sai da mutumin ya dawo yace.
"Eh baki biyo mu bane! Muje na kaiki inda Mamanki take."

            A hankali nake takawa har muka kule, ji nake Kamar na juya na kalle shi amma munyi nisa, wani daki aka kai mu, inda yake cikin wasu manyan dakuna, karasawa nayi inda Annah take, tana kwance. Zama nayi ina kallon fuskarta da tayi fayau.

      Rike hannunta nayi kwalla na zubo min.
"Matsa za a dubata."

   Matsawa nayi gefe can, duk wata kulawar da take buƙatar anyi mata, amma no any holp.
        Ina ji ina gani jikinta ya kuma birkicewa, har me dubata ya mike ya fita, cusa kai na nayi cikin cinyoyina ina gigitaciyar kuka.
      "Ke dalla kiwa mutane shiru kinzo kin addabi mutane da kuk."

          Shigowar shi tare da mutanen da suka kawo da abokin tafiyar shi yasata shiru ta fara kirari.
"Lafiya zaki! Namiji uban yan boko! Giwa me tafiyar kasaita! Uba a gida Uba adawa! Lafiya Sarkinmu na gobe Yariman na yau Allah yaja zamanin Yarima Jamal."
   D'aga mata hannu yayi sannan ya kalli Likitan yace.
"Dr meye matsalar!"

     Gyara tsayuwar likitan yayi sannan yace.
"Wallahi birkicewar da numfashita yake shine abin tashin hankalin, kowani lokaci zamu iya rasata"

     Kwalla kara nayi na rarrafa zuwa inda take na rungume kanta, nace.
"Don Allah! Karku ce min zan rasata, wallahi ita d'aya gare Ni ban san kowa ba sai ita, idan na rasata zan iya shiga wani hali bani da kowa sai ita"
     Fita suka yi can sai ga wata mata sun shigo tana gaba yana Binta a baya, cikin tsantsar girmamawa.
         Zuwa tayi inda nake ta janye hannuna daga jikin Annah tace.
"Muje!"
    "Don Allah!"
    Yatsa tasa a bakinta, sannan tayi magana kasa kasa tace.
"Ke mace ce! Bai dace kina d'aga muryarki a gaban kowa ba, sannan wancan me sanye da zuleka da kaftanin Yarima ne, kuma idan kika cigaba da damun shi  zai iya sawa a hukuntaki, muje."

              Haka muka fita ina waiwayen Innata, hawaye na zuba min, har muka ficce a dakin, jingina yayi da jikin bangon, ya zuba mata ido sannan yace.
"Yanzun meye tunanin ka akan halin da take ciki?"

         Gyara tsayuwar likitan yayi sannan yace..
"Ranka shi dad'e! Zan kira abokina ko Allah zai taimaka ya mana wani abu.

           Fita suka yi bayi mata manya da yara sai zub'ewa suke a gaban shi suna kwasar gaisuwa. Hannu kawai yake iya daga musu, sannan ya isa wani madaidaiciyar daki yayi sallama, fitowa matar tayi ta barni. Can kasa da murya yace.
"Umma! Kice karta dami mara lafiyar da kuka"

        "Toh! Ranka shi dad'e"
   Duk sai yaji wani iri duk lokacin da magana ta haɗa su toh tana bashi wani irin girma ne na daban shi kuma a nashi bangaren a Uwa ya dauketa baya jin dadin kiranshi da take yi a Yarima. A hankali ya juya tare da barin kofar ta ya fita daga katangar bayin.

           "Tashi ki je! Karki yi kuka ki mata addu'a Allah zai bata lafiya kinji Y'ata"

    Gyad'a kai tayi sannan ta juya ta bar d'akin.

      .......
    Shiga Zauren Umma Fulani yayi ya sami Jakadiya tayi tsamo tsamo, kamar wacce aka zuba mata ruwan zafi.

        Wani irin kallon ya bita dashi wanda yasata mik'ewa zata bar zauren, cikin muryan shi ne sanyi yace.
"Dawo!"

          Yadda yayi maganar Ita kanta Fulani sai da ta kalle shi, balle Jakadiya.
   "Tuba nake! D'an Sarki jikar Sarki, na tuba Saraki bazan kuma ba, a yafe Min laifina."

            Bai kalleta ba ya nuna mata hanyar fita, da sauri ta fita har tana tuntube. 

    Zama yayi bai ce komai ba itama Fulanin bata ce mishi cikanka ba, sai da ya mik'e zai fita tace.
"Yarima! Yawan shekaru ba shine wayo !"
   Ita Fulani tana nufi me yasa zai yi abu bai sanar da ita ba,
     
 Dawowa yayi ya zauna cikin damuwa sannan ya gyad'a mata kai yace.
"Afuwa! Umma na, bazan kuma ba Insha Allah."

        Bata kuma kallon shi ba.
"Kafin kowa sanin guntun goro yafi, katon dutse"
       Sannan taya zai danganta alamarin ga wata ba ita ba.

    Shiru yayi cikin jin haushi amma dole yayi Hakuri dan yasan wannan aikin Jakadiya ce,
    Shigowar Jakadiya ta zub'e a gaban shi tare da cewa.
"Takawarka lafiya, Ga Aswad a waje yana jiranka"

       Kallon Umman shi yayi sannan yace.
"Allah ya hucci zuciyarki! Baza a kuma abinda zai fusata ki ba."

        "Hmm! Umma! Umma biyu amma umma d'aya tafi su."
Ni dai nice Mahaifiyarka, babu wata bayana,
    "Tuba nake! Allah ya huci zuciyarki."

    Fita yayi yana kuma jin dole ya takawa Jakadiya birki, dan ya gama fahimtar.

       Yana isa gurin Aswad, ya kura mishi ido.
      "Akwai matsala! Dan matar kamar ta rasu, itama  Yarinyar tana can cikin wani hali."

      "Ta rasu ne ko kamar ta rasu?!"
          "Muje dai mugani"
    Suna zuwa likitan na fitowa shi da abokinsa.
"Kuyi hakuri! Matar ta rasu"

       Wani gigitaciyar kuka Junnuh ta saka tare da nufar dakin, ta fad'a kan gawar Mahaifiyarta.
      "Me yasa? Wacece Ni? Me yasa baki gaya min komai ba?"

         Shiga wasu mata suka yi aka fito da ni, sannan suka kai Ni d'akin matar nan, kuka nake naji jiri ya kwashe Ni a gurin,

       Bayan wasu lokuta na farka, ina kallon dakin, a hankali abinda ya faru ya shiga dawo min.
             Mik'ewa nayi na zauna kamar wacce aka tsikare da allura na fito daga dakin, kowa sha'anin gabanshi yake, rab'ewa bango nayi na nufi dakin matar da aka saka Innata na bud'e zan sakai naji tana cewa.
"Dole! Na dakatar da Rumana, sannan shima Yarima dole nasan abinyi akanshi"

    Tura kai nayi nace.
"Ina Innata take."
Kallona tayi sannan tace..
"Taje lahira koyar sallama! Maza fice min a d'aki"

  Da sauri na fita, kallon Jakadiya tayi sannan tace.
"Jakadiya! Ya zaki yi da wannan Yarinyar."
    
"Wacce kenan?!"
    "Wannan da tashiga mana! Kinga abinda na samu a tsumokarar uwar! Sannan wannan hatimin wata."

          Karba tayi tana juyawa sannan ta duba cikin tsumokarar, tasami wani abu da suka danganci Junnuh amma dan bakar mugunta, suka kasafta kayan tsakaninsu.

      Komawa daki nayi na Zauna, tare da fashewa da kuka.

       Ina cikin kuka Matar nan ta shigo, gani ina kuka tace.
"Subhanallahi kuka kuma! Ki tuna sallah?"

Girgiza kai nayi cikin shashekar kuka nac.
"Banyi ba"

      "Tashi maza kiyi ga buta nan! Ban daki yana can gefen"
    Haka na fito daga dakin nayita kallon yadda suka rayuwar su nayi, a hankali na wuce na kama ruwa sannan na fito.



     -----
  Abinci ya gama bawa Abban shi, sannan ya kalle yadda Jamal din yake cikin damuwa.
"Jamal!"

  D'ago kan shi yayi yana kallon Abban shi, Sannan ya mai da kanshi kasa.
"Akwai matsala ko? Sannan baka gaya min abinda Magaji yace maka ba."

   Gyara zama yayi sannan yace.
"Abba! Abinda yace min gaskiya bazan iya ba! Abba Ni bana son sha'anin mulki da abinda yake cikin ta, bana son rayuwar da batawa bace."

           "Ta yaro kyau take bata karko."
         Dama yasan haka zai faru dan tun tashin Jamal bai damu da sha'anin mulki ba, gwara Jamala, ita kan tana son mulki da abinda yake cikin ta, ita badan mace ce ba, da babu abinda zai hanata zuwa hawar sallah wato itama ta hau doki.

             Abinda Ya sani dole Jamal ya zama sarki.

    "Shi kenan tunda baka son mulki! Idan nayi lissafi daidai shekaru ka 25, kuma iya kwalejin gwamnati  kimiyya da fasaha dake Katsina kayi, ko dan degree nan babu. Iya rayuwarka kana yinta ne saboda Ni, zan baka sako zuwa ga mataimakin shugaban Jami'ar AHMADU Bello kaje kaima samu ilimin da zaka kwaci kanka a hannun Jama'a."

"Abba idan na tafi waye zai kula da kai! Don Allah kabar maganar tafiya ko ina nan"

      yayi maganar kamar zai saka kuka.
"Kai ko kunya baka ji ba! Jamalah ta gama jami'ar ta, yanzun tana bautar ƙasa kai kana nan a gida kuma kace baka son mulki"

        "Shi kenan, Abba na! Amma akwai wata nauyin da na d'auka na, kuma zan tafi nabarta."

     Kallon shi yayi sa'annan yace.
"Wacece ita?!"

      Nan ya zauna ya bashi labarinta, sannan yace.
"Abbana! Mahaifiyarta ta rasu! Ban san me zan iya mata ba, kuma ina tunanin Yarinyar bata san daga inda suka fito ba!"

   Murmushi Mai Martaba yayi sannan yace.

  "Wato tsuguno bata kare ba, an sayar da kare an sayi mage,  kenan saboda ita zaka hakura da tafiyar kenan?!"

4️⃣

D'ago kai yayi sannan ya kuma sunkuyar da kanshi, kafin yace.
"Allah ya hucci zuciyarka! Tuba nake Ni me biyayya ne ga duk abinda kace min, Mai Martaba da kai da kaya duk mallakar kace, komai kace Ni meyi ne."

    Murmushi yayi sannan yace.
"Allah Yayi maka albarka! Ka shirya tafiya Zaria."

    Gyada kai yayi sannan ya yunkura zai mik'e Mai Martaba yace mishi.
"Baka gaya min ma zaka karanta ba!"

  Karamar murmushi yayi sannan yace.
"Abbana! Duk abinda kaga ya dace dani kace na karanta Ni zan karanta!"

      Duk cikin Ƴaƴan shi babu kamar Jamal! Duk da su Goma ne shine na goman  din su, amma yafisu hakuri da nutsuwa, Uwa Uba tausayi da kamala, sai abinda yake daure mishi kai ba komai bane sai rashin son mulkin Jamal, abin yana matukar damun shi, a wani lokacin kuma idan yayi abu sai yaga Kamar Rumanah ce, musamman kwaryar idanun shi, farar fatar jikin shi, maganar shi da mu'amalar shi da kowa, sam bai dauki izza ya daurawa kanshi ba, dan  babbar Y'ar Shi.
Rukayyah tana matuƙar Adawa da Jamal.

    "Shi kenan! Na zab'a maka Kimiyyar siyasa" ya faɗa yana kallon shi,
    "Godiya nake da zab'inka Allah ya kara maka nisan kwana ya kuma baka lafiya ina godiya akan haka da kuma kasancewar ka mahaifina Na Barka lafiya"

"Allah yayi maka albarka"

           Bayan fitar shi ya juya tare da ɗaukar diary din shi ya fara rubutu.

        -------
Masarautar Daurah.
An kafa shine tun shekaru dari biyu,B.C ma'ana (Before Crisis, shine B.C,  A.C. yana nufin After Crisis, kafin yakin duniya)

   Tun kafin shigowar *BAYAJIDA* dan haka masarautar ta cigaba mulki har zuwa lokacin Dauramah itace mace ta farko da ta mulki yanki daura da wasu sassar su, har wucewar karnin na 1720 lokacin zuwan Usmaniyya wato Usman D'an fodio, ya samu ratsa mulkin shi har cikin masarautar, har dai Turawan mulkin mallaka suka shigo yankin (Kuyi hakuri ban yi zurfi cikin binciken ba ga dai abinda na samo) har zuwa lokacin da aka sami Hausa Bakwai_ tare da banza bakwai! ( A YAU INA RUBUTU AKAN WASU ALA'ADUN HAUSA! MUSAMMAN KARIN MAGANA DA AKA YI MANA A PRIMARY DA SECONDRY! WAI KUN MANTA BAKU GANE ZUB'E KO HARSHEN DAMO BA! CHINA DA INDIA! SUN CIGABA NE SABIDA HARSHEN SU! A WANI IRI DA NA TAB'A SHIGA A SHAFIN FATIMA Eladan,  munyi musayan kalamai da wata mazauniyar Goa! Ta gaya min baƙin cikinta akan Yadda hausawa suka manta da Hausa! Lokacin ban tab'a tun rubutu ba! Dan har nake ce mata taya zamu gyara tace bazamu iya ba! Kuyi kokarin saukar Da Application na karin maganar Hausa! Kuna rubutawa short mit kuma bayarwa yaranmu dan zasu taimaka musu Am so sorry ina Kishin Yarena ne! Bayan Uwata Yaren Hausa itace Uwata ta biyu da nake girmamawa musamman harshen 😊😘 Duk wacce tayi korafi zan sanyata a Banza Bakwai 😎🙄)
   
Fulani sun amshi mulkin masarautar inda aka samu auratayya tsakaninsu da wasu sarakunar Hausarwa, a hankali zamani ya cigaba da juyawa har sarautar ta fadu kan sarki.
Aminullah Mahaifin Sarki Jalaludeen.

    Sarki Aminullah shima shi ɗaya ne namiji a cikin mata tara a dakin su, sannan yana da yan uwa wanda suke yan uba. Maza da mata, kasancewar yana da yayu manya Maza,  Umar, da Abdulhakeem,  sannan kanen shi maza da mata.

     A lokacin Mahaifiyar su Umar ita ce da gidan, kuma take baza mulkinta son ranta, mulki yasa Abdulhakeem da Umar kin tsayawa suyi karatu, dan haka lokacin da aka zo diban yara za a turasu karatu sai aka had'a da Aminullah, zuwa Ingila domin yayi karatu dan sun yi karatu primary a kasar bishiya kuma yana cikin dalibai masu Hazaka, dake Uwarsu bata da iko a gidan yaɗata ta zuba ido aka fitar dashi daga cikin gidan zuwa London, uwa uba tana kunyar tayi magana ya zama abin gori, asali ma ita da Yaranta basu da kima a idanun Sarki Habibullah, sam baya nuna musu kauna, dan Yayar Aminullah bata son mijinta haka ya bada aurenta ga d'an Sarki Zazzau, haka ya cigaba da nuna wariya a cikin yaran shi har girma ya cimma shi, Aminullah kuwa bakin cikin abinda Baban su yake nuna musu ya hana shi dawowa koda ya gama karatun shi zama yayi ya fara aiki da wata Oxford University. Daga shekaru 1955-1961.
    Wani bikin sallah da akayi Sarki Habibullah ya fado daga, bayan dokin shi, yana faduwa dokin tana take mishi kirji, a shekara 1962, kwana shi bakwai a cikin asibitin dauran wanda Turawa suka gini

(kun gane ina amfani da number shekaru dan wasu dalilai dan haka ku rike haka zai muku amfani idan muka fad'a chakwalkwalin chakwakiyya dan bazan dawo baya dan warware abinda na gama ginawa ba, idan kina da hawan ruwa manta kawai dan zaki gamu da sambad'e bad'e a labarin kun san sarakuna no excuse)

Rasuwar Sarki Habibullah ya tilastawa Aminullah dawowa gida, akayi duk wata abinda ya dace.
    Kwanan shi goma sha biyar ya tattara kayan shi zai koma aka dakatar da shi sabida zab'ar sabon Sarki Aminullah ya je Pada ne da niyyar zab'an Umar dan shine ake ganin Yafi dama dama akan Abdulhakeem, sai kuma zab'en ya koma kan shi, dole suka hakura, amma maganar gaskiya Hausar nan ce da ake cewa.
"Kare zai mutu da haushin kura"
  Shine abinda ya faru, sai Ya zamana ana samun takun saka tsakinmshi da yan uwan shi abinda ya haifar da k'iyayyae zafi tsakanin su Abdulhakeem.

     Lokacin da aka nad'a shi an so ya tara Mata yace fir bai san da haka ba, asalima matar shi ba jinin sarauta bace, mahaifinta malami ne, yazo daga can Sokoto, da aka dame shi da surutu akan me yasa zai auri yar baƙi, abinda ya fito bakin shi. Ya sanyayya musu jiki dan yace.
"Me zan yi da Yar attajirai! Idan tazo ba wani abu zata min ba sai nuna min ita wata ce dan babanta wani ne, Idan jinin Sarauta ce burinta ta haifi magajin masarautar ba! Bata da abinda tasani sai mulki komai sai an mata tsabar son mulki, kuma babu ruwan ta da d'an kowacce mace ita idan D'anta zai zama sarki na kowa ma ko oho, Ni kaina na tashi da k'iyayyar Mahaifina! Yaje masarautar su Ya auro Mahaifiyata, amma sabida Haihuwar Ƴaƴa Mata ya juya mata baya. Idan Ƴar Sarkin Malamai na aura tana zuwa zata fara jin ai zata yi watsi da al'adun da ta karanta, N auri Dijeh badan komai ba, sai dai ina da yakinin bazata haifa min matsala ba."

  Dan dole suka kyale shi, sai da ya shekaru sha biyu mulki, Ya rasu dan shi bai tara Mata ba, dan lokacin  Jalaludeen ne babba sai Sa'adatu, sai Fatimah.

    Bayan rasuwar Sarki Aminullah wanda shima ya Fado ne akan doki, ta take mishi ciki 

     A lokacin ba aka bawa Abdulhakeem Sarauta, sai da yayi shekaru bakwai shima ya fado daga doki dan shi da wuya ya fado, tun har lokacin Jalaludeen bai kai da gama makaranta ba, sai aka bawa Umar mulki, kuma kowanne su yana da Yara, Shamaki shine babban dan Abdulhakeem, shi kuma Umar bai da d'a namiji sai Ƴaƴa Mata, dan haka aka bashi, abin da ya razana mutanen daura ba komai bane sai mai-maituwar tarihin fadowa daga dokin ya sanya su sakai ayar tambaya akan Anya masarautar basu yi wani abu da ya shafi tsafi ba ko kuma wata tsatsuba, abin ya d'aga hankalin duk wani me ruwa da tsaki a masarautar, kuma abin mamaki da aka fara bincike an samu sanyawa hannun mutane da aljanun wanda suke bibiyar duk wani sarki da zai shigo masarautar, musamman idan ya kasance matar sarkin ta kasance kyakyawa a cikin Masarautar, koda kuwa k'wark'wara ce, toh tabbas sarki zai fado.

  Dan haka sai wasu daga cikin malamai suka bawa sabon sarki Jalaludeen shawarar kar ya kuskura ya nemi kyakyawa ya nemi mace mara yalwanta kyau.

    Sarki Jalaludeen yana da mata huɗu rus, Fulanin Hasiyah itace Uwar gida, kuma Yar sarkin Kano tana da yara Shida, dan tayita wabi(ma'ana tayita haihuwa suna mutuwa) Sai Fulani Bilkisu Yar sarkin Kwantagora, tana da Y'a d'aya dan da zaran sun haihu na farko basu kuma haihuwa. Sai Fulani Adamah Yar sarkin Gombe itace ma har ta haifi yara biyu, sai Fulani Furerah tana yar cikin masarautar ce, dan kanwar Wazirinsa ce, tana da Y'a daya.  Dake lokacin idanun shi ya rufe da son D'a namiji sai ya nemi sha

Yaran Fulani kuwa(Wallahi kar wata tace min ta rud'e) sai k'wark'warah shi Rumana, duk cikin matan sarki babu mace kyakyawa iri Rumanah,  sai da matan sarki suka haifi mata, takwas kafin Fulani ta haifi Jamal da Jamalah. 

        Dan lokacin da sarki yake son haihuwar d'a namiji,  ga duk matan shi mata suke haifa, dan haka yaje ya sami magaji da matsalar shi, babbar damuwar sarki gorin d'a namiji da ake mishi, kuma dole idan ya mutu a bawa wani daga cikin gidajen nan guda uku, Waziri da Gidan Galadima, ko kuma Shamaki.

  Kasancewa babu wani d'a namiji babba a cikin masarautar sai Shamaki, yasa hankalin Sarki Jalaludeen ya mutukar tashi.

       "Magaji kafin kowa sanin halin da nake ciki, har yanzun bani da d'a namiji kuma lokaci na kara ja baya."
 Gyara zama yayi sannan ya zubawa Sarki Jalaludeen idanu, kafin yace.
"Tabbas kana cikin damuwa! Shugabana ina ganin kayi hakuri da d'a namijin mana! Domin idan ka takura kanka da son D'a namiji za a iya samun shi!"

Mik'ewa Sarki Jalaludeen yayi zuwa bakin tagar dakin magaji, ya dage hannun shi guda yana kallon sintirin mutanen, tasowa Magaji yayi ya dafa kafad'arshi yana cewa.
"A cire batun shugabanci! Jalaludeen matanka hud'u ras!  Kuma"
     Juyowa Jalaludeen yayi ya zuba mishi ido sannan yace.
"Kuskure ne idan na nemi baiwa na yantatta!"

   Rike hannunsa Magaji yayi cikin damuwa yana fad'in.
"Shine mafi girman kuskuren da zaka aikata kuwa a rayuwarka! Baka san kai har kafi sauran tsawon rai bane? Abdulhakeem shekarun shi, nawa ya fado a doki? Umar Shekarun shi nawa ya fado a doki, don Allah ka hakura da niman Baiwa, domin rikicin shekaru dari da hamsin zai shafe ka."


      "Kenan kasan wani abu akan masarautar nan?! Da kuma dalilin da yasa sarakuna suke fadowa A doki?!"

     "A'ah! Bani da amsoshinka! Bani da abinda zan ce maka! Amma tabbas masarautar tana cikin masarautun da a shakara (1675) kamar yau (1975) haka aka fara samar da irin matsalarka, kuma abin ya faru ne akan sarakunar Maguzawar da aka amshi mulki a hannun su tare da taimakon wasu hadiman su, don Allah kayi hakuri idan ka nace zaka samu amma kai ma dole ka mutu dan Alwashinsu ne haka, me yasa haka bai faru da kai ba, sabida duk cikin matan ka babu kyakyawar da ake bukata, idan ka kuskure shawarata. Akwai Matsala babba"

      "Magaji! Ita matsalar da kake gudun samuwar toh shikenan! Zan kiyaye faruwar haka."

    "Yawwa Abokina, kayi hakuri Allah zai kawo maka d'a namiji har cikin gidan ka."

   Da wannan tattaunawar suka rabu, Magaji d'an uwan Sarki Jalaludeen ne, ta gurin kakar sarki Dijeh, kuma malami ne sosai. 
         ----
          Bayan shekara biyar 1979.......

[6/11, 12:55 PM] My Zahrah: 🌹🥀🌷

5️⃣
        Tafiya ta kama sarki Jalaludeen zuwa bakin iyakar inda yake mulka, tafiyar da kai shi har iyakar Nijar a tafiyar ne ya had'u da wasu fulani, dake mutum ne me daɗin zama,  abinda ya zaunar da shi bayan ya   sayi nono, ba komai bane sai Rumanah! Dan tunda suka haɗu ya ganta da kayan saƙi, tunda ya ganta hankalin shi ya tafi gareta, dan ganin farko ya fad'a sonta, amma babu yadda ya iya dan matan shi huɗu ne, kuma ya rufe kofa.

    Sarkin Pada shi ya bashi shawara ya karb'eta a saɗakarshi, dukda yaso k'in amincewa, sai da sarkin Pada ya samu nasara akan shi, har ya nemi ta, su kuma iyayen ta suka bashi ita.

Lokacin bata wucce shekaru sha huɗu ba, haka yazo ya sakata a cikin matan shi dukda ita bata cikin su.

     Sai da ya barta ta cika shekara sha bakwai, sannan ya sanya Jakadiya ta kawo mishi, ita.

      ..... Har wani al'amari me girma ya shiga tsakaninsu.

       A sannu komai yake tafiya, bayan lokaci me tsayi dan har Sarki Jalaludeen ya cire tsammani da samun d'a namiji sai gashi Fulani ta Haifa ita kuma Rumana ta rasu.

A Ƴaƴan shi mata tara ne sai Jamal na goman su.
Rukayyah,Hadizah, UmmulKhair, Zakiyah, Raihanah, Hafsah, Sai Ummi Salmah, A'isha, Sai Jamalah, Jamal,

  

        -----
Kwalla ya goge sannan ya ajiye alkalami hannun shi, yana me kurawa Table din d'akin kallo.
Murmushi yayi,  a yaran shi yafi son Jamal da Jamalah, sai Raihanah sune kad'ai yake jin dadin su, amma Rukayyah da sauran Yan uwanta baya jin daɗin su, asalima nunawa suke yafi kaunar su Jamal ko. Sam baya jin dad'i irin kallon da amma zai cigaba da musu addu'a.
 
     ------
Tun bayan sallah Isha na kwanta ina goge kwalla na, gefe d'aya ina tuna iya rayuwar da muka yi da Annah, a hankali barci ya fara dauka na, har dai yayi nasarar
Yin gaba dani.

       Ji nayi an tab'a ni, a hankali na buɗe ido, fili na gani sai wata mace da wasu gungun mutane sun kewaye wani mutum me sanye da fatar b'auna, sai kan shi da ya makala. Kan damisa, yana tsaye, juyawa take tana niman tsira babu hanya,

      "Lallai Zulumi! Ka aikata kusakura wanda zai jagoranci al'ummar kasar ka, ka kyale ni na tafi inda maka fito."

  Dariya ya saka sosai sannan yace.
"Ziriya! Ban tab'a alkawari ban cika ba"

      Kuka tasaka sannan ta zub'e kan gwiwar ta, ta shiga rera kuka .
   Shigowar wani dattijo sanye Da Kayan saki da hula me habb'ar kada, yasa kowa ya juya.
 
   "A'ah! Zulum! Ita din tankar Abin bauta ce! Dan haka ka kyale mu zamu bar maka kasar ka."

 Dariya ya saka har da mutanen da suke gurin, sannan yace.
"Dole sai na cimma burina! Dan na mai da ita ƙwarƙwarah ta, har na tsawon kwanaki yanzun kuma zan karasa abinda nayi niyya, dakaru ku kame min Sarkin bokaye."

         Daga nan ban kuma fahimtar abinda ya faru ba, sai dai naga wata mata tabi wani hanya na daban dan haka nayita Binta, sanye take da doguwar riga Ja idan tana tafiya rigar na jan kasa sannan yana fidda ruwan jini....
  Yadda take tafiya a hankali gashinta har gadon Bayan ta, ga wani irin kambu na zinariya akanta, a haka muka Kure bango masarautar, inda naga wani kyakyawar doki fari tass dashi, sai wani kujerar zinari, ga wasu mutane a durkushe a gabanta, zama tayi a kujerar.
    Kanta da a sunkuye gashinta ya rufe fuskarta.

   Sake kallon mutanen da suke gurin nayi naga sun kuma cika fiye da farkon zuwanta, ciro idanu nayi,. Jin muryan Annahta naji tana cewa.
"Karki kuskura! Karki jefa kanki cikin abinda bai shafe ki ba."
 Buɗe baki nayi  tare da juya baya zan duba ta inda muryanta yake fitowa
Zan juya kenan sai naji an dafani. Kara na kwalla wanda ya tilasta Ni farkawa.  Tare da kame jikina ina hakki.
"Junnuh! Lafiya kuwa! Zaki farka babu salati"

       Tana magana ne tare da kara hasken wutar d'akin, wul naga wuccewar abu da sauri na koma jikinta na b'oya jikina na rawa.

        Shafa kaina tayi cikin mamaki sannan tace.
"Junnuh! Me nene?! Wani abu ya faru ne?!"

          "Umma! Tsoro nake ji! Nayi mafarkine."
  Shafa cukurkuɗaɗen gashina tayi sannan tace.
"Aitoh addu'a zaki yi ba wai ki tsorata ba! Kuma idan kika tsorata haka ai zaki cutar da lafiyar ce."

         A hankali take tofa min addu'o'i har barci yayi gaba dani.
    Kwantar dani tayi sannan ta d'auki fitilar aci balbal ɗinta ta fita, shashin Jakadiya ta nufa.

           Ko tsoro babu a ranta, tana zuwa bata buga ba ta shiga, a firgice Jakadiya ta mike daga kwanciyar da take.

        Zubawa juna ido suka yi kafin Jakadiya tace.
"Rumana! A cikin talatin min dare nan Lafiya?!"

"Ita Yarinyar ce?! Bakuwar Jamal ce! Kuma bana son wani abu ya kuma samu ta domin kamar sarki na karaga ne, ba a hana ungulu kewaye shi"

        Murmushi Jakadiya tayi sannan ta mik'e tace.
"Gargad'i kika zo min?! Ko shawara kika kawo min?! Zan fi tunanin umarni kike bani?!
Idan zaki yasan zai samu abincinsa cikin sarƙaƙiya, da babu abinda zai fidda shi sarari"

     "Daga fake ruwa, sai shuka kabewa! Karki fara zaren ba kalar kayan bane."

     "Rumana me kika dauki kanki,? Wato Samun sarari kuturu da ga’da a cikin rama."

  Dariya Rumana tayi sannan tace.

"Ba a ‘kwace wa kuturu zobensa! Sabida shi yasan yadda ya sameta ya kuma"

            "Ganin damar kare kura tace mishi mayye, dan haka ki ficce min a d'akina, ko kuma abinda kike tuhumata na kai ki kara ga sarki."

     Har gabanta ta isa sannan tace.
"Zan so haka! Idan zai taimaka min, sai dai kwanannen abinci bata Zaki bace! Dan shi ke fita farauta yaci ya barwa sauran jama'a! Jakadiya abinda ya faru shekaru aru-aru! Kar ya shafi Jamal ko wani wanda nake da yakinin yana tare dani! Idan ba haka ba zan fusata zanyi abu, kisanni bafulatar daji ce! A cikin dawa! Aka haife ni, dan haka Kato uku nake, gagara ‘dauri."

    "Lallai Rumana!"


  A fusace tayo kanta,  tsabar tsoro sai da Jakadiya ta manne da bango, tana kallon idanun.

 "Zan tafi ne sabida yarinyar da na bari a daki! Jakadiya kuyi kokarin sanya kanki cikin duniyar Jamal sai kin fuskanci mutuwar kaskanci."

"Rumana! A shekaru na 1787 Sarki Zulum ya mutu bayan ya kashe Zuriyar 1778, A shekara 1987 aka Haifi Jamal! Hahaha ita kuma Yarinyar indai lissafin dai dai ne- shekaru sha biyar ya bata, Ni kaina ban isa goge zanen kaddarar su ba, dan fansa ce da take mace! Karki bari tarihi ya mai-maita kan shi."

     Tsaki Rumana tayi tare da ficcewa a dakin, murmushi Jakadiya tayi sannan tace.
"Na rantse sai kun biya jinin da kuka zubda"

              Tana shiga dakin ta same Ni ina sauke ajiyar zuciya.
        Zama tayi a kusadani, kanta ya gama kuncewa. Dole Junnuh ta bar Masarautar domin kuwa zamanta a cikin masarautar zai iya haifar da wani abu na daban.

   -----
Washi gari..
 Da wuri ta fita zuwa gidan Jamal, tana shiga ta same shi kallon shi tayi sannan ta wucce inda zata dafa mishi ruwan karyawa.
     

 Bayan awa guda ta fito dauke da tire, ta ajiye mishi.
   Zama yayi dan yana jin daɗin abincinta.

 Sai da ya gama, sannan ya kai tiren din da yaci abincin.

"Umma! Wani sati zan tafi zaria karatu"

     "Ubangiji ya bada sa'a! Ya kuma ba da abinda aka je nima"

     "Amin Ya Allah!"

      Shiru ne ya gifta a tsakanin su, can yace.
"Sai naga kamar akwai abinda yake damunki!"

      "Hmm! Dama Yarinyar can ce, inda hali tayi nesa da wannan duniyar."
       
   "Shi kenan! Zan kaita gida Raihanah, a can kaduna."

 Ajiyar zuciya ta sauke sannan ta cigaba da aikinta shima kuma ya fahimci iya abinda take buƙata kenan sai ya bar inda take.
      ----
  Gyara zama Jakadiya tayi sannan tace.
"Ranki shi dad'e! Ko zan kawo miki Yarinyar da Yarima Jamal."

"Sabida tana kanki! Umma na a dakatar da Jakadiya."

       Kallon shi Fulani tayi sannan tace.

"Shi kenan! Haka yayi Jakadiya kinji dai ya dakatar dake"

       Gyad'a kai tayi sannan tace.
"Allah ya huci zuciyarka!"

  Mik'ewa tayi tabar zauren Fulani, tana fita waje tace.
"Tabbas idan kaji mutum na tsoron dare ba a, daure shi ya kunce bane! Hmm dole sai kun biya zunubin da kuka aikata."

       Bayan fitar ta ya gyara zama tare da gaida ta, sannan yayi shiru. Itace ta gaji da shirun tace.
"Jiya baka shigo ba"

    Gyara zama yayi sannan yace.
"Afuwa! Umma wani Uzuri ya rike Ni"

 "Wani irin Uzuri ne haka?!" Ta wurga mishi tambaya,

        Sunkuyar da kai yayi sannan yace.
"Zan koma makaranta ne! Shi yasa na fita tura sakona."

    Jinjina kai tayi sannan tace.
"Allah ya taimaka!"

   Kamar bazata kuma magana ba tace mishi.
"Ita yarinyar fa! Zaman me take yi."
   D'ago kai yayi sannan ya kuma sunkuyar da kan shi yace..
"Umma ta faɗa min inda danginta suke za'a mai data can ."
                Har ya bar dakin bata ce mishi komai ba, sai ma binshi da tayi da ido. Sam tsoro yanayin shi ke bata, musamman shirun sa wanda yake nuna mata alamar yana cikin damuwa ko kuma wani dabi'ar da take tunani.
     Kauda tunanin tayi a ranta.

      ......
Tunda na tashi mafarkin da nayi yake yawo a kaina, a hankali na fara bin hanyar da matar cikin mafarkina take bi, nima na shiga bi. Sam bana jin komai sai karar takunta, bana jin wani abu sai sautin jan doguwar rigarta, har na isa inda na tsaya ina kallon wata tsohuwar bishiyar kuka, daga shi sai ganuwar masarautar, dai dai inda aka saka kujerar zinari nan.

       Ashe bayan fitowa ta, wasu bayi suka je niman Umma aka gaya mata, lokacin tana tare da Jamal da Aswad. Wanda suka zo gaya mata zasu tafi Zaria tafiyar ujila ta kama su, Balaraba take ce mata.
"Rumana! Ga bakuwar ki can a bayan ganuwa kusada wancan shaidaniyar Bishiyar kukan."

  A gigice ta juya da sauri suma suka rufa mata, baya koda suka zo nayi nisa a ganin abinda yafi karfina, dan a jikin Ganuwar nan naga jini na kwaranya, kuka da ihun sautin mace nake jiyowa, jikina na rawa, sosai dafani da akayi ya sani zub'ewa.......

6️⃣
   "Ranka shi dad'e! Naji Yarima Jamal yana cewa zai tafi birnin zazzau domin b'idar ilimin zamani"

    Tashi yayi zaune yana kafe ta da ido, murmushi yayi sannan yace.
"Tashi kije"

     "Godiya nake!"
Fita tayi sannan ta saki murmushi tace.
"Idan ba a rabu da sa ba, ai a rabu da igiya"
   
      Dariya ta sake sannan ta nufi gidan Galadima, da tashiga gidan kai tsaye d'akin baiwar.

    Tana shiga gidan ta wucce dakin Tabaiye.
"Lalle maraba! Da bakin hantsi"

   Washe hakorar tayi sannan tace.
"Tabaiye! Fansar zuri'anmu ta kusan tabbata, dan an sake haihuwar ziriya."

     Murmushi matar da aka kira da Tabaiye ta girgiza kai sannan, tace.
"Ai an jima da haihuwar Zulum."

       "Toh! Amma bani tunanin tarihi zai mai-maita kan shi"
   "Zai mai-maitu! Na rantse sai ya faru"

   Shiru tayi tana kallon ta, kafin ta cigaba da abinda take yi.

"Taya kika san da haka."

         "Jiya Yarima Jamal ya kawo wasu baki Uwa da ƴa, amma uwar ta rasu a jiyan sai Ita."

    Shigowar wata baiwa yasa Jakadiya juya maganar da cewa.
"  Ri ya rin Ray  E ca, War Yak yak,  mar ka ri ya zi."
(Ga zaurance nan🤣😂 karku manta koyo nake)

    "Ke! Sahura Kes da ga!"
   "Wallahi! Na bi ki shi ba tsen ra"
        "Na ta ta rau sa ma zun ya?!"
Mik'ewa tayi sannan tace..
"Da yar ki ne ba?!"
"Bu ha! Wu yar na, da yar ki na ma"
      "Go na di! Sai so!"

Sannan ta fita daga gidan tana cewa.
" Na barki lafiya!"

"Nima haka"

     -----
"Subhanallahi tare ta mana Jamal"
   Inji Umma ta faɗa da karfi, tana me isowa, d'ago Ni yayi yaga fuskana tayi fayau.
     "Dole tabar masarautar nan! Dan kar wani abu ya sameta."

       "Ko zamu kaita gurin Baba malam ne, kamar matsalar mutanen b'oye." Inji Aswad,
  
          Tsananin kunyar Umma yasa shi mikawa Aswad Junnuh yace.
"Karb'eta muje"

Tunda D'an Uwan Baban Aliyah yazo naga takaina, domin zai zauna ya ta bina da  ido, babban mayafi Maman Aliya ta bani ina yaga, sabida kare jikina.

        Ranar da na cika wata guda sai gasu sun zo ashe wai an koma makaranta ne, Alhamdulillah dan yanzun ina gane komai dan ma wancan mayyen yana kara koya min wasu abubuwa.

       Tunda yaga su Aswad yake murna, sannan ya shiga rabtab'o musu bayanin ai shi ya haukace a kaina Aswad yasa baki mana, dan bani kula shi, gudun shi nake.
            "Toh ai sai kaje ka cinyeta! Tunda kamar ka boyeta kake ji" Jamal ya bashi amsa,

             Fitowa nayi na gaishe su, sannan na koma ciki, tunda na gan shi zuciyata kamar ta fado daga kirjina, haka kawai naji ina son nayi kuka, sai da nayi me nayi sosai sannan na goge idanuna, ina zaune Aliyah ta shigo.
"Yaya Jannart! Wai ki zo Inji Kawu Yarima"

      A hankali na mike muka fita tare, basu falon bin bayan shi nayi suna cikin motar su, a sannu nake takawa har na isa jikin motar, na buɗe sannan na shiga.

      Tunda muka fara tafiya yake tsaki.
"Toh yallabai tsaka kayi hakuri Wallahi dodon kunne na zai tashi aiki"

    Buga jikin motar yayi ya juye da kan shi  baya cikin  masifar da Aswad bai san yana da shi ba yace..
"Wallahi na kuma jin kin sakewa wani fuska yana bada labarin zak'in muryanki sai na."

    "Wai meye matsalar ka ne, dan yana sonta sai ka shiga mata fad'a ai ba ita ta kira shi ba, wannan abin ba dad'i wallahi jifa yaushe ta rasa mahaifiyarta kasata a gaba kana mata fad'a akan abinda bata da laifi"

  "Dalla kayi min shiru! Baka ji sunan da yakirata da shi bane wai Jannart! Uban shine ya rad'a mata suna nayi magana kana mai da Alamarina karami!"

    "Koma meye Jannart din ma yafi wancan sunan, kuma ka daina haka ba dadi kaji yadda take kuka mana Abin duniya mene ne? Kaza ta jawo muzuru kwanan gida."
    
"Toh wallahi ba dai wannan kazar ba, kuma na kara jin yayi wani magana sai na nuna mishi danyen kai,"

            Me Aswad zai yi ban da dariya sannan yace.
"Ko dai ko dai!"
     Zai yi magana ya kuma kware, tari ya fara, tare da rike kan shi. Cikin karamar murya yace.
"Ka gani ko! Ban saba rigima ba, amma zuwan wancan Yarinyar tasani zan kware meye matsalar ne, tayi karamar da maganar soyayya, tasan kanta ma tukun ko"

            Tunda yake masifar shi nake kuka ina jin ban kyauta mishi ba.....

9️⃣
       "Zan ci mutuncin ka! Kafita hanyata ya ina mata fad'a kana min maganar banza ko dai kaniya! Kana ganin Yarinyar wani na shirin lalata mata rayuwa."

      "Haba Jamal! Ai haka bai dace ba kuma kasan Samim kanin mijin Maman Aliya ne, kuma babu yadda zata bari haka ta faru kawai wani abu na daban ya rufe maka ido kake tunanin wani abu na daban"
Inji Aswad,

      Juyawa yayi yana kallon Aswad idanun shi sun yi jajjur, sannan ya mai da kan shi can gefe, yana ciccira har suka isa Queen Amina College.

     Karku manta a lokacin makarantu biyu ne ake ji dashi a cikin arewa daga 1990 zuwa ƙasa.(Ku rike numbers din)

             Daga shi a lokacin sai Barewa College, shine na maza.
  
Har cikin makarantar suka shiga da ita, suka je kai tsaye Office d'in  shugaban makarantar.

     Juyawa yayi ya kalle ni cikin jin haushi yace.
"Ranki shi dad'e! Gimbiya Jannart sai ki mana shiru tun da nake ban tab'a  dogon magana ba amma zuwan ki nayi dogon surutu."
     
    Yana gama fad'ar haka yaja min tsaki yafita. Cusa kai nayi ina shasheka, ban san dawowar su ba sai ji nayi sun ya buga motar wanda ya sani razana, na d'ago kaina ina kallon Aswad wanda ran shi ya kai kololowar ɓaci.
            Tsaki yayi cikin ku in kula, yace.
"Ban san kana yi ba! Sai ka rufe Ni da duka"

     Karfi da yaji ya zama masifaffe, ina jinsu har muka bar makarantar, idanuna zafi suke saboda kuka. Amma bani da tacewa.

       Koda muka dawo gida sai da Maman Aliya tayi mishi fad'a sosai sannan ya sauko daga dokin zuciyar da ya hau.

     Ni kam ɗaki na shiga bazan iya jarabar shi, tunda na shiga na kwanta kaina ya fara ciwo kamar zai tsage.
        Ganin wasu irin mutane nake sun fara, sheeeeee naji sautin jan rigar ta.
   Buɗe ido nake son yi amma babu halin yin haka dan dole na rufe, daga nan ban kuma sanin inda nake ba, sai da na bude ido naga Shi a can, can gefe ya hard'e hannunshi a kirjinshi.

            Mik'ewa nake son yi amma na kasa dan dole na koma na kwanta, tare da rufe idanuna.

       "Sannu! Jannart ya jikin naki"
     Inji Maman Aliyah, 
     
   "Da sauki" nace mata,
   "Ina ke miki ciwo yanzun?!"
        "Babu!"
       Murmushi tayi min, sannan ta fita, kallon d'akin nayi naga ashe d'akin mu, goge zufar da ya karyo nayi.
           
           Wato wannan mutumin yana tsaye ko Sannu bai ce min ba, sai ma b'ata rai da yayi, wato irin shi ba zai min yaji ba, ina jin Aswad yana ce mishi  "ya jikinta"

Daga bakin kofar dan ya hana shi shigowa.
"Hmm"
Yace a d'an hasale, dan haka na rufe ido na. Sabida tsoron shi da nake ji,

        Fita yayi abin shi kamar bai damu da halin da nake ciki ba, har ya fita sai kuma ya d'ago labule ya kalle Ni tare da tanke fuska yace.
"In dan makarantar kike wannan farfagandar ki daina! Domin haka bazai kare ki da kome ba, ki tashi kiyi karatu dan shine gatanki! Ni nawa taimako ne bawai na tsaya zuba ido akan wasu al'amura ba, kiyi yaki da kanki domin tsayawa da kafarki."
   Yana fada min magana nifa ba Fahimta nake yi ba, idanuna na kan bakin shi da fuskarshi, ina kallon yadda yake juya su, tare da kifkifta idanu.

    Haka kawai sai naga yayi min kama da Umma da na zauna a d'akin ta.

        Har ya sake labulen ban san ina nake ba, sai da Maman Aliya tashigo sannan ta taimaka min na tashi, abinci ta bani sannan na fara ci ta dan kalle ni sannan tace.
"Ko baki son makarantar ne?!"
    Girgiza kai nayi tare da ajiye lomar abincin hannuna nace.
"Ina so!"
    "Sosai ko dan ki farantawa Jamal zaki?!" Ta wurga min tambaya,
    
"A'ah Mamah zanje da duk zuciyata ne"

Rike hannuna tayi cikin wannan irin damuwa tace.
"Ya damu sosai, da yaga kin suma har yake ganin kaman laifin shine da yayi ta miki fad'a."

  Murmushi yayi sosai sannan na gyara zama nace.
"Allah Mama ba laifin shi bane, kawai ciwo ne kawai!"

       "Amma yace za a fasa kai ki makarantar"
      Sunkuyar da kai nayi sannan nace.
"Ina son makarantar!"
     "Kin tabbatar!"
  Gyad'a mata kai nayi, alamar eh.

           Abinda Ya faru da ni kuwa, tunda naga wasu irin mutane a kaina nan sume, shirun da Maman Aliyah taji ne yasa ta zuwa. Ta duba ni shine ta same Ni a sume.

           Ta fita ta gaya mishi yana zuwa ya tambaye ta ina ta ajiye Rubutun da yazo dashi, shine ta dauko ya bata ta shafa min.

               ++++
Bayan an gama min sayayya sannan suka sanya Ni a gaba har da ita, aka kawo ni makaranta.
      Wannan zuwan namu ne naji faduwar gaba, ina kallon cikin makarantar.
         
     Har Office d'in shugaban makarantar muka je, sannan yayi komai. Gurin saka suna ne ya kalli Maman Aliyah da Aswad yana rike da alkalami. Sai ya kalle ni sai ya kuma kallon su sannan ya kalli Shugaban makarantar.

        Zuwa inda yake Maman Aliyah tayi, ta amshi alkalamin.

   Sannan ta sanya sunan na kafin shi ya zama wakili na, Jannart Jamal.
         Kallon shi tayi sannan ta sake mishi murmushi, ta juya suka fito aka bar Ni dashi, juyawa yayi yace.
"Tsayuwar me kike!"

    Juyawa nayi na fita, sannan ya kalli Shugaban makarantar ya zauna a kujeran dake kallon shugaban makarantar, kasa kasa yayi da murya sannan yace.
"Ita d'in Y'ace daga masarautar Daura! Kuma ina son a haɗa ta zama da yara masu nutsuwa ba waɗanda zasu gurbata mata tunani b, Ni ne wakilinta kamar mahaifinta nake, dan haka a kula da nutsuwarta idan ban mata ba Tsohon Sarkin Daura yana cikin masu tallafawa d'aliban makarantar! Insha zai cigaba tunda ga D'iyarshi a cikin makarantar nagode."
Ya mik'e zai fita Shugaban makarantar yace.
"Insha Allah! Yarima Jamal zamu yi yadda kace! Sai dai nan da watanni zan ajiye aiki amma Insha Allah duk wanda za a kawo zan nuna masa ita"

   Murmushi Jamal yayi sannan ya fita.
   Muna ƙasar bishiyar maina, wato dalbajiya ko lim, duk sunayenshi ne.
        Kallona yayi ganin yadda na rab'a kaina a kafadar Maman Aliyah, ina share kwalla.

      Fitowar wani dattijo daga ofishin shugaban makarantar, ya tawo har inda na muke zaune, yace.
"Yallabai yace nakira muku wacce zata kai Yarinyar dakin kwanan su!"

      "Toh"
Suka ce mishi.ya juya da sauri, can sai gasu tare da wata mata, sai a lokacin na samu damar kuka sosai dan suna zuwa suka kwashe kayana, har  na tashi zan bi matar ya tako inda nake yace.
"Zan sami nutsuwa a duniya tane da zaran kin nusar da tunaninki guri guda!"

      Gyad'a mishi kai tayi sannan tace.
"Nagode"
   Bai ce komai ba ya juya gurin mota, sannan nake gurin Aswad nace.
"Yaya zan tafi!"
  "Allah ya baki abinda kika zo nima! Ki nutsu sosai"
   "Amin! Insha Allah"

Juyawa nayi gurin Mama ina had'iye kukan da nake nace.
"Mamah yaushe zaki zo!"
  
Dariya tayi sannan tace.
"Insha Allah! Duk karshen wata dani a cikin masu zuwa. Kiyi karatu banda Kawayen banza banda zuwa gurin kowa ki nutsu."

    "Toh! Mama" nace ina kuka,
    
   Haka na tafi ina kuka, shiga mota yayi, bai ce musu komai ba har suka bar makarantar, suna cikin tafiya ne Aswad ya wurga mishi tambaya.
"Naga Maman Aliyah tasaka sunanka! A jikin nata amma babu wata matsala"

         Kauda kan shi yayi yana kallon waje yace.
"Babu wata damuwa sabida Ni a matsayin Uba nake a gurinta."

"Uba?!!!"
Suka haɗa baki suna tambayar shi, cikin gamsuwa da abinda yake tunanin dai-dai ne yace.
"Eh! Kamar Y'ata cikina na dauke ta"
     "Lallai ka zauce!"
    "Fiye da haka Maman Aliyah! Ya haukace!"
         "Eh duk abinda zaku ce duk d'aya ne" ya basu amsa fuskarsa a tamke!
       Tsabar bakin ciki Aswad kamar ya tsayar da motar ya naushi sa, dan ran shi yayi mugun ɓaci.

     "Toh masifar da kake ranar dan Samim yayi mata magana meye ma'anar haka"

     Hararar Aswad yayi cikin jin haushi yace..
"Don Allah! Ka kyale Ni kaina na ciwo!"
    Murmushi Maman Aliyah tayi sannan tace..
"Toh tunda kai ne mahaifinta! In ba damuwa ina nimawa Kanin mijina soyayyar Yarka"

Juyawa yayi yana kallon ta, kafin ya tab'e baki yace.
"Eh toh! Amma yanzun ta fara karatu kar wani kato ya raba mata hankalinta gida biyu"

        "Mara mutuncin zaka yi magana ma, ban da ina da wacce nake so wallahi sai na Kure iskancinka kuma na fad'a soyayya da Yarka kuma akan idanunka na aureta."

Murmushin gefen baki yayi tare da mai da hankali shi kan titi yana kallon yadda ake tuki.
   Har suka isa gidan Maman Aliyah, suna shiga gidan Samim na shigowa yana ganinsu ya shiga raba idanu yana cewa.
"Matar Yaya na! Ina Queen take?!"
    Murmushi tayi sannan tace.
"Ai mun kaina ta makaranta"
      
"Haba! Insha Allah ranar karshen mako zani"
   
A hasale Jamal ya juyo cikin masifa yace.
" Kar naga kafarka a makarantar su"
      "Toh wai Jamal meye matsalar ka damu ne! Yarinya tana sona kuma nima ina sonta! Na yarda tayi karatu zan aureta idan ta gama karatunta"

           
          Nan ya tsaya yayita balokoko dan kawai ance ana son Yar shi. Idan kaga yadda yake fad'a sai ka rantse da gaske Yar shin ce.

               Maman Aliyah da Aswad da suka harbo jirgin shi suka yi ta Dariya.
       ++++
   Kallon harabar makarantar nake ko ina zagaye yake da bishiyoyin, sai d'alibai da suke kaiwa da komowa, a hankali muka isa, dakin kwanar d'alibai na Ado Bayero house, sannan matar ta kaini daki na goma (A) dalibai ne mata manya da yan matasa irina, suke kaiwa da komowa a cikin ɗakin sannan ta kaini wani gado kusada na wata yarinya wacce zata kai sa'ata, kallon matar tayi yarinyar sannan tace.
"Mama Mero! Zan sha tea!"
Ta faɗa a yatsine tana wani tale baƙin ta,

     "Toh zan had'a miki amma ga bakuwa nan itama daga masarautar Daura take ince zaku yi ƙawance da ita"

   Tura baki tayi kamar zata fasa kuka tace.
"Ni tea nake so!"

   Kuskure nayi a pagen d'azun shine 8 ga tara yanzun.

9️⃣
Cike da mamaki nake kallon ta, yadda take talle baki tana kuka, sai na tsaya ina kallonta kamar wata wawuya.
 "Yarinya! Ki haura sama! Kisaka kayanki a cikin drowen can", kai jakata nayi zan Shirya kayan cikin rigima tace.
"Ni sama nake so! Ita ta zauna a kasa" ta faɗa tare da tura bakinta,
      "Toh shi kenan, sauko bar mata saman." Haka na fasa hawa saman na dawo kasa, buɗe ma ajiyin kayan nayi na jera komai tsaf, tana ganin haka kawai ta kuma botsare itama sai na jera mata.
    Yau daga zuwa na, nasha aiki domin yarinyar ta cika shagwaɓa ban damu ba, dan nima bana son Magana.
    Lokacin Sallah azhar ya fidda mu, muka je alola. Muna dawowa na wuce inda kayana yake na ciro abin sallar.

     Ina cikin sallah sai ga wasu dalibai, suka shigo ina idarwa na tashi na tsaya kallon su.
           "Ke! Uban me kike kallon zo nan."

           Sunkuyar da kai nayi naje gabansu, dake ina kumshe gashina, tawowa suka yi dukkan su suka jani zuwa waje, ita wannan yarinyar nan tana zaune ko inda take basu kalla ba, koda suturar jikinta ne ko kuma sun santa ne. Oho,

           Ina jin wata tana cewa.
"Shegiya ta cika kyau! Ku duba mana ita ko ta girma."

         Ban fahimci abinda suke nufi ba, sai dai  kawai sun cire min lullube na. Suna cewa,
"Kai! Kuga Uban gashinta mana ku d'ago min fuskarta daga yau na dame lover"

         Sam ban fahimci abinda suke nufi ba, sai da naji Mama Mero na cewa.
"Hana mana Sakinah bakuwar ce fa! Kuma Yar sarkin daura ce."

    Juyawa tayi a masifance tana hararar Mama Mero, tare da fisgo Ni zuwa dakinsu dake gaba da namu.
       Kuka na saka ina cewa.
"Don Allah! Karki bari a tafi dani, Don Allah ku kyale Ni."

      Rashin sani yafi dare duhu, domin da sun san abinda zai biyo baya da basu fara daukata ba, domin kuwa suka kaini d'akin su, suka wurga jikin gadon su kaina ya bugu da gadon aikuwa kaina ya fashe saukar jinin ya sani jin wani irin abu ya gifta ta idanuna lumshe idanu tare da cewa.
 "Rus ku re ai la lla ay yi in kuy , aj ku ki ji ya ri zo wo da"
     (Yanzun sai kuce baku fahimci Zaurance ba! Lallai  Kunyi kuskure ja ziriya ta dawo.)

      "Sister Sakinah me take cewa"
"Nima ban sani ba."
   D'aya ta faɗa, Ni dai abinda na iya sani fitar jini daga goshina yasani rasa tunani na, farkawa nayi nagan a kwance bisa gadona, ina barci kamar ma komai bai faru ba.
      Tashi nayi naga babu kowa a dakin sauka nayi na dauki Yar karamar butana, nayi fita ga dandazon yan makaranta suna gani na kowa ya dare, komawa ciki nayi na na leka dakin Mama Mero nace mata.
"Mama ban daki nake son shiga."
    Itama da naga kamar a tsorace take dan daga cikin dakin tayi min kwantaccen ban dakin naje na kama ruwa nazo na fara tadda sallah, amma sai na rasa nutsuwa dan wasi wasi, dakyar nayi sallah sannan na zauna ina tuna Maman Aliyah dasu Aliyah duk sai naji ina kewar su, uwa uba Yarima Jamal, tun da na dub'e kayana na ciro kunshin kilishin da Mama ta bani tace inji shi na zauna na fara ci, ina share kwalla ina jin kewar shi sosai har ji nake zuciyata na bugawa.

       +++
Zama tayi tana kallon Rumana da ta kafeta da ido, ranta na kara ɓaci.
"Ki gayya min meke faru! Me yasa sai ita?!"
     
"Rumana ko na gaya miki bazaki gane ba! Ki fita ki bani guri"

  Tsinka mata mari Rumanah tayi tare da cukumo kwalarta tace.
"Ki gaya min meke faru"
   Jin irin shaƙar da Rumana tayi mata yasata fara magana.
"Zan gaya miki abinda na sani! Sauran kuma ki ceto d'anki ga fad'awa masifa! Dan an jima da haihuwar Zulum, sirya ma an kuma haihuwar shi, tunda kiga yarinya taje ganuwa tabbas itace kakanin mu suka jima suna jiranta! Tabbas idan d'anki ya rabu da ita Shamaki zai farmake shi, dukkan su biyu garkuwa ce ga junansu, zaki ji maganar kamar almara, amma ba haka bane tsagwaron gaskiya ce, zaki ce na kauce hanya Eh haka ce amma gaskiyar ce tazo haka! Ki raba shi da kowa."
  Ture ta Rumana tayi cikin zafin rai tace..
"Tur da halinki masu yarda da bokaye da tsubbace tsubbace, Allah yayi min tsari da yarda da abinda kika fad'a sannan ban san inda kika dosa ba, ina raba shi da ita sabida me?! Sabida munafucinki da kuma na sauran shaidanu irinki idan turo ki akayi tabbas kin makaro dan baki isa gaya min shirme na dauka ba."

   Tana gama faɗar haka ta juya ta ficce daga dakin, har ta kai bakin kofa Jakadiya tace mata.
"Ga wannan na yarinyar ce! Ki bata idan kun haɗu jita dakyar nayi barci naga masifar Ziriya dan tafi kowa son abinta! Wannan rigimar tun shekaru da suka gabata aka lakatota Ni ban ita nace komai akanta ba, abinda na sani ita ɗin zata kasance garkuwa gareta yayinda shi kuma zai bada rayuwarshi domin ceto nata rayuwar, zaki iya min kallon mahaukaciya eh na yarda amma tabbas an tabka kuskure, haduwarsu shine karin tasirin a jikin Yarinya, nisantar shine mafi alkhairi amma bazata tab'a barin a zubda jinin ta ba, mikan ki tafi ki bata ko zata iya sanin daga inda tafito, na rabu da masifa."

  Tsaki Rumana tayi tana alwadar halin Jakadiya da yarda da canfe,
  
          Tana shiga d'akinta ta a dana kunshin inda ya dace, tana jin haushin Jakadiya akan wani shirme sun jawa Yarinya ciwo.

          +++
   "Sannu Abba! Ga abincinka nan" inji Jamalah, tace tare da dire mishi abincin a gaban shi tana kallon yadda yayi shiru.

   Cikin sanyin murya tace.
"Kayi hakuri yana hanya Insha Allah"

1️⃣1️⃣
A razane na juya ganin yadda ya cikine wuyar Samim, yana muzurai, ran shi ya kai kololowar ɓaci, dan yaki sake wuyar Samim, Mama sai magana suke mishi ita da Aswad amma yaki sake shi sai da naje na rike hannunsa na kalle shi, cikin rauni murya nace.
"Ga baki daya jama'a kallon ka suke! Indai nice bazan kuma ba kayi hakuri."

      Ture shi yayi sannan ya fincikoni muka nufi Office d'in shugaban makarantar, ya zauna a kujeran Ni kuma ina tsaye sai huci yake, mik'ewa shugaban makarantar yayi ya bashi ruwan kwalba ya amsa sai da ya shanye! Sannan ya sauke a jiyar zuciya  yafi minti biyar yana sauke numfashi kafin ya buɗe baki yace.
"Akwai wani yaro"

   Shiru yayi kafin ya cigaba da cewa.
"Koda zai zo gurinta karku barshi ya ganta sunan shi Samim Mai Nasara"

"Kina kuka dan na mare shi ko? Toh idan ya kuma rike rigarki b'atar da shi zan saka a min sai Kiyi kuka da tushe, sakarya tunda nike ko tsawa ban tab'a yiwa kowa ba, amma kin sani abin kunya a gaban mutane! Duk kin sani darajata da kimata ya zube a kasa. Na kuma ganinki da shi sai na gotar mishi da haɓa."

    Har muka fito nuna min hanyar ɗakin mu yayi sannan yaje ya samu Mama sun tafi, sai Aswad ne yake jiran shi. Yana zuwa ya samu duk abinda aka kawo min har da na Samim duka ya kaiwa motar tare da jan tsuka yace.
"Har da Shirgin wancan mahaukacin aka kai mata,"

   Wannan karon bai zo ba sai dai Samim zo, kawai na saki jikina ne dashi sabida Idanun Mama, har suka tafi bai zo ba, koda na shiga dakin mu sai da na zubda kwalla. Amma nayi alkawari bazan kuma damuwa da rashin sa ba, tunda na fahimci wasa da hankali Ni na zai yi Insha Allah bazan kuma damuwa da shi ba.

Ina fahimtar karatun sosai, sai mafarkin da nake yanzun babu, asalima bana ganin abubuwan da nake gani da, wannan sauyin da nasamu ne ta hanyar mai da hankali ni, ga karatun da Mama Mero take koya mana dan tana da ilimin addini sosai, ga addu'o'in da take bamu, sai sallah dare da. Alhamdulillah dan na samu sauki sosai.

               Watan mu uku muna cikin na hudu muka yi jarabar zangon farko, murna da farin ciki zamu koma gida ya hana mu sakat,  rana da zamu yi hutu Samim da Mama suka zo daukata, sai da zan tafi ne kuma muka fara kuka ni da Sarah dakyar muka rabu dan motar su Mama ya riga na gidan su zuwa, amma Mama tace  mu jira  azo daukar ta, haka kuwa ya faru bamu bar makarantar ba sai da muka fita tare, ina daga mata hannu tana daga min hannu.

           "Sarauniya ta! ina kishin kawarki nan naga kin wani damu da ita madadin ki damu dani"

NAGAWURTA🌹

Yana shiga zauren yaje gaban Shamaki ya zauna, sannan yaja tumtum ya kishingid'a akai yana kallon shamaki, wanda yake rike da bakin shi da hannun shi na dama, sannan hannun shi na hagu rike da wuka, kurawa Jamal ido yayi, sai gani yayi a idanun shi kamar yana caccaka mishi wukar a wuyar shi, ga Jamal sai motsi yake, tafi Jamal yayi sannan yace.
"Kawu Shamaki! Farka daga mafarkin da kake yin nan ba dagaske bane, ajiye wukar muyi magana da kai, idan kuma ka d'aga muryanka zan maka ba dad'i, na fi karfin na tsorata"

  Ya fad'a mishi haka, yana kuma kafe shi da idanu.

               Tashi zaune Jamal yayi sannan ya diba mishi ruwa, ya mik'a mishi, sannan yace.
"Ban damu ka cigaba da mulki ko karka cigaba ba"

"Kai har ka isa ka min kashedi ne! Ko Ubanka bai isa ba! Balle ke da baka san waye kai ba."

"Kowa ka gani da tabarmarsa sai dai ya rasa wurin shimfi’dawa! Dan haka sanin Ni bai da muhimmanci! Abinda zan iya aikatawa shine me muhimmanci! Zaitun!!"

"Sha!"

Toh ya zaka ji idan nace maka sirikina! Abbana yayiwa Jamalah miji! Dan haka salin alin zasu zo niman aurenta a cikin saba'ar watan nan shine nake son tun wuri ka cire wancan zarerren d'an naka a layin da ka shatawa Jamalah! Domin kuwa idan hakan bai samu ba, ba abin mamaki bane agwagwa da kin ruwa! Yare na gaba kai zaka bada labarin yadda muka yi dakai!"

"Nafi karfin na tsorata!!!"

Iya Ni ke zuwa farauta bana niman y'an tayi! Bana cin sauran kowa sai dai a ci saura na! Na gaya maka matukar ka nace tabbas zan mai da maka d'anka mace, Zaitunah kuma zan shafa mata bakin fentin wanda bakin cikin haka zai sanyan ka barin daura da kafarka! *Nafi karfin na tsorata!!!"
     Da zaran yaji kalmar hatta hantan cikin shi kad'awa take, dan haka gari na wayewa ya tura aka kira mishi duk wani shugaba dake cikin masarautar, sai da suka taru. Sannan ya fito niman guri yayi sannan ya zauna, yana kallon su kamar yadda suke kallon shi, su kallon yadda yake muzurai yake.

shigo d'akin ta same Ni har lokacin ina barci, ranta ya b'aci sosai dan haka ta fita dan tana tsoron kar tashe Ni ta kuma b'allo musu teku.

   Mik'ewa yayi sannan ya wuce dakin da take, ya d'aga labulen. Fuskata ya hango wanda yayi fayau kamar babu jini a cikin jikina, shiga dakin yayi yana mamakin yadda na koma, sai hasken da nayi.

"D'an banza makiri wato kar na fad'awa kowa ya kawo min ajiyar Yarinya in ba haka ba zai iya cire suna na"

"Meke damun ki?!"
 Ya tambaye ni cikin wani irin murya.
       Jingina nayi da wata bishiyar durimi, hawaye ya zubo min daga cikin idanuna nace mishi.

Shi kad'ai ya san yadda yake ji a ran shi, dan ji yaƙe kamar ya dauketa, cak ya tafi da ita a hannun shi amma babu halin haka, dole ya cigaba ba hakuri su isa bakin hanya yaje dakin makaranta ya dauko kudi ya kaita kaduna.

    Iska yaja sannan ya fesar sannan yace.

          Tallabe ni tayi kuma nufi cikin gidan, inda ta kwantar dani.

*Masarautar Borno*
Kodayake Daular Kanem Borno dake kusa da kogin Chadi ce ta mamaye Arewacin Najeriya har na tsawon shekaru dari shida, amma suma wadannan garuruwan sun kasance cibiyoyin kasuwanci a yankin tsakanin kabilar Berber na Arewacin Afirka da kuma garuruwan dake karkashin daular Kanem Borno...
Sannan masana tarihi sun tabbatar da cewa sun amshi musulunci kafin zuwan Daula Uthmaniyya, sannan suma sunyi yaƙe yaƙe, sannan suna da karfin mulkin sosai a wancan lokacin.


Daular Elkanem ita ba akai mata musulunci ba dan dama ita ta samu abinta ne ta hanyar masu shigowa ta tafkin Chadi, sannan. Masarautar tana da dinbun tarihi sosai musamman mutanen yanki da suka kasance masu tarin Alkhairin, sannan mutane ne da suka san karamci da sanin ya kamata.
Wannan kenan...

Da sauri tashigo dukda laffayar da take taɗeta bai hanata tattaro shi ba, daga sama sannan ta kara saurinta duk inda ta wuce sai an gaisheta, amma bata tsayawa burinta kawai ta isa gurin mai martaba, Sarki Kazeem tana isa, shashin sa tasame shi tsaye a jikin taga yana kallon barga inda ake kula da dawakan masarautar.

"Kazeem! Don Allah kasa baki a fidda Yaganah! Bata da sa hannun akan komai da ya faru! Don Allah kasa a saketa na had'aka da Allah" ta fadi haka tana kuka,
Zuciyarshi tayi nauyi sosai, kallonta yayi, sannan ya sunkuyar da kan shi kasa, cikin nutsuwa yace.
"Innah kije zan saka a bude ta!"

Gyad'a kai tayi sannan cikin kuka tace.
" Ka yafe min abinda ya faru! Zan saka Jakadiya ta kawo maka! Qoiseh"

Jin sunan sai da ya fad'ar mishi da gaba, lokaci guda ya zuciyarshi ta fara bugawa, kallon Innah yayi sannan ya d'aga mata hannu.
" Ba sai tazo ba!"

"Kazeem!!!"
"Don Allah!" Yace mata, dole ta hakura da abinda take da niyyar fad'a ta sakai tafice daga cikin zauren shi jikinta a mace.

A cikin ranta kuwa tsaki take taya komai ya kwance mata kamar ba abinda ya faru.
*Allah yasa ya manta da komai na baya"

Bayan fitarta ya koma jikin tagar, yana kallon yadda dabobin suke rayuwar su, cikin aminci da kulawar me su.

*_Yallaɓe nasan iya abinda kake bukata bai wuce cikin jikina ba! Sai dai ina rokon Alfarmarka da ka bani damar shayar da shi kamar yadda kowacce uwa take buƙatar haka sai dai da kai da kaya duk mallakar wuya ce_*

Tun lokacin da muka dawo,Mama na shiga dani cikin gida na sume mata a jiki shine suka kawo ni asibitin wani balarabe Yemen, sannan asalima garin Kaduna tana cikin manyan biranen Arewacin Nigeria, tsabar girmanta da yawan mutanen cikinta wanda suka goge, a ilimin boko yasa ake kiranta bariki, dan ita Headquart arewa lokacin mulkin mallaka, dan turawan Ingila sun zauna a garin, me cike da kabilun mabanbanta addinai,! Sannan a karfin siyasa da tattalin arziki, Kaduna tana cikin jaga a wannan bangaren, har ila yau tana cin gajiyar haka, domin duk wani wayayyen dan siyasa zaka samu yana tun kawo da siyasar Kaduna.

"Mamana!"
Na kira sunanta, rike hannuna tai sosai. Sannan tace.

Dafa Ni da Mama tayi ya sani sake kuka, da duk zuciyata ina kuka me cin rai. Zuba min ido tayi cike da mamaki, tsuke fuskar ta tayi sannan ta daka min tsawa tace.

"Yanzun kukan Kawu bai zo ba ya kare tunda dai jinin shi ke yawo a jikin Yarshi,"
Da ɗan sarssarfa na shige ban dakin, ban dakin nayi alola dan akwai buta, da bokitin wanka har da ruwan wankan, har zan fito sai naga dacewan nayi wanka sannan na fito.

Abin sallah na samu ta shinfida min da babban mayafi na lullube(kusan daga yawaitar hijab ya faro ne daga kan 2000 zuwa sama! Amma daga 19 babu hijab sai babban mayafi😎)
