Zaria
Tafiyar mintinu arba'in da biyar ya kawo su Zaria, dan har kofar makaranta aka kawo shi, dukda jirin da yake ji haka ya daure ya sani gashashen nama! Sannan ya wuce, d'akin shi ya buɗe. Tare da kwanciya, dakyar ya mike yayi Sallah tare da shan magani yaci namar kadan sannan ya kwanta, sabida ciwon da kan shi yake masa.

.... Washi gari ya tashi hatsi kamar ba jinin shi aka diba ba, sannan yaje ya dibe ruwan wanka yaje yayi sannan ya tafi masallaci, yana fitowa daga sallah, abin karyawan ya zauna ya had'a tea ne kawai sai sauran naman jiya, idan yana irin wannan rayuwar sai yaji a ran shi yafi kowa dace, dan a wannan rayuwa yake jin nutsuwa da fahimtar rayuwar wasu, yasani idan yace yana bukatar kulawa zai samu amma ba burin shi bane nunawa shi din Basarake ne, sabida baya son kambawar mutane ya sashi ya fara jin shi din wani ne ba, Idan a akwai abinda yafi tsana a rayuwarsa Mulki, ko wacce iri ne baya maraba da ita, dan shi tsuntsu ne yana son yaga ya bautawa kasarshi bata hanyar mulki ba ta hanyar yin aiki tukuru, ya nunawa duniya, shi ba kowa bane.
*JANNART ITACE KADDARATA*
Sauke numfashi yayi sannan ya zuba akan kofar dakin shi da yaji ana tab'awa, kurawa kofar ido yayi. Baisa shi magana ba sabida jin muryan wanda yake tambayar ko yana nan.

"Aswad!"
Ya kira sunan da dan ƙarfi!
"Nagode sosai yana ciki ma!"

Turo kofar yayi ya hango shi hakimce a gaban abinci, sai kallon juna suke.

"Assalamu alaikum!"
Murmushi Aswad yayi sannan yace.
"Amin Waalaikumun Salam! Tunda banyi sallama ba."

Murmushi yayi wanda ya karawa idanun shi masu kalar ruwan gwal kyau, sake kallon shi Aswad yayi sannan yace..
"Yau na yarda da hasashen da nake gaskiya ce, domin kamar ku da Matar da ka kai Jannart gurinta a can gidan bayi ya ɓaci wallahi da gaske nake, sai dai ita ta manyanta. Kuma ta dishe ba kamar kai da kake jin samartaka ba"

Girgiza kai yayi sannan ya tashi ya shiga had'a kayan shi yace.
"Karfe sha daya zan dawo akwai komai"

"Hmm! Nima fita zan yi yanzun zanje naga jannati dan goggo ta bani aikarta har garin dakuwa."

"Ban gane kace min humm! Ko baka son zuwa na ne"
"Gashi ka rufe min kofa ba"
Cikin jin haushi yace.
"Mara mutunci kai! Ka rage kishin jaraba!"

Bai tsaya jin shi ba, yayi ficewa me shi dan bazai iya daukar zantuttunkar Aswad, ranar da suka kai Jannart gidan Yaya Shatu sai da suka yi kaca kaca a hanya, akan Jannart shi Aswad yana ƙoƙarin nuna mishi soyayyar da yake b'oyewa shi kuma yana ƙoƙarin nuna mishi babu wata soyayyar da yakewa Jannart.

Rufe kofar Aswad yayi sannan ya fita daga Shashin ya nufi wajen makarantar, tafiyar kafa yayi har gidan. Yana shiga Yaya Shatu tana leka bayan shi ko da Jannart ya dawo mata.

Ajiyar zuciya ta sauke sannan tace.
"Hmm! Yarinyar da kuka kawo mana ashe mayya ce! Sai da ta zuke mana jini dan jaraba! Wancan takaddariyar yarinya, sai da ta karyawa Mansoor hannu, Ni kuwa ta min mugun duka yarinya tsabar maita har bango take bi."

Duk yadda yaso ya danne dariyar shi sai da ya dara, domin tsakani da Allah take bayani kamar zaka ce kass aka mata.

"Kenan Jamal yasan bata nan ya barni nazo dama aika na kawo mata." Ya fadawa Yaya Shatu,
"Hmmm ina rabaka da kiwon tumaki kana kyalla ta haihu! Shikenan tunda wanda bai ji bari ba zai ji hoho!" Tace mishi, 

Mik'ewa yayi sannan yace..
"Tun ranar farko kika nuna baki yinta! Da ta zauna a gidan Raihanah babu abinda ya faru bai a gidan ki."

Share shi tayi shima kuma ya fita, abinshi.

.... Karshe sha d'aya minti goma ya shigo d'akin shi, ya sami Aswad ya zauna yaci banza yaci wofi.

Zama yayi sannan yace..
"Aiko ka a kayi?!"

"Sakon Mai Martaba! Sannan wannan kakar sakon Fulani ce kilishi da dambu zabuwa"

"Ka tafi kabar baya da kura! Domin kuwa! Shamaki sai sauke masu mukamai yake sabida sun mara maka baya! Daga cikin su kuwa Akwai Galadima da Wambai tare da Chiroma! Waziri kuwa dakatar da shi yayi sannan. Kai kuma yace ku zuba sha waye zai nasara! Ni zan tafi dan ban so na kai dare yanzun hanyar ba kyau da'u suna tsare mutane a hanyar."

Rakashi yayi har inda motar yake, sannan yace.
"Albishirinka! Juya an kawowa Mai martaba kyautar mota kuma ya bar maka, shine ya bar min wannan, har yace idan bani so a sauya min."

"Wannan tsakaninku ne Ni kan dan kallon"

Haka suka yi bankwana, sannan ya koma cikin dakin shi, ya janyo jakar ya cire aikar Abban shi, ya ciro.

Warware takardan yayi sannan ya bude cikin, kurawa gefe guda idanu yayi sam jikin shi ba kwari dan baya son yaga abinda zai dame shi, a duniya zai iya jure ganin kowa cikin kunci, banda Mahaifin shi dan a duniya babu abinda Abban shi zai ce yace A'a, sabida tausayin mahaifin shi da ya ginu a ran shi, gashi yana biye hakikanin gaskiya, kallon wasikar yayi sannan ya fara karantawa kamar haka...

Kome wasikar ya kunsa......

        *_Assalamun Alaikum_*

    _Da fatan sakona zai isa gareka cikin amincin Allah! Da farko abinda zan fara cewa shine Allah ya jibanci al'amuranka! Yayi maka albarka cikin rayuwarka baki daya, yasa ka gama da duniya lafiya! Alhamdulillah ko yau na mutu bani da tararradin abinda na bari zai tsaya domin kwatowa dinbin Al'ummar shi hakokin su! Naji dad'in yadda ka gigita duniyar Shamaki domin naga madarar tsoro a cikin idanun shi! Sannan wani abin alfahari a yau  goyan bayan da kasamu daga wasu masu rawanin da suke cikin Padar! Jamal Allah yayi maka albarka! Sai dai ina jin tsoron takaddiranci Shamaki! Idan da hali rijiya ta ba da ruwa guga ya hana! Da wannan nake maka fatan Alkhairi_

                   Mahaifinka
          Jalaludeen Aminullah

Murmushi yayi sannan yace.
"Abbana kenan! Ai wukar yanka Giwa ba girma gareta ba, sai kaifi! Akan Shamaki kake shirin hanani zuwa gida! Shikenan naji Abbana, sai dai me kaya ya k'iyayye mai zamani"

        Haka yayita juyar maganar har lokacin sallah yayi.

*
      Tsakurar abincin nake wanda Samim ya kawo mana daga gidan Yayar shi da take aure, a unguwar sunusi, dan can ta tura su Aliyah da Halifa, ajiye abincin nayi nace.
"Mama na koshi?"

     Kallona tayi na wani lokaci sannan ta tab'e baƙi, kafin tace.
"Ina kyautata zaton Jamal zai tawo anjima, kuma idan yaga baki cikin abinci akwai matsala!"

      Kallon abincin nayi sannan na shafa cikina wanda yake had'e da baya, kwalkwal nayi da idanuna nace.
"Allah cikina fashewa zai idan naci abincin nan dukka"

    Bata kuma kula Ni ba, har likita ya iso, ya gama dubani sannan ya bamu sallama.

       Muna cikin shirya kayan mu ne, Samim yazo tunda yace min.
"Ya jikinki?!"
Nace da sauki ban kuma kula shi ba har muka tafi inda motar da yazo da ita, muna shiga tace mishi.
"Jiya nake lissafin dawowar  Yayanka"

Murmushi yayi sannan yace..
"Insha Allah! Yana dawowa Sai karin matsayi! Daga nan sai a maidaku Lagos dan nafi tunanin haka"

     "Babu inda zani! Ina da yara da suke karatu bazan so a kasa musu hankali ba,"

     "Har da Sarauniya ta?!" Ya tambayeta yana satar kallona ta madubi ina kwance bisa kafadar ta,

          A hankali suke zantawa har muka iso gida, ina fita daga motar na kwashi kwandon da aka kawo mana abincin, na shige cikin gidan ina jin kaifin kallon shi a jikina,  da sauri na shige cikin gidan.
           Dakin mu na wuce na zauna dan saurin da nayi na gaji, kwanciya nayi tare da lumshe idanuna, ina kara girma jinin dake zagaye a jikina, Jinin Yarima Jamal. Murmushi nayi tare da gyara kwanciyata.

*
    Sati na d'aya sai gashi nan yazo shida Aswad, na gaza b'oye farin cikina. B'oya nayi a daki nayita dariya. Ina rufe idanuna ji nake kamar naje na shige jikin shi. Ban san yadda nake ji akan Kawu Jamal.
              Shigowar Mama tayi ta ganni kaina tsakanin cinyoyina, sai murmushi nake dokawa, shafa kaina tayi, sannan ta zauna kusadani.

      "Jannart! Kina Son Jamal ne?!" Ta wurga min tambaya,
A firgice na d'ago kaina, ina kallonta nayi idanuna na cikowa da kwalla.

             Rike hannunta nayi kwalla na zuba min! Sannan nace.
"Wallahi ban sani ba! Amma tun daga ranar da na ganshi nutsuwa ta ta kare, farin cikina da burina na ganshi, damuwata naji yana kusadani koda bazai min magana, Mama ban san me nake ji akan shi ba, amma tabbas ina ji da Kawu jamal" na karshe maganar cikin kuka, janyo Ni jikinta tayi tana buga bayana, tace.
"Hmm! Karki tab'a barin yasan kin damu dashi! Ki girmama shi a matsayin shi na Kawunki, karki bari so ya hanaki karatu! Ki nutsu kiyi karatu! Idan kika yi haka, Ni kuma zan taimaka miki ganin kin sami abinda kike so! Amma dole sai kin mai da hankalinki a karatu."

   Hawaye ne ya zubo min, na kifa kaina akan cinyarta nace.
"Mama nagode"
"Allah yayi miki albarka! Tashi ku tafi suna jiranki"

    Tashi nayi a hankali ina kuka saboda bana son rabuwa da ita,  haka muka fito suna kallona ina goge kwalla na.
"Raihanah! Me yasa batayi kitso ba?!"

Kallon juna muka yi Ni da Mama,  nayi rau-rau da idanuna, cikin ko inkula tace.
"Itace bata kaunar kitso! Domin jiya sai da aka fara, taki zama."
    B'oye kaina nayi a bayanta, tare da cewa.
"Mama zai min faɗa"

      Cikin rashin damuwa ya mike, ya nufi kofar fita. Yayinda Aswad yayi mishi rakiya da ido yace.
"Baki ganshi ba kullum kamar bayahude, gashin kanshi da na fuskarshi a aske ba."

      Jin shi nayi kawai domin ki dai yayi min a haka, Indai yadda zuciyata take nuna min shine toh yafi kowa, a haɗuwa.

 .. Munyi sallama da Mama, tana d'aga min hannun har muka bar gidan, a hanya sai hiransu suke, Ni kuma ina shiru.

       Mun isa makarantar wajen karfe goma, dai dai an kawo Sarah, kafin motar ya tsaya ta balle tafito da gudu, haɗa ido muka yi dashi, yasani komawa na zauna. Ba tare da damuwa ba ta shigo motar da sauri ta fad'a jikina, tana dariya. Nima dariya nayi tace.
"Aminiyata nayi kewarki sosai!"
  Nima rike hannunta nayi, sannan muka fito, juyawa tayi ta kalli Aswad da ya zuba mana ido, tace.
"Yayunki ne?"
 Kafin na bude bakina nace mata, wani abu, ta leka inda yake zaune. Ji nayi kamar na fincikota. Zuba mishi ido tayi har da baki a buɗe.
      Janyota nayi tare da ce mata.
"Muje kawa!"
          Ajiyar zuciya ta sauke, sannan tace.
" Hmmm Kawata muje"
     Ko kallonta banyi ba na tureta a jikina, kallona tayi tace.
"Kin ture Ni!"
 Wayancewa nayi nace..
"Kai Kawata! Wasa nake miki, ban isa ture Gimbiya yar Sarkin Bauchi ba."
 
Murmushi tayi sannan tace.
"Hmm! Wancan waye?!"
  Share ta nayi kamar ban jita ba, nayi wuccewa ta bayan motar na kwaso kayana, na soma jidarsu. Dan a halin da nake ciki kuka nake son nayi, amma babu halin yin haka.

          Ita bayin da suka rakota da babban Yayansu ne suka kwashe mata kayanta, Ni kuma ina kwashe nawa, ganin haka shi da Aswad suka kwaso min sauran dan iyayi takai hannunta zata amshi na hannun shi ban san ya akayi nayi Wuf na sha gabanta ba. Na amshi kayan fuskanta ba a sake ba.
         Sannan na nufi ciki da shi, tsayawa tayi a gaban shi, ta fara zuba mishi surutun tsiya, ina fitowa na gansu ko sallama banyi musu ba, na koma ciki na samu wasu bakin ɗalibai har mutum biyu, zama nayi a kwanarmu. Sai gata nan tazo ina ganinta na rufe idanuna kamar na fara barcin.
  
    Dafa min kafad'ana tayi sannan tace.
"Wata Uku da nayi dake! Ban tab'a ganin b'acin rainki ba! Asalima, baki iya karya ba dan haka ki tashi muji suna jiranki."

        Banza nayi da ita dan haushi take bani, taya daga ganin mutum zata shiga wani iyayi da rawan kai akan shi, na tsani irin haka wallahi ture hannunta nayi sannan na sauka ta d'aya barin gadon na fita, tunda na fita na tsaya kerere, shi kamshi Aswad dariya na bashi sai cika nake ina batsewa. Ban ishe shi kallo ba yace..
"Kina bukatar wani abu ne!"
   "Babu"
Nace a gatse, kallona yayi sannan ya d'aga kafad'arshi alamun oho, Ni nasani, can sai gashi ta dawo bamu gama magana ba nace.
"Ku sauka lafiya zan koma na shirya kayana"

 Na juya zan koma ciki, sai ji nayi ya riko hannuna, kuka na sami shi zuba min ido yayi sannan yace.
"Me aka miki?!"
      Buɗe baki nayi, ban san lokacin da na kara saurin kuka na ba, kowa sai kallona ake.
"Ki min shiru!"
 Ya buga min mugun tsawa da tasani kifa kaina a kirjinshi, nasaka wani irin kuka. Jikin shi ne yayi sanyi, ya kai hannun shi bayana yana bubugawa, kome ya tuna kawai ya cire Ni a jikin shi ya ce.
"Bani son shashanci, ki nutsu da abinda ya kawo ki!, Yawwa Yan mata meye sunanki"

     Mamaki ne ya kamani yadda take wani kakkafewa tare da wani salo zata faɗi sunanta nace.
"Mara adam! Sarah" sannan na juya ga Aswad nace.
"Kawu Aswad ka gaida min Goggo! Idan muka sami hutu zanzo!" 
 Bari gurin nayi ina kuka, Sarah ta jima a gurin su sannan tazo tare da mika min takarda wanda yake nade da wani leda me kyalkyali tace.
"Yanzun wani ya bani wai na baki, sunan shi Samim"

Dira nayi a gadon na fita  babu alamar shi.
 Ina zuwa dakin na yaga ledar sannan naga wasu takardu irinta masoya, an Kawata takardan da zanen zuciyoyi, kasan anyi rubutu da barkar alkalami (wato Black pen)
*_Soyayya ta gare ki gaskiya ce, kuma ta ginu ne daga ranar da na fara ganinki, ina kaunarki fiye da yadda zan fada maki._*

               Samim....

Sai ɗaya takardan yana kunshe da sakon fatan Alkhairi ne. Tattara sakon nayi na saka, a kasan kayana dariya Sarah tayi  min sannan ta shiga cewa.
"Kodai kodai"

   Daukar matashin muke kwanciya dashi na buga mata, tare da tura baki, dariya ta saka min tare da tsugulina nayi yar kara, kafin wani lokacin na manta haushinta da nake ji, mun shiga jera kayanmu. Zama tayi ta fara bani labarin gida nima ina bata labarin har da jinyar da nayi, ta tausaya min.

               Shak'uwa dake tsakanin mu da Sarah ya b'aci, kuma wani gefe ina yawan jin zafin ta, sabida tana yawan tadin Kawu, ban san yadda kowa zai fassara abin ba, amma zan iya mata komai dan ƙawancen mu, amma bani jin zan barta ta alakantu da abinda nake masifar bukata, matsalar da na fara fuskanta Sarah bata da maida hankali akan karatu, dan yan mata biyun da aka kawo mana suna zuzzuta mata kyanta, abu na farko Ni haƙa ce, amma ina da sirritaccen kyau, kamar kamar Geneve, haka  fatar jikina yake. Ina da manyan idanu, kuma ba laifi ina da hanci. Sai bakina shima ba laifi ina dashi, dan dayawa suna cewa. Da nice da fatar Sarah sai na fita kyau, Sarah tana da kyau sosai dan fara ce ainun kuma tana da gashin kai irinta fulani, yayinda Ni nawa gashin yana da tsayi sai dai irin me kamar roba ne, ga cika ga tsayi, wani lokacin idan zan take sai na jika da ruwa sabida zafi, amma dawowa na din nan Mama ta haɗa min man gashi.
  
           Shigowa tayi suna bin bayan ta, dan suma yaran manyane.  Zama tayi kusadani sannan ta kwace littafin hannuna, tace.
"Kawas! Wannan ziyarar Kawunki zai zo dan wallahi ina Son na ganshi, na gaji da son bawa mutane labarin shi, kin Latifah Jafar tana son ganin shi, sai Humairah Yusuf itama tana son ganinshi, kawaye kunga yadda Kawun Jan ya haɗu kuwa! Sai ma kunga idanun shi kamar na kuliya, komai nashi ba laifi."

        Bance mata komai ba,na amshi littafina na cigaba da abinda nake, sake fisgewa tayi, dan raina ya kai iya ɓaci, ban san lokacin da na rike mata wuya ba, idanuna a rufe, wasu irin guguwa nake ji na tashi a cikin kaina, cikin ikon Allah bakina ya fara ambatar Allah,  sake wuyarta nayi cikin tsoro nace.
"Don Allah Kiyi hakuri! Wallahi bada sani na bane."

Murmushi tayi tana kallon, hannunta a wuyarta tace.
"Ba komai ai nasan baki da lafiya! Mantawa nayi  tunda nasan baki son a takura miki."
 Amsar littafi tayi sannan tace.
"Kwanta ki huta!"

          Kwanciya tasani nayi sannan ta haura  gadonta, tacewa kewayen ta.
"Mu haɗu anjima! Dan bazan iya fita nabarta a cikin wannan yanayin ba."

                  Jikina ne yayi matukar sanyi, domin kuwa tayi min abinda bazan iya ba, na mata abu ta min Uzuri a bani da lafiya, sannan ta sallami Kawayen ta saboda ni. Take zuciyata tace min..
"Idan ta nemi ki sadaukar mata da wani abinda kike bukata da?!"
 Dafe goshina nayi cikin damuwa nace..
"Haka ma bazai yu ba!"

             Na fada a hankali, yadda bazata iya jina ba,

    ........ Bata kuma min maganar shi ba, dan ta dauko min hirar shi kwanakin naki bata damar muyi magana akan shi karshe Humairah tace.
"Ke Jannart ba halin Sarah ta tambayeki, labarin kawunki sai ki bata rai kamar wacce take niman wani abu a gurinki"

 Dake ban iya rigima ba, sai kawai nace mata.
"Don Allah ku kyale ni, idan yazo ta tambaye shi labarinshi mana."

    Haka ba karamin basu haushi yayi ba, kuma baki sauraron su, a haka muka cinye wata gudu, kuma babu ranar da bazan yi addu'a, Allah yasa kar yazo. Domin bana son suna haduwa.

#MY ZAHRAH🌹
Gabaki d'aya Labarin sadaukarwa ce gareki😍
zɑɦʀɑɳ-ɑɗɗɑ ʀɑɱʆɑt😊

1️⃣6️⃣

   Ranar ziyara tun wuri na tashi na shirya, ina kallon yadda Humairah da Latifah, suka kewayenta sai naji ba dad'i dan nima wani lokacin miskili ce na  gaske, kallonta nayi cikin damuwa nace.
"Sahibah!"
D'ago idanu tayi sannan ta lumshe, duk sai na damu da kaina, nace.
"Baki da lafiya ne?!"
Tashi tayi zaune ta mai da gashin kanta baya, sannan tace.
"Lafiyata lau! Kawai ina jin ba dad'i ne tun ba yau ba, nake rokonki ko kawunki zai zo amma kika ki kulani" 
Yadda tayi maganar a raunane yasani jin ba dad'i fiyye da can baya, zama nayi kusada ita. Tare da dafa kafad'arta nace.
"Kuyi hakuri! Zai  idan ya sami lokaci idan kuma bai samu ba, karki zarge Ni"

   Dira tayi a gadon ta wuce, ban daki tayi wanka, sannan tazo. Ta shirya, lokacin har na gama.

     Muna zaune sai ga Mama Mero, tashigo sannan kallonmu tayi sannan tace.
"Sarah kizo mutanenki sun iso"

Riko hannuna tai muka fita, inda suke har ofishin shugaban makarantar, tayi mamakin ganin har can muka je, ba kowa bane sai Mai Martaba sarki Adamu na Bauchi, Ni kaina nayi farin cikin ganin shi balle ita. Wacce ta zub'e a jikin shi ta fara sakalci tare da bashi labarin Kawuna.
  Murmushi yayi ta mata, sannan ya nuna min guri kusada shi na zauna, bayan na gaishe shi. Ganin yadda take bashi labarin Kawuna, sai ba zuba mata ido. Abinka da babba har ya fahimci, halin da yar shi take ciki Ni, ina zaune aka ce nazo nima yan uwana sun zo.

     Sallama nayi musu, sannan na fita tashi tayi a jikin, tace.
"Atta! Bari naje na gansu!"

      "Allah yayi miki albarka!"
"Amin" tace mishi.

      Ina fitowa na hango shi sanye da wata shadda ghalila sai ɗaukar ido take, ga motar shi sabuwa fil, gefen shi Mama ce ita da su Aliyah, da sassarfa na iso gurinsu sannan na zube a jikinta ina sauke ajiyar zuciya, nace.
"Nayi kewar ki?!"

     Rungume Ni tayi sannan tace.
"Kodai kewar kawunki!"
  Ina d'ago kaina naga Sarah tasa shi a gaba, sai zuba take mishi kura musu ido nayi raina ya fara ɓaci musamman da ya bata lokacin shi har da dariya yake mata, ban san me ta gaya mishi ba naga yabi bayanta sun tafi sai Aswad da shima isowar shi kenan, bin bayan su nayi da ido, had'iye kukan nake karshe dai sai da na fashe da wani irin kuka, wanda ya sani tashi na kwasa da gudu na bar filin.

   Nayi kukan da tun bayan rasuwar Annah banyi ba, sabida bakin ciki, Mama har dakin ta biyo Ni, na juya mata baya. Sabida jinin dake fita a idanuna. Tana dafani jinin na d'aukewa! Murmushi nayi mata sannan nace mata.
"Mama kiyi hakuri!"
"Rike hakurinki! Kuma kizo muje ko na saba miki"
Tana gama fad'ar haka tayi ficcewarta, haka na tashi na bi bayan ta.

    Har na koma bai dawo ba, tun suna hira har hiran ya koma har dani da Aswad, muna cikin haka sai ga Samim. Nan shima ya sani a gaba da kallo, tunda ba sunkuyar da kai ne, nake wasa da kasa.

     Can sai ga Sarkin Bauchi, sun fito suna tafiya kafad'a da Kafad'a, Sarah tana Tsakiyar su,  har suka iso inda muke, sannan Nama ta mike suka gaisa da Sarkin Bauchi, sai da Jamal ya raka shi har bakin motar shi, ina kallon ikon Allah.
"Karki yarda ki bari tasan shine a ranki! Har shima jamal din"

Gyada mata kai nayi sannan na maida kwalla dake idanuna.

   Suna isowa Sarah tazo ta zauna dani har da daura min hannun akan cinyata, tureta naso yi Mama tace min.
"Kul!"
      Kwallar da nake son b'oyewa ne suka so zuba min na shanye su. Jikin tane yayi sanyi tace.
"Mama! Kinga haka kawai sai tayita fushi dani."

Ta faɗa cikin sakalci da shagwaɓa, irinta yaran manyan mutane. Kallona Mama tayi na sunkuyar da kaina.

Tsaki yaja sannan yace.
"Bani son sakarci! Akan me zaki na fushi da ita?! Dan ta damu dake?! Toh wallahi na kuma jin haka Ni dake ne, dan sai na saba miki. Maza bata Hakuri dan na fahimci kin tsiro da wani tashin hankali baki son zaman lafiya."

    Cikin kuka nake basu hakuri, sannan na mike zan bar gurin. Ya daka min tsawa tare da cewa.
"Dawo! Ana ganinki shiru shirun nan, kin fi me baki munafunci, zauna tunda ke yar kanki ce ba a miki faɗa."

         Zai kuma buɗe baki yayi magana Mama ta dakatar da shi da cewa.
"A'ah! Bana so faɗar da kayi ba laifi bane! Cin fuskar kar yayi yawa, kafi kowa sanin bata da lafiya! Idan ciwon ya tashi zaka fadi gaskiyar ka, Jannart ki daina fushi da Kawarki sannan ku zauna lafiya."

     Nasiha tayi mana sosai sannan suka ci-gaba da hirar su, Aswad yana kallon mu bai ce kome ba, har suka tashi zasu tafi. Abubuwan da suka kawo min, Sarah ta taimaka min muka kai cikin dakin mu sannan muka dawo, dukda daurewa nake sai dai nayi alkawari bani kuma damun kaina da Kawu jamal, Insha Allah!

    Ita kuma zan Hakura mu cigaba da ƙawance mu, bayan tafiyarsu. Zamu koma cikin dakinmu sai ga Samim, wanda yake dauke da manyan jakar leda, kallona yayi a rude sannan yace.
" Kinyi kuka ko? Kuyi hakuri waye ya tab'aki?"

         Ban san ina Son yin kuka ba sai da yayi min magana, na fashe masa da kuka, tare da komawa inda muka taso dasu Mama. 

             Hango Sarah nayi tana zuwa nayi maza na goge idanuna nace.
"Ina kewar Mahaifiyata ce,!"

        Sai jikin shi ya mace, murmushi yayi sannan ya bani farin kyale na goge fuskana ya zauna muka shiga hirar, har Sarah ta iso suka gaisa, dana ga zata ishemu da surutun bala'i na mike tare da cewa.
"Zamu koma, kaga yamma ya fara yi."
Mika min kayan yayi sannan yace.
"Da fatan d'akina sun isa gareki! Yau ma ga wani na sa miki ina jiran sakonki nan da wata me zuwa."
Sunkuyar da kai nayi cike da kunya wanda yake tattare da kuruciya nace.
"Don Allah! Karka bari Kawuna yaji haka fushi zai yi dani!"

        "Babu abinda zai miki kinji"

Gyad'a kai nayi cikin gamsuwa da abinda yace min.

       Sallama muka yi ya tafi Ni kuma na koma d'akin mu.
   
      Na kasa fushi da Sarah sai ma kokarin b'oye abinda yake tsakanin mu, tun daga lokacin na fita hanyar fushi da ita, haka zata na bawa sauran daliban labarin matsayin shi da kuma Darajar shi, na dan sarkin daura. Da kuma yadda Mahaifinta ya karb'e shi. Iya nan kawai take tsayawa bata kuma kara wani abu.

      Daura.
   "Jakadiya! Baki ji abinda yake faru bane a masarautar nan! Wai  dakatar da manyan su Galadima da kuma su Wambai."

         "Eh naji Sabuwa wani sabon abu ne dan haka ya faru a cikin masarauta me dadden tarihi haka."

         Tashi tayi ita sabuwar tafiya tana mamakin Jakadiya, tana fita Jakadiya ta sake murmushi sannan ta mike ta fice itama ma zuwan gidan Shamaki.
 Dakyar aka barta ta sami ganin shi. Tana ganin tace.
   "Masoyin kwa’do ba na kifi ba ne."

       Kallon tayi yayi sannan yace.

 Murmushi tayi sannan tace.
"Gaba salama baya salama! Ai ba 
   Niman aure keda wuya ba shige da fitar ce magana!"

    Dariya yayi sannan yace.
"Sai kuma kika ji abinda ya faru hatta wancan musakin koran shi zanyi daga masarautar!"

"Hmmm! Allah ya sawwake wahala! Tsuhuwa taga ajalinta kusa! Ba shine matsalar ba! Kawai abinda na sani ka nemi masana su duba maka,"

    Juyawa tayi gefe dama da hagu sannan tace.
"Ranka shi dad'e! Dole sai ka shiga bincike domin ina hasashe akwai lokacin da zasu tureka karfi da yaji a kan kujeran ka,Idan juninka sammako, to wani a tafe ya kwana."

"Umarni kike bani ko shawara?!"

     "Allah ya huci zuciyarka! Ban isa ba baka umarni ba ko shawara sai dai tuni! Ai amfanin zunubi, romo."

   Daga nan cikin gidan ta wucce gurin Fulani! Zama tayi sosai sannan tace.
"Uwar d'akina! Ya kuka ji labarin Sadaukin Maza! Namijin Duniya! Hadarin gabar me karya zuciyar malalaci! Rana me budiyar Alkhairin! Farin wata sha kallon ragayar ajalin me daki, Giwa me tafiyar kasaita, karo da karo sai rago, rana me rab'awa kowa aiki, sau daya ake haihuwar shi a karni, alfadari me kashin arziki, babban bijimi rurun tsakar gida, tabaryan d'inya me karkon Alkhairin, na gaida samarin watan shara, Jinjina gareki uwar da tahaifi Sarkinmu na gobe!
 
Dakatar da ita Jamalah tayi sannan tace.
"Jakadiya! Na baki kyautar sarkana ta zinari da sauran dangin shi sabida kirarin da kika wa Hussaini na"

      Itama Fulani Hasiyah murmushi tayi sannan ta ɗauki zoben zinarinta takarawa Jakadiya, godiya har da kuka.

          Gyara zama tayi sannan tace.
"Hmm! Al'amarin duniya da mutanen cikin su sai dai abarwa Allah! Amma ranki shi dad'e!.a tsaya sosai akan Sadaukin maza dan na fara jin kishin kishin, amma ban tabbatar ba."

        "Jeki an jima ko dawo ki amshi sakonki" inji Jamalah,

 Kamar munafuka haka tafice, tana jin haushin dakatar da ita abinda ta kawo, tana fita Jamalah ta kalli Mahaifiyar ta, tace.
"Umma! Kinsan dalilin da yasa Jamal shigowar Jakadiya wannan sassan kenan tsegumi da gulma kuma halo kina biye mata wallahi zata iya gini miki ramin mugunta kuma ki hau ki rubta! Mu zaki jawa abin kunya! Don Allah idan kuma da wata a kasa ki warware kafin lokacin ya Kure miki"
 Har ta mike! Sai ta juya tana kallon Ummanta kafin tace.
"Jikina da zucyata! Suna min wani irin zargi sabida shekaran jita naga wata mace wacce take mugun kama da Jamal! Umma dukda ban tsaya sosai ba amma ina da yakinin akwai wani sirrin da ake b'oyewa a masarautar nan don Allah Ummana karki saka kanki a ukun gidan nan, domin abin magana baya kankanta! Kuma baya girma, idan wani abu ya fito wallahi zan iya juya miki baya na kalubalence ki dan kinsan abinda na karanta kenan kwatarwa me gaskiya hakkin shi, Ni daiki rabu da Jakadiya, inda hali tabar aiki a nan sassan haƙa shine mafi alkhairi."

  Tana gama fad'ar haka, tayi wucewar ta.
          Idan ran Fulani yayi dubu toh tabbas ya b'aci dole ta idda nufinta akan Rumanah dan itace kawai zata kawo mata iyaka da muradinta akan Jamal.

*
       "Don Allah, matar Yaya kibarni na nimeta tunda shi baya kaunarta! Wallahi Ni ina mugun sonta kuma dan bata da asali zan zauna da ita! Har karshen rayuwa! Sai dai kuma idan zaki nuna min son kai ne sabida shi d'an uwanki ne Ni kuma kanin mijinki ne"

          Shiru tayi tana kallon shi sannan ta sauke ajiyar zuciya tace.
"Ni babu ruwana a cikin al'amarin kawai ina son ka dan dakata ne! Zuwa wani lokaci idan kuma zaka iya da jarabar jamal toh bismillah,  Ni damuwata karka saka shi ya kuma kaita inda zata wahala ne."

   "Shi kenan! Nagode 🤝 sosai"
 Yace mata,

    *
   Bazan iya kwatanta shak'uwar dake tsakaninmu da Sarah, yayinda fahimtar dake tsakanin mu, ya kara nunawa duniya muna tare.
        
 Yau mun fito sallar dare Ni da ita, toh tarigani shiga ban daki, Ni kuma ina tsaye, tana fitowa Ni na shiga. Koda na fito na sameta ta gama alolar tana jirana.

Nazo na tsuguna zan yi alola kawai sai ji nayi ta ture ni, ita kuma ta fasa ihu tare da zuɓewa a gurin tana shura shure, sai lokacin na lura da wani katon bakin maciji da yake gangarawa kasa, ihun da nayi ya tashi duk yan layin gidanmu baki d'aya, a guje Mama Mero tazo ta dauketa, muka nufi asibitin makaranta sai kuka nake, koda muka isa.

Abinda aka fara mata shine alluran da zai rage karfin dafin macijin,sannan suka shiga tambayata wani iri ne macijin nace.
" Ni dai baki na gani, kuma yana da girma, sannan ban san!"

          Rasa abin yi aka yi inda suka nufi gidan ko za a same shi dan wani lokaci idan aka kashe maciji ana samun saukin alamarin dafin shi, babu shi babu dalilin shi.

     Duk da sun sanya mata ruwa, kafin wani lokacin jikinta ya fara baki, tare da fitar da wani irin zufa.

       Kamar mahaukaciya, haka na koma ko takalmi babu a kafana, ina kuka tare da Fad'uwa. Sabida ban tab'a kawowa zata iya bada Rayuwarta sabida Ni ba, gashi tana gab da mutuwa. Zuwa nayi zan shiga dakin Humairah da Latifah suka hanani tare da ture Ni Suna cewa.
"Muguwa! Gashi ke baki damu da ita ba ita ta bada rayuwarta saboda ke, zata rasa numfashita da ruhinta idan ta mutu sai ki zuba ruwa kisha, muguwa azzaluma kuma wallahi tana mutuwa kece zamu ce kin kasheta sabida kece kika tasheta sallar dare, tunda bata saki."

    Girgiza musu kai nayi sannan nace.
"Don Allah karku min mugun zato, tun a masallaci tace na tashet.."

 Kafin na karasa abinda zance, Humairah ta mare Ni har sai da ana fadi tare da buge goshina, wanda yayi sanadin zubar jini daga kai na.

_Assalamu alaikum Jama'a barkanmu da Allah! Ubangiji ya mai-maita mana_

1️⃣7️⃣
   
Wani irin haske na gani tare da karar kacar dake jikinta, rike kaina nayi, lokacin guda guguwa ta taso me mugun ƙarfi wacce tadda wani irin kura da ya haifar da wani irin duhuwa, wanda yasa makarantar yayi bak'ikirin, ko tafin hannunka baka gani. Sai ihun dalibai da mallamai yake tashi a makarantar.

       Tafiya nake akan karamin yatsar kafana, cikin inda gidan mu na koma, na shiga can bayan dakin mu naje, akwai wani tsohon bishiyar rimi can naje na saka hannuna har cikin ramin dake cikin bishiyar na zaro macijin, wanda a zahirin gaskiya bani bace.
            Ina ciro shi na ya zama wani tsohon tukuf, kallon shi tayi sannan tace.
"Ka wuce!"

        Zuɓewa yayi akan gwiwar shi yace.
"Ya shugabata! Ba Ni ne nayi laifin ba, a cikin jikokina ne! Amma na amsa zanyi yadda kika ce haka zan miki"

        Haka suka bar bishiyar zuwa cikin asibiti inda Sarah ke kwance, sam bazaka iya fahimtar abinda ya faru ba, amma tabbas zuwan su shine mafi alkhairi domin kuwa da guguwar da sauyawar halittar Jannart basu fi minti talatin ba, sai kuma Humairah da Latifah, wanda aka jiyo ihun su ana zane, koda isar ta lafa, can aka sami Jannart yashe a tsakiyar makarantar kura da yayi tare da kasa sun rigeta, Dalibai da Mama Mero suka dauketa, zuwa dakin su inda suka ga ta koma fayau kamar babu jini a jikinta, ga hancinta shima haka jini na diga.

            ...... A can asibitin kuwa farkawa Sarah take son yi, amma ganin halin da Jannart take, ya rud'ar da ita cikin tashin hankali tace.
"Sahibah! Jininki ne ke zuba!"
   Murmushi tayi mata, sannan tace.
"Indai zaki tsira da rayuwarki toh tawa rayuwar fansa ce a gareki!"

    "A'ah Jannart karki min haka!"
    Murmushi tayi mata sannan ta b'ace! Ita kuma ta farka da sunan ta.

        Ƙoƙarin mata allura suke amma taki tsayawa, sai da aka danneta sannan suka mata alluran da ya sata barcin dole, sannan suka fita, suna mamakin yadda suka samu nasarar magance dafin macijin.

*
     Kwana uku tsakani aka sallami Sarah wacce take da labarin ciwon Sarah, tayi wani irin lokaci guda, tunda ta koma d'akin sun taga yadda ta koma sakata tayi a gaba tana kuka, tare da rike hannunta wanda suka dan kumbura, jikinta har kore kore yayi.

"Sahibah! Don Allah tashi haka! Kinji don Allah karki bani tsoro bana son ganinki cikin tashin hankali."
             Haka tayi ta surutu ita d'aya, wasika aka tura Kaduna. Wanda ya d'aga hankalin Mama da Samim suka tawo akan kafarsu. Ganin halin da muke ciki ya kad'a mama, dan haka suka dauke ni aka dawo dani gida, koda muka iso kaduna. Asibitin da na kwanta Nam suka kaini. Muna zuwa a gwajin farko jini aka bukata, Samim ya bada nashi. Abin mamaki ana samin jinin jikina yaki amsa, ya shiga wani irin jijjiga, dole Samim ya nemi wani abokin shi ya tura shi Zaria aka, aka fadawa Kawu Jamal.

     Yana da gwaji ranar amma ya tsallake ya tawo, Kaduna tunda ya iso aka gaya mishi ai jini nake bukata, bata tare da b'ata lokaci ba suka je aka dibe jinin, sannan aka samin. Tunda jinin ya fara shiga jikina. Na fara komawa kalar fatana, shi kuma ya koma inda ya fito ko dawowa dakin bai yi ba, dan leda biyu aka bukata. A karo na biyu jinin Kawuna yana yawo a jikina.

        Sai da nayi sati guda na dawo dai dai, sannan aka sallame ni. Muna dawowa gida yazo shida su Mama aka mai dani makaranta, tunda muka shigo ban san waye ya gayawa Sarah na dawo ba, dan tana cikin aji sai gata nan da gudu, tana ganin shi tayi wani turus, tana kallon kasa. Jikina ta dawo ta lafe, tare da ce min.
"Sahibah!"

Raina ne ya b'aci, nayi ƙoƙarin b'oyewa sannan na cirota daga bayana nace.
"Mama ban gaya muku abinda ya faru ba, amma ita ta bada rayuwarta saboda ni! Domin maciji ne yazo zai sare ni, shine ta ture Ni ya sareta."

    Sunkuyar da kai tayi, ya kura mata ido, sannan yace.
"Nagode!"
Yi nayi kamar ban gansu ba, muka cigaba da magana da mama tana jin dadin yadda Sarah tayi taimakon da ta min.

    Muna tsaye ta lalle shi tace.
"Kawunmu zaka zo mana ziyara kuwa! Dan nasan wannan karo Ammyna da Daddah zasu zo min tunda mai martaba yazo ya duba ni, har nake gaya mishi Sahibah bata da lafiya, yace wai yana da wani uzuri ne da yaje, Kaduna ya duba ta, sannan yace wai tana da kirki."
   Dafe goshina nayi cike da takaici, yadda take zuba shi kuma yana gyad'a mata kai irin ya gamsu da Abinda take gaya mishi.

         "Sahibah! Kawar kawu bari naje daki na sauya kayana, sai nazo muje aji" na fada mata a sanyayye sannan na bar gurin su.

         Koda na juya baya hawaye ne yake zuba daga idanuna, abin mamaki hawayen amadadin ruwa sai nake zubda jini, da sauri na isa dakin mu na zauna a bakin gado na, tare da fashewa da kuka. "Me yasa? Ban cancanci zama abokiyar rayuwar shi bane? Me yasa baya jin komai a kaina? Shin meye LAIFINA?"

    Wannan sune tambayar da nayiwa kaina wanda na tabbatar bani da amsar badawa, kayan makaranta na saka sannan na shirya, na goge idanuna.

        Na koma gurinsu na dame su suna shirin tafiya, rike hannuna Mama tayi. Numfashi ta sauke sannan tace.
"Allah yana tare da masu hakuri!"
     Gyad'a kai kawai nayi, dan bani da abinda zan iya ce mata kuma idan na dage kuka zanyi.
"Raihanah! Mu tafi kina b'ata mana lokaci dan zan koma makaranta yau!"

     Wani irin kallon tayi mishi sannan ta juya muka rungume juna, sannan ta shiga motar yaja su. D'ago kai yayi daga motar ya kalle ni, sunkuyar da kai nayi. Ina jin idanun shi a cikin jikina, amma nakasa d'agowa har naji tashin motar, da sauri nake bayan motar na tsaya.  Kallon junan da baki muyi shi muka yi Ni da shi, dan ta madubin muka zubawa juna ido, kauda kai yayi daga kaina ya maida kan motar shi.

       "Ya Allah! Ban san laifin da nayi ba, nake fuskarta wannan bala'i ba Ya Allah ka yaye min!"

     "Baki yi komai ba Sahibah! Kaddararki ce haka! Ki godewa Allah da ya jarabce ki haka"

     Zare hannunta nayi daga kafad'ana, na juya mata baya sannan na bar mata gurin ina share kwallar da suka cika min idanu.

   
            +++
Bayan shekara biyu lokaci muna aji hudu dan Sarah tariga Ni shiga makarantar, dan tana aji biyu na sameta muka tafi tare, a wannan lokacin na iya b'oye damuna,dan daga baya na fahimci cewa babu soyayya a tsakanin su kawai dai suna abotane, a tunanin Jamal kenan amma a tunanin Sarah ba haka bane. Abinda na fahimta a tare da ita shine tana ƙoƙarin gano ko yana sonta, amma bawan Allah nan haka ya share batun soyayyar da take nuna mishi, dan zaku sha mamaki idan na gaya muku, Sarah ta gama gayawa mutanen makarantar mu da ɗalibai ai soyayya suke da Kawuna, duk wanda ya same ni da batun sai nace.
"Haka na gansu!"
Dake Allah yayi mata shege baki mara sirri komai faɗa take, idan ta bani haushi sai na tsiro mata da magana cikin magana. Tuni take shiga hankalinta.

               +++
Daura.
     "Tuba nake gamji! Na rasa yadda."
    "Hmm! Jamal shine damuwarksa. Idan aka ajiye shi a guri d'aya, zakafi samun nutsuwa, dan haka ga wannan kullin,  a zuba a kofar dakin shi har ranar da zai dawo, sai kuma wannan a dinga zubawa dokin shi, yin haka zai sa ayi tunanin ko shima ya shiga jerin sarakunan da  boka Arih ya tsinewa albarka ne, ta sanadin dokin Ziriya Has. Wanda fatalwar shi take bibiyar jinin masarautar nan! Sai dai zai iya samun lafiya ba kamar sauran sarakunar ba, sabida su basa soyayya. Shi kuma yana dab da fadawa soyayyar wata wacce. Nake hango itace zata kawo sanadin da zaka bar mulkin, domin bincikena ya tabbata akan ka, An haifeka a shekarar da Zulum ya cika shekaru dari cik da mutuwa Sanadin Has, sai shima An haife shi bayan Fairo ya cika shekaru dari da Ashirin da biyar, sai ita wacece banga fuskarta ba, Sarauniyar matsafa, an haifeta ne bayan Ziriya tacisu shekaru dari tamanin da yan kai, 
       Tarigada tashigo cikin masarautar ka, sai dai bani da tabbacin zata dawo ko bazata dawo ba, amma ka sani laifin banaka bane! Na wasu Al'ummar da suka gabata ne, dole sai kayi da gaske, akan su domin tarihi karya maimaita kan shi, Ni kuma zanyi ƙoƙarin ganin na dakatar da faruwar Komai. Tashi kaje"

      Haka ya zuba mishi silala, har zai kusan fita sai Gamji yace.
"Zai iya yiwa yazo bikin sallah tunda azumi yazo, ko kuma idan ya gama makaranta ya dawo tunda An hanashi zuwa saboda kace zaka kashe shi, idan ya farga da abinda ka shirya mishi, ga wannan zumar yana cikin abinda yake so, sai a bashi a matsayin kyauta, ka nime wani ya kai mishi. Karka yarda yasan daga gare ka yake, Ni kuma zan dakatar da Ziriya da Fairo"

  Gyad'a kai yayi sannan ya dauki katon kwarya cike da zuma.

            ++++
Kana naka Allah yana nashi, na Allah ne kuma gaskiya, domin Kawu ana shirya mishi tuggu amma mai martaba ya hana shi zuwa, sai aike.

      Haka suka gabatar da Bikin Jamal da Ahmad Halwani, dan sarkin Kano Abu Turab, bikin da ya dauki kwanaki ana Shagali.

        ------
A yau muka fito jarabawarmu ta gama Secondry, sai murna muke. Shi yazo daukana, a ranar da zamu rabu munyi kuka sosai domin kuwa Ni kuka na, na abinda ta aikata ne, dan akaina aka dakatar da ita a zana jarabawar shekarar dubu biyu da d'aya, sabida kamani da akayi ina satar amsa,  a take na nuna musu ai itace tayi laifin tana tsoron kar a kamata shine ta wurga a jikina, ido da ido tayiwa kanta sharri dan ni, kallonta nake cike da mamaki, tace.
"Wallahi nice na shigo da satar amsar, Allah ba Jannart Jamal nace nice na wurga a jikinta."

          Shiru Malaman suka yi, sannan suka, tasa keyarta zuwa waje, a take aka cire sunanta a cikin daliban shekarar......

      Koda iyayenta suka zo, nasha mamaki da suka nuna babu wani kuskure da ta aikata. Haka Allah Ya nufa, amma Ni da akan nafita shiga cikin tashin hankali, domin har muka rabo kuka nake, ina kallonta suka shiga motar su, taki kallona. Jikin motar nake ina cewa.
"Maryam Adam! Don Allah ki yafe min! Maryam Adam me kike so na miki"

          Hannunta ta kawo sannan tace.
"Nasan Ni abinda nayi, ba wani abu bane, idan nayi dubi da maraici. Ni zan koma gida shekara d'aya zanyi na sake jarabawar ke kuma Kawu da Mama tare da Samim da Aswad sun sanya ranau akan ki, idan na bari aka cire sunanki a cikin daliban shekara bazan yafewa kaina ba, Wallahi babu abinda zaki min, amma kisaka a ranki Allah ya hadamu ne cikin amincin sa, kuma mun zauna cikin amincinsa iya haka yayi Allah ya sadamu da Alkhairin."

   Daga haka suka ja motar muna kuka, wannan shine rabuwarmu da ita. Kawar da bazan kuma samun irinta ba, har abada. Kuma nima zanyi ƙoƙarin kyautata mata idan Allah ya bamu aron kwana, domin nuna mata girman Soyayyar da ta min domin Allah ba tare da wani algus ba.

        Ina tsaye har suka iso.......

Suna isowa abinda Kawun ya fara min shine tambayar Sarah!

     Goge kwalla nayi nace.
"Yanzun suka bar makarantar!" 
"Hmm! Shine ɗan shagwaɓa kikewa mutane kuka?! Ko Aliyah bazata yi shirmen da kike ba, balle Halifa, mtseew?! Ya jamin tsaki,

    Sunkuyar da kaina nayi ba tare da na kalle shi ba, wani irin b'acin rai nake ji, dan bamu tab'a haduwa bai gaya min magana son rainshi, daukar kayana nayi na saka a Bayan motar, sannan Aswad yace mishi.
"Wallahi abinda kake yi baka kyauta ba, taya zaka zauna kana gaya mata magana son ranka! Banza kawai."

     Ina gamawa na shiga cikin motar nayi zamana, har suka gama kus-kus din su, sannan suka shigo motar.

     Tunda muka fara tafiya na lumshe idanuna, ina jin hirar su har Allah ya kawo mu cikin kaduna ajiyar zuciya na sauke wanda ya sashi juyawa ya kalle ni.

     Har muka iso gida, yana satar kallona ta glass. Amma baki na bud'e idanu, kuma ina jin haka a jikina.
      Aswad na tsayuwa na balle kofar na fita, da sauri dan ina kewar Mama dukda ina zuwa hutu, ina fita da gudu Aliyah na fitowa muka rungume juna, wacce itama yanzun ta zama yan mata, tace.
"Yaya Barka da zuwa! Muje ciki."

"Ke!"
   Na juya tare da zuba mishi ido, sannan na nuna kaina.
Tsuke fuskarshi yayi sannan ya zabga min hararar kafin yace.
"Waye ɗan dakonki?!"

             Ajiyar zuciya na sauke sannan na tawo, na bude motar na sauke kayana dan ya hana Aswad ya taimaka min, ina gamawa na juya ba tare da nace musu cikanku ba nabar gurin.

       Koda muka shiga, na same Abbansu Aliyah a falo Mama tana sanya mishi abinci, durkusawa nayi har kasa na gaida shi zan mik'e yace.
"Da fatan kun gama jarabawar ku lafiya?!"
    "Alhamdulillah! Baba."

     Da sallama suka shigo, ina ganinsu na mike na bar falon, na shiga ciki, bazan ce ga lokacin da suka bar gidan ba, amma ji mama tana fada sosai akan abinda ban san meye musababin haka ba. Shigowa tayi cikin fada wanda ban tab'a ganinta tana yi ba tace.
"Kina Son Samim ne ko kina wahalar da kanki har yanzun?!"

   Tana gama fad'ar haka ta nuna min hanyar fita, da sauri na wuce gaba muka fita, jikina babu kwari muka nufi inda mata ke zama.

    Tunda na zauna a hankali na dafe goshina, ina jin hayaniyar jama'a. Ji nake Kamar kaina zai buɗe. Har aka fara wasan, bani jin daɗin zaman gurin.

    Kifa kaina nayi ina jin wani irin a jikina, ga kuma ciwo da kaina yake. A hankali hawaye ke zuba a idanuna, sosai ƙoƙarin danne kuka na nake, Ni kaina ban san dalilin kukan ba, amma nasan yana da nasaba da ganin wannan matar, tashi nayi da sauri na bar gurin, da ido Mama ta rakani. Ina fita ban zame ko ina ba sai can inda Bayi ke zaune ina shiga zauren da zai kai Ni gurin su, kawai sai na d'imauce. Ganin zauren nayi ya koma min kamar wani shahararren pada wanda aka yi shi tsawon lokuta me tsayo, cikin padar kuwa cike yake da kayukan kwarangwal, sai kuma jini da yake gudana, mata da maza ne a duke daga su sai d'an fatar b'auna ko kura, gefe can wani yagwalgwalen tsoho ne a duke yake tafiya, matar nan tana zaune gaban wani mutum, yana gyara mata gashinta, kura musu ido nayi cikin mamaki, dan kamar zahiri kamar mafarki, juyowa tayi ta kalle ni, sannan ta juya abinta suka ci-gaba da abinda suke, ina son nayi magana na kasa, sabida harshena yayi matuƙar nauyi sannan kuma na kasa motsawa, domin ji nake kafaffuna kaman an daure min su, a hankali naga babu kowa a cikin fadar sai matar da mutumin ya dauketa, suka shiga wani guri kaman mayafi aka ja musu, shigowar wasu mutane da gudu tare da wasu muggan makamai, suka shiga sararran mutumin, wanda suka fidda shi, sai lokacin na lura rabin shi daga cikin shi zuwa sama mutum ne, rabin shi zuwa kasa ta dawakai ne! Cikin wahala yacewa wani mutum wanda kan shi yake sanye da kan damisa.

              Dafani akayi na fasa wata irinya ihu wanda ya karaɗe masarautar baki daya, na zube a jikin Jakadiya. Dariya tayi sannan tace.
"Yarinyar! Soyayyar da ba a cika ba shi ake son a cika, Fairo ya dawo! Ziriya ta dawo toh waye Zulum! Har yanzun ban ganshi ba, dole naje kwatarkwashin domin niman Zulum?! Ko ki mutu ko kiyo rai! Wannan ba wani abu bane dole kece muke bukata dan zaki kawo mana karshen zaluntar mu da akayi, aka amshe mana sarauniyar mu"

          Cikin ta wuce da Jannart, bata kai ta d'akinta ba sai ta nufi kofar Rumanah, take ta buga mata dan lokacin tana kuka ne, share fuskarta tayi. Sannan ya d'aga taburman karaunin da aka rufe shi a matsayin labule.
"Lafiya Sahurar?!"

        Yab'e baki tayi sannan tace..
"Wannan! Abun na gani a can shine na kwaso miki ita dan naga ranar a dakin ki ta kwana."
  
A halin da take ciki bata son wani damuwa, karb'an Jannart tayi ta shige da ita d'akin sannan ta kwantar da ita.

            Ruwa ta diba a tukunyar ƙasar ta, sannan ta gyara mata kwanciya, ta kuma shafa mata ruwan a karo na biyu.

         Ajiyar zuciya na sauke, a hankali nake bin dakin da kallo, tashi nayi na zauna, sannan na kuma mik'ewa zan fita riko hannuna tayi. 

 "Don Allah ki barni naje zasu illata shi." Na fada mata kaman zanyi kuka,

    "Idan hakan na cikin kaddarar shi babu abinda zaki iya mishi" ta fada min yayin da take me juya min baya,

           "Toh ni bazan iya ba dole naje na gan shi don Allah ki barni naje, karsu mishi wani abu."

         Juyowa tayi tare da zuba min ido, sosai fuskarta ya juye kaman nashi, sunkuyar da kaina nayi wasu kwalla masu zafi na saukan min. Ta cika min idanu bazan iya musanta mata ba.

          ---
A can filin kuwa bayan kowani tawaga yafita, shima nashi tawagar suka fita, kallon Aswad yayi cikin damuwa da gajiya yace
"Wallahi bani jin dadin jikina! Sai yanzun nake dana sanin fitowa da nayi jikina kaman an zuba min wuta ta ciki."

   A lokaci guda zuba ya jika mishi kayan shi, sosai har yana d'iga, sam bayi da karfin da zai iya wani abu, a lokacin aka saka wasan zilla. Ita wasan za a sakawa wani doki tutar masarautar ce su kuma mahaya, su shiga binta dan su kwace tutar.

    Daga nesa Shamaki sai dariya yake, saboda ganin yadda Jamal ya koma, a hankali aka saka wasan, daga cikin masu yin wasan har da Abdulkareem dan gidan Shamaki, a tarihin wannan wasan duk wanda ya cire tutar a jikin dokin, ana da tsammanin zai iya zama sarki, kuma kimanin shekaru sha takwas ba ayi wasan ba, wani abinda ya dame mutanen a cikin wannan wasan sarakunan baya suka rasa rayukansu, domin sarki ne da wasu manyan Pada suke yin wasan, toh bayan dogon lokaci ana ayi wasan ba, sai gashi Shamaki ya tadda wasan kuma yace manyan yaran masu rawanin masarautar sune zasu wakilci iyayen su.

       A cikin masu wasan akwai, Abbas dan Galadima, sai Habib dan Waziri, sai Alkassim dan Chiroma, sai Mas'ud dan Wambai, sai Jamal dan Sarki, sai Aswad dan gidan Liman., Da kuma Abdulkareem dan Shamaki.

           Suna gama haduwa Sarkin Pada ya mike, cikin amsakuwa, yace.
"Sarki na muku Barka da sallah! Sannan yana gaishe ku, a yau ma bayan shekaru sha takwas baya , yau mun kuma dawo muku da wasan zilla, ga mahayan mu sun shirya tsaf. Dan haka kamar yadda kuka san al'adar masarautar, duk wanda ya samu damar kwace tutar muna mishi tsammanin shine sarki na gaba. Dan haka zaku iya farawa."


       Jan linzamin  Jamal yayi yana jin kamar ana sararran jikin shi, ga idanun shi da suke mishi wani irin, matsowa Abdulkareem yayi sannan yace.
"Babban yaro naji bana ka gama degree d'inka! Gashi kazo wasan kasada! Waye yasani ko zaka yi rai ko zaka mutu."

            Murmushin wahala Jamal yayi ma shi, sannan yaja linzamin shi, buga tambura aka yi suka sukwane dawakan su a guje.

        ...... Mik'ewa nayi jikina na rawa nace mata.
"Zan koma!"

   Bata iya ce min komai ba, na ficce da sauri dan karta hanani, har inda su Mama suke nazo lokacin su Jamal suna kan niman kama dokin, sai mugunta Abdulkareem yake musu, kuma a a ka'idar wasan duk wanda aka ture ya fadi ya fita gasar kenan,  duk da ya ture mutane uku, shi harin shi. Kawuna ne.  Kuma wani ikon Allah da zaran ya kawo mishi hari zai kwace, abin gwanin tashin hankali, mara mutunci ya cire Alkassim da Aswad, ya kuma fitar da Abbas  saura Habib da Mas'ud, nan gasar ta zafaffa Sosai wanda ya mugun d'aga hankalin kowa, domin  Habib ya fidda Mas'ud, sai ya rage Jamal da Abdulkareem da kuma Habib, ganin haka, Abdul ya fidda Habib sai ya rage Jamal da Abdul zuwa lokacin karfin Jamal yayi kasa sosai domin kuwa ya fara zubda jini daga hancinsa, da kuma bakin shi,  farin rawanin shi ya ɓaci da jini.

    Rike hannun Mama nayi cikin kuka nace.
"Wayyo Allah na! Kawuna sun cutar min dashi, Mama duba ki gani aman jini yake,"

           Kallon Mai martaba Shamaki yayi cikin murmushi sabida suna zaune kusada juna, sosai, yace.
"Jalal! Kasani ɗaukar matakan akan tsageran ɗanka, bani da burin cutar da lafiyar shi, amma ganin yana shirin b'ata min gobe na yasani yin abinda ya dace!, Idan ya rayu kace mishi ya guji sa'in'sa da manyan mutane irina domin bamu fada sai dai a gani a aikace, mune ake kira da a faɗa ta gudu, abarta ta lalace."

       Har zuwa lokacin sosai suke danbarwan kwace tutar,  amma Abdul ya kasa ya tsare, sai niman hanyar da zai cutar da Jamal yake, sau uku yana saka kafa ya ture dokin Jamal, amma basu fadi ba, kuma karku manta shi faduwar bada ta wasa bane, dan idan baka Karye ba, toh tabbas sai kaji ciwo ko kuma ka bugu. Haka suka ci-gaba da kaiwa juna mugun nufin musamman Abdul, wanda burin shi kawai ya cima dokin.

     Ana cikin haka kawai dokin Jamal ta fara hauka, da tsalle tsalle, har ta cimma wancan dokin dakyar ya kai hannun shi ya fisgi tutar, dai dai lokacin da Abdul ya kawo mishi duka da gwiwar hannun shi, ai kuwa suka tintsira da dokin, ta kuma tashi ta fadi, sannan ta kuma mik'ewa tayi ta juyi da Jamal, tana buga shi da kasa, tana jan shi a kasa.
        Ban san na tsallake jama'a, na fito da mugun gudu, nace.
"Hasssss! Kunba nuba! Jiyya hiyya, huuuuuuuuuu"

 Zuɓewa dokin yayi na karasa da sassarfa na zube akan gwiwarta na fashe da wani irin kuka, na shiga  ƙoƙarin d'ago kan shi na daura bisa cinyata.


       (Saura ku tambaye ni Yaren da nayi🤣🙄😣🤔)[6/11, 12:56 PM] My Zahrah: ƘWARƘWARAH!!?

   *_Kalmomin da nayi amfani dashi pagen jiya!  Yaren Ham ce wacce itama tana cikin harshen banza Bakwai! Kuma masu Yaren suna nan tsakanin! Kafancha! Bauchi! Da wasu shashin banza Bakwai! Dan haka Yaren Hausa ce! Kafin a rarrabe ta! Sannan masu tambayar Meye Jannart ta faɗa 👉🏻na sake muku fassaran a cikin wannan pagen zaku gani sabida kuma ki fahimci shashin na labarin Nagode 🤝 sosai)_*

     

   
2️⃣1️⃣

Girgiza shi nake, ina tab'a ciwon dake gefen fuskar shi, ina faɗin.
"Tashi Kawuna! Babu abinda zai faru da kai, Wayyo Allah kawuna, Innalillahi wa'inna ilaihi rajioun! Tashi babu abinda aka isa maka,"

Karkad'e kasar da take idanun shi nayi, sai a lokacin na lura da idanun shi da ya cika da ƙasa, wani irin ihu nasaka, tare da rike kan shi a kirjina ina kuka Kamar zararrariya, ina juya kaina da sauri dogaro suka iso filin, tare da Mama cikin tashin hankali, wani daga cikin dogaran ya mika hannunsa yayi da niyyar zai d'ago kan Jamal,  kamo hannun shi nayi cikin zafin nama na rike sai da kashin hannunshi yayi nayi wanda yasa shi sake mugun ihu, idanuna jini na zuba a cikinsu.

         Sai da hannun ta koma kamar babu kashi sannan na sake, gaba ki ɗaya an rasa yadda zasu karb'e shi a hannuna, dole suka ja baya, cike da tsorona Mama ta iso gurin, ta mika min hannu, d'ago kaina nayi tare da zuba mata ido, jikinta ne yake rawa.

Sunkuyar da kai nayi Sannan na mika mata kanshi janyo shi tayi sannan ta mik'awa, Abbas shi Ni kuma tayi maza tasa aka rike Ni ta baya, suka fita damu.

         Jini ne ke fita ta hancin shi da bakin shi, yayinda ni kuma nake kwara amai kamar zan fitar da kayan cikina..

     Kuma anan fa wani irin ne fari kal, kafin mu shiga cikin gidan nayi fayau alamun babu jini a jikina, a take Aswad yake cewa.
"Mu wuce dasu asibiti dan Jannart itama akwai matsala barinta a gidan."

          Haka suka saka shi a motar shi da mai martaba ya bashi, Ni kuma Aswad ya sanyani a motar shi.

   Muka nufi asibiti dake Abbas ya iya mota shi yake jan Jamal, tare da Alkassim.

   Ni kuma Mama da Aswad, sai Yaya Rukayyah. Haka suka nufi asibiti damu, koda muka isa an karb'e mu inda aka shiga duba nashi Ni kam jini aka buƙata, Aswad ya bada na shi, amma bai dace da nawa ba.

     Gashi rashin jinin yasa na fara wani irin kumburi, fuskana har ya fara kore Kore, dake an kawo mai martaba ya biyo bayan su, yaji ana niman jinin da za a sanya min dan bazai yu a sanya min na Kawu ba dubi da halin da yake ciki, gudu kar wani abu ya biyo Baya Aswad da Mama suka nuna likitan yace Jamal ne ke bukatar jini, ai kuwa aka gwada jinin da na mai martaba,  kasa samu yayi.. daya da nawa shine suka diba leda biyu aka ce mishi wa Jamal za'a sakawa.

      An sami jinin cikin awa daya sai gashi jikina ya fara dawowa dai dai.

   ..... Hmm Jamal kam ba a cewa komai dan a idanun shi da hancin shi an cire kasa ya kai kusan cokali uku, banda wuyar shi da ake da kyautata zaton ya karye, sai kafafun shima haka da kuma hakarkarin shi, ya goce.

            
     Abinda me Martaba yake iya fada shine "Alhamdulillah innalillahi wa'inna ilaihi rajioun! Alhamdulillah Innalillahi wa'inna ilaihi rajioun" wannan itace kalmar da zuciyar shi take ambaton, tare da kiran sunayen Allah.

              Umma Fulani tazo cikin tashin hankali, kuka take kamar karamar yarinya.

         Tun safe suke gurin har aka kira azhar, sannan suka watse.
         
            ---
A filin masarautar kuwa watsewa aka yi, cikin wani irin yanayi Abdul yabi bayan Mahaifin shi yace.
"Baba! Nifa yarinyar nan tayi min! Kuma ina sonta."

       A fusace Shamaki ya kamo wuyar shi tare da had'a shi da bango, yana fidda idanun shi waje yace.
"Idan mafarki kake! Ka tashi idan magagi kake ka ware! Idan kuma giya kasha ya sake ka, yarinyar muradina ce! Ko inuwar ta ka rab'a sai na raba gangan jikinka da numfashinka."

Sannan yayi wurgi da D'an nashi, dariya Abdul yayi sannan yace.
"Toh wallahi sai dai ka kashe ni! Amma tabbas sai na sameta idan kana ganin karya ne! Ni ba d'anka bane, kuma ni sonta nake da aure amma idan nayi yadda nake so da ita, tayu kai ka hakura kabar min ita dan na lura kai ma baka jin sosai"

      
A fusace yayo kan Abdul ya kuma tsaya mishi gar, ganin yadda d'an ya tsaya babu ko tsoron shi a idanun d'an sai yaja da baya dan bai san me d'an yake takama dashi ba, kuma ance mai laya ya kiyaye mai zamani.

          Cikin mugun nufin ya juya abin shi, tare da sakawa a ranshi sai ya bawa D'an shi wahala akan Jannart.
      
         ---
Ban farka ba, sai karfe biyu na dare, naga Mama rike da hannuna, a hankali na motsa baki na nace..
"Zan sha ruwa!"

     D'ago kanta tayi cikin sauri tace.
"Jannart!! Kin tashi ne? Sannu bari na baki ruwan"

         Diban ruwan tayi ta d'agani, sannan ta daura min kofi a baki har na shanye.

      Kafeta nayi da idanu sannan nace.
"Mama Kawu!"

   "Lafiyan shi lau! Sosai yaji sauki har ma"

     Kuri na mata da ido, kafin na juyar da kaina gefe nace.
"Ba halinki bane! Mama Ina yake?!"

   Rike hannuna tayi sosai sannan ta sanya a gefen fuskarta tace.
"Jannart! Jamal addu'ar mu yake bukata."

             Juyowa nayi na kalle ta, na wasu dakikai, sannan nace..
"Yaji ciwo sosai ko?!".
                Shiru tayi sannan ta shiga bani labarin halin da take ciki, lumshe idanuna nayi Sannan na sauke akan ta tare da jefa mata tambaya.

"Kenan bazai kuma gani ba!! Bazai kuma tafiya da kafar shi ba ?! Me yasa sai shi? Me yasa suka mishi lahani?!"

     "Babu wanda yayi mishi ko mai, kawai abinda ke bibiyar masarautar ne ya juyo kanshi"

"A'ah! Ba hass bane! Shiryawa suka yi."
     Cike da zulimi tace min.
"Me kika sani?!"
Hamma nayi sannan na juya mata baya, nace.
"Mama ban san komai ba, amma ba doki hass bane. Sun dai shirya hakan ne dan cimma muradun su. Mu kwana lafiya amma zaki iya tambayar Abba abinda Shamaki ya gaya mishi."

     "Hmmm!" Tace alamar taji tsoron abinda nake faɗa, Ni kuwa barcina nayi cikin salama.

       Ta jima zaune tana auna maganar da nayi mata.

       ... Kwance yake idanun shi rufe da farin kyalle, sai kafad'arshi zuwa hakarkarin shi da aka daura wani farin zare, sai wuyar shi da aka saka wani roba, wanda zai taimaka mishi sabida ciwon da yake wuyar shi.

            Kura mishi ido Mai Martaba yayi yana jin ciwon abinda ya faru, *Me yasa Shamaki yayi min haka?! Meye laifin Jamal?!*

      Wannan sune tambayar da yakewa kanshi wanda amsar a bayyane take, ko yaki ko yasa dole ya janye daura Jamal a matsayin sarkin daura, tunda gashi a kwance rai kwai-kwai mutu kwai-kwai.

         Ajiyar zuciya ya sauke sannan ya shafa kan Jamal ɗin yayi cikin damuwa wanda ya gaza b'oyewa akan fuskar shi, ajiyar zuciya ya sauke sannan ya kura mishi.

        ---
"Ka tabbatar itace! Kuma kaji wasu kalmomin da ta faɗa! Kasan me take nufi da wadannan kalmomin.
"Hasssss! Kunba nuba! Jiyya hiyya, huuuuuuuuuu! A wancan lokacin kafin samu harshen Hausa, itace Yaren da Al'ummar wancan lokacin suke amfani da ita."

   Shiru yayi sannan ya kurawa wata gunki ido kafin ya zuba mata jinin da take cikin kwaryan gaban shi.

      Gyad'a kai yake kamar kadangare, tare da yatsina fuska yana.

"Lallai kuwa! Tarihi zai mai-maita kanshi, dole a kashe yarinyar da duk wanda ya tsaya mata, ko kuma su kifar da mulkinka. Idan kuwa haka bai samu ba toh zamu dauke hankalin duk wanda ya tsaya mata daga cikin masarautar. Muyi masa katanga da masarautar baki daya."

        Kallon Gamji yayi tare da shafa kan shi.
  
"Gamji! Abdul yana son saka kanshi cikin damuwa, Ni kuma gaskiya jinina ya tafasa da yarinyar, koda sau daya na kasancewa cikin koramar ta, so nake naji ina linkaya bisanta"

       "Tabbas rayuwar ka tana cikin garari domin na hango maka iyakar ka. Tabbas idan kace zaka kusanci inda inuwar yarinyar take hatta d'anka kashe ka zai iya kasheka ko kai ka kashe shi, sannan ita kanta tana karkashin kulawar wani ne!"

Murmushi Shamaki yayi sannan yace.
"Idan muguwar kasata ta fara shiga akurki, kowacce ta zo sai ta tsareta"

Daukar isgar shi yayi ya dibe jinin ya watsa a fuskar gunkin shi. Sannan yace,
"Mutum bayi cewa kan shi matsiyaci. Dan kana tare da wani abu karka yi imanin cewa kome zaka iya samu, domin kuwa."

   "Gamji nifa duk abinda nasa kaina sai nayi. Ba a hanani ba a sani! Kuma kafi kowa sanin kirarina  b'auna Saniyar sake, komai nasa gabana bani ganin girman shi, koda kuwa kisan kiyashin ne, balle na d'an mutum. Kawai ka bani damar"

   "Zan iya baka dama! Amma ita Ziriya bazata tab'a barin ka a raye ba, domin abinda kake shirin mallaka ta karfin tsiya haka wanda suka fika sun mutu ta hanyar da kake shirin bi, zaka rasa ranka.  Sannan ita Yarinyar wanda yake tsaya mata bazai kyaleka ba, ka fita hanyar ta."

                "Gamji razana Ni kake?! Kafi kowa sanin ban san tsoro ba! Kuma bani fatan sanin shi, namiji nake a gaban ka ba mace ba, ka bani damar haka."

       "Ga fili ga me doki! Idan ka samu nasara ka dawo nan"

        Da haka Gamji ya mike ya bari inda yake zaune,  ya koma bakin kofar da zai sadashi cikin kogon da yake sannan yace.
"Hasssss! Kunba nuba! Jiyya hiyya, huuuuuuuuuu! Hass karka cutar da ubangidan ka! Ziriya ta dawo kuma kana fita daga nan zaka fara haduwa da fushinta, ga laya can ka saka dan kare kanka ko kuma ka rasa rayuwarka kafin ka fita bakin hanyar domin ita zakanya idan zata fita farauta shiryawa take domin bata son haduwa da kowa a dawa."

Yana gama fad'ar haka ya b'ace gaba ki ɗaya, sai sautin muryan shi yake tashi yana dariya me cika kunne da hana kwalkwalawa sukuni, cikin kwarin gwiwa Shamaki ya mike tare da ɗaukar layar ya fita, yana fita bakin kofa ya saka hannun shi a cikin aljuhun ya ciro wata sarewa, sannan ya shiga busawa, a hankali iska ya fara kad'awa. Can guguwa ya taso sannan ya cure guri guda, can wani haske ya bayyana, ya zube a gaban Shamaki.

        "Umarninka nake jira ya shugabana!"
      
"Na gaida Shin gul! Fadar sarkinku nake son zuwa!"

2️⃣2️⃣

Durkusawa Gul shin yayi, Shamaki ya haura bayan shi, shi kuma ya baza kukkufe shi ya tashi sama, wani irin kara me firgitarwa Dajin yayi dariya ce ta kwace mishi.

            Sunyi tafiya sosai kafin suka isa wata tsibirin. Suna isa Gul Shin ya dira da kafafun shi, sannan Shamaki ya sauka a bayan shi, sanya hannun shi yayi a cikin aljuhun ya ciro zobensa ya saka, sannan ya murza. Take gurin da babu komai ya bayyana.

     Wani irin ƙasaitacciyar Pada ce ta bayyana a gaban shi, wanda aka gine shi da asalin dutsen jahuri, a hankali yake takawa har  ya isa kofar da zai sadashi da cikin masarautar.

              Buɗe mishi aka yi, ya shiga cikin isa da izza, yana fadin.
"Abokina! Sarki Alfza gani nan na dawo bayan shekaru sha bakw....."

           Bai san lokacin da ya taune harshen shi ba, tare da kwarewa da yawun har sai da ya fito ta hancin shi, wani irin zufa ke karyo mishi me sakamakon abinda idanun shi ya gani,  ji yayi kamar zawo zai zobo a jikin shi, ga wani rawan da jikin shi yake, zuciyarshi kuwa wata irin bugawa take kamar zata tsagaita jikin shi ta fito.

"Barka da zuwa! Babana! Kaje gurin Gamji ya hanaka! Amma dake kana da taurin kai. Idan nace ina son abu, ka mata yayi ka janye kudirin ka tunda Ni nace ina so! Akan damuwarka kaje can Ni kuma na zo nan akan Yarinyar da na gani, dubi yadda afroze ya zana min hoton ta! Dukda na fahimci mulkin ce baka son rabuwa dashi. Toh na yarda amma akan Wancan Yarinyar zan shayar da kowa madarar mutuwa! Sai nayiwa mutane kisar wulakancin, dan haka ka fita hanyar rawa ce ni d'aya koda bazata zo a mata na. Ba dole sai na mallaketa ko ta K'wark'warah sai na mallaki wancan kyakyawan suran"

         Aro jarumta Shamaki yayi cikin fusata sosai kamar zai fidda zuciyarshi waje yace.
"Alfza kaci amanata! Taya muna tare da kai zaka juya min baya, ka koma hulda da Yarona duk sai na dauki fansa ta akan ku."

       "Zaka kayi mana shiru ko sai na daddatsa namar ka, na zubawa kuraye su cinye ka." Inji Abdul,

     Cike da mamaki yake kallon D'an shi na cikin shi ne yake mishi wannan abu, sabida tantiranci.
"Abdul karim! Ni fa mahaifinka ne? Ni kake dakawa tsawa? Me kake nima a duniya wanda ban baka ba." A raunane yake tambayar d'an shi,

         Lakuce hancin shi Abdul yayi sannan yaja wani irin iska sannan yace.
"Abu biyu zaka min! Sai na iya kyaleka, ka sauka akan mulkin da kake, kuma ka cire ranka akan Yarinyar! Dan matuƙar na sami matsala zan kashe kowa hatta ita yarinyar zan iya kasheta idan tayi min abinda bai min ba, balle kai mahaifina."

  Dariya Shamaki ya saka sosai har da rike cikin shi, yana kallon aljanun da suke zaune a Padar, shafa kambun da yake dantsen shi yayi sannan yace.
"Matukar ina raye sai na nisantaka da ita! Kuma sai na."

Wurgo mishi wani wuka Abdul yayi, saura kiris ya cin mashi suka nemi shi suka rasa, juyawa Abdul yayi kan sarki Alfza cikin fusata yace.
"Me haka?! Me kuke nufi?! Ban fahimta guduwar da yayi a cikin wannan padar ba?!

    "Kayi hakuri Ranka shi dad'e! Munyi alkawarin bazani tab'a barin a cutar da Shamaki ba koda da kwayar zarra! Mulkin Daura taka ce! Amma faruwa shudadd'en alamari yasan ya bana tunanin zaka yi karkon kifa daga ruwa zuwa wuta! Wancan hoton da afroze ya zana naka! Anyi ta karni kusan biyu da suka gabata koma fiye da biyun! Yayinda aka Haifi Fairo daga cikin jinsin mu! Itama Ziriya an haifeta a cikin jinsin bil'adama, kuma Zulum Sarkin da ba a tab'a yin irin shi ba, a nahiyar Abdul bayan wannan karninka da suka wuce ba a kuma samu shaidanin sarki kamar Mahaifinka ba, dan shekaru talatin da daya baya yazo nan dan niman taimako, sabida hadamu da Gamji yayi, na nuna mishi kar ya karfi mulkin masarautar da karfi ko ta hanyar kafin sabida za a wayi gari wata rana haka dai iya faruwa dashi, amma fit yaki  badawa, Ai kana gani a idanunka Gamji ya hana shi bibiyar al'amarin yarinyar can! Hmmm tabbas Akwai gwagwarmayar me ƙarfi domin wanda yake tare da ita bazai tab'a barinta ta fahimci soyayyar kowa ba, al'amarin da ya faru can baya zai kuma faruwa yanzun. Shawara guda zan baka! Karka duba mulkin dake hannu mahaifinka! Tayi nisan kiwo kamar yadda suka ce da Gamji zasu kauda tunanin kowa akan mulkin, bazaku tab'a haɗuwa da ita a nahiyar ku ba, sannan koda kun haɗu bazaka sameta yadda zata baka aron lokacin ta ba, kaje akwai zab'abben lokacin da da kanka zaka yarda ka sameta, a lokacin kuwa duk wani abinda kasan zai tasirantu akanta shi zaka bijira mata, sai dai ka sani so d'aya ne tak! Kuma tarigada ta bawa wanda take so zuciyar ta sai shi! Kaina dalilin dalili ne zai kunsantar dakai gareta!"

    Wani kallon banza Abdul yayiwa rauhanin, cikin masifa yace.
"Zan tafi da afroze idan bukata na tabi ya zan dawo muku da shi idan ba tabi ya ba zan kashe shi."

         Haka ya cakumo kwalar rigar Aljanin suka b'ace.
    Kallon Sarkin su sauran suka yi, sannan suka ce.
"Shugaba! Akan idanunka fa?!"

"Wannan da kuke ganin shi yafi mu Hatsabibancin! Zulum yana tare dashi dan haka  babu wanda ya isa tsayawa a gaban shi, matukar yana tare da shi dole mu shafawa kan mu ruwan sanyi, a tarihinsa shine mutum na farko da ya kashe jinsu mu kuma ya hana jinsu mu sakat, lallai tarihi zai mai-maita kanshi ne tunda dukkan su uku suna raye! Ubangiji ne kadai zai iya takaita alamarin ba tare da an zubda jini ba, amma."
 
   Haka yayi shiru bai kuma magana ba..
     ---
Shamaki bai bayyana a ko ina ba sai a kogon Gamji, zuɓewa yayi a gaban Gamji ya takarkare ya fasa ihun da  yasa kogon amsashi baki daya, ya kuma sake wani irin kuka.

"Gamji!!! D'an cikina ne yake barazanar kashe ni?!"

   Ya faɗa da mugun karfi! Bayyana a gaban shi Gamji yayi cikin murmushin mugunta yace.
"Ka gode ma sa baka yi ido biyu da Ziriya ba! Tabbas ita nata me sauki ne! Babban tashin hankalin d'anka! Domin kana kuskure kad'an sai gawar ka! Kuma na gaya maka ka fita harkan Yarinyar! Shima d'anka ka bashi mulkin ka huta in ba haka ba zai kashe ka a banza!"


           "Gamji! Ka  kashe min sh."

Ya faɗa da karfi, kamar zai fasa kogon! Kallon shi Gamji yayi sannan yace.
"Ko Ziriya! Bana shakkar dawowar ta, amma zoka ga wani abu!"

     Dawowa yayi gaban Madubin tsafin Gamji, ya shiga shafa mishi wasu abubuwan, kafin wani lokaci sai ga Abdul! Duk inda yayi wani baƙin hayaki na bin bayan shi,

    "Ka gani! Toh bari na nuna maka abinda kake yunkurin aikatawa!"

        Zane yayi jikin madubin sai ga wani  haske nan ya bayyana wanda ya kusan makantar musu da ganin su! Cikin tashin hankali Gamji ya ajiye abun ya hannun shi ya shiga watsawa madubin wani irin ruwa, lokaci ƙalilan sai ga madubin  ya dawo dai dai! Sannan ya juya a raunane ya kalli  Shamaki.
"Ban tab'a kawowa duba waye d'anka zai zama da ajalin madubina ba, sai dai abinda na fahimta idan kana son ganin karshen muradib shi dole ka aura mishi mata a cikin manyan masarautun mu da suke kewaye damu, kuma ka cika mishi mata Hudu yadda bazai tab'a tunanin wancan Yarinyar, amma matukar tazo zuciyarshi zai nimeta, kuma komai zai iya faruwa ne da zaran sun hadu! Shawara ko baka nima mishi huɗu ba, toh a nima mishi wacce zata dauke mishi hankali! Wacce take da abubuwan hutawa! Wanda duk inda aka shiga mace ce! Ka nima mishi wacce zata mantar dashi wata mace, zan baka wannan kawon ka binne a cikin gidan ka ɗan yanzun haka yana gidan kana komawa abinda zaka fara kenan! Kana binnewa kaman ka binne tunanin wancan ce yarinyar! Karka fusata shi Kabi shi yadda zaka kora shi daga kasar baki daya, domin yin haka shi ne kwanciyar hankalinka kai ma ka cire ranka a kan Yarinyar bibiyar  yarinyar shine taddo rufaffen rikicin da ya wucce, sannan kahon da na baka shi zaka busata kafin ka saka a ramin duk wani wanda yake son ganin karshen ka zai manta burin shi da kudirin shi, sannan a cikin sharuddan da zan gindaya maka karka kuskura ka takali kowa da maganar Jamal dan kujeran shi ne kuke danbarwan akan shi! Karka kuskura ka gayawa Sarki Jalaludeen da maganar! Sannan nan da wasu lokuta JAMAL ZAI WARKE! Wannan shi ne abinda zan gaya maka! Domin Alfza ya bawa d'anka damar duk ranar da  suka haɗu toh Baka da tsimi baka da dabara! Domin kuwa komai zai iya faruwa daga wannan lokacin babu wanda ya isa dakatar da shi."


       A dame ya kalli Gamji kamar zai yi kuka yace.
"Me zai hana a kashe shi kawai?"

      Wani irin kallon Gamji yayi mishi irin baka da hankalin nan, cikin b'acin rai ya juya kan Shamaki yace.
"Gamji Duk bai kai haƙa ba"
 Cikin fusata yace.
"Uwar me zan maka! Akan idanunka!  Madubina ya kusan fashewa, amma dake baka da yakana a ranka kana gaya min cewa babu yadda zan yi ne! Idan akwai abinda zanyi nayi toh baki yi! Idan ka kuskura ka bani haushin koran ka zanyi kaje da taurin kan ka! Ko arnan farko basu kaika taurin kai ba, maza dauki kahon kabar min kogo kafin na fasa taimaka maka!"

           
        Kaskantar da kai Shamaki yayi sosai sannan yace.
"Tuba nake! Ubangidana, bazan Kuma d'aga maka murya ba Ni din banza Ni din wofi! Kar naga goben da bazai nuna yau ba! Tuba nake baya goya marayu! Sarkin bokayen duniya baki! Gamji kake sara da sassaka baya hana ka towo!      Dakatar da shi  Gamji yayi cikin tsare gida.
"Zaka  iya tafiya"
     Haka Shamaki ya mike zai bar kogon sai yaji ya bashi tausayi ce mishi yayi.
"Shamaki! Babu abinda zai kuma faruwa, kawai kayi abinda nace maka. Domin abokin ciniki mushe shi ake nunawa wuka"

   "Godiya nake!"
   Sannan ya fita da sauri, ajiyar zuciya Gamji ya sauke sannan ya cigaba da aikin da yake, yana kallon madubin tsafin shi.

          .....
Barin Shamaki kogon dajin, bai tsaya ko ina ba, sai gida yana shiga ya fara niman shine diga,
  Ya tone rami kamar yadda Gamji ya gaya mishi, sannan ya fidda kahon ya busata, kafin ya wurgata cikin ramin, ya rufe da kasa kamar babu Abinda yayi a gurin, ajiyar zuciya ya sauke me cike da farin ciki, domin in ba haka ba tabbas Abdul kashe shi zai yi kai Jama'a ka haifi d'an a cikin ka ya nemi kashe ka ɗan wata bukatar shi ta rayuwa wannan wacce irin rayuwa ce, 
Dole ya cika mishi gida da mata kyawawan, yadda bazai tab'a jin sha'awar wacce yarinyar ba, murmushin mugunta yayi dan yasan yanzun duk wani abinda zai iya kawo mishi matsala a mulkin shi yanzun babu ita, sannan dole ya koyawa kan shi son Sarki Jalaludeen da D'an shi.

Ajiye numfashi yayi dan aiki ne gundumemme a gaban shi, abinda bazai iya ba kenan toh amma ya zai yi indai yana son tsira da mulkin shi dole ya maida makiyan shi masoyan shi, shifa a halin yanzu bayi da wasu makiya sama da Abdul dan yasan banda ya b'ace mishi da yanzun gawar shi za a kawo, a sallace gobe gawar shi.

     ---
Washi gari...
Tun asuba na tashi muka yi sallah, zubawa Mama ido nayi.
Murmushi tayi sannan tace.
"Yar kawu! Irin wannan kallon tashi muje ki gan shi, dan wannan idanun naki ba zai barni nayi addu'a ba"
      
Kunya ce ta kamani, wato mama ta fahimci halin da nake ciki, Allah sarki ni.

            Jagora tayi min har dakin da yake kwance, Mai Martaba yana zaune akan keken shi, durkusawa muka yi tare da gaishe shi, gyada mana kai yayi, sannan ya mai da idanun kaina, sai naji kunya ta tukukwaikuye ni.

        Sannan ya janye idanun shi ya mai da kan kawu, yana kallon yadda yake fidda numfashi a wahalce.

        "Abba! Ko zamu koma dashi Lagos ne! Dan naga kaman akwai wasu abubuwan da suke faruwa a cikin masarautar" inji Mama,

             Zuba mata ido yayi sannan ya sunkuyar da kanshi, ga dai alamun baya son rabuwa da Kawun?! Tunda Abba baya son Kawu yayi nesa dashi."

         Zuba min ido suka yi, sai naji kamar nayi musu katsalanda akan sha'anin su. Ni wacece da zan shiga al'amarin su, sai naji na muzanta.

      "Jannart! Ai jinyar namiji sai namiji, ina ke mace zaki iya jinyar Jamal, sai dai ko ina zamu tafi dashi." Inji Mama,

      Girgiza kai yayi alamun baya son maganar tafiya da mama.
             Turo keken shi yayi zuwa gaba na, sannan ya kalli inda Jamal yake kwance, sannan ya kuma kallona.
  
Kallon Mama yayi sannan ya girgiza mata kai, murmushi ne ya kwace min nace.
"Mama! Kinga Abba baya son Kawu yayi nisa dashi."

          "Jannart bamu guri!" Yadda tayi maganar sai naji babu dad'i. Da sauri na ficce daga d'akin,

        Ina fita ta durkusa a gaban shi, ta dafa keken shi tayi cikin damuwa sannan tace.
"Abba! Zaman Jannart a jikin shi akwai matsala! Inda hali."

     Girgiza kai yayi cikin murmushi a kawai bai ce kome ba sabida shi dai yana na'am da shawarar da na bada.

            Duk yadda taso ya fahimci halin da take ciki, amma ya kasa fahimta. Dan haka ta hakura tunda ba magana yake ba. Ya fahimce ta amma shi yana son zaman Jannart, sabida yafi yarda da ita akan wasu mutanen.

              ..... Har aka kawo abin karyawa, Mama ta kawo min amma yanayin fuskar ta na kalla na fahimci ranta a b'ace, ajiye min tayi sannan tace min.
"Kinyi kuskure babba! Jannart martaban ki zan kare, amma kika nuna ke zab'in ranki kike so. Zan barki a nan idan ta kwaɓe miki kiyiwa Aswad magana ya dawo dake. Ba baki nake miki ba! Iya abinda zai iya dawowa nake gaya miki. Gidan sarautar mu cike yake da abubuwan damuwa wasu na sani wasu kuma jiya kika farga dani, ban san yadda zaki dauka ba amma zaman da zaki yi a jikin Jamal babu wani Alkhairin sai damuwa, da tashin hankali da bakin ciki."

              San ban ji ko d'ar da abinda take fada min ba, asalima bana jin wani abu zai iya faruwa kawai dai naji a raina ina son zaman jinyar shi.

     Haka ta karaci fadar ta ban damu ba, burina kawai na a barni a jikin shi.
     
                        Ko karyawa bata yi ba, ta ficce Ni kad'ai na ci abincin. Sai naji na damu da kaina, tana shigowa na kalli ta kamar zan yi kuka nace..
"Mamahna kiyi hakuri"

   Murmushi tayi sannan tace.
"Ya wuce! Allah ya miki albarka!"
      "Amin, Mamahna"
    
Duk da  tana cikin damuwa amma ta b'oye min damuwar ta, nima haka ina cikin domin cikar muradina, da kuma faranta ran Mahaifin su.

Allah ya gani bata son wannan zaman da zan amma babu yadda ta iya, tana tsoro sosai amma idan ta tuna mahaifinsu zai kula da komai.


     Kusan bata da sukuni amma dan kar na fahimta, sai ta bar abin a ranta.

            Koda likita ya shigo bai wani rike Ni ba, ya sallame ni. Gida Aswad ya dawo dani. Amma naki shiga cikin gidan. Shashin bayi naje, koda nake sai ga Bayi da yawa suna min wani irin kallo. 
"Wani irin kallon ne haka?! Ku gaisheta dan kusan Sarauniyarku ce babu kallon reni a tsakanin ku" Inji matar da ta kawo Ni d'akin umma jiya.

        Sunkuyar da kai duk suka yi tare da wani irin kaskantar da kai, duk suka zube gwiwar su a kasa. Tsayawa nayi ina kallon su, a cikin abinda bai fi dakika  sittin ba, kawai na hango Matar da nake mafarki da Ita, dafe goshina nayi tare da yin baya kamar zan fad'i sai ji nayi an rike ni.
"Sannun! Jannart."
    Kallon matar Umma tayi cikin b'acin rai tace.
"Wai nikam Jakadiya meye matsalarki da Jannart?! kiyi bi hankali in ba haka ba sai na gayawa Jamal"

   D'akin ta shiga dani, jini ne ya zubo daga hancina, nayi maza na rike hancin Cikin damuwa.
"Umma! Bana son zubar jinin nan! Gashi wanda yake bani bai da lafiya"

   Matsawa jikinta nayi na kwantar da kaina, ina jin sauƙin damuwa ta, kallon ta nayi bayan kaina na jikinta nace.
"Umma! Kina kama da kawuna sosai! Haka ma Mama kuma kama sosai, kamar ku da Kawu yayi yawa."

     Shafa kaina tayi cikin mamaki sannan tace.
"Ki daina yawan magana! Baki ga kawunki da Mamanki basu son magana ba! Toh ki koyi shiru! Ba kowani zafi bane take tab'a zuciya ba, akan iya koyan shiru dan samun zaman lafiya da dorewar kwanciyar hankali, ki yawaita addua yana kariya sosai daga duk wani abin cutarwa."

   Gyad'a kai nayi idan na fahimci Umma na daina maganar kamanta dasu kawuna.
Kwanciya nayi bata ture kaina ba, sai dai har zuwa lokacin tana nazari sosai toh taya haka ya faru.
        Ita a saninta ta haihu sau biyu kuma duk yaran basu rayu ba, amma wani abin rud'arwa kamar su Da JAMAL! Ba karya suna kama da Jamal dan bata taɓa Mantawa shekarun baya da tafito gurin Jakadiya suka haɗu da shi, sai abinda Shamaki ya gaya mata jiya.

     Ajiyar zuciya ta sauke, ta ina zata fara?! Waye zai tsaya mata domin ganin ta amshi hakkinta.
*_Babu wanda zai baki goyan baya?! Dan Jalaludeen ya manta dake! Da kuma duniyar ki_*
 Wannan shi ne abinda zuciyarta ya tuna mata, numfashi ta sauke, sannan tace..
"Jannart! Ya jikin Kawun naki?"
    Tashi nayi na kalle ta, sannan na koma na kwanta, cikin damuwa nace..
"Da sauki! Amma Sunce ba lallai bane ya kuma gani, shine nace zan zauna dashi kafin ya warke tunda sakamakon mu bai fito ba."

           "Hmm" ta iya cewa.
   
Abinda na fahimta da ita shine tana cikin damuwa sosai.
"Umma! Insha Allah zai warke kiyi hakuri da d'aga miki hankali da nayi, da magana ta bazan kuma ba."
 Shafa kaina tayi sannan ta sake murmushi tace.
"Ba damuwa! Kuma baki d'aga min hankali ba, kawai ina tausayawa Kawunki ne, da kuma zaman jinyar da kika ce zaki yi kina ganin babu matsala?!"

   Sosa kaina nayi cikin jin kunya da kuma gudun karta hanani nace.
"Umma babu komai Wallahi! Kawai zan na duba shi ina kulawa dashi sai ina dawowa nan na kwana."

         Murmushi tayi wanda ya kuma kara fito da ainihin kamar su da Kawu da Mama..
"Koda nan zaki zauna ba zai hana ni fada miki gaskiya ba! Kinsan da zaran mace da namiji suka kebe na Ukun su sha'dan ne! Dan haka karki manta ke mace ce mai daraja da kima! Karki sake ki zubda kimar ki dan har abada bazai tab'a kwasowa ba."

Gyad'a mata kai nayi sannan tace..
"Karki manta wacece ke? Daga ina kika fito?! Su waye a halinki?! Suwaye zasu yi kuka idan abin kuka ya dame ki?! Dan haka ki kula da kanki kawai kiyi abinda ya dace bawai ki biyewa son ranki ba. Dan a wani shekarun naki kamata yayi ace kina makaranta amma zaki karshe rayuwarki a nan jikin majinya ci, tashi kije karki manta kiyita yawan addu'o'i saboda shima makamin mun'munin ne, Allah yana tare da masu niman kariyar shi. Ban da tsayawa jin shirmen mutane."

         Mik'ewa nayi sannan nace.
"Toh Umma zan koma."

    "Allah yayi miki albarka! Ki kula da kanki banda wasa da ibada itama wata kariya ce babba duk me ibada Allah yana kare shi daga wasu sharrin ababen halitta! Babu wanda ya isa cutar dashi facce Allah bai kare shi ba."

 Numfashi ta sauke sannan ta cigaba da cewa

 "Idan kuma kika ga an sami nasarar a kan shi toh haka Allah ya nufa yayi haka ne dan cika imanin shi ne dan kar yaga ya same komai , kuma yana yin komai cikin jin dadi. Kinsan Bahaushe yace idan Allah yaki addu'ar biri sai ya mutu a gonar bamaguje"
