2️⃣4️⃣

"Nagode Umma! Insha Allah, zan tsaya nayi ibada da kuma k'iyayye abinda Shari'a tace."

"Allah yayi miki albarka, ki kula da mutanen da suke kewaye dake, wasu ba dan Allah suke tare da kai ba dan cimma wata manufar suke kewaye da kai, sannan karki yarda da labarin kanzon kurege, dan yin haka zai ja miji shirka. Karki tab'a yarda da canfe. Domin yarda da shi shima kuskure ne."

Shiru tayi tana kallo na, sannan tace.
"Abu na gaba, kiyi hakuri! Dan zaman da zaki yi dashi dole sai kinyi ƙoƙarin kauda kanki sabida duk wanda zaka ya zaman jinyar shi toh sai ka kai zuciyarka nesa"

Duk sai naji takara min kwarin gwiwa, ta kuma samun wani irin girma da daraja. Ina jinta har cikin zuciyata, ina kuma kaunarta da zuciyata baki d'aya. Abubuwa masu wuyar Fad'uwa.

Tasowa tayi kusadani sannan tace.
"Ba zan ce miki komai akan Jamal da Raihanah ba, amma nayi imani da Allah ina da nasaba dasu! Don Allah ki rike min amanar shi, Ni kuma zan yi kokarin na sanya ku cikin addu'o'i na, ki kula min dashi don Allah karki bari a karasa cutar da shi."

Hawayen dake zuba a idanun uwar da bata san da ƴaƴanta ba na matukar tab'a min zuciyata, kallon ta nayi a karo na ba adadi nace.
"Umma! Kema Kin san yaranki ne?! Toh kiyi magana mana"

Rike hannuna tayi cikin damuwa tana goge kwalla da bayan hannunta tace.
"Anya Yarana ne?! Kai bani tunanin haka kawai ina jin damuwa ce akan su"

Kura mata ido nayi yadda lokaci guda ta nuna ba yaranta bane, kallon bakin kofar dakin ta nayi naga ashe inuwar wani ta gani shi yasa taki magana. Wani abu ne ya wuce min cikin ƙwayar idanuna da karfi nace.
"Sahurah ado warka!"

Da sauri ta shigo dakin, tare da zuɓewa akan gwiwarta, tana me kaskantar da kanta.
"Sa ya me! Ke ki ke le?! Na so ki ki ran ci ɓa"
"Me yasa kike kike leke! Kina Son ranki ya ɓaci ne?"

Jikinta ne ya dauki rawa, har tana fidda zufa, cikin tashin hankali tace.
"Ba na tu! Ce gar fa."

Daka mata tsawa nayi sannan nace.
"Zakaran da Allah ya nufa da cara, ana muzuru ana shaho sai ya yi. Rumanah ba sa'arki bace, Sahurah kun Zalince ta karki bari raina ya b'aci! Idan ya b'aci zan yi komai!"

Kuka take sosai sannan tace.
"Ba zan kuma wa kai na sagegeduwa. Ba tuba nake ziriya"

Juyawa nayi zan wa Umma magana ta watsa min ruwan addu'o'in dake bakinta wanda tasha kamar tana jin ƙishirwa, zuɓewa nayi a gurin, sume.

Kallon Jakadiya tayi ranta na kuma b'aci a hasale tace.
"Sahurah!!! Sau nawa na kira ki?!"

Cikin masifa itama Jakadiya ta mike tace.
"Yau naga dalili, mai sa a leƙa gindin surika."

Kamar Umma zata rufeta da duka tace.
"Wallahi ki raba Ni da Goɗiyar sanƙira, a hauki a riga ki tashi. Na gaya miki ki fita harkokin Yarinyar nan ko?!"

"Hhhh! Lallai kuwa, biri, tumakan banza. Allah bazan bari ba" inji Jakadiya,

"Tabbas na yarda da abinda zuciyata take gaya min! Ba wahalalle, sai mai kwaɗayi kuma naga zahirin gaskiya magana. Kin kusa." inji Umma,

Gyara tsayuwar tayi sannan tace.
"Idan wani ya taka rawa aka bashi kuɗi, in wani ya taka duka zai sha, kuma har yanzun ina kan takawa kuma kudin nake diba!"

Murmushi Umma tayi sannan tace.
"Tabbas bahaushe yayi gaskiya da yace. Ka kiyayyi me nima"

"Bar ganin kin tara! Wannan karon zan d'aga miki kafa ne sabida wannan Yarinyar. Kallo hadarin nesa, ya sa an yi wanka da kashi. Da alamu baki jan lissafin ki." Inji Jakadiya,

Shiru Umma tayi sannan ta nuna mata hanyar fita da dan yatsar ta.
"Oho dai!Mai tarkon tsuntsaye, baya ɗaga kara." Tacewa Umma, har ta isa bakin kofa Umma tace mata.
"Ranar biyan buƙata, rai ba a bakin komai yake ba. Ki ajiye wannan maganar wata rana zai miki amfani."

Dariya tayi sannan tace.
"Oho dai!Mai tsoron ta mutu, shi yake maho"

Banza Umma tayi da ita sabida ta fahimci halin Jakadiya irin mutanen nan ne da addini bai wadace su ba, har takai bakin kofa sai ta waiwaya sannan tace.

"Shege, ka fasa."
Murmushi Umma tayi sannan tace.
"Ta fi nono, fari."

"Dole ne, cin kasuwa da maƙiyi! Kuma muna nan dake, ki ajiye a ranki!"

Tana gama fad'ar haka tasa kai ta ficce a dakin, Umma ta dawo kaina. Ruwan addu'o'in ta shafa min.

Ajiyar zuciya na sauke sannan na tashi zaune, Jini ne ya d'igo daga hancina.
Addu'a ta bani wanda zai zame min kariya dare da rana, sannan. Nayi mata sallama na bar d'akin, ina shiga zauren da zai fidda daga Shashin, na fara jin sautittukar muryoyin mutane.

"Karka kuskura ka tab'a min Y'ata! Dan zan maka abinda zai dame zuri'arku kafatan!"

"Zokul maamana kai so kuruu! Ban ga duwa"
Wannan matsalar kace Boka, Ni bani da wata damuwa sai na Yarka (duwa)

"A'ah! A'ah zulum! Gurga duwa kurfawa, dunduguru"

A'ah a'ah zulum! Karka kusanci abinda ya shafi Y'ata domin akwai baikon aljanun kanta.

"Zurgugun su kurfawa dunduguru! Kursa kuyau ziriya fafu!"

Wannan matsalar ku ce, da baikon da kuka mata da aljani! Ni dai abinda na sani kubani ita, dare d'aya zan b'ata da ita.

Toshe kunne na nayi na fita da mugun gudu, sabida kaina ya fara sarawa, ganina yana shirin daukewa.

Garin gudun ban san inda nake jefa kafana ba, har muka yi karo da Aswad da Aliyah da suke nima na.
Kallona yayi yaga yadda jini ke d'iga daga hancina.
"Innalillahi wa'inna ilaihi rajioun! Ina kika je Kuma?! Me ya faru dake?!"

"Gurga annada miro be kana, furaga mine, fuka mike Aswad"

"Ɗauke ta ku bar nan! In ba haka ba, jinin ta zasu dinga zukewa Aswad"
(Wannan kalmomin babu ita! Nayi amfani da ita ce dan karawa labarin armashi!)

       Kifawa nayi a kirjin shi, jini na fita ta hancina da kunne na da sauri ya fita dani, zuwa cikin gidan.

     Cikin tashin hankali Mama tazo kaina, tana shafa min ruwa.
"Ni kam Aswad zan tafi da ita kawai jikina yaki barina akwai Alkhairin! Kuma ina jin tsoron kar a cutar da ita, shekaru kusan hudu kenan ko biyar."

Numfashi taja sannan ta kalli shi,ganin yaki magana tace.
"Bani son shiru!"

"Toh Raihana me zance miki?! Kawai ki barta koda wata uku tayi ai sai na kawo miki ita."

      
Shiru tayi cikin damuwa sannan tace.
"Baka ga yadda take zubda jini bane?! Kuma nayi imanin akwai wani B'oyayyen al'amari a tare da ita wanda ya kasance a tare da ita! Sannan ita kanta har yau bamu san wacece ita, kaga kenan bazamu iya barinta ta cigaba da da wahala a nan ba"

       "Hmm! Masha ruwa magirbi, zakarar da Allah ya nufa da cara ko ana muzuru ana shawo sai yayi. Shin Raihanah kin manta me rabon ganin bad'i."

     Kallon yadda nayi wani fayau ne hankalin Mama ya kuma tashi. Ta kalli Aswad tace,
"Yanzun ma jini ake bukata?!"

          "Eh dole a nima mata jinin tunda haka ya zame mata wani yanki na rayuwarta"

   Hawaye ne ya sauko mata, kamar yadda take ji da Jamal haka take jin Jannart dan tana jin kamar itace ta haifeta.

    Mik'ewa tayi tace.
"Bari na duba Jakadiya yanzun zan zo"
Da sauri ta fita ta nufi shashin bayi, har ta kusan isa sai ta hango wata mata tana sauri. Tsayawa tayi tana kallon ikon Allah sabida hatta tafiyarsu iri d'aya ce, su uku da matar da Mama da kuma Jamal,.kusan duk cikin Ƴaƴan Fulani su biyu ne suka kasance farare, sannana kuma kyawawan, Uwa Uba suna da tsayi sosai.

  Kafin ta dawo daga duniyar tunani, har ta b'ace mata. Ajiyar zuciya ta sauke. Karshe zuwan da bata yi ba kenan, ta koma shashin Fulani. Tana don ta fadi taga matar, amma sai ta manta sakamakon ganin yadda jikin Jannart yake kuma rikicewa.

        Dan dole suka nufi cikin gari da ita gidan Baban Aswad, Magaji.
     
      Yana zaune da dalibai, ana daukar karatun rana.
        Ganin su ya rabu da daliban shi, ya shigo cikin gidan.

     Kallon ta yayi sannan yace.
"Ku bani ruwan a kwarya!"
Goggo Mahaifiyar Aswad ce ta koma daki ta dauko mishi kwaryan da ruwa a ciki.

      Addu'a sosai yayi ya jima yana addu'o'i, kafin ya mik'awa Raihanah yace.
"Bata!"
Karb'a tayi ta bata sai da tasha rabi sannan ta yace su shafa mata.

     Suna shafa mata kamar wanda akayi ruwa aka dauke, take sai ga fatarta yana dawowa dai-dai, kafin awa guda ta farka a yunkurin amai.
       Tashi nayi na Zauna, tare da rike cikina. Amai nake son yi tare da rike cikin. kafin kace me na fara wani irin amai, sosai fa wanda yake cike da wasu irin tarkace.

         Har da wani fatar kura, abinda ya d'aga hankalin su kenan, baba Magaji ya sunkuya ya dauki fatar kuran warwarewa yayi. Da wasu irin rikitattun kalmomi masu mugun hawa kai yayin karantawa,

*Gar kum fara kir furaga*
       Mun dawo kuma sai mun dauki fansar mu!

        Juya kalmar yake cike da damuwa, yana nazarin inda ya tab'a ganin wannan Yaren. Idanun shi ne ya sauka kan Aswad.
    "Na tab'a baka wani littafi ka kaiwa Jamal ka bashi?!"

   "Eh na kai mishi!"

     Shiru yayi sannan ya sunkuyar da kan shi, kafin ya d'ago kai yace.
"Tashi kage gidan ma'ajiyi kace ya duba maka a cikin kundin masarautar."

       Fauce takardan aka yi a hannun shi, cikin mamaki tayi sama. Cikin iska,

        Haka suka bi takardan da ido, cike da bakin cikin. Toh kida sun sami kundin babu amfanin dubawa tunda Yaren yake son sani, kuma iska ya tafi da takardan, dan sun barshi a iska ce kawai amma a zahirance ba iskar b'ace wani dalili ne na daban wanda yake bibiyar ta Jannart din, 

   
        Dan haka suka bar zance kawai ya shiga rubuta mata addu'o'i,  domin shine kawai mafita t
a karshe.

2️⃣5️⃣

.......
"Takawa! Ina ga ba wani abin d'aga hankali bane? Idan kayi dubi da Jamal din yana kwance koda ya tashi ba wani abu ya rasa ba sai idanu, dan haka ka bar d'aga hankalin ka, zai ji sauki"

    Cike da mamaki yake kallon Fulani Furerah, wacce take bayani kamar wata na Allah.
Murmushi yayi me ciwo dan yasan iya cutarwa Shamaki ya cuce shi sai dai ba komai Allah yana tare da shi, kamar yadda ya sashi cikin damuwa haka shima Allah zai jarabce shi da damuwar da tafi tashi.

       Shigowar Fulani Hasiyah ya sashi sauke ajiyar zuciya, bayanta Jakadiya ce rike da kwandon abinci, sai Yaya Rukayyah tare da Raihanah, da Yaya Shatu, wacce labarin faruwan abu ya isa har Zaria, sai kuma Khadijah.

    Zama suka yi akan babban dardumar da aka shimfida a d'akin, gaishe da Mai Martaba suka yi tare da tambayar shi ya jikin kanin su, murmushi kawai yake musu, tare da nuna mishi Jamal ɗin.

       Cike da tausayin Jamal din suke tattaunawa kasa kasa, yana jin su da yadda kowa ke kawo shawara, amma duk cikin su shawaran Raihanah ce yayi na'am da shi inda tace inda hali a fitar dashi koda kano ne balle ma sa a iya fitar dashi ƙasar waje.
Caaa suka mata kamar zasu cinyeta d'anye, yadda suka mata yasata fashewa da kuka, dan sun hayayyako mata, har Yaya Rukayyah tana cewa.
"Dan kin auri Me kuɗi sai aka ce miki kowa ma haka ne! Idan aka tattara kudin sabida jinyar shi dan son kai."

      Cikin kuka tace.
"Yaya Rukayyah Ni kam ban san laifin da muka miki ba. Ni da Jamal kina tadda fadar ki zaki ce ina son kai ko ina nuna nafi kowa son shi, dukkan mu ciki d'aya muka fito amma kulli yaumin sai kin nuna mana tsana, toh ko me ya kawo maganar miji da arziki oho, naga Kema mijinki me kuɗi har yafi Hamis. Amma kullum idanun ki yana kan shi.

      Wallahi Fulani keda Abba ku ja mata kunne dan gaba idan ta gaya min magana mara dad'i zan mata rashin kunya, tunda na fahimci rashin kunyar take buƙatar a mata."

          Kurawa Yaya Rukayyah idanu yayi, cikin alamun  b'acin rai ya nunawa Yaya Rukayyah hanyar fita da idanun shi.

       "Toh Abba ba halin ayi ma Raihanah da Jamal Fad'a sai a nuna maka wariyar launin fata, sabida su fararre ne masu zubin Buzayen." Inji Yaya Rukayyah,

       Mai da idanun shi yayi kan Raihanah, yana kallon har cikin ƙwayar idanunta, da fuskar ta daga nan har yatsun kafarta.

           Juyawa yayi kan Jamal, yana nazarin maganar ta.
*_Masu zubin buzayen! Rumanah!!?*
Abinda zuciyarshi ya ambata kenan, sunan Rumanah.

     Zubawa Jakadiya idanu yayi, ita kuma ta takura gefe guda. Tana satar Fulani wacce itama take dafe da goshinta, sabida bakin cikin katab'aran da Rukayyah tayi musu, sai da ta haneta da saka Raihanah da Jamal a gaba, amma dake bata jin magana sai da yi  abinda zai b'ata mata rai yanzun wata amsa zata bawa.
Mai martaba kauda kai yayi amma yasan ruwa bata tsami banza, da walakin goro a miya.

       Haka d'akin yayi shiru, har ba wani lokaci. Suna cikin wannan halin sai ga Shamaki da dogarai.
     
Yana shigowa ya zubawa fuskar Jamal ido, kauda kai yayi a ranshi yace.
*_D'an banza me bakin naci yo dan kwal uban shi yaki mutuwa! Gashi sanadin ka na ga samu naga rashi_*

   "Sannun ku da zuwa Baba Shamaki!" Inji Rukayyah,
           

     Kauda kai Raihanah tayi dan bata kaunar ganin shi, haka kawai take jin haushin sa. Tunda Jannart ta gaya mata sunan shi a cikin masu son ganin bayan kaninta take ji mugun tsanar shi.

     "Sannu! Jalaludeen ya jikin Yarima Jamal!"
     Gyad'a mishi kai yayi kawai, nan sai suka gaishe shi. Banda Raihanah wacce take cin cin magani da cira, kamar wacce aka zageta.

        "Raihanah!!"
Ya kira sunanta, kallon shi tayi kamar ba zata amsa ba tace.
"Mmn"
 "Ko ubanki gashi a zaune bai isa ya ganni ya kauda kai ba! Balle kuma keda wancan abin, wanda idan da za'a bi salasalar ku babu marabanku da. Wanda ya ci ka, ya mallake ka duka da kai da iyalinka."

   Wani irin kallon tayi mishi sannan tace.
"Ko ina mai aro za shi, dole da sanin mai riga, kuma fankama da ake da rigar aro wata rana za a cire ko ka cire da kanka ko kuma a cire maka."

       Kura mata ido Abban su yayi yana jin wani irin karsashi na hauro mishi, a da yana cire tsammani zai samu wanda zai kare masa kimar shi ba. Sai gashi mace ta kare shi.
 Tura kekenshi yayi gaban ta, ya girgiza mata kai sannan ya koma ga Shamaki, yayi ta bashi hakuri Da kai da kuma hannun shi.

         "Hmm. A juri kai zuwa rafi, wata rana gora zai fashe. Maza ma basu iya dani ba balle ke, ce miki aka yi hamisu yana zaune dake dan kin isa sabida mu yake zaune dake " Inji Shamaki,

   Murmushi tayi sannan tace.
"Aikuwa dai! duk wanda ya daka rawar wani, zai rasa turmin daka tasa. Dan haka kai Uba ne karka bari. Kimar ka ta ragu a idanun mu y'aya."

           Sai da Fulani Furerah tayi magana, sannan aka samu sauƙin musayar kalamai masu zafi.

     Da yazo fita ya tsaya kamar abin arziki, yace.
"Ƙadangare...."

 Cikin sauri ta amshi maganar da cewa.
"Shiga ruwa kazama kado! Baba kenan, ai an wuce lokacin da za'ayi wasannin motsa rai, ka jira fitowar Barden guza"

   Murmushi yayi cikin izza yace.
"Na zo kenan, zuwa sojan badaskare!"

     Dariya tayi sannan tace.
"Bakin da Allah ya tsaga, ba zai hana shi abinci ba. Sannan zakarar da Allah ya nufe shi da cara ko ana muzuru ana shawo sai yayi, muna nan da kai Baba Allah zai d'aga kafad'arshi, Saraki na zuwa. Kuma wancan gasar sai an mai maita shi domin akwai lulalliya a kasa"

        Ran shi ya b'aci sosai, ji yaƙe kamar ya rufeta da duka, ko yasa a badda ita, cikin fusata yayo kan Abban su sai yaji yo muryan Gamji.
"Kul karka lalata aikinka! Zaka iya rasa komai indai Kabi ta zuciyarka. Kyale su domin kalubale na gabanka! Rabu da ita domin kuwa Abdul na gab da fita hayacin sa, ka koma gida "

  Da sauri ya ficce, ba tare da ya waiwaya ba, dan a halin yanzu idan aikin Abdul ya lalace ai ya kad'e har buzun shi.

           Bayan fitar shi suka kuma mata cak ita ko bata damu ba, kawai dai sunyi surutun su ne, suka yi sallama dukkan su suka fita.
    Bayan fitar sune ta share kwalla ta mike zata fita tace.
"Abba bari na je na dawo!"
      
Murmushi yayi sannan yace.
"Kiyi hakuri!!"
     Zaro ido tayi ta kalle Jamal taga yana barci, sannan ta juya bakin kofa taga kofar a rufe yake.
"Kina Mamaki ko?! Sarki Jalaludeen ke magana ba wani ba. Amma ya zama sirrin mu."

    Zuɓewa tayi akan gwiwarta, ta kalle shi tace.
"Abba! Kaine?!"
  Gyad'a mata kai yayi sannan yace.
"Daga Ni sai Jamal, muka san haka."

     Kuka ta fashe da shi, tana cewa.
"Alhamdulillah! Allah ya kara muku lafiya"

     "Amin Ya Allah! Kema Allah yayi miki albarka"
   
   Da haka ta mike ta fita, sai abin ya sakata samun kwarin gwiwa, tana wa kowa kallon ayaba, su kuma suna mata kallon biri.

         ---
Sai da nayi kwana uku kafin na samu nutsuwa,a lokacin na sami labarin Kawu ya farka amma baya gani, ranar nayi kuka dukda dai sunan fada cewa.
Ba lallai bane ya gani sabida idanun shi da kasa suka cika cikin su.

             Lokacin da muka je gaida shi, komawa nayi daga baya. Ina kallon shi. Aswad na bashi kunun tsamiya. Gefe wata me kama Da Umma Fulani tana bashi labari. Sai murmushi yake, kamar yana ganinta.
 D'ago kai yayi ya tsira min ido kamar yana gani, diriricewa nayi na shiga nonnokewa. Kauda kan shi yayi yana murmushi yace.
"Jamalah! Naso ace ina gani naga irin kibar da Aswad ke ihun kinyi. A sanina Fulani bata baki abinci me mai ko, naji ance kin tara tsokoki."

 Yadda yayi murmushi yana watsa idanun shi ko ina, yana jiran me zata ce.
"Abba! Kaji abinda kanina yake cewa min ko?"

     "Kai! Har yanzun baki tashi daga barci nan na kiran kanki da Yayata ba! Raihanah! Gaya mata ina da Ƴa"
   
    Cikin jin haushi tace.
"Yar kaniya! Allah Jamal nice Yayar ka?!"
      Murmushi kwace a fuskar Mai Martaba, tab'a Jamalah tayi tare da nuna mata Ni tace.
"Ga Yar shi nan tana jikinki kina kwashewa kawunta albarka"

     Tura baki tayi, dan itama shagwaɓaɓɓiya ce, zuba min ido tayi taga na sunkuyar da kaina, tace.
"Yar Tuzuru! D'ago kai na kalle ki, idan za ayi tuwona mai na toh"

     Kura min ido tayi na cigaba da mata bayanin yadda za a zauna lafiya dashi. Kuma na fahimci ta gamsu da baya.

   "Toh idan aka ce ke ki zauna dashi zaki iya duk abinda kika lissafo min? Idan kece zaki zauna domin jinyar shi?!"

    Zaro ido nayi waje! Ina girgiza mata kai sannan nace mata.

"A'ah Ni kam bazan iya ba! Dan bani da kwarin gwiwar kula dashi kina ga yadda kuma baku sha dashi ba sai nice zan iya  kasassaɓar zama dashi ba."

Girgiza kai nayi cikin damuwa na nuna bazan iya ba, kawai zan bi mama mu koma gida.

Tashi tayi ta isa bakin kofa, ta rike kofar tana kallona, sannan ta lashe bakinta kafin tace.
"Zaki iya!"

Sunkuyar da kai nayi ina kallon kasa, kwalla na ciko min ido.
D'agowa nayi da niyyar na mata magana, sai girgiza kai tayi sannan tace.
"Zaki iya fa! Kawai kina tsoron Jamal ne amma zaki iya shi jinya dole ka cire shakkar mutum da kuma, koma dai yayi ne kiyi hakuri amma tabbas kece zaki zauna dashi."

Tana gama fad'ar haka tayi ficewar ta.

    Dafe goshina nayi cikin damuwa, domin kuwa na yankowa kaina damuwa, dan nasan Kawuna ba sauki ne dashi ba.

          ---
Kallon shi Shamaki yayi a ranshi kuwa dariya yake, amma a fili fuskar tankar babu komai a zuciyar shi.
      "Ina son ka shirya zuwa Yola! Kaje kaga yar Sarkin Haris"

           Idanun shi a lumshe kamar bada shi ake ba, yace.
"Ni bana son auren kowacce mace."

  Cikin fusata da jin haushi yace mishi.
"Sai wacce?!"

     Buɗe idanu yayi kafin uban, sai da shamaki yaja da baya. Jan da idanun shi yayi har wani wani irin haske.
"Kai lafiyarka?! Dubi yadda idanunka suka yi ja! Anyi maka wani abu ne?!"

      Tashi yayi zaune, yana jin haushin da yake ji kamar ya saka a kashe uban, amma ya rasa gane ta inda zai yi haka. 
"Kawai ka kyale ni! Aure kuma ka nimawa kanka."

Yana gama fad'ar haka ya ficce daga gidan, fita yayi daga cikin masarautar baki d'aya, da kafa yake tafiya shi kanshi bai san inda yake takawa ba, kawai tafiya yake.

              Sai da yayi nisa da masarautar ya bi ta bayan masarautar inda babu yawaitar jama'a yaje ya zauna, shi d'aya ya zauna ya kurawa gurin ido. Sam baya cikin nutsuwar shi, abu d'aya yake bukata itace Mace macen ma Jannart.

   Ajiyar zuciya ya sauke kamar wadda yayi gudu, muryan mutane yaji suna tun karo inda yake, juyawa yayi zuwa inda yake ji.

         Yan mata ne su biyu tare da bayi  mata biyar, sun kurawa d'aya daga cikin su ido yayi yana jin wani irin yanayi na taso mishi, idan idanun shi basu yi karya ba, Labiba ce da Kanwar shi. Ga baki daya yaran sun yi wawan girma. 

Wata mahaukaciyar ajiyar zuciya ya sauke yana kallon yadda yarinyar take takawa, suna isowa inda yake bayi suka zube a gaban shi, su kuma suka gaishe shi a duke.

               Lumshe idanunshi yayi sannan ya bude akan su, abinda ya gani shi ya kuma. D'aga mishi hankali.

                 Shakar iska yayi sannan ya d'aga musu hannu tare da nuna musu hanyar gida. Suna juyawa yabi bayan Labiba da ido, dole yasa a dauko mishi ita. Bai tab'a ba kuma akan ta zai fara.
         ---
  Duk yadda naso kin amincewa da zaman jinyar shi sun kasa fahimta, dan haka nayi shiru dan bani son yawan musun da manya kamar rashin d'a'a ce.

      Ina zaune dan su Aliyah sun jima da komawa kaduna saura mune muke jiran jikim shi yayi sauki.
            Ina cikin daki naji yo muryan su suna tattauna yadda suka kalubalenci gasar da aka yi, wanda Jamalah ta nuna cewa bazai yiwu a yanke hukunci ba, tunda Jamal ne ya kama tutar kuma ya sami matsala da dokin shi idan duniya da gaskiya. D'aga wasan za ayi ba ace za a yanke hukunci kai tsaye ba, ba tare da bin tsarin gasar ba, dan haka tayi  dogon rubutu aka turawa, alkalin gasar.
     
Shi kuma ya kai pada, dan dole manyan suka d'aga wasan.

    D'ago kai tayi tana kallona, sannan ta kalli agogon dakin tace.
"Zaki zauna a gurin Umma Fulani ne ba zaki na yawo ba, sannan zaki nutsu ki kula dashi babu abinda zai miki! Kinga yanzun karfe tara saura."

          Share kwalla nayi da bayan hannuna, sannan nace.
"Mama ki tafi dani kawai. Allah ina jin tsoron zama a nan."

"Babu inda zani dake! Tunda tun farko naso karki shiga maganar kika yi tsagal kika shiga. Yanzun kuma kice baki zama ai Abba da Umma Fulani suka ji haka nice zasuwa fada. Ko kina son a sani a gaba kamar ke ana miki faɗa?!"

girgiza mata kai nayi. Ina kallon yadda take had'a kayanta.

             Tana gamawa ta zauna kusadani muka cigaba da hira.
       ---
Kwance yake rubda ciki, ki yassa yadda zai isa cikin gidan su ba tare da kowa ya ganshi ba, dan zuciyarshi tana ga surar Labiba. ya kuma san yanzun haka tayi barci. Dai dai yadda zai cimma muradin shi kenan!

          Mik'ewa yayi  daga saman gadon shi, ya koma gaban adakar shi ya dauko wata bakar rawani. Ya naɗe fuskar shi sannan ya isa bakin Kofar shi ya kai hannu zai tab'a sai kuma ya fasa. Kamar wanda aka ingiza shi ya tura kofar da sauri, ya fita.

  Bai tsaya ba sai dakin da yan matan ke kwance, yana zuwa kofar ya tura yajita a bude shiga yayi cikin sanɗa ya wutar dakin ne a kashe, da  warware rawanin yayi sannan ya isa bakin gadon, kanga fuskar shi yayi na wani lokaci, kafin ya d'ago kan shi. Juyawa yayi ya kai fuskar shi, bai b'ata lokaci ba ya saka hannunshi ya dauketa cak yayi waje da ita. 

        Cikin wani irin zafin nama yake tafiya da ita, kafin kace me har ya isa d'akin shi da ita,  duk yadda yaso kaucewa abinda yake da niyyar yi ya kasa domin ji yaƙe kamar idan bai kusanci mace ba a lokacin Akwai Matsala.
   
   Dan haka cikin sauri ya shiga rabata da kayan jikin ta, a dai dai lokacin ta buɗe idanu, ihu ta fasa yayi maza ya rufe mata bakin ta, kallon surar jikinta yayi kamar mayye haka ya koma, jin yayi sam bazai iya haka ban.

           Tashi yayi daga kanta ya juya mata baya ya ce.
"Fita kafin na ida nufina!"

           Kuka ne ya kwace mata, ta tattara kayanta ta sauka akan gadon ta fice da gudu, tana kuka. Me tattare da tsoro hadi da firgici.

     Dafe goshinsa yayi cikin tashin hankali, wannan yanayin da yake ciki da daure kai yake.

         Tunda ta fita dakin su ta koma. Kuka take wanda ya tada kanwar shi a barci, tace.
"Lafiya?!"

"Damar kece ta sauya kaddarar ki da ta wasu! Lokacin ki ne na nunawa Ahalin Zulum kin dawo! Ranki shi dad'e! Karni uku zuwa hud'u kika dawo! A shirye muke mu bada rayuwar mu dan sabunta masarautar da aka Zalince ki."

"Wai ke meye matsalarki da Rayuwa?! Meye kike bukata dani?! Meye na tsare miki ne?! Me yasa kike kambama al'amarin  canfe, wanda babu shi cikin addinin Musulunci."

Yadda take kallona ya kashe min jiki, k'in karb'an abin wuyar nayi na juya abuna ina cewa.
"Ban da ina jin kunyar ki Tabbas da na baki mamaki mata duk kin hana Ni sakat"

       "Ranki shi dad'e ki amshi wannan sarkar wata rana zai miki amfani."
   Juyawa nayi a fusace kamar zan rufeta da duka, sai zuɓewa tayi akan gwiwarta. Abinda Ya kuma kashe min jiki kenan.
"Wai me kike bukata dani?!"

        "Ki amshi wannan sarkan. Shi d'aya ya rage zan baki domin tuna wasu shudadd'en alamari dake tattare dake. Ba a san maci tuwo ba, sai miya ta kare"

Durkusawa yayi a gaban Jamal ya rike hannunsa. Cikin kwarin gwiwa yace.
"Babu dawainiyya a tsakanin mu! Sai zumunci da soyayya ta yan uwantaka. Dan haka babu godiya a tsakanin mu! Dan abinda nayi maka shi zaka min ko bazaka iya bane?"

"Assalamu alaikum warahmatullah!"
       

Tsuke fuska yayi sosai kamar bashi ba.

Murmushi nayi sannan na mika mishi tiren, na koma gefe ina kallon yadda komai na cikin zauren yayi kura, kallon Aswad nayi sannan nace.

"Ko zaku shiga cikin dakin ne na share nan yayi kura ba kyan gani!"

    "Toh ai ba damu! Kiyi aikinki."
Inji Aswad,
        
Daga yanayin fuskar Kawu na fahimci bayi na'am da abinda zan yi ba. Ban gama ayyana haka a raina ba yace.
"Bana bukata zaki iya tafiyarki!"

"Haba kai kuwa dube yadda ko ina yayi kura! Ke yi aikinki, idan na wannan ne babu wanda ya isa mishi gwaninta"

         Gyad'a kai nayi, shi kuma ya tura keken Kawu zuwa cikin dakin. Sannan ya fito ya dauki abincin su.

        A nutse na fara aikin shara da goge goge! Ina gamawa na nufi inda ya ajiye wasu kayan shi sun min kyau, ban san tsautsayin da ya kaini tab'a wani kwalba a gurin ba, aikuwa ya fad'o ji kake fasssss.
              A tsora ce na kai hannuna  tare bakina, dan nasan sai yayi faɗa.

" Duba min meye ta fasa min? Dan wallahi bazan dauki hauka ba!"

                "Garin ya-ya kika fasa kwalba?!" Inji Aswad,
     
Cikin inda inda da kame kame na fara magana.
"Na goge zan! Mai dashi inda yake shin."

   "Jamal!!" Aswad ya kira sunan shi.
"Kai ma kayi min shiru! Dan Allah ya jarabe ni da laluran ido sai a cuce ni, wallahi bazai sabu ba. Na gaya miki karki kuma min barna dan wallahi zan baki mamaki."

"Allah ya baka hakuri!"

Kwallar da nake tarewa ne ya sauko min! Tunda nake shi daya ne yake gaya min magana hankali kwance! Ba tare da ya damu da maganar zai dawo ba.

"Innah! Kisan yadda zaki yi da kazeem, na gaji da halayyar da yake nuna min! Shekaru da dama komai sai na tambaya hatta hakkina! Gaskiya nayi danasanin yin ruwa da tsakin akan komai!"

Ba shi da wata amsa da zai kare kan shi! Tunda shima hasashen yake kaman ya tab'a rayuwa da wata amma ba wai ya tabbatar bane.
     *_Eh mana ka tab'a rayuwa da wata! Kayi soyayya da wata! Kun raya da dararraki masu darajar kun shimfida soyayya mai ma'ana_*

Dafe goshinsa yayi cikin rashin nutsuwa. Yana sauke wata irin ajiyar zuciya idan bai manta ba.
Akwai wasu kalmomin da yake.
Rutsa idanun yayi dan kan shi ya fara sara mishi a hankali kalmomi.
*_Amfanin rai a rayu! Amfanin hutu aji dad'i! Amfanin murmushinki  jin daɗin raina!  zan  iya ba da rayuwata akanki! Hakazalika zan iya raba wani da tasa, akan farin cikin soyayyarki, akan nutsuwar da take tarayya da ganinki! A gare Ni kece ma'anar sa'ada! Ga zuciyata kece ma'anar Salama_*

    Kafin yayi wani yunkuri ya fadi rike da kan shi, da yake barazanar fashewa. Idanun shi sun yi jajjur kamar gauta, ya manta wacce yayiwa wannan kalaman sam ya manta da wacece! Amma Tabbas zuciyarshi bata manta kalmar da suka yi musaya ba daga bakunan su.
        Kwanciyarshi yayi a kasa yana jin wani irin zafi a zuciyar shi.
        .....
Tun da tafita zuwa d'akinta. Take kuka me cin rai. Me yasa?! Bai manta da Fannah ba?! Ko dama labarin da ake badawa na tushen su Fannah gaskiya ce? Suna da alaka da mayun gado tunda Asalinsu ba yan wannan yankin bane zuwa suka yi daga wani guri na daban! Indai haka ne tsugune bata kare mata ba. Dan a yanzun take buƙatar mijinta! Kuma yana gudun ta!

      Hannunta biyu ta daura akanta. Tasan tana zuwa gurin Innah ba zata saurareta ba. Tunda  ta cika mata burinta, abu daya ta gaza cikawa Innah nata burin shine haihuwar D'a namiji wanda zai Gaji Kazeem!

             ---
Bayan kwana biyu...

    Duk saurin da nake na yau kar na makara yaci tura, dan na shiga na same su zaune da Umma tana kallon shi, cikin tausayawa. Sosai kamanin su ya fito sak, dan dai ita ta manyanta ne! Shi kuma matashi.

       Ina shiga tana mik'ewa ta kalle ni, sannan tayi murmushi tare da dafa kafad'ana tace..
"Kiyi hakuri"
Murmushi nayi mata tare da gyad'a mata kai. Fita tayi na ajiye tiran a gaban shi. Na kurawa kyakyawar fuskar shi ido, wanda yake kara jefani cikin wata alkarya na daban akan son shi.

"Zaki daina kallona kiyi abinda ya kawo ki ko Ya-ya! A haka ake kula da mara lafiya? Baki da wani aiki sai na kisaka ni a gaba kina kallona dan bani ganinki sai aka ce miki bana fahimtar kome, idan bazaki yi aikinki ba zaki iya haɗa kayanki."

        Shiru nayi tare da zuba mishi kayan shayi a kofin da nazo dashi. Dake jikina na rawa ban san na zuba mishi siga da yawa ba, ban juya ba na mika mishi kofin, amsa yayi yana jan tsaki.
    Sannan na dauki wani karamin ture na zuba mishi farfesun bindin Saniyar da aka yi mishi.

      Kurb'an shayin yayi ai kuwa ya furza min a fuskana, na sake tiren hannuna akan cinyata tare da faɗin.
"Wayyo Allah na!!!"

  Da karfi! Fuska yayi kamar bai ji abinda yayi ba, ta tattaro duk wata masifar da yake ji fuskar shi yayi a ya zubga min harara kamar yana gani na, yace.

"Maza dauki kisha kafin raina ya b'aci sosai?!"

Cikin rawan murya nace.

     "Kee! Bani son hauka akan me zaki saka dattijo a gaba da rashin kunya." Inji Kawuna,

    "Eh wallahi! Ranka ya dade! Na rantse da Allah ban dauki komai ba." Mutumin ya faɗa da sauri!

       Kafin ya rufe bakin shi na fasa wata irin ihu! Wanda ya haifar da tashin wasu irin zuma bakaken a kaina, gashin kaina kuwa suka mike sai karar zumar kake ji suna "wummmmmm"

Da yatsa na nuna musu Wanzamin, zuɓewa yayi akan gwiwar shi, tare da fashewa da kuka yana cewa.

"Don Allah! Ki rufa min asiri , ina da iyali nima biya na aka yi nazo na cire gashin kanshi. Na tuna bazan kuma ba."

          Zuɓewa nayi a gurin, jini na diga a hancina.

         "Gani gashin kaje kai da Allah! Wato dan bani gani shine bari ka cutar dani duk irin kyakyawar da muka maka! Kaje ba komai" Jamal ya gaya mishi,

        Fita yayi jikinshi a sa ke, yana fita. Jamal ya gangaro daga inda yake zaune, yana lallubar inda Jannart take kwance, abin gwanin tausayi.  Dakyar ya samu hannun ta, cikin sauri da kuzari ya yaje ya d'agota ya daura kanta akan cinyar shi, yana murza mata tafin hannunshi a nata, sun jima a haka kafin tafarka ganinta akan cinyar shi d'are dare yasata mik'ewa tana cewa.

"Ayya kawu! Kayi hakuri ban san.". Ture kaina yayi daga cinyar shi.

       Ko karasa maganar ban yi ba, sannan ya shiga lallubar keken shi. Mik'ewa nayi cikin sauri na kawo mishi ina cewa.
"Kawu! Ga kujeran"

       Wani b'ata rai yayi kamar na zage shi. Dole na sunkuyar da kaina kasa.

         A ranar haka muka wuni har yamma! Sannan na koma cikin gidan.

   Borno...

   Kallon Ma'aji yayi sannan yace.
"Ina son wani littafin tarihin masarautar nan!  Zan karanta"

        "An gama ranka shi dad'e!"

   Haka yayi ta bin Littafin da idanun,ya rasa wanda zai karanta.

"Bazan yi tunanin kai ke ka haife Ni ba! Kuma bazan damuwa da kai waye a gare ni ba!"

     Buga kwalbar yayi da kasa, ya nuna mishi wuyar kwalbar ya cigaba da cewa.

"Wallahi zan sanyaka a loto, nayita daɓɓa maka wuka, sai naga ka daina numfashi zan kyale ka. Ban tab'a tsintar kaina a duniyar mace ba sai da nayi ido biyu da ita! Bana jin akwai macen da zata dauki lalurata irinta shine kayi min bakin ciki haduwa da ita! Toh ba makawa zan tafi amma ka guji dawowa ta domin guguwar sauyi zan zo maka da ita."

    Yana gama fad'ar haka yayi tafiyar shi yana had'a hanya, amma dake Shamaki ba damuwar shi bane kawai burin shi ya rabu da Alakakai din Abdulkareem.

           Jinjina kai yayi sannan ya nufi dakin Magajiya.
Bai shiga cikin dakin ba ya tsaya daga bakin kofa, ya kafeta da ido.

     "Wallahi! Idan baki hana yaronki wannan shirmen da yake ba zan sanya akama shi kuma rufe d'an banza zai."

       Shiru yayi yana kallon yadda ta sunkuyar da kai, sabida bayin da suke d'akin.

        "Na dai gaya miki ki ha mishi kunne, kuma ya fita yanar min masarauta kafin na sanya a mishi kisar wulakancin!"

   
A razane ta d'ago kanta tana kallon shi, gyad'a mata kai yayi cikin nuna zai iya aikata komai akan abinda abinda yasa gaba 

       Juyawa yayi tare da barin kofar dakin.

     Jikinta yayi masifar sanyi dan tasan halin Shamaki tunda yace zai aikata ne fa.

       Dan babu ruwan shi da wani d'an shi ko matar shi, dan uwansa ko wani na shi. Mulkin da Ya saka a gaba itace duniyar shi, itace D'an shi itace yan uwan shi, kuma itace  matar shi.

     Shamaki yayi nisa sosai, sai dai a mishi fatan shirya. Amma tabbas ya soma wuce iyaka.

    Allah ya shirya mana shi.

*_Dukken sakon murmushi ne, dukkan kalma tamkar fure ‘ce soyayyarka a rayuwata rayayye ce! A koyaushe kaine mafarkina! Zan ji dad'in idan na mallaki wata shashin na zuciyarka! Yallabai.        Birkita Qoiseh yayi yana kallon ta. Itama shi take kallo,

3️⃣2️⃣

Koda bata ce min na basu guri ba na fahimci shawaran da zata bashi mai muhimmanci ne. Bayan fita na ta kalle shi sosai sannan tace.
"Nasan kana gani!"
D'ago kan shi yayi cike da mamaki, sannan ta isa gaban shi. Kafin ta cigaba da cewa.
"Wannan ma duk ba laifi bane! Amma ka tashi daga barci da kake, ya isa haka."

  Daga nan ta isa gaban shi ta sunkuyar da kanta fuskar shi ta fara mishi magana, cikin nuna mishi abinda yake tafe da ita, kwalla ne ke sauka daga idanun shi.

    Sai gyada kanshi yake, tana gamawa take zata fita tace.
"Kana da ilmi karka zauna a haka damarka ta subce maka. Akwai wani kuskuren da kake son tafkawa, don Allah karka bari wannan damar ya subce maka karka bari ka rasa iri me kyau. Duk da bahaushe yace.Kome tsawon hannun mutum ba ya cude bayansa. Ina son naka hannun ya cudi bayanka."

          Kura mata ido yayi sabida ta daure mishi kai, wacce irin dama ya samu kenan.

"Insha Allah! Zan kiyayye, kuma zan yadda kika ce."
Ya bata amsa.
   
   Wato abinda na fahimta, akwai wani irin munafunci a cikin masarautan, wanda idan aka cire maka wasu mutane ƙalilan kowa da ka gani munafiki ne.

    Domin bayan Sarkin da Ya fito da nashi a fili, wato Shamaki. Toh har gwara shi domin kuwa bazaka tab'a fahimtar komai akan waye mugu ba, sabida duk Fuskar su d'aya.

     Abinda ya sani yin tunanin haka fitowa ta daga ciki na bawa su Umma guri, ba kowa na gani ba sai Fulani Furerah da wani babban Bafaden Shamaki.

   Sai nayi kamar ban gan su ba, nayi nisa cikin nazarin me ya kawo su?! Me suke nima?! Meye alakar su da Juna?! Wannan shine abinda ya tsaya min a cikin kwalkwalwa ta.

      Kara kallon kusurwan da suke nayi, sai na cigaba da abinda nake yi.

        Ina son sanin me suke faɗa amma bani da yadda zanji abinda na sani na ke kuma tunani, suna son cutar da Kawu ne taya zan taimaka mishi kar kudurin su ya isa gare shi?!

      Wani irin abu naga ya gifta ta gabana, ban kuma jin komai ko fahimtar kaina ba, sai bayan wasu lokuta na tsinci kaina a dakin Umman da ta kawo mishi magani.

       "Innalillahi wa'inna ilaihi rajioun!" Da haka na fara magana tare da goge damshin da ya zubo min. Ina dubawa naga jini ne.

Tashi zaune nayi ina kallonta.
"Sannu Jannart! Ya jikin naki?!"
Ta tambaye ni, tana me kallona.

    "Umma da sauki! Ya Kawu yake?! Wani hali yake ciki?!. Yaci abincin kuwa?!"

         Murmushi tayi sannan tace.
"Kiyi sallah sai na rakaki, Kiga halin da yake ciki daga nan ki wuce shashin ku."

               A hankali na tashi tare da fita a dakin. Alola nayi na dawo.
              Tunda na idar da sallah, na kalleta, itama Ni take kallo cikin tausayawa, mik'ewa tayi  sannan tace.
"Tashi muje!"
     Haka na mike ta kaini har inda yake, ina ganin shi yana barci,
wucewa muka yi ta kaini wani inda zai kai ni, cikin gidan bata bar kofar ba sai da taga na shiga gidan sannan ta bar kofar gidan.

     ....... Abinda ya faru bayan wucewar wannan abun, fita hayacina nayi, daga inda nake na kalle su. Sannan na dukar da kaina.
          Daga nan inda nake zaune wasu irin bakaken kwari suka nufe su, da mugun gudu.

Kafin su ankara tuni sun shiga kai musu cizo. Ihun su ne ya farga da mutane halin da suke ciki Ni kuma na yanki jiki na suma a gurin.

          Shine jin hayaniyar jama'a yasa Umma Fitowa tana gani na ta dauke ni zuwa cikin gurin su.

           .....
Gyara zama Jakadiya tayi sannan tace.
"Sai wani ya rasa wani kan samu. Ki nazarci abinda na fada."

   Gyad'a kai Fulani Hasiyah tayi tana wasa da, warwaron hannunta.
"Tafasar tukunya bata gefe guda bace. 
Baki tunanin duk ranar da duniya tasan abinda yake faruwa nice zan kwan ciki ba?! Ko ance miki. Koshin wake na ruwa ne, kafin ki zarta da hukunci dole kasan ta yadda zaka yi da jama'a. Rumanah ta mutu! Shekaru kusan talatin da wani abu! Yau Rumanah tana raye. Taya zan fuskanci kalubalena?  Sahurah shi mulki ba shirme bane! 

        Burina akan Jamal ya rushe tunda bai da amfani a gare ni, ko yau duniya tasan d'an rumanah ce bani da kaico nasan ta yadda zan kare kaina.

      Amma ina mafitar take? Dani dake da Shamaki da Galadima, mun hada kai dan ganin bayan sarki amma abun ya faskara."

    Numfashi ta sauke ranta na soya alamar tana bukatar nazari sosai.

     "Idan na bada goyan bayan a kauda Jamal da Ubansu! Meye makomar Raihana, kuma nayi imani da Allah tafi Jamal zafi shi nashi zafin sabida bai da laifi ne, amma ita nata a jininta yake tun tana yarinya! Sahurah karki manta, babu wani sarkin da ya isa yayi dogan zamani sai ya kasance ruwa biyu! Kuma bana tunanin zasu bashi mulkin, dan shima Shamaki tsoron Abdul yake.

    Sahura ina cikin tashin hankali, na rasa yadda zanyi."

Murmushi Jakadiya tayi sannan tace.
"Domin rana ‘daya ba a ‘kin guzuri.!"
     
Girgiza kai Fulani Hasiya tayi sannan tace.
"Ba rikidan kura ke wuya ba, a yi wutsiya! Ko me kike nufi?!"
      "Allah na dawo inji me yawan duniya! Uwargijiyata ki barni na baki labarin yadda zamu b'ullowa ja'irin yaron nan tunda dama kece kike hanani yin wani abu."

       "Humm! Tunda na iya dakatar da Rukayya babu wanda bazan dakatar dashi ba, Jakadiya ina tsoron abinda zai dawo.! Hmm"
     Tayi shiru kamar ruwa ya cinye ta, sannan ta kafe Jakadiya da ido.
       

     Murmushi Jakadiya tayi sannan tace.
"Uwargijiyata! Ba a san maci tuwo ba sai miya ya kare, fatana kar idanun Jalal ya buɗe, shi kuma Sarki Jalaludeen ya cigaba da zama a kujeran nakasassu ba kafa ba baki."

     Jinjina kai Jakadiya tayi, cikin yarda da cewa ashe ita karamar muguwa ce. Tunda taga yadda Fulani take hada nata gwarman.

       Mik'ewa Fulani tayi cikin kwarin gwiwa tana murmushi cikin jajjircewa tace.
"Jamal! Da Uban shi zasu mutum? Sannan kafin su Matan sarki biyu nake son a ciyar da su!"

"Fulani?!"
     Jakadiya ta  ambataci sunanta da karfi.
"Me kike nufi? Don Allah ki rufa min asiri, wallahi na lahira sai yafi Ni jin dadi. Bazan iya ba!"

    "Hhhh! Zaki iya sahurah tunda kika kashe Balaraba waye ba zaki kashe ba!"

      Zuru zuru da ido Jakadiya tayi tsabar tashin hankali.
"Kiyi min rai! Wallahi masifa tana tunkaro mu, kuma babu wanda ya isa dakatar da ita sai Allah"

        "Koma meye ke kika sani! Bana ra'ayin. Sauran matan suci tuminin takaba, kuma bani muradin araba gado da yaran su inda hali a kauda kansu akan dukiyar da Takawa ya bari, Jamal da Uban shi yau zan gama da aikin su, domin Galadima ya amso min komai yadda zanyi aikina ba tare da kowa ya ankara ba."

         "Amma sai nake ganin kamar kuskure zaki aikata! Domin kuwa akwai tashin hankali a gabanki! Jamal bazaki iya mishi komai ba, dan akwai Ziriya a tare dashi."

     Shiru tayi na wani lokaci, kafin kuma ta ce.

"Toh Jeki zan nime ki nan da wani lokaci!"

  "Ki huta lafiya!"
Jakadiya ta mike jikinta na rawa.
    
Murmushin mugunta Fulani, tayi tana kallon yadda Jakadiya ta tsorota, ƙarara.
"Sahurah zaki shigo hannuna, har ni kike nunawa iyakata, kike boye min abinda kika sani!"

          ---
    Lokacin yana tafiya fiye da yadda baka tsammani, domin kuwa Ni kaina nasan yanzun Kawun ya fara samun sauki, kuma yana d'an takawa da sanda, dan watan da ya gabata mun kaishi asibiti nida Aswad yaga likita aka kuma ce zai iya takawa. Idanun shi ma yana gani da taimakon madubi.

          A yanzun duk wanda ya ganmu ya tabbatar da cewa mun shaku da juna, dan ko ban je ba zai yi kafa biyu uku dan ya gan halin da nake ciki.

        Yau ma kamar kullum na kai mishi abincin shi zan fita, dan na same shi yana tare da wasu matasa, masu mara mishi baya.

                 "Yallabai tunda ka sami lafiya, ina ga me zai hana ka wakilice mu a matsayin d'an majalisan wakilai, mu wannan shine bukatar mu!"

  Murmushi yayi sannan ya zuba musu ido, dukda hankalin shi Yana tare dasu, amma nutsuwar shi yana tare dani ne, kuma ba komai yaja haka ba sai dan rigimar da muka yi, sakamakon fitar da nayi jiya har dare ban kai mishi abinci ba, ni kuma ban gaya mishi ba. A bakin Umma Fulani yaji ai nafita tare da Zahrah jikar Fulani yar gurin Yaya Rukayyah ta dame Ni na rakata, shine garin dawowa muka dawo da wani dan anace yana tabin mu wai na saurare shi.

       Ashe Kawuna ya fito kofar masarautan yana jiran ta inda zamu ɓullo. Aikuwa sai gamu a cikin wata yar mota karama, tunda ya ganni a gabar motar ya cika fam, duk hirar da Aswad yake mishi bai amsa ba, har na fito.

          Shima mutumin ya sauko  daga motar.

    "Yawwa! Jannart idan nazo nace ina niman Gim....!"
   Wani mugun naushi ya saukewa mutumin a fuska,  ya kuma cigaba da dukar mutumin, ko nace suka kwamtsame da dambe, abinka da mulki fadawa na zuwa suka shiga zane mutmin yayi fada da Yarima Jamal.

      Nikam sai na rasa abin cewa.
Yana jogana sanda ya fincike hannuna har cikin gidan, a falon fulani ya wurgani.
"Fulani! Kar na kuma jin yarinyar nan ta fita, idan kuma naji wani ya dauke ta sun fita, wallahi sai na karya shi, tun safe rabona da abinci sai naga yarinya da wani kato ya kawota. Hmm"

   Ya dauki  sandar shi ya fita yana mita,

      Harara na Fulani tayi cikin nuna min k'iyayyar ta, dan tun kwanakin baya da na kamata tana wani abinda bai dace ba.
Har na fita hayacina na makureta a daki,  tun daga ranar take nuna ta tsane ni. Dan ma abun  faru akan idanun Jakadiya, Dakyar ta kwace Fulani shima sai da tayi min kirarin Maguzawan asali.

      (Toh kirarin Maguzawan asali, bada wani abu bane Ashar ce, kuma maganar gaskiya dole zan yi kadan nan )

    "Takawarki Lafiya Ziriyah kaga yar abu kazan uba, kaga yar  jar abu kazan kaza" kun gan yar kutumar! Babbakeren kaza, yar abu kazan kaza."

      Sake Fulani nayi tare da yankan jiki na suma.
      Tari Fulani take ba kakkautawa.

   "Wannan wacece ita?!"
"Itace sarauniyar ziriya! Wacce a zamanin ta ba ayi wata budurwa me kyau da kwarjini irinta, ranki shi dad'e fada da wannan bayi bane! Domin kuwa kece zaki fad'i a shirye take ta kare Jamal!"

           Toh tun daga nan ne ta tsane ni.
"Mu gidan Sarautar bamu yarda da sakarci ba. Domin muna da da'a, an mana shaidar tarbiyya da kamewa irinta hausa Fulani."

      Cikin sanyin jiki nace mata.

         "Kika ce?!" Ta tambaye ni,
"Hmm!"
Na fito daga dakin, kallon shi nayi ya kafe ni da ido, mik'ewa yayi da sandar shi yayi gaba, kin binshi nayi na tsaya abuna, kamar ya san bana bin shi ya juyo tare da zuba min ido.

"Sarauniyar! Ko sai na turo a dauke ki ne?! Dan naga kinfi karfin tafiya!"

    Ajiyar zuciya ya sauke sakamakon ganin yadda nake takawa, cikin izza da nutsuwa. Wanda ba komai ya haifar da haka ba sai kirana da sarauniyar da yayi. Sau da dama ba fahimci duk lokacin da za'a danganta ni da Jinin Sarauta toh haka kawai zan tsinci kaina da jin wani irin iko da izza tana hawa Kaina.

       Har zauren shi muka shiga, zama yayi cikin nutsuwa sannan ya kalle inda tiren abinci yake yayi sannan ya kauda kan shi.

        Haushin sa nake ji, dan haka ban yi mishi magana ba, naje na zuba mishi abincin na ajiye a gaban shi.
          Zan mike ya dakatar da ni.
"Zauna muci!"
D'ago kaina nayi domin ban tab'a kawowa zai ce haka ba."

     Gyaran muryan shi ya katse min tunanina.
    "Kawu na koshi!"
   "Hmm! Dan na hanaki tsayawa da wancan mutumin ranar shine har yanzu kike fushi da ni?! Amma idan na barki ai bazaki nutsu kiyi karatu ba, shine kike fushi anya kin min adalci. Ki cire shirme a ranki kiyi karatun ki mana"

      Kauda kai nayi raina na tafasa.
"Humm! Ranar itace ta farko da muka hadu da shi. Kuma batun karatu naga wasu ma ai suna karatun ake aurar da su sai nice ban isa auren ba."
      
Numfashina na sauke, kafin na cigaba da cewa.
"Bazan maka butulci ba, amma ina rokon Alfarmarka ka basaka a maidini Lagos, ina ga haka."

    "Dama Fulani da Jakadiya sunce min baƙi da kunya shine kike gaya min maganar da yayi miki! Toh kiji babu inda zaki. Kin dawo nan kenan."

                Iya fusata na fusata, dan a hasale nake, amma ganin shine shugabana ya sani sunkuyar da kai na, cikin nutsuwa nace.
"Allah ya huci zuciyarka! Ba rashin kunya bace. Kayi min afuwa haka bashi kuma faru, amma jumma'ar da take da kyau tun daga Laraba ake ganeta. A iya sani na, Kawuna ba me daukar gutsiri tsoma bane! Amma lokaci guda ya koma fusatace. Afuwa ranka shi dad'e.

        Abinda ka aikata kwanaki a kofar masarautar nan ta sanya ji jin kunyar kaina, sabida kuwa karo na farko da aka samu Yarima Jamal yayi dambe akan mace! 

      Me haka ke nufi? Sannan kuma aka hana ita mace fahimtar kanta balle zuciyata, haka zai iya zame mata kalubalen Rayuwa! Har ila yau ina kara bawa Yarima me jiran gado hakuri.

    Kana da dama a rayuwa na zab'an abinda yayi maka, kana da dama a rayuwa nayi abinka kake so! Sai dai ita wannan damar sau daya ake samunta, da zaran ka rasa har abada bazaka kuma samun ta ba, ka gafarceni."

Ina gama fad'ar haka na mike zan fita yace.
"Jannart! Zoki gaya min damar da nasamu nake wasa dashi."

     Juyawa nayi sannan na sake mishi murmushi nace.
"Ka fito cikin Hausa Bakwai! Ashe dunkulalliyar magana ba bako bane a gurinka, ka hutu lafiya."

  Na juya tare da barin gurin shi, Tabbas da ba Kawu Jamal bane, babu abinda zai hana ban kafta mishi magana ba ikon Allah ne.

   Sabida gaskiya nan ya take ta, kamar bai san komai ba, har gwara min da yake tare da cutar makanta.
         D'aga kanshi yayi cike da damuwa, yana nazarin meye haka ke nufi. Umma ta gaya mishi kar yayi wasa da damar shi. Haka zalika kar yayi sake ya rasa abu me daraja meye wannan abin.

         Sosai ya shiga tunani akan haka. Me Yarinyar nan take nufi?!
   "Meye Jannart take buƙata daga gare Ni?!"
  "Wata zab'e nake dashi a rayuwata?!"
      "Toh me yasa suke ganin ina tafiyar mara mabulla?! Toh me yasa suke ganin ina wasa da damar da nasamu?!"

  Shiru yayi na wasu lokuta kafin ya sanya abincin a bakin shi, sai yaji sam baya jin daɗin komai, sabida abincin bai mishi ba. Ko kuma wacce ta dafa abincin ranta ba dad'i.

        Ajiye cokalin yayi cikin tiren tasar dake gaban shi, Tabbas akwai wani abu me darajar da ake ankarar dashi ya rike da kyau. Toh koma meye bazai d'aga hankalin shi akan kome ba, idan akwai abinda ya d'aga mishi hankali. Toh zantuttunkar da tayi, yayi imanin da Allah akwai abinda suke boye mishi.

           Cikin damuwar da bai san lokacin da ta aure shi ba, ya shafi kan shi. Mik'ewa yayi cikin kasala ya nufi har da zai fidda shi daga dakin.

       Cikin gida ya nufa har shashin me Martaba, ya same shi zaune da Fulani Kareemah, cikin girmamawa ya gaida ita, sannan ya koma gefe ya zauna kanshi a sunkuye.

        Mik'ewa Fulani Kareemah tayi cikin jin haushin Jamal, domin kuwa kowacce bata da d'a namiji. Kuma har haihuwar ta tsaya basu yi b'arin da namiji ba, ba iya haka ba. Akwai abubuwan da dole sai sun kauda kansu akan Jamal ko kuma su fuskanci b'acin rai ga mai martaba, dukda baya magana amma bazai tab'a kyale su ba. Dan ko Fulani Hasiyah da ta zare hannunta akan jinyar Jamal shima zare hannun shi yayi akan komai nata.

   Sai da ta haɗa da Jamal din sannan Mai martaba ya sauko, dukda baya musu magana, amma Tabbas yakan musu aiki da idone kana tab'a Jamal duk wani abinda yasan yana fita ta hannun shi watsewa yake, amadadin haka ya janyowa Jamal din kima. Sai ya kuma rura wutarta gaɓar shi da yan uwan shi tare da matan mai martaban tare da wasu masu son ganin bayan shi.

         Zama sosai Jamal yayi yana me sunkuyar da kanshi.
      Shafa kan shi yayi cikin nutsuwa sannan yace.
"Ranka shi dad'e!"

   "Masata sun zo niman ka tsaya musu a takara dan majalisa me wakiltan su ko?!"
             
    D'ago kai yayi na dan wani lokaci kafin ya sunkuyar, murmushi mai martaba yayi sannan ya kai hannun shi kan gefen gadon shi ya mika mishi wata takarda, ya mika mishi.

      "Ban san lokacin da Mulkin gidan nan zai dawo gare ka ba, domin na fahimci duk wanda ka aminta dashi zai iya juya maka baya, ban san dalilin su nayi haka ba, sai dai A safiyar Jiya laraba, aka kawo wannan takardan daga fadar shugaban kasa! Zuwa ga gwamna jahar Katsina. Muhammad Yusuf! Shi kuma ya nuna min shi din Haifaffen d'a ne, dan haka ya turo aka bawa Magaji."

    Ajiyar zuciya ya sauke, sannan ya cigaba da cewa.
"Magaji ya turo Ammar! Dan Aswad baya nan, ya tafi wani aiki can taraba, koda Yaron ya kawo min na duba, sai naga Ofishin harkokin waje kasar nan suna nimanka kaje musu aiki! Duk da Shugaban kasar mu ba musulmi bane. Amma mun yi makaranta daya dashi! Obafemi  Christ Olawali, nasha mamaki da ya turo da tarkadan izinin yana son kaje kayi aiki da ma'aikata waje."

Sauke numfashi yayi cikin damuwa, kafin ya cigaba da cewa.
"Karka manta da asalin ka! Karka manta da waye kai! Karka manta da akwai mutanen da suka saka rai da kai, karka manta akwai wacce take jingine da kai, Karka manta Raihanah tana faɗi tashi sabida kai, karka manta da mahaifinka yana na a zaune, na fahimci kafin ka isa ga wancan kujeran dole sai ka same dabi'a irinta rayuwa, dole sai ka sami gogewar rayuwa, dole sai ka fahimci waye kai"

   Lumshe idanun Mai martaba yayi cikin jin dadi a ran shi. "Kayi ƙoƙarin boye duk wata kasawarka! Karka bari ka nunawa duniya gazawarka! Ina maka nasiha da kaji tsoron Allah! A duk alamarin ka, za aka kaika inda baka sani ba, kuma baka san mutanen gurin ba! Abu daya zai kwace ka! Kasan me?!"

   Girgiza kai Jamal yayi, cikin nutsuwa da son jin me zai karu dashi.
"Gaskiyar ka! Itace tsanin duk wani shaidar da za a bayar akanka! Itace madubin rayuwarka ta baya da ta gaba! Itace linzamin ka! Itace mafi girman nasarar ka! Za a iya cika da yaki, amma idan kana da gaskiya toh babu wanda ya isa kalubalantar ka."

     Gyad'a kai kawai yake a nutse, sabida wani irin nutsuwa yake ji na shigar shi ta ko ina.

             Bai tsammaci maganata zata fito daga bakin Mai Martaba ba.
        "Ya kuke tsakaninka da Yarinyar da tayi jinyarta?!"

    Ware idanu yayi akan fuskar Mai martaba, cikin tsoro bakin shi na rawa yace.
"Ai ni, ai ni babu wani abu a tsakanin mu, kawai ita ke nata shirmen amma Ni...ni babu komai a raina."

   "Nutsu! Jamal! Ka nutsu, ja numfashi sosai sannan ka sauke"
    
Kamar yadda Mai martaba yace mishi.
      Sannan ya buɗe ido shi akan mai martaba..
"Yana da matuƙar wahala kasame wacce zata bada rayuwarta domin ka.  Ba sauki mace ta nunawa duniya tana yinka. Ba rasa gwani bane matsalar waye zai cika gurbin da aka rasa, Ubangiji yana bamu dama sau daya ce, idan kuma ya kara bamu haka yana nufin cewa yana son mu da Rahama ne, Karka yi saki na dafe, domin kuwa ba kowa yake ganin koshi yayi kwantar yunwa ba."
Shiru mai martaba yayi cikin damuwa domin shi kaɗai yasan dalilin da yake son Jamal ya aure ni.

       Domin ko a cikin sakon da Ammar ya kawo mishi akwai Maganar niman amincewar Jamal ya aureni.

     Amma mutumin nan ya bad'a toka a idanun shi yace.
"Abba nifa ba na..."
"Kaje kayi shawara da zuciyarka! Kayi tunani da zuciyarka, idan ta kama kayi da wani, na baka nan da sati uku! Domin akwai wani abu da ya taso, amma ina son ka sani daga wannan lokacin bazan saurari korafin ka ba! Ba kuma san saurari cewarka ba! Kuma bazan tsaya maka ba, na duba duk cikin wannan yanayin baka fahimci inda ka dosa ba, ina tuna maka dab kake da wasa da damar ka! Idan ka kuskura ka rasa shi.......

08130269641... Ayi min magana ta Whatsp

3️⃣4️⃣

"Karka yi tunanin zan saurari koda Raihanah ce, bazan saurari kowa ba kai koda Fulani ce bazan saurare su ba, na baka dama nan da sati Uku kayi shawara da zuciyarka, abinda ka yanke dashi zan yi amfani dashi, Jamal wannan shine damar da na baka!!! Kaje kayi nazari akan Jannart! Na yaba da nagartattun halayyar ta, Jamal samun mace irinta zai yi wuya, ta sadaukarwa da farin ciki saboda kai, hmm Jamal kenan kaje kayi shawara da zuciyarka."

A iya fahimtar shi ran mai martaba ya kai kololowar ɓaci, amma dake yana son shi yaki nuna mishi, cewa bai damu ba.

Haka Jamal yayi mishi sallama ya fita.
_Kana dab da rasa damarka!_
Haka Umma tace mishi, Yau Jannart ta gaya mishi, kuma ga Mai martaba ya gaya mishi. Kai hatta Aswad ya gaya mishi kar yayi wasa da damar shi.

Shi wata dama ce haka yake wasa da ita a iya sanin shi babu wata damar da yake wasa da ita, tunda Allah ya gani bai san komae ba, ita kanta JANNART baya jin komai akanta!

A hankali yake takawa har ya isa shashin bayi ya shiga, kai tsaye ya nufi dakin Umma, tun da ya shiga yayi shiru, kan shi a sunkuye.

Kallon shi tayi cikin damuwa sannan tace.
"Yarima Jamal?! Kana da matsala ne?!"

"Hmm" yace mata,
"Toh meke damunka?!"
Ta tambaye shi,
Gyara zama yayi sosai, kafin ya fara da cewa.
"Umma ni bani son Jannart!"
A hankali ya warware mata matsalar da yake ciki.

Murmushi tayi sannan tace.
"Toh indai haka ne, ya kamata ta fidda miji tayi aure, dan babu amfanin ajiye baligar budurwa a gabanka!"

Da wani irin sauri ya kalli Umma? Murmushi tayi sannan tace.
"Baka son ta auri wani? Kuma kai baka sonta? Anya kayi mata adalci? Kai fa Shugaba ne nan gaba. Tunda ba kayi ka bawa wasu guri su yi, amma kaje kayi shawara da zuciyarka!?"

Shiru yayi cikin tashin hankali.
_Allah ka gani bazan amshi abinda bani so ba_
Ya fadi haka a zuciyar shi, mik'ewa yayi tare da mata sallama ya bar dakin.

---
Fadar Shehun Borno...

Kurawa Bulama idanu Kazeem yayi, cikin damuwa sauke tagumi yayi tare da ajiye idanun shi akan fuskar Bana, lumshe idanunshi yayi sannan yace.
"Kowace rayuwa akwai jarabta! Ni tawa jarabta, na manta abinda suka gaba ne! Iya tunani na rasa fahimtar meye na manta, amma tabbas akwai abinda na rasa me daraja! Shin kuda kuke y'an uwana bazaki tuna min meye na rasa ba."

Shiru fadar yayi cikin jin haushi abinda ya sami dan uwan su, su kansu basu san ta yadda zasu mishi bayani ba, tunda an wani gari ne aka gan shi ya fadi a ban daki. Tun daga nan bai kuma fahimtar meke faru ba, kuma ba zai iya tuna komai na abinda ya shafi rayuwarshi ta baya akan gidan shi ba.
A yau yana zaune ne a matsayin Shugaban al'ummar Borno, amma a zahirance, dashi da babu duk d'aya ne! Tunda ko yau ya bar duniya bashi da Magaji.

Babu wanda zai maye gurbin shi, wannan dalilin yake karawa yan uwan shi bakin cikin abinda ke faruwa.

Kuma suke kara jin b'acin rai da halin da yake ciki, babu yadda suka iya ne, amma tabbas da suna da hali sai sun nimo Mishi farin ciki fiye da kima.

Gyara zama Bana yayi cikin damuwa sannan yace.
"Ranka shi dad'e! Mu dai bamu ga wani sauyi a tare da kai ba, asalima wanda muka sani shine har yanzu a sarkin mu! Wato kai"

Juyawa Bana yayi ga Bulama cikin don ya amsa da hakan, gyad'a kai Bulama yayi sannan.

"Ranka shi dad'e! A iya sanin mu babu wata damuwa da zamu boye maka."

Cikin sanyin halinsa yake bin su, dama can shi mutum ne me sanyin hali. Balle kuma da ya fuskanci wata matsala a rayuwar shi sai sanyin ya kuma ta'azara.

Shi mutum ne mai matukar hakuri, amma baya son cin amana ko zalinci, wannan dabi'ar tana matuƙar kara mishi daraja a idanun jama'a yankin shi.

Tashi yayi zai bar fadan yace.
"Na barku cikin amincin Allah"

Jikin sune yayi sanyi sabida sun rufe mishi gaskiyar da ya dace, ace ya sani. Sai mai dashi wani irin mutum na daban.

"A gaskiya Innah da Qoiseh basu kyauta ba, sun Raba shi da matan shi, sannan sun koma kan ita wacce take sanya shi farin ciki. Allah zai bi mishi hakkin sa." Inji Bana,

"Hmm!" Inji Bulama!

---
Biris me Martaba yayi ya kuma zubawa Kawu ido, ban san wainar da ake toyawa ba, sabida kwana biyu nan na daina kai mishi abinci, na huta dan ranar da mai martaba ya haɗa mishi chakwakiyyar. Na kai mishi abincin sai da ya sani kuka sosai nima nayi zuciya, da shi. Domin ya gaya min magana masu zafi, wanda ban tab'a kawowa zai gaya min ba.

"Wato kin shigo rayuwata dan ki tarwatsa ni ko? Gaki karama da ke sai rashin kunya da zubda kima, wai sabida ni aka yi ki ne? Ni ina da irin mace da nake so! Ba macen da bata san ciwon kanta ba, wacce duk lokacin da namiji ya nemi ta bashi kanta zata mika! Wawuya sakarya kawai, ko sanin darajar kai batayi ba.

Wallahi kika ja min damuwa a gurin Me Martaba Ni dake ne, dan zan iya sanya a b'atar min dake"

Kura mishi ido nayi cikin bakin ciki da b'acin rai domin na hasala, har jini ya soma diga daga idanuna zuwa hancina.

Gaba shi naje cikin tashin hankali wanda ya juye fusatar da nayi zuwa kuka nace.

"Fairo! Karka min haka, ka tuna mun yi rayuwa da kai karni da suka gabata, karka rabani da kai. Ka amshi soyayya ta zaka tuna da Wani abu me darajar da ya faru a baya."

Cikin kuka nake bin shi amma sam yaki saurarona, domin kuwa 
Shine damar karshen da nake dashi dan na tuna mishi baya amma yaki halkanta.

Tsayawa nayi cikin kuka nace.
"Ka manta da alqawarin mu! Kai ka zab'i mutuwa da kaga an tozarta ni, kai ka zab'i tafiya ka barni da kaga wani ya rab'e ni, me yasa yau ka zabi juya min baya, kai ka min alqawarin ko ka tafi zaka dawo, nima naji a raina zan dawo. Karka manta munyi alqawarin haka a gaban Tsila, kuma ya sanya mana albarkan shi.

Na zuba gwiwata a kasa karka watsa min kasa, ka duba rayuwar da muka yi zaka iya tunawa."

Fashewa nayi da kuka, me cin rai tare da kifa fuskana a tafukan
Hannuna ina me sake wani Irin kuka, duk da ya shiga rud'a ni amma taurin kai ya hana shi fahimtar ina mafita.
D'ago kai nayi cikin kuka nace.
"Don Allah ka tab'a ni, ka tab'a hannuna. Zaka iya tuna komai! Na yi maka alqawarin idan baka tuna Ni ba, zan tafi har abada bazan dawo ba.

Idan baka tunani ba ni ba, zai koma inda aka gine ni, zan tafi na barka, amma nasan wannan karnin ya cutar dani matuƙa gaya, ya lalata min kome nawa na shekaru aru aru, ya warga zanin duk wata damar da nake dashi.
Zan iya yafewa wanda ya cutar dani, amma tsinuwata zata iya tabbatar a cikin wannan masarautar har karshen rayuwata, kamar yadda na fada a shekarun baya, yau ma na sake fad'a sai kunyi kuka akan soyayya! Musamman kai sai kayi jinyar soyayya, baka tuna ni ba ko?!
Sai kayi wahala akan so! Damar da kayi wasa dashi sai ya zame maka damar da zaka yi rayuwa ko mutuwa. Ka boye min gaskiya, sai gaskiyar ta zame maka guba.

Shirun da kayi min sai ya zame maka mafi girma bakin ciki a duniya, Fairo zan tafi kai da kanka zaka zo, lokacin ina kaucewa ganinka, Wato sabida Zulum da Gunda ka juya min baya.

Ba iya kai ba hatta Jamal din sai yayi bakin ciki me girma a rayuwar shi, Ga Jannart din ni zan tafi. Ba sati uku ka rasa ba, bakin ciki karni ce ka rasa"

Ina gama gaya mishi haka na yanki jiki na fad'i a gurin, dummm kan shi yayi tare da sarawa.

Wani irin haske yaga ya gifta a idanun shi, zuɓewa yayi a hankali hancin shi ya fara fidda jini kamar yadda nawa yake fiddawa.
Lumshe idanunshi yayi, kafin ya d'ago kan shi, yana kallona matsowa yayi.
"Ziriya! Karki tafi na dawo sabida ke. Ziriya alkawarin mu amintacciya ce! Karki juya min baya, Ziriya ga faironki na dawo gare ki, karki tafi."

Ina ta tafi, wani irin ihu ya saka tare da fashewa da kuka, me cin rai. Yana tab'a hannuna tare da girgiza ni.

Mik'ewa yayi kamar wanda ya sha abin maye ya d'ago bayana, dai-dai wuyana yaga zanen rubutun, kamar irinta yan China, d'ago ni yayi ya rungume ni, tare da fasa wani irin kuka, me cin rai.

Yayi biyu babu, yaga samu yaga rashi, shikenan ta tafi shima tafiya zai yi.

Atishawa yayi sau uku kafin ya koma gefe ya kwanta kanshi yana matukar sarawa, 

Anan muka cigaba da Barci, sai da muka yi awa guda me kyau sannan muka tashi, shima sabida zuwan Ammar wanda Baba Magaji ya turo shi ya tafi da Jamal,

Tashi na yayi na zuba mishi ido, sannan na mike ina don tuna abinda ya faru na ƙasa sai na manta kawai na bar mishi gidan shi.

Shima a bangaren shi kokari yake ya tuna abinda ya faru, amma ya kasa sai ya share kawai.

Tun daga nan ne naji bani son zuwa in kai mishi abinci.

Duk wanda mai martaba yake ganin inda ya tura mishi Jamal zai ji maganar shi akaina abin ya faskara, domin ido da ido ya nuna musu shi baya yi na, taimakona yayi kuma babu digon suna a ranshi.

Lokacin da Aswad ya dawo, daga Taraba ya same labarin turka turkan da ake da Jamal, sai da suka yi kaca-kaca, amma bai sanya Jamal ya gane abinda ake nufin shi da shi ba, karshe Baba Magaji yace musu kar su matsa mishi tunda yace ba yayi da gaske ne, kuma kowa ya san k'iyayyar namiji akan mace!!
[8/3, 10:59 PM] My Zahrah: ƘWARƘWARAH!!?

{ITAMA MATAR SARKI CE}

Amma ban yi muku alkawarin posting Kullum ba, sabida uzurin gabana! Insha Allah! Duk bayan kwana biyu! Zaku sami Update da yamma ko da dadare..........

  Kai jama'a an hanani cakat 🙄
3️⃣5️⃣

         Tunda na wayi gari naji na tsani Daura, ba iya dauran ba hatta mutanen cikinta, bana son mu'amala da kowa.

         Hada kayana nayi. Na fita daga gidan sarautar na nufi gidan su Aswad wanda sai da na b'ata sau uku.
        A kofar gidan na same shi, kuka na saka mishi tare da cewa.
"Don Allah ka mai dani Lagos!"

     "Shine matsalar ki?!" 
"Eh, bana son garin nan"

   Gyad'a mishi kai nayi, hawayen na zuba daga cikin su.

   Tabbas kawu yayi sake reshe kama ganye.
           "Toh kije gobe Insha Allah! Zaki bar garin nan! Amma karamar shawara, daga yau karki kuma nunawa duniya kina son wani. Gudun kar yayi miki irin abinda wancan mara mutuncin yayi miki.".. 

Gyad'a mishi kai, nayi tare da ce mishi.
"Nagode!"
            A sanyayye na bar kofar gidan, tare da alkawarin na raba da Kawu har abada,

           ban kuma zuwa inda yake ba, sai dai na tattara kowa na zuba a gefe, ko abincin da nake yi naki kai mishi.

. Washi gari.
       Tun kafin asuba na fidda kayana, ko Fulani banyiwa sallama, ba na nufi gidansu Aswad wanda muka hadu dashi a hanya, kayana ya saka a cikin mota sannan muka bar garin. Dake shatar motar yayi.

          Karfe ɗaya yayi mana a garin kano, daga nan muka tickets guda biyu ya saya mana na gurin zama, sai jana da hira yake. Amma na kasa sake jiki dashi.

     Har jirgin mu ya tashi jirgin kasa fa,
   .......

     A cikin Daura hankalin kowa bai kawo bana gidan ba sai bayan wani, lokaci da Jamal ya iso nima na ban kai mishi abinci ba. Nan aka shiga dakin nima na aka samu wasikar da na bari 

      Bashi akayi ya fita, amma zuciyar shi zafi take ace zan bar garin bai sani ba, toh ina naje. Dan na raina shi,(🤔☹️😿😒 da kai nake JAMAL)

         Yayi tsaki yafi a kirga sannan ya tattara ya zauna tare da bude takardan.

        𝘐𝘺𝘢 𝘴𝘰𝘯 𝘥𝘢 𝘯𝘢𝘺𝘪 𝘮𝘢𝘬𝘢 𝘬𝘢𝘥𝘢𝘪 𝘺𝘢 𝘪𝘴𝘢 𝘯𝘢𝘺𝘪𝘸𝘢 𝘬𝘢𝘪𝘯𝘢 𝘧𝘢𝘥𝘢, 𝘥𝘢 𝘯𝘢𝘴𝘢𝘯 𝘪𝘯𝘥𝘢 𝘋𝘢𝘯𝘨𝘪𝘯𝘢 𝘴𝘶𝘬𝘦 𝘑𝘢𝘮𝘢𝘭 𝘣𝘢𝘻𝘢𝘯 𝘵𝘢𝘣'𝘢 𝘵𝘴𝘢𝘺𝘢𝘸𝘢 𝘢 𝘬𝘶𝘴𝘢𝘥𝘢 𝘬𝘢𝘪 𝘣𝘢, 𝘈𝘮𝘮𝘢 𝘈𝘭𝘩𝘢𝘮𝘥𝘶𝘭𝘪𝘭𝘭𝘢𝘩𝘩, 𝘥𝘰𝘮𝘪𝘯 𝘬𝘢 𝘬𝘰𝘺𝘢𝘳 𝘥𝘢𝘯𝘪 𝘥𝘢𝘳𝘢𝘴𝘪𝘯 𝘳𝘢𝘺𝘶𝘸𝘢 𝘯𝘢 𝘵𝘢𝘧𝘪 𝘬𝘢𝘫𝘪 𝘢 𝘳𝘢𝘯𝘬𝘢 𝘪𝘥𝘢𝘯 𝘺𝘢𝘳𝘬𝘢 𝘵𝘢 𝘧𝘢𝘥𝘢 𝘴𝘰𝘺𝘢𝘺𝘺𝘢 𝘪𝘳𝘪𝘯𝘵𝘢𝘸𝘢! 𝘠𝘢 𝘻𝘢𝘬𝘢 𝘫𝘪 𝘯𝘢 𝘣𝘢𝘳𝘬𝘢 𝘭𝘢𝘧𝘪𝘺𝘢........

𝘩𝘢𝘳 𝘬𝘶𝘭𝘭𝘶𝘮
        𝘑𝘢𝘯𝘯𝘢𝘳𝘵 𝘑. 𝘑𝘢𝘮𝘢𝘭
       𝘑³
      
             <𝘈𝘮𝘪𝘯𝘶𝘪𝘴𝘢𝘩 𝘯𝘢 𝘬𝘢𝘭𝘶𝘣𝘢𝘭𝘢𝘯𝘤𝘦 𝘬𝘢😎👈🏻 𝘩𝘢𝘬𝘢 𝘠𝘢𝘯 𝘮𝘢𝘻𝘢 𝘴𝘶𝘬𝘦 𝘨𝘢𝘳𝘢 𝘬𝘢𝘯 𝘠𝘢𝘯 𝘔𝘢𝘵𝘢 𝘢𝘴𝘩𝘦.>

           Hawaye ne suka cika mishi idanu, taya haka ta faru. Shi dai yasan ba son Jannart a ranshi Amma yana jin ba dad'i akan abinda yayi mata, iya wahala ta sha akan shi.

      Ga kwanakin da Abban shi ya bashi yana karatuwa.

        ----
   Washi gari.
   Da misalin karfe tara na safe muka isa Lagos, kai tsaye muka sami karamar motar da zata kai mu. har gida.

         Mun isa wajen sha biyu, sakamakon rashin samun hanya da wuri, tunda muka tsaya na fita da gudu, na buga Kofar Mamah ta bude min.

     Fadawa jikinta nayi tare da sake wata irin kuka, tare da rungume ta, ina kuka.

  Shiga dani tayi tare da zaunar dani, cikin sanyin jiki ta kalli Aswad.

      Gyad'a mata kai yayi, daga nan muka gaisa . Nikam kuka ya hanani magantuwa.

    Aswad da Mamah suka tattauna,. Bayan mintoti yace zai koma.

        ..... Haka muka yi ta god'iya.
      
     Tunda na dawo, mama bata min magana ba, ta barni da halin da nake ciki ma ya ishe bata son ta kuma aro damuwar wani ta kara min.

      Amma zuciyata ba dad'i, ko kadan domin ina cikin bakin ciki.
         **
Kwanakin da aka dibawa Jamal ya cika, dan haka hankalinsa yayi masifar tashi ya rasa inda zai cusa kan shi da halin da yake ciki.

          Tun safe yake son zuwa gurin Annan shi tsoro ya hana shi.
       Bayan salla la'asar yaje, ya sami mai martaba zaune rike da Littafin fikhu,

        Sau daya ya d'ago ya kalli Jamal, sannan ya cigaba da abinda yake.

      Tunda ya zauna zuciyarshi take bugawa, kamar zata tsaga kirjinshi. Sai zufa yake kamar ya had'iye kunama.

            "Barka Da hutawa! Abba!"
       Jinjina mishi kai yayi sannan ya cigaba da abinda yake, yana gama abinda yake ya rufe littafin sannnan yace mishi.
  
   "Ya muke ciki?!"
     
   Girgiza kai kawai yai ba tare da yayi magana ba.

       "Toh shikenan! Kaje Ka shirya ranar Juma'a! Za a daura maka Aure da Gimbiya Maryam Adamu Yakubu sarkin! Bauchi. Daga nan zasu tsaya a Zaria za a daura maka da Gimbiya! Zaituna Bintu Ahmad! Sai nan Daurah Akwai Badeeeya Yar gurin Chiroma. Sai Hafsah Aghale yar Sarkin Maraɗi! Allah ya baka ikon rike su da Amana"

         Zufa ke tsatsafo mishi ta ko ina na jikin shi, kanshi a sunkuye nake amma jikin shi wani irin rawa yaƙe, me dauke da dinbun tashin hankali.

          Duk sauran matan basu ga Sarah! Kawar Jannart!

        D'ago kai yayi a karo na biyu ya bude baki zai mgn Abban shi ya daga mishi hannu.
    
   "Na baka dama ko ban baka ba"?! Sai da na baka har sati uku!  Toh meye matsalar?!" Mai martaba ya tambaye shi a dan fusace amma bazaka gane haka ba, saboda yadda yayi maganar yana murmushi.

      "Babu Abba! Nagode da adalcin da kai min! Nagode sosai! Kuma zaka dame ni me biyayya! Insha Allah. Bazaka same a masu bijire maka ba, Nagode sosai!"

      Tausayin Jamal din sai ya cika mishi zuciya har yake ganin kamar ya so kanshi da yawa, amma bai da zabe.

       Da Jamal ya fidda wacce yake so da basu iso zuwa nan ba, Allah ya gani zuciyarshi Jannart ce, dan yafi kwadaitar Halayyar ta, yadda ta iya zama da Jamal din na wasu watannin, haka ba karamin burge shi yayi ba, sannan uwa uba tana kamewa da nutsuwa.

        Hankali da kamala, amma haka Jamal ya shura musu kasa a maganarsa sabida bai san inda ke damun shi ciwo ba. Musamman da yarinyar ta nuna nan duniya bata da wanda take so sai shi.

        Tsuke fuska Mai martaba yayi sannan yace.
"Allah yayi maka albarka!"
   Daga bai kuma ce mishi kome ba, a sanyayye Jamal ya mike, idanun shi cike da kwalla.

    Amma kafiya da taurin kai ya hana shi fahimtar halin da yake ciki, gani yake ai sabida yaki amsar JANNART yasa aka mishi dole, in ba haka ba taya za a mishi auren mata hudu russ kuma rana daya.

Kamar mai martaba yasan shirin Jamal, bai kuma bin takan shi ba,
  Sai da yazo gaishe shi sannan yace mishi.
"Kasan makaho baya sanin ana kallon shi..."

        Gyada kai yayi, cikin nazarta maganar Abban shi, kafin ya d'ago kan shi cikin nutsuwa, idanun shi na iyalin kwalla yace.
"Abba na tuna!"
Murmushi Mai martaba ba yayi, sannan yace.
"Ko kadan ban rike ka ba, amma kasan idan farauta taki maka, kunkuru ma tsare ma harinka yake, sabida baka yiwa tafiyarka shiri na musamman ba"

   Bai ce kome ba, dan bashi da abincewa.
               Haka mai martaba ya gama mishi wanka me kama da jurwaye.

        ---
"Rumanah! Itace matsalar mu, domin bamu isa cutar da yaron nan ba, matukar muka ce zamu yi haka toh tabbas wukar yankar giwa."

               Tana kishingide ta sake murmushi sannan tace.
"Ba girma gare shi ba"

      Jakadiya ta karshe da cewa.
"Sai kaifin Bala'i! Yaron nan yana da, taurin kai, duk abinda ya same shi bai sanya shi ya furta yana son Yarinyar ba, dukda da kuwa ta..."

    D'aga mata hannu Fulani tai, tare da take fuskarta, sannan ta nuna mata hanya dole Jakadiya ta bar cikin zauren, ranta yana kara b'aci. Wato dan an sami damarta shine bari a dinga wulakantata.
Fita tai tana kwafa tare da cikin alwashin sai ta rama abinda aka mata.

         ....
   Zaune yake a gaban Rumanah, ya tallafi fuskar shi da hannun shi duk biyu daga ita har shi an rasa wanda zaiwa wani magana.

          

   Ajiyar zuciya ta sauke sannan tace.
" Bahaushe yayi gaskiya da yace!
Sai wani ya rasa, wani ke samu, toh k'i dinbun Alkhairi dake tattare da yarinyar gashi nan an baka mata hudu lokaci guda.

        Sannan shima Jalaludeen ai ba haka ake rayuwa ba, taya kana matashin ka, ace zayi maka Auren gata, ai bai dace ayi irin wannan abin ba, lokacin sun wuce.

    Dukda ba zai rasa dalilin shi nayi haka ba, amma mata hudu a lokaci guda, ba abu me sauki bane.

          Idan shi yayi haka ne, dan ya horaka ai haka ya isheka ishara, Yarinyar nan tayi maka biyayya, ka hanata sauraron kowa, amma daga baya kace kai nan duniya baka sonta, wallahi baka kyauta ba.
     
  Yau gashi abinda ake gudu ya faru, da baka san waye mahaifinka bane shi yasa kake tunanin zai janye maganar shi, baya saka doka ya karya.

    Kuma baya magana biyu, gaskiyar da ya faɗa itace gaskiya..bai yi Kuskure a hukuncin shi ba, sabida iya gaskiya ya bika da ita.

     Nayi Imani da Allah, da yau kayi hakuri ka fadi JANNART kake so, babu wata macen da zai kara maka ita.

    Domin ya Fika sanin darajar rayuwa, ya kuma Fika sanin darajar soyayya, amma kaki bawa Kowa hadin kai, toh shi kuma a matsayin shi na Uba yayi abinda ya dace ne!

       Domin idan har aka Barka zasu cigaba da cutar da kai, yan matan da aka zab'a maka iyayen su san manyan sarakunan kasar Hausa ne.

    Shi yasa ya amshi tayin su,.kuma yi hakuri kayi mishi biyayya, Allah zai cika maka burin ka, na Alkhairi."

   Gyada mata kai yayi dan maganar da tayi mishi ya shige shi sosai, ya kuma kashe mishi jiki, kuma a take a gurin ya gano Kuskuren shi, dole yaje ga Jannart dama haka ta fuskanci ciwon abinda yake mata, na nuna mata baya sonta, gashi yau za a bashi abinda baya so, yaki Mai martaba yace yana kallon shi da abinda yake shirin aikatawa.

        Lallai masu iya magana sunyi gaskiya da suka ce.
_Tafasar tukunya bata gefe daya bace! Yau gashi ya saki reshi ya kama ganye! Zurfin cikin shi ya ja mishi damuwa_

Waye zai samu ya faɗa mishi gudarin abinda yake ji game da Jannart, waye zai saurari abinda zai ce ko zai nuna akan Jannart.

   Kowa ganin laifin shi ake kamar yayi wani mugun abu, Bayan sun san cewa, haka Allah yayi shi da mugun zurfin ciki.

    Shi bai san ya fadi abinda yake so ba, sai dai aga mishi abu, a kuma ce abin ya dace dashi.

       Lumshe idanunshi yayi cikin damuwa, da yake jin kamar ya masa bindiga da kwalkwalwan shi, gabaki daya baiyi da wata fata akan wannan yanayin da yake ciki.

  .. ina da hannun agogo zai dawo da yayi gyaran ban mamaki, domin zai gyara kusakuran da ya tafka ne.

     Amma ina lokacin ya wuce, rayuwa ta tafi, ya sannan halin mahaifin shi, kaifi daya ne. Idan Yasaka abu a gaban shi babu me dakatar da shi, yau yana zaune ne dan cimma wata muradin zuciyar shi, wannan dalilin ya cigaba da sanya shi nunawa har yau bai samu sauki ba.

     A yau ilimin siyasar da ya karanta bai mishi rana ba, dan gashi nan ya rasata kenan har Abada.

    Tunda tayi fushi ta tafi ko sallama bata yi dashi ba.

      Mik'ewa yayi sannan ya mata sallmaa ya bar gidan.

       Shashin Fulani yaje, ya sameta ana shirya wasu kaya a manyan akwatina, juyawa yayi zai fita tace.
"Yarima! Baka ga abinda ake yi bane, naga baka farin ciki da wannan karammawar?!"

    Jikin shi ne ya kuma sanyi, a ranshi yake tambayar kanshi.
_Waye zai fahimci ainihin abinda nake ji? Waye zai gane ciwon da zuciyata take yi?! Waye zai gane Kuskuren da na aikata! Babu! Babu! Babu_

Wannan itace kurmus, domin Jamal ya faɗa kumar jama'a, dariya Rukayyah tayi sannan tace.
"Kai Umma! Ana nunawa ringigin watane! Ai Yaran da ake so, sune aka basu kyauta na musamman, Shalelen mu, Autan mu, Mata hudu russs! Ai ka more"

             Murmushi yayi wanda yafi kuka ciwo sannan ya juya abinshi ya fita,sun san halin shi kome zasu yi ba zai tab'a magana ba.

   Shi yasa ya musu murmushi, dan itace kawai hanya mafi sauki ya jefa musu shakkar shi.

         Tunda ya fita, bai koma cikin gidan shi ba, da kafa yake takawa har gidan su Aswad! Tunda ya shiga da sallama, ya zauna a gaban Baba Magaji, ya sunkuyar da kanshi.

   "Baba! Ba za a iya dakatar da auren nan bane? Wallahi bazan iya rike mata hudu lokaci guda ba, kuma."

       "Yarima Jamal!" Sai da baba Magaji ya kirashi sau uku, kafin yau me nace maka akan Yarinyar nan Jannart!"

    Shiru yayi kamar ruwa ya cinye shi, ya rasa inda zai cusa kanshi dan tashin hankali, nan ne damar na karshe, indai har Baba Magaji yaki toh wallahi babu wanda zai iya sanya mai Martaba ya sauko daga dokin nak'i.

       "Ka fahimci! Dalilin da yasa Mahaifinka yake son ya Baka damar zab'ar abinda ranka ke so?! Da yau ka amshi Jannart, wallahi babu wanda ya isa dakatar da kai, babu wanda zai maka magana ka kara aure, dan duk wata kyaun hali da tarbiya yarinyar nan ta samu, amma kayi fatali da kaunar da take maka kace kai baka sonta.

   Duk ba laifi bane, amma kasani babu wanda zai iya dakatar da Jalaludeen, duk wanda zaka tunkara, bazai tab'a hana shi ba.

         Naso ka da yarinyar, sabida ita daya ce take da nasibin da zata iya jagoranceka zuwa ga kujeran da Mahaifinka yake dako! Domin ita ce zata yi abu kai tsaye ba tare da wani ya dakatar da ita ba.

  Kasan me yasa kowa yaji babu dad'i, sabida itama son da take maka ba yin kanta bane!  Abinda kowa keson dakatarwa fiye da shekaru dari, yau ka samu. Ka juya mashi baya.
        Ai sakawa a baka, ya fi a rataya. Dake ka dauka kowa zai lallaba alamarin kane yasa ka juya mana baya, kaje Allah ya tabbatar da Alkhairi."

        Kasa tashi yayi, yana zaune. Abin tausayi duk ya rasa inda ke mishi dadi, ya kuma fara kalubalantar kanshi da son Zuciyarshi.

     Yarinyar da tun daga haduwar su ya mutu da sonta yau gashi yana ji yana gani abu biyu sun raba su, Girman kai da Zurfin ciki. Yau ya kuma tausayawa kan shi na zama d'a namiji kwal kamar rai, yau yaji baya kaunar zama a cikin gidan Sarautar.

     Dan itace take iya hana mutum sakewa da yin abinda yake so, ina ma da kome zai kuma dawowa da ya shelantawa Duniya matsayin Jannart a zuciyarshi.

    Da zai iya dakatar da wannan Alakakai din da za a zarga mishi, da yayi wani abin da zai zama tarihi a cikin wannan karnin, dan yasan dole ya shiga cikin kundin tarihin masarautar.

   A wanda yafi kowani Yarima bajinta, amma ina tun ran gini tun ran zane, tun daga samar da halittar shi, aka shata mishi kaddarar shi, dole sai yabi labi zuwa labi, tsani zuwa tsani.

      Kusurwa zuwa kusurwa, dole sai yabi dai-daiton rayuwar shi, toh yaushe zai ga cikaro burin shi, taya zai samu cikar Muradin shi, gaskiya ya tabka wautancit, wanda har duniya ta tashi bazai daina dana sani ba.

Yayi abinda babu wanda zai kuma aikata irin shi, dole ya cigaba da biyayya tare da amsar laifin shi, amma yasan yayi rashin macen da kowa zai so kilacewa a gidan shi.....

"Jannatul Fidausiiiiiiiii!" Ya ambaci sunana.

     Ina shiga d'akina, na fashe da kuka, tare da dafe goshina.

             Goge fuskana nayi tare da murza kwalli a idanuna,  haka kawai nake jin na cab'a ado, dan na kuma bawa zuciyar Samim daman amayar da goyan sonda yake min.

     Me narke zuciyar wacce akayiwa,  kallon shi nayi tare da sake murmushi nace.
"Kayi hakuri! Na b'ata maka lokaci"

       Fad'adda murmushin sa yayi cikin nutsuwa yace.
"Ai duk lokutana, da na yanzun da kuma na baya sadaukarwa kice, zamu iya tafiya Gimbiya Janny"

            Leka kitchen nayi, na ganshi rike da kofin ruwa, mama na wanke namar da zata yi mita dashi, Aliyah da Khalid, tare da Muhsina sun tafi islamiyya.

    A hankali na shiga kitchen ɗin, na je na rungume bayanta, nace.
"Uwata me share min kuka na, zamu fita da Mim!"

      "Allah yayi miki albarka!"
"Ni kuma ya kwashe min albarkan ko?! Wallahi kika sake kika fita a gidan nan sai na lalata miki, zuciya."

    Sake mama nayi ina kallonta, idanuna cike da kwalla.
             
   "Jeki Allah ya tsare! Karku jima?!"
      Tace min,
   Tsare hanyar yayi, d'ago idanu nayi cikin nashi.

"Kace me? Bazan fita ba! Matsa min tunda sauran kimarka, idan ba haka ba, zan baka mamaki"

        Nad'e hannun rigarshi ya fara, yana cewa.
"Bani mamaki! Mara kunya, kuma sai kin gaya min meye alakarki da Aswad, dama wancan mayyen ba yau take nuna zallamar shi ba, shi kuma shugaban munafukan daura.

    Zanyi maganin shi, sai kin gaya min meye alaƙar ki da Aswad."
     
Lallai Kawu na takalata da rigima, Mama kuma da gaya taki magana.
       
Kallon agogon hannuna nayi, sannan na saka kai zan rab'a gefenshi na wuce ya koma ya tsaya, tare da zuba min ido. Da suke cike da zallar masifa.

       Murmushi nayi sannan nace.
"Kawuna! Sauri muke, kuma bana son Mim yayi fushi idan na dawo zan duba amsar da ta dace da tambayarka"

      Na wucce abuna, kallon Raihanah yayi, cikin bakin ciki mara iyaka.

          Gintse dariyar tayi sannan tace.
"Toh, yanzun Jamal meye matsalar ka da Jannart! Aure zaka yi, sannan karewa da kawarta, sannan yanzun ka....."

       Had'iye sauran maganar tayi sakamakon ido biyu da muka yi da ita.
               Bakina na rawa nace.
"Aure zai yi?!"
   Dan iya abinda naji kenan! Murmushi nayi me ciwo, sannan na juya zan bar kitchen ɗin yace.
"Fidausiiiiiiiii!"
   
Cak na tsaya, ya tako gabana yana kallon fuskana, Sannan ya d'ago hannuna, Yasaka a kirjin shi.

      "Yau kimanin sati biyu zuwa uku! Barci me kyau ya gaggara ni! Fidausih saurari sakon zuciyata."

"Toh Allah ya ba da zaman lafiya, da zuri'ar na gari. Nagode da kusakuran da nayi abaya."

Kwana biyu kenan! Kafin ya fito na gama kome na, har na shige na kama gabana, gefe guda kuwa na hakura zan amshi tayin Samim, bani da zab'in da ya wuce amsar auren shi.

       Zura min ido yayi, yana sauke ajiyar zuciya, nazo na zauna kusada shi, ina kallon shi.

Yadda ya kafe ni da ido ya sani, sunkuyar da kaina. Ina wasa da bazar hijab dina, tasowa yayi ya dawo gabana.

        "Yau kwana uku kenan! Rabona da nasaka ki a idanu, inda kike zuwa karatu ma, suna niman ki."

"Bani jin dadi ne!"

             Murmushi yayi sannan ya gyara zaman shi yace.
"Toh ni dai don Allah, ki taimaka ki amince min na kai ki gidana, ku haɗu ku biyu da Madam, kuyita nuna min soyayya."

    Murmushi nayi, ina kallon shi kunya ce ta kamani, na sake sunkuyar da kaina nace.
"Zaka ji amsa a gurin Mama,"

        Kamar ya fasa ihu, dan farin ciki.
"Zaki aure ni?"

         Kawai ganin shi muka yi a tsakiyar mu, yana huci.
"Bazata aure ka ba! Algungunmi! Annamimi, wallahi zan sauke maka habb'a.". Ya shiga ƙoƙarin dambe.

   Tsakiyar su na shiga, tare da kura mishi ido.
"Malam auren ka zaka yi!"

"Mata hudu ma kuwa, wannan satin. Abin bakin cikin har da Yar Sarkin Bauchi! Maryam Adamu Yakubu." inji Samim

         Wani irin kaina yayi kamar an cika min iska, dumm nake ji.
     Bakina na rawa, na shiga kallon shi, jikina kamar mazari, hantar cikina kad'awa yake kaman wacce aka sanya ni cikin ruwan k'ak'ara.

       Juyawa nayi zuwa kitchen na dauko wuka.

"Ke!!! Jannatul karki fara haka, Wallahi...."
