       "Kaje bana sonka! Na kuma cewa bana sonka! Kawata zaka aura, na rantse da jinin da aka zubda sai na fanshi abinda kai min, na rantse sai kayi jinyar so! Na fada maka daga yau kayi rashi kuma ka rasa ta."

     Abinda nake fada kenan, ina kuma rubutawa, a juye. Kuka nake, kamar raina zai fita daga gangan jikina, ya kalli Samim cikin fusata ya makure shi kamar zai aika shi lahira.

           "Wani irin Iskanci ne wannan abinda ka aikata, kai jahilin ina ne? Kaga irin haukar da ka aikata abinda ka janyo min ko."

             Kamar wasa ni ina yaki, da yanayin da nake shirin shiga, ashe su har sun kacame da bawa hammata iska, wannan zafin rai na Kawu na bani haushi, tsabar rashin adalci, ya dake ni ya hanani kuka.

    Jin karar fashewar flower vasses yasa Mama tafito, ta sami kartin maza na dambe, da sauri tazo ta rabasu dakyar.

    Tare da shiga tsakiyar su, kowa kaina suka yi, sakamakon yadda na koma wata irin lokaci guda fatana ya koma kore, idanuna sunyi wani irin kore, Kamar idanun mage.

     Da sauri suka tawo kaina, nan ma suka kuma kacamewa da Bala'i, ran Mamah ya b'aci duk ta buga musu, tsawa tace.
"Na rantse da Allah! Dukkanku zaku iya rasata, jahilan banza. Dubi halin da take ciki, amma tsabar kafirin zuciyarku zaku tsaya haukar banza, kuma ku bar min gidan mijina.

      Tunda babu gumin ku a cikin gidan, marasa hankali da mutunci, kai kuma Jamal ka bar min garin nan ko kuma na tura da sakon ga Abba yasan abinda kake aikatawa, kasan kana sonta kake bata wahala sai da lokaci ya kure uban me zata maka? Bayan gaya mata da kayi baka kaunarta!

    Wallahi baka gigice ba, sai an daura maka sassari ya'yan manyan, masu asali nan ne zaka gane saken da kayi.

     Zaka gane ba kowacce mace ake wulakantawa ba, dan bata da gata! Baka san wacce kaddarar ce ya kawo kata ba, Amma ka zauna ma wulakantata, son ranka.......

     Na gaji......

Lumshe idanun shi yayi, bakin ciki kamar ta kashe shi,  a take wata zuciyar tace mishi.

_Bar ganin laifin kanka! Ai babban mai laifin ne Aswad! Ban da bakin munafunci me ya sani?! Ince yana ganin lokacin da kake kusantar Sarah me yasa bai hanaka ba, burin shi ka rasa Jannart shi ya sameta_


Wani irin bakin cikin ne, ya turnuke shi, duk baya hango laifin shi, na wulakanta Jannart. A'a laifin Aswad yake gani, har da yin tsaki, har ji yaje me yasa bai shararawa Aswad din mari ba,

          Wannan abin da me yayi kama, ace amininka shi yake cin amanar ka, wallahi ASWAD ya gama dashi, yarinyar da yake burin ajiyewa a matar shi. Itace yake so, tabbas sai ya wulwulawa ASWAD tijara dan kar ya kuma cin amanar ruhi, kamar yadda yayi mishin nan.

      Kwafa yayi yana niman ajin da zai saka Shugaban Munafukan Daura wato Aswad, dan sunar da yafi dacewa dashi kenan, kai shifa yana zargin da saka bakin Aswad a zuga mai martaba.

   Can wata Shashi na zuciyar shi ta kwaɓe shi, amma ya b'ata rai kamar a zahiri aka kwaɓe shi.

        Shi yanzun tashin hankalin shi daya ne, Allah yasa Jannart bata ji wannan rungutsimen da aka hada mishi ba, domin.

Bayan Fitar Aswad daga gidan shi, shashin bayi ya tafi inda yaje ya sami Rumana, yana bata labarin abinda ya faru, murmushi tayi me cike da tausayin Jamal, sannan tace.
"Zurfin cikin shi yayi yawa! Kuma ina gaya maka, idan na JANNART ba, babu macen da zata iya da halin shi, ka ajiye wannan maganar ranka,

           Itama sai ta wahala, kafin tasan meye yake bukata, Duk macen da zata zauna dashi, zata zauna ne sabida abu uku, sabida kyanshi, mulkin da wata rana zai amsa, sai dan dukiyar da yake dashi.

         Dan haka zata zauna dashi, amma ita Jannart kaunar shi take, da duk zuciyarta, kuma bazata iya rabuwa dashi ba, dan koda tayi fushi zata dawo gare shi.

           Wanda kowa sai ya fahimci ya faɗa soyayyar wata Ƴa mace.

             Dan bai kyauta mata ba, sam bai yi aiki da ilimi ba."

"Umma! Matsalar shi da yake kama dashi, rashin soyayyar uwa ce! Da ace Umma Fulani ta jashi a jiki, ba makawa da yau yasan fa'idar SO! Amma babu wannan soyayyar, daga kula da Mai martaba, sai karatun, yarinyar ce kawai tayi nasarar, tsayawa a ranshi.

       . Umma wallahi Jamal yana kaunar Jannart, girman kai ne kawai da shegen zurfin cikin dake samun shi, dan ko yanzun da na fito gidan shi, Umma har cakume wuyar rigana yayi."

              "Ai yayi maka da sauki, matuƙar ya dawo kaga bai sami nasara ba, ka cigaba da tunzura shi, haka zai saka shi ya fadi yana sonta, dan nima yarinyar tayi min"

"Umma! Wallahi toshe ni zai yi, kawai, zan cigaba da tsokanar shi daga nesa"

      Kai Jama'a, zurfin cikin Jamal bai yi ba."

"Umma! Jamal zai iya kwana ya wuni sannu sai ya gadama ya amsa, kuma idan zaki shekara kina magana, idan bai yi niyya ba, sai ya lumshe idanunshi. Wai ma karki ce yana jinki.

       Jannart tasha wahala dashi, sosai a wannan jinyar da yayi, ga shi lokacin ya koma mafadaci kamar ita ta sanya mishi ciwon.

        Dakyar yarinyar nan ta sha a hannun shi, dake bai da kirki.

      Kullum sai munyi fada, kuma bazai fasa ba, dan yasha gaya mata. Shi baya kaunarta bayan sonta, amma da zaran yaga Inuwar wani a tare da ita, zai diririce ya kama dambe! Zaki rantse dama can dan dambe ne"

          Murmushi tayi tare da jan hancinta, tace.
"Zai daina! Ka zuba ido kaga yadda kome zai faru bata na gurin Raihanah bane,  ai sai ya girbi abinda yayi mata, mara kirki"

Washi gari.
   Karfe shida na safe, jirgin su ya iso Lagos, a hankali ya fito da jakar shi, yana tafiya a sanyayye dan ji yaƙe bashi da wata kuzari.

"Ina ganin girman ka! Karka yi sake ya zube, ka bar min shi dan shine mutum na farko da ya fara sona! Sai Asw"

   "Don Allah! Muje Samim, muna b'ata lokaci." 
Na fada a hasale, dan na lura renin hankalin shi yayi yawa.

      "Yawwa! Muje Gimbiya ta" ya faɗa cikin niman Tsokana.

   Amsar jakar kafad'ana yayi na sake mishi, dan tun a cikin gidan yake son amsa naki.

        Gabaki daya Jamal yaji duniyar tayi mishi, juyin waina. Dan kamar ya zuba hannu a tsakar kanshi ya zabga ihu. Kasa hakuri yayi cikin b'acin rai yace.

"Wallahi kika kuskura kika bar gidan nan kin gama zaman garin nan kenan! Idan na isa dake ki dawo...."

      "Toh ishashe! Yarima Jamal, ke Jannart kuyi tafiyarku! Ai nasan zaka zo, mara kirki maza tafi abinki. Kuma wallahi bazaka kuma mana wannan ikon ba, kasan bazaka iya jurewa ba, wani munafukine ya hanaka amsar tayinta."

Rayuwarta take, kada kayi tunanin dai kaine gatan ta, har kasami damar juyata son ranka, toh bazan dauka ba, dan kana farawa zan gaya mata gaskiyar maganar Kawarta zaka aura, tun wuri ka juya ka koma inda kake da masoyan ka.

       Itama kuma kabar mana ita, tana da masoya ga Samim da Aswad."

      "Yanzun Raihanah dake ake cin amanata, dake ake hada baki ake cutar da ruhina, toh wallahi dauketa zanyi naga karshen munafukai, tunda kin zab'i bare akan dan uwanki, shakikinki.

    Cikinku daya, yau kika nuna mishi bakin cikinki akan abincin ruhzin shi, Allah yau zata bar garin nan tunda ai ni na kawo miki ita."

          Ya yanke alaƙar shi da Raihanah, dan tayi mishi karan tsaye a cikin rayuwarshi.

 Bata kula shi ba, tayi wucewarta kitchen, tana saka ranta sai dai ya mutu, domin Jannart aure zata yi.

 Bata kai aya ba, ya zabga mata harara, tunda ta ajiye abincin yaji kamshin girkin na Jannart ne, amma taurin kai da zuciyar shi, bai ji ya ci abincin ba.

          Kuma abin dariya, yunwa ce take kuka a cikin shi, domin ana ajiye abincin, ya shaki kamshi, cikin shi tayi kukan yunwa.
            Tashi Raihanah tayi tana matse dariyar ta, domin taga yadda yake muzurai, kamar an shake shi, kuma yunwa ce ke karta yaki a cikin shi.

                Tana barin falon, ya dauki Flast, din tea ya siyaya a cikin cup, kamshin kayan yayi ya daki hancina shi, kumshe idanun shi yayi.

    Tare da sauke wani irin ajiyer zuciya, wacce ta sauya mishi duk wata yanayi a cikin lokaci ƙalilan.

           A hankali ya dauki ruwan tea din Yana kurba, tare da lumshe idanunshi, yana tuna wasu shudadd'en alamari da suka wuce, a lokacin jinyar shi, da ta tab'a dafa mishi irin wannan tea din, kin sha yayi wai zata kashe shi ta cika barkonon da citta, yana jin shashekar kukarta amma bai sanya shi jin ko dar ya daina mata cin fuska ba.

     Dan a ranar ya gaya mata, magana har da ce mata.
"Ke da baki da asali kike tunanin soyayya dani! Ke Ni bana kaunarki, koda idanuna balle yanzun da nake ciki makanta"

   Ji yaƙe kamar yanzun yake gaya mata maganar, bai tab'a sanin darajar rayuwa ba, sai yau me yasa yake raye ne ma? Kwallar da yake dannewa tun jiya ta zubo cikin ruwan shayin, ashe haka take ji, lokacin da ya gaya mata baya kaunar ta.

   Yau gashi da bakinta tace a duniya maza biyu ne, suka kauna ce ta, shi kuma fa? Ina zata saka kaunar shi, ina zata ajiye begen da take mishi a da? Shin da gaske ta daina kaunar shi.

    Haka kawai ya fita yawo, ko zai samu sauƙin ciwon da zuciyar shi take.

            Duk wanda yaga kayan jikin shi, yasan mutum ne mai daraja, amma kuncin da yake bisa fuskar shi kawai take sanyawa a kalle shi, haka ya gama yawo. Tun sha daya, bai dawo gidan ba sai karfe biyu.

Tunda na ganshi, zuciyata tayi wani irin bugawa,  jinin jikina ya tsinke, na kasa katabus. Wani irin mahaukaciyar kewar shi wanda yake hade da son shi ne, suka shiga Zarya a cikin jikina.

     Dan haka koda ya buga mana tsawa, na tsorata. Amma tunawa da irin abinda yayi min ya sani jin na bana kauanr ganin shi, bana son magana ta had'a mu sai dole.

         Amsar da na bashi ba ita bace a raina, hakuri naso na bashi, amma kawai naji hakan ya so min kan harshena.

                   Dan haka da naga zai iya takura min nabi Samim Mika tafi, tunda na shiga motar Samim, nayi shiru.

    Kallon yayi sannan yace min.
"Janny! Ya dai"
Juya idanuna nai akanshi, sannan nace.
"Me ka gani?!
 
"A'a kawai naga sauyawa ce lokaci guda! Da fatan ba zuwan Jamal bane ya sauya min ke ba?"

      Wani karamin murmushi nayi, ina kallon gefena sannan nace.
"Yana raye! Baya raye! Yana nan baya nan, duk bazai hanani abinda nake so ba, kuma ba zai tab'a sauya min Alkiblana ba, kawai ka manta da akwai wata abu a tsakanina dashi, mu fuskanci rayuwar da zamu gina namu"

      Tun bayan dawowata daga daura, Samim ya kuma dawowa rayuwata, da kwarin gwiwan zai zauna dani, dukda na gaya mishi wacece ni, Amma bai sanya shi juya bayan shi ba, kuma na sake samun goyan baya daga Mama.

        Dam haka na cire Kawu a samar zuciyata, na dawo dashi kasar zuciyata, na rufe na sanya kwad'o da mukuli na garkame shi, sai dai kashe yau ganin shi ya kuma buɗe magarkaman zuciyata ya fito, ya koma mazaunin shi na da.

              Amma wannan karon babu daukar reni.
      ---

"Janny! Ko zamu dan fita ne?!"

   Da wutsiyar ido na kalli yadda yayi futu-futu, take na ga sauyi a fuskan shi, murmushi nayi nacewa.
"Toh! Mim ina zuwa!"

        Tashi nayi zan wuce, ya fincikoni, kamar zai jefani cikin kirjinshi.

       Kallon cikin idanuna yayi, jikin shi na rawa.

   Janye rikon da yayi min nayi, tare da sake yar murmushi kasa kasa, nace.

     Wanda zan gamsu da rayuwata na sami abokin rayuwata, ba kai da baka san yakana da kawaici ba, zaka biya abinda kayi min, dan naga zahirin sakamako a cikin idanun ka, Kawu! Kasan yadda naji lokacin da kace min baka kaunata?!"

"Innalillahi wa'inna ilaihi rajioun!"

"Jannatul Fidausiiiiiiiii!" Ya ambaci sunana.

     Ina shiga d'akina, na fashe da kuka, tare da dafe goshina.

              Jamal ya cuci rayuwata, ya san na gama mutuwa akan kaunar shi, sai da yaja sanadin da na tsane shi.

             Goge fuskana nayi tare da murza kwalli a idanuna,  haka kawai nake jin na cab'a ado, dan na kuma bawa zuciyar Samim daman amayar da goyan sonda yake min.

       Fad'adda murmushin sa yayi cikin nutsuwa yace.

            Leka kitchen nayi, na ganshi rike da kofin ruwa, mama na wanke namar da zata yi mita dashi, Aliyah da Khalid, tare da Muhsina sun tafi islamiyya.

    A hankali na shiga kitchen ɗin, na je na rungume bayanta, nace.
"Uwata me share min kuka na, zamu fita da Mim!"

"Ni kuma ya kwashe min albarkan ko?! Wallahi kika sake kika fita a gidan nan sai na lalata miki, zuciya."

"Kawuna! Sauri muke, kuma bana son Mim yayi fushi idan na dawo zan duba amsar da ta dace da tambayarka"

               Bakina na rawa nace.
"Aure zai yi?!"
   Dan iya abinda naji kenan! Murmushi nayi me ciwo, sannan na juya zan bar kitchen ɗin yace.
"Fidausiiiiiiiii!"
   
Cak na tsaya, ya tako gabana yana kallon fuskana, Sannan ya d'ago hannuna, Yasaka a kirjin shi.

"Jamal! Kayi hakuri, bani da ikon dakatar da Alkhairin dake cikin auren nan."

   Ajiyar zuciya yake saukowa sabida tashin hankali da bugun da zuciyar shi take, a hankali ya mike yana tangaɗi kamar zai fadi kasa. 

     Juyawa yayi ya kalli Abba sannan ya saki murmushi, yace.
"Zan maka biyayya! Insha Allah zaka same ni a me biyayya, Amma Abba!"

     Yana gama fad'ar haka ya juya tare da barin gidan Mai martaba,.
Murmushi me cike da tausayi yabi gudan dan bashi, shi yasa Manzon Allah ya hanemu tsannanta k'iyayya, ya kuma hanemu da tsannanta Soyayya.

        *(Na kuma Fada Kwarkwarah! Bakandamiya tace! Ina kuma godewa Allah damar da ya bani na rubuta Hikayata! Alhamdulillah! The first online book wanda ko a littafin da ake bugawa a kasuwa babu Sunan Irinshi! Zuwairah! Zainab wowoh, sunyi alfahari da labarin su, sai nice bazanyi ba! Duk wacce taje ta zage ni, ita da Allah 🤷🏽‍♂️ na fada Labarai na ba irin na marubuta bane! Da kana fara karantawa zaka ce Book ɗin nan irin wani book ne!)*

            
                   Tunda ya bar shashin mai martaba, ya shiga gidan shi, ya samu an kwashe kayan gidan babu kome, niman gefe yayi ya zauna tare da cusa kanshi tsakanin cinyoyin shi. Ya dinga sake ajiyar zuciya.

           Zuciyarshi tana kuma gaya mishi iya daga gobe ne fa. Zai zama dole ya zauna da mata huɗu.

            --
Mun iso lafiya, tunda muka shigo gidan na makale a d'akin ko fita banyi ba, amma zuciyata tana kwadayin fita zuwa shashin bayi.

       Ina zaune ina jin hirar da ake a falo wanda baki dayan shi, magana ake cusawa Mama, tashi nayi na fito dan tunda tashigo tayi sallah magarib. Naga akwai damuwa a fuskar ta.

         Sanye nake da doguwar riga me tsantsi, na yane kaina da dan kwalin gyalen, nace.
"Mama bari naje na dawo!"

          Gyada min kai tayi, sannan na fita.
               Juyawa tayi ta shiga magana cikin b'acin rai tace.
"Dama naƙi amsa muku ne, sabida ita! Dan bana son tasan ina rayuwa da dabbobi irinku, na rasa fahimtar meye muka tsare muku ni da Jamal.

  Kuka dauki wutar kiyayya kuka aza mana, Toh sai Meye idan mai martaba ya nima mishi auren yaran Manyan mutane.

   Wallahi kuji tsoron Allah, kome aka yi da jaki sai ya ci kara."

      Tana gama fad'ar haka ta bar musu falon.

"Eh! Ai dole kiyi magana, tunda gashi kome ku ake fifitawa, tunda mutane basu gani ba toh wallahi sai munyi magana."

     Haka ta barsu da halin su.
      --
Ina tafiya, kawai naji an kira wani suna a can gefe me yawaltan duhu.

   Da sauri na bar gurin, naji an kuma kiran.
"Zirya!"
      Kara saurina nayi, har ina tuntube.
           Ina kallon inda ake kirana, gummm naji nayi karo da abu a gabana, wani irin tsoro ya kamani, lokaci guda turaren shi ya sani sake ajiyar zuciya, sannan na rab'a gefen shi.

   Rike hannuna yayi cikin damuwa yace.
"Fidausih! Na kuma dawowa gareki"

       Murmushi nayi sannan nace.
"Kawuna kenan! Ai na gaya maka, ni bana son ka. Bana kaunarka!"

     "Anya daga zuciyar ki, kike wannan maganar ko kuma daga harshen ki?!"
     D'ago kaina nayi domin na bashi amsa, sai naga bazan iya ba, sabida ya cika min ido.

Rab'a gefen shi nayi zan wuce ya riko hannuna, cikin damuwa yace.
"Karki yarda a rabamu?!"
   
Murmushi nayi, tare da zare hannuna daga cikin nashi nace.
"Rabamu ai kai ka raba kome! Kai ne kayi abinda ya raba duniyoyin mu guda biyu.

        Ban tab'a ganin wanda ya rayu da bautawa kanshi kamar kai ba, Dan haka kawuna bana ji. Zan iya dawowa qarqashin ikon ka, ka barni naje nayi rayuwa tankar Sarauniya."

                 Rike gyale na yayi, cikin dimauta, ya rasa inda zai fara bani hakuri.
"Toh waye yace miki bazaki rayu kamar sarauniya ba? Waye ya gaya miki na raba duniyoyin mu, Jannart! Me yasa ba zaki fahimci halin da nake ciki ba! Yau ce kawai nake da ita, dan na sake kwanakin sun kwace min ba tare da masaniyana  ba.

       Kece saka raina na ƙarshe, idan ban samu damar daga gare ki ba, har Abada na rasa."

       Juyawa nayi na dawo gare shi, ina kallon fuskarshi. Murya na ya rawa.
"Aminiyata! Ta sadaukar da Rayuwarta sabida Ni! Ta kuma sadaukar da Farin cikin ta sabida ni.
     Taya zan shiga tsakanin Soyayyarku! Koda baka sonta ita tana sonka! Koda baka kaunarta ita tana kaunarka."

    Shiru nayi ina sauke wata irin kuka me tsuma rai nace.
"Ban san taya zan iya gyara kome ba, dan karya fahimci alaƙar mu da kai, Amma dan girman Allah karka nuna mata mun tab'a samun saba'in soyayya!

   Tayi min kome! Tabani aron lafiyarta! Ta kuma bani aron lokacin ta na shekara biyar.

       Ana amsar kome domin radin kai, amma ana iya hakura da halas domin kunya, Jamal ka manta dani, na barwa Sarah kai har duniya ta tashi. Bana son ta fahimci na tab'a furta maka kalma mai tsada, da wasu suke jiran na gaya musu.

            Nasan bazan iya daina sonka ba, dan a jini na yake yawo. Kai ma kayi ƙoƙarin ka zauna da matanka lafiya"

    Daga haka na juya na bar shi a gurin, dan komawa nayi gurinsu Mama, tunda na shiga daki na wuce ban daki.
         Kuka nake kamar idanuna zai fita, nayi kuka har na godewa Allah sannan na wanke fuskana na dawo dakin.

        Tunda na zauna nake taryo irin wahalar da nasha tun daga tasowata da mahaifiya ta, har zuwa rasuwar ta.

         Daukar shi a matsayi wanda ya taimake ni, har zuwa wanda ya bani damar zama wani abu a rayuwata.

            .....
Bayan tafiyata na bar shi a gurin, dakyar ya koma sabon shashin sa da zai zauna da mata huɗu russ.

     Kwana yayi a zaune kamar yadda na kwana a zaune ina kuka.

   Shi kuma kukan zuci, wanda yafi na fili ciwo.
        
       Duk wani bugun agogo dai-dai yake bugawa da zuciyoyin mu, gani muke zuwa gobe mun rasa junar mu.

            Har aka kira Sallah asuba, ina zaune.  Dakyar na tashi na gabatar da sallar.

            Sannan wani irin Barci yayi gaba dani.

            Tunda ya fito masalaci ya nufi gidan mai martaba ya same shi zaune, yana azkar.

  Zama yayi  a gefen mai martaba wani irin zazzaɓi yana taso mushi, ya sunkuyar da kanshi.

                 Har mai martaba ya gama, azkra din ya kalle shi kafin yace.
"Karfe goma na safe, fadar Sarkin Maraɗi zasu daura nasu auren. Karfe sha daya fadar Zazzau zasu daura auren, sai nan Katsina wanda za a daura karfe biyu da rabi bayan Sallah Juma'a,

      Sai sarkin kano, karfe uku zasu daura nasu. Ina maka fatan Alkhairi, Allah ya baku zaman lafiya zuri'a dayyiba"

            Wani irin iska ya fesar, me cike da bakin cikin auren da yake ganin da kashe shi akayi ya rantse da Allah babu wanda ya isa tuhumar shi da cin amanar aminta,

    Wayyo Allah na, Abba ya kashe ni, ina zan saka mata huɗu. Ni da bani da tabbacin lafiyata, shine za a hada ni da mata har huɗu bayan an san ina da wanda nake so a rayuwata.

      Duk a zuciyar shi yake wannan mitar.

"Yawwa! Idan ka tashi akwai kayan da zaka saka na zuwa na jima a cikin d'akina, sannan a can shashin da zaku zauna zaka ga sabuwar akwatin ka, duk kayan fitar biki ne! Daga Mijin Raihanah!"

A saba'in ya kalli mai Martaba, idanun shi yana kara kankance su, wato tsanar da Raihanah da mijinta suka mushi har ya kai, su dinka Mishi kayan fitar biki.

Bai gama tunani ba, mai.
Martaba ya katse Mishi tunanin shi da cewa.
"Akwai karamar akwati na hulluna, da Sarkin borno turo maka, yace bazai samu zuwa daura ba, amma zai samu halartar Bauchi, akwai sakon mijin Jamalah, takalma ce ta Alfarmar.

          Sai sako daga Sirikinka, Waziri, rawani da alkyaba, ta zuwa daurin auren Balkisu. Idan akwai abinda kake bukata ka gaya min, domin asan ba a fafe fita ranar tafiya, Mai kamar zuwa, akan aika. Inji yan magana, dan haka ayi hakuri da duk abinda yazo maka shine rabonka, kawo ka ganshi toh kabar shi da zanen kaddarar shi a tafin hannunshi.

         Ina me baka hakuri abisa abubuwan da suka faru, da wanda zasu kuma faru, bani da zab'i ne, shi ya sanya na amshi tayin su, kuma ka baka damar ka zab'i abinda ranka yake so, amma baka zo min da shi ba. Allah ya baku zaman lafiya."

4️⃣2️⃣

Wato idan ya fahimci mai martaba kamar da gayya yake mishi bayani, bayan yasan halin da zuciyar shi take ciki. Shine zai bari ya kuma kular dashi da maganar an kawo mishi gudummawar.

      Yana fita yaga an fara buda algaitu, tare da kad'e kad'e, wani sabon bakin cikin ne ya kuma turnuke shi, kamar ya fashe da kuka.

    Wannan wacce irin rayuwa ake son jefa shi ne? Bayan kowa yasan halin da yake ciki.

          Haka ya wuce, yana shiga ya hangi Aswad da sauran yan uwan shi na family maza.

"Ango ya sha mai tun ba'a fara amarci ba, har ka fara baza mana kura!"
  Bai magana ba yayi wucewar cikin dan ya fahimci fitinar shi suke nima.

       Imran da yake siriki a gurin Jamal dan kanwar shi ce zata auri Jamal, dan gidan waziri.

       Kallon Aswad yayi cikin mamaki yace.
"Meke damun uban yan zurfin ciki!"
          "Kyale banza halin ne ya motsa! Yawwa naji kunce akwai liyaffa maza da mata, shine nima nake son na gayyaci yarinyar da zan aura zata zo gurin liyyafan"

           "Kai mugu ashe dama kana da budurwa?!"
    Cikin d'aga murya yce.
"Baka sani bane? Wai ai sunanta Jannart!"
            
      Tsabar tsokana, haka yayi ta bawa Imran labarin Jannart, dan dagaske daga murya.
      
Daga shi sai yar zanin wanka ya fito duk kumfa a jikinshi yana tsima, shi kanshi bai san zuciyarshi ta zo har wuya ba. Yana fitowa ya cukumo rigar Aswad, yaja shi kamar kayan wanki, ya watsa a kofar fita.

 Yana muzurai, tare da dirka mishi zagi, yana fadin.
"Algungunmi munafuki, Wallahi wutar maciyar amanar ruhi daban take, kaje na Barka da Allah, wallahi Aswad ka cuce ni..

   Duk duniya babu munafuki irinka, zaka bar min gidana ko y'aya."
           "Haba Jamal meye kake yi haka, ai ba girmanka bane."

Juyawa yayi yana kallon Khalid, wanda yayi mishi magana.
         Bai ce kome ba yayi wucewar shi daki ya karasa wankan sannan ya fito. Wata danyen shadda ya saka tare da fitowa fuskar shi buzu buzu.

         "Wallahi! Baka isa ka fita haka ba, haba kai kuwa dubi fuskar ka, duk wannan uban gashin da ka tara, dalla zauna a gyara maka fuska."

    Babu yadda ya iya dasu dole suka gyara mishi, fuska idan Aswad ya kalle shi sai ya fashe da dariya, sabida a halin yanzun an daura auren shi da Balkisu.
Ƙarar busar algaita da kakaki.

      "Allah ya tabbatar da Alkhairi! An daura Auren Yarima Jamal da Gimbiya Balkisu, muna saka ran Karfe biyu za a daura da Gimbiya Maryam ma!"

             ......

          A hankali nake rera kuka, tare da jin ina ma bana raye, yau nice nake ganin bakin ciki.

            Karfe biyu da rabi aka daura auren shi na barci, yayinda aka daura na Gimbiya Yahanasu,. Yar Sarkin kano.

#MY ZAHRAH🌹
Gabaki d'aya Labarin sadaukarwa ce gareki😍
ZAHRAN-ADDA RAMLA

      

4️⃣3️⃣
       Ganin zai zube Aswad da Khalid suka rike shi, cikin wani irin yanayi ya rike hannun Aswad ya mana a kirjin shi, dai dai bugun zuciyar shi.
"Kaji! Kamar wuta ake hura min, ji nake kamar ana watsa min ruwan zafi! Me yasa ba zaku fahimci itace abincin ruhina ba?

    Me yasa ba zaku gane, matsayin ta ba? Me yasa babu wanda ya damu da yakana da nayi ba?!"

      "Jamal kayi shiru kaga an daura maka Aure, kuma kowa najin ka muje cikin gida."

         Kamar karamin yaro haka yake sambatu.
"Kyale ni! Barni Khalid karku min magana, ita na fara so! Ita na fara gani a mace.

     Sonta daban ce a zuciya ta, Aswad ka gayawa Abba zai rasani, matuƙar na wayi gari naga ana zarga mata igiyar wani. Ita tawa ce"

          Cikin gidan shi, aka kai shi. Sannan suka kwantar dashi, fitowa Aswad yayi yaje ya nimo Dr Saheeb, mijin Jamalah shi yazo ya duba shi, sannan ya rubuta sunan alluran da ruwan da za a kara mishi.

    Khalid da Aswad suka tafi sayowa, suna dawowa Aswad yaje ya nimo Raihanah dakyar.

      Sannan ya gaya mata halin da yake ciki, Allah sarki kuka take sosai. Har suka iso dake Khalid ya tawo da kayan, suna tsaye aka saka mishi ruwan.

          Kallon su Dr Saheeb yayi cikin damuwa yace.
"Jamlah tana gaya min twins broda Yana da Zurfin ciki, ban tab'a yarda ba sai bayan fitar ku, yake fadan irin sonda yakewa, Jannart! Don Allah a kiyaye abinda zai na  damun shi, ko tuna mishi wani abu da ya danganci wacce yake so, domin haka zai iya samun zuciyarshi taki samun sukuni, don Allah ku sanya idanu akan al'amarin shi."

     A gaskiya ka mata yayi a bashi abinda yake so, dan in ba haka wata rana zaku ganshi ya fadi ne, sabida yayiwa yarinyar mugun so, zai yi wuya ya daina sonta."

      Kamar ruwa ya cinye su, nan Mama ta fara magana da cewa.
"Ban tab'a ganin mutumin da bai san hakkin so ba, sai Jamal tun al'amarin nan tana yar karama naso a kasheta, sai da aka bashi tsawon sati biyu. Yayi tunani akan Jannart amma ya rufe idanun shi yace baya kaunarta.

        Kana da ita sarafa kowacce irin baiwar da Allah yabaka, lokacin amma soyayyar Gaskiya baka isa dakatar da ita ba.

   Allah yana bamu damar fuskanci matsalar da zata iya zuwa mana, dan mu maganceta, amma shi tashi matsalar boyeta yayi a cikin shi, wannan wacce irin rayuwa ce.

              Jamal shi ba mace ba, amma yana ganin kasawa me ya gaya maka matsalar shi, idan tura bata kai bango ba, bazai tab'a gaya maka matsalar shi ba, dan bai dauki kowa da daraja ba.

    Toh yau ga abinda muke guje mishi, haka zai hakuri itama yarinyar tayi hakuri har Allah ya bata mijinta na gari, tunda shi yayi musu sanadin tsiya."

              Fita tayi daga gidan ranta a b'ace, tazo ta same ni. Nima cikin wani irin hali, ban daki yaje ya dibo ruwa ta yayyafa min, sannan ta cigaba da tofa min Addu'a, ina farkawa na fashe da kuka.
   Shafa baya na, take har nayi shiru sannan tace min.
"Rayuwa da mutuwa, duk na Allah ne, dan haka kiyi hakuri Allah yana sane dake. Bai manta dake ba.

  Ubangiji yana son bayin shi masu tawwakali, ki godewa Allah da ya jarabce ki ta nan, idan da a ce lafiyarki ya tab'a fa.

      Jannat Aurenki da Jamal babu Alkhairi, domin da akwai Alkhairi, duk yadda muka kai domin nuna muna sonku a tare Allah yaso sai yaƙi barin kudirin ku ya cika, toh gashi  yadda yayi ikon shi, toh ki kara mishi godiya dan yana tare dake.

    Wallahi idan kika ga halin  da Jamal ke ciki sai kin zubda kwalla, sabida kece a cikin zuciyarshi, yayi ƙoƙarin fito dake duniya ta sani toh ya kasa.

      D'azun sai da Abba ya gaya min cewa.
"Raihanah! Naso ace Jannart tana cikin matar Jamal, Amma Allah bai nufa ba, wallahi ko yau yace min yana sonta, zan nime Izinin Hamisu, 

     Amma Raihanah dan uwanki yaki gaya min kone, wallahi duk cikin yan mata nafi sonta dashi, amma Alhamdulillah, Allah yasa haka shine mafi alkhairi.

         Kinji abinda yace, kowa nasonki da Jamal, ki cigaba da addu'a Allah zai amsa miki, amma ki daina kuka."

    Taya zan daina kuka, sai dai na hana idanuna zubda kwalla. Na cigaba da kukan zuci.

     Abinda nasan zan iya kenan, dakyat na kyale mama ta koma gurin Yan uwanta.

                ---
Bayan awa biyu, a hankali yake buɗe idanun shi, har ya sauke buɗe idanun shi, wanda suka wadata da eyelashes masu tsayi, gasu zara zara, har wani jeki suke. Kamar an shafa musu mai.

               Tashi yake kokarin yi amma ya kasa, dole ya lumshe idanun shi. Dr Saheeb yace mishi.
"Yarima! Ya jikin naka? Da fatan babu inda yake maka ciwo."

         Girgiza kai yayi sannan ya cigaba da lumshe idanun shi dan baya son magana, tashi Dr Saheeb yayi sannan ya mika wa Aswad takardar maganin, akan a sayowa Jamal.

           Tare suka fita da Aswad daga  gidan, bayan wani lokaci Aswad ya dawo dauke da maganin, yana shigowa yaga masu masu jere daga bauchi, shigewa yayi ya sami Jamlah da Mama, sun saka shi a gaba,

  Suna rarrashin sa, sai wani daure fusaka yake, shi nan an mishi laifi.

            Dakyar suka samu yaci abincin, sannan suka bashi magani yasha, da ruwan.

            Zama shiru yayi yana juya mata hudun da aka zarga mishi igiyar zu, Maryam, Balkisu, Yahanasu, da Nanah.

   Ritsa idanun shi yayi cikin wani irin bakin ci mara misaltawa, ina ma da za a bude kirjin shi, tabbas da hanga yadda sunan Jannart yake rubuce a kirjinshi.

       Da za a cire duk wata tsammanin shi, da wata buri na gidan duniya, zai iya rokon ALLAH ya dawammar da kaunar Jannart a cikin zuciyarshi da jinin jikin shi.

        Tabbas Jannart itace fatar shi, itace irin macen da yake bukata, itace irin macen da yake da yakinin zai iya zuba duk wata gajiyar shi ba tare da ta nuna gajiyawar ta ba.

         Ya karanci littafin da aka yi rubutu akan halittar Y'a mace da da namiji, yayi imani Jannart itace dai-dai dashi dan yasan zata jure duk wata damuwa ko takuri, sabanin sauran matan shi da suka taso, uwa ba kwab'a uba babu tsanani.

Kiciniyar kwace kaina na fara yayinda cikina ya hautsine, kirjina ya shiga lugude. K'asa-k'asa yace min.
"Ki nutsu! Karki yi abinda zai ja miki Magana"

         A tsorace nace mishi.
"Hmm!"
         "Kee! Jannat tashi bude idanunki, innalillahi wa'inna ilaihi rajioun! Jamlah kira min mijinki."

      Girgiza ni yake sosai wanda ya kuma fidda tsoratar dake kan fuskar shi.
      Ganin haka yasanya suka shiga kokwanto ba mamaki ba abinda suke zargi bane!

       Cak ya mai dani samar kujeran dake falon shi ya shiga min fifita. Yayinda na dauke numfashi kamar da gaske.

      Bazan yi mamakin da yadda yace ba, domin kuwa yadda na firgita dole jinina ya haura.

           Bayan fitar Dr Saheeb ne, Jamlah ta rike ni zuwa cikin gidan. Tun a hanya take min fad'a akan abinda suka gani.

Ina ganin su, naji wani mugun tsoro ya kamani, kallona suka yi sannan suka fita. Can jimawa sai ga wasu har da tsofi suka leko. Suka kare min kallo, suka fita.

"Kiyi hakuri!"

"Dama kun shigo ne kuyi min Kasafin yadda zanyi da rayuwata?! Allah sarki toh haka ma yayi, dan ba fasawa zanyi ba."

Da fitina. "

"Da sannu matankadi ke shiga gora" tana gama fad'ar haka ta juya ta bar cikin dakin.

Make min bakina yayi wanda ya sani fashewa da kuka, tare da komawa na zauna, ina shashekar kuka.

            "Baka kyauta ba! Aure kuwa zata yi da izinin Ubangiji, nine nan zan bata wanda take so, mutumin banza" 
   Inji mai Martaba, juyawa yayi yana kallon Abban shi cikin tashin hankali yace.
"Abba! Don Allah karka rabani da ita wallahi itace abincin ruhina, idan aka min haka tankar an kashe ni ne, da sauran kwana na."
     "Ka jima baka mutu ba, idan kai fatalwa ne, zan zuba maka guba sai kaci kowa ya huta, fitinanne mutum kawai, fita kabar min Y'a ta huta da masifarka."

  Kamar zaki yafita fuuuu, gurin liyyafan da bai koma bake nan.

   Hmmm taron da aka tashi da b'acin rai, duk bayanin da akawa Sarah dariya tayi. Tacewa yan uwanta.

"Tsakanin su Babu aure! Kawai yana son tayi karatu ne mai zurfi! Dan shi Kawunta ne, kunga kuwa dan ya ganta da wani saurayi dole ya fusata, kuma koda akwai aure a tsakanin su, ta ina zai sakata?

          Sannan nasan tana da wanda take su Samim, dan haka kawu Jamal bazai damu ba, kawai yana nuna haka ne, dan kawai ta nutsu tayi karatunta."

     Duk yadda suka so, su mata bayani taki kula su, sabida son da take min, babu sharri ko kulata a cikin shi. Kawai abinda tasani a da, yake gaya musu.

         ....
       "Kai bana son tashin hankali, ina ka kai musu yar mutane? "
    Kallon Aswad yayi, cikin fushin da yake niman inda zai sauke yace mishi.
"Nayi abinda ya dace da ita!"

   Ya fada tare da tsare Aswad da ido, murmushi yayi sannan yace mishi.
"Gaskiya shi yasa naga kayan ka na jikinka, kaga Ni ba damuwa nazo dauka ba, ina Janny take?"

       Tashi yayi cikin fada yaci kwalar Aswad,yana huci.
"Kanwar kace? Meye hadinka da Kaunata? Me yasa kake son shiga tsakanin soyayyarmu! Wallahi zan iya kashe ka, sai dai na kare a gidan yari. Ka bar min abinda zuciyata take so! Ka bar min muradin zuciyata, Aswad gaya min nawa zan biya ka bar min JANNART! Ita daya nake so!

    D'aya nake mafarkin samun zuri'a daga gareta, Kaiiii, kasan karni nawa na na shafe ina Jiran Ziryaaaaaa!"

     Jini ne ya shiga diga daga cikin idanun shi, fuskar shi ta koma baki, haka ma kwayar idanun shi ta juye zuwa fari, jikin shi na rawa.

  Ya cigaba da cewa.
"Nayi Kuskuren! Na dawo gareta! Duk wanda ya Kuskuren ya faɗa cikin soyayyar mu, zai mutu! Karka kuskura ka shiga yakin da badakai aka fara ba, karka fara tarar abinda babu ruwanka, idan ka shiga zaka mutu, zaka mutu, 

  Ga duk wanda ya kuskura zai mutu, Zirya tawa ce ni daya, zan kashe kuma bazanyi dana sani ba, karka fara karka shiga."

   Rike kanshi yayi, cikin wani irin kuka sannan ya zube a jikin Aswad, wanda shima kuka yaƙe sosai.....

Me kuke tunanin zai iya faruwa! Shin da gaske Bakin da Zirya tayi mishi ne yake bibiyar shi ko kuma

#MY ZAHRAH🌹
Gabaki d'aya Labarin sadaukarwa ce gareki😍
ZAHRAN-ADDA RAMLA

  _Ina son na kuma tuna muku! Cewa wannan labarin yana tafiya ne kafin shigowar musulinci kasar Hausa! Duk da lokacin da musulinci ya shigo Sarkin Daura shi ya fara amsar musulinci! Kuma idan baku manta ba, nace muku  rike code din shekarun da anyi ta amfani dashi, dan haka labarin yayi tafiyar bazata tun kafin zuwan Musulunci, sannan labarin yana nuna muku yadda aka yi imani da canfe! Da yadda akayi rayuwa da kwarkwarori! Ban tab'a cin karo da a kan labarin KWARKWARAH ba, sai yadda akace haramun ne! Har malam Abubakar Muhmood Gumi yace haramun ne, amma idan muka yi dubi da Mariyatul kid'iya! ita kwarkwaran Manzon Allah ce! Kuma ta haifa mishi Ibrahim! Dan haka sai na ajiye wancan batun haramcin, sosai nayi bincike akan labarina, dan haka ku bini a hankali, duk abinda ya faru daga 1370Ds zuwa 1875Ds, akan kwarkwarah ce,duk abinda ya faru akan samuwar kwarkwarah ce a cikin daular ce! Dan haka abin ya faru shakaru 200yrs da suka gabata! Ban tab'a labarin da yabani ciwon kai kaman wannan ba, dan haka zaku min uzuri sosai, dan matukar na kafe a hanya zan iya ajiye labarin sai farfaɗo! Da wannan nake baku hakurin Bazan cigaba da HABEESHAH ba! Kwarkwarah ce kawai zan cigaba da rubutawa Nagode for Long writerup 🌷🥀🌹_

      
4️⃣5️⃣

Wannan wacce irin ƙaddara ce mara mafari, rungume jaamal yayi cikin matsanancin kuka, sabida tsabar tausayin.

"Wani irin bashi ne da ake son sai kun baya shi, kaunarku me zafi ce mara sarki da algus"

     Kwalla yake zuba mishi sharr, abun gwnini tausayin.
  
              Haka ya d'aga shi, ya kai shi har dakin shi, yana zaune dashi har aka kira sallar farko, sai lokacin ya farka da salati, 

A hankali ya mike tare da kallon Aswad da yake zaune kan dan drowar gadon.

      "Aswad! Ina yarinyar nan take? Kira min ita, kuma ka tabbatar Khalil ya bar garin nan, dan babu wani abinda zanyi bikin ya kare."

      "Don Allah! Ka sawa zuciyarka hakuri ka rungume matanka, ka bar rayuwar Jannyh ta huta, ina dalili zaka sanya yarinyar mutane a gaba da fitina.
Wa take dashi da zai tsaya mata, dan bata da galihu ai bata cancanci kyautar haka daga gare ka ba. Don Allah ka barta tayi rayuwarta tunda Allah bai hada ku a ma'aurata ba"

           Duk yadda naso yayi magana haka ya hakura, tare da zubawa sarautar Allah ido.

Bayan sallar asuba, ne yaki shiga cikin gidan, ya koma dakin shi.

     Ni kuwa a shashin Abba na kwana ina kuka, shi kanshi ya ganin laifin kanshi a raba masoyan asali.

       Sai da aka fito Sallah Aswad yazo ya rakani cikin gidan, a nan naga irin kallon zargin da ake min, kasancewar lokacin da Kawu ya ture ni na buge gwiwata.
         
   Nan aka kuma ririta alamarin ya zama babban abu, kafin kace kwabo labarin ya tarwatsa cikin gidan da baki, ai Jamal ya keta min alfarma, shi yasa yayita ihu a gurin bikin shi, ya tsallake matan shi.

        Wannan dalilin yasa Yaya Shatu da Yaya Rukayyah, suka rufe ni da duka, duk yadda Mama da Jamlah suka so amsata. 

      Umma Fulani tayi musu baki idan wani ya Kuskura ya kawo min dauki.

            Cikin gigicewa, nake kiran Mama jikina na rawa,  Muhsina ce ta fita da gudu taje ta kirawo, Kawu wanda yake kwance a kujera ta shiga tana tsalle tace.
"Kawu! Ana duka Adda Janny, da bulala, kazo tana kuka."

    Shi kansa bai san ya buga tsalle ba, sai da ya isa bakin kofar shi, ai ko takalmi bai saka ba, ya nufi cikin gidan, inda yake jin ihuna,  cikin wani irin sarsarfa ya isa inda suke dukana, yayi maza ya min rumfa tare da rungume ni.

  Tsayawa suka yi da dukar, d'ago fuskana yayi, tare da kallon cikin idanuna yace.
"Kiyi hakuri!"
          "Fulani zoki ga abinda nake gaya miki, Kinga ya tabbatar mana jiya ta kwana a dakin shi, shine har da rungutsimen ta."

      "Rukayyahhhhh"
    Ya daka mata wani mugun tsawa, ya juya gareta.

"Wallahi! Ban da matsayin Yayata ba, toh wallahi sai na karya miki habb'arki."
   
Nuna min hanyar da zai kaini dakin mu yayi, sannan ya taka har gabanta yana fadin.
"Yarinyar da Nake mutuwar SO ce zan kwanta da ita! Aiko a gidan karuwai na ganta bazan tab'a kwanciya ita ba.

       Bari in gaya miki, tun babu komai a kirjinta, na bata masauki zuciyata.

      Ke har kin isa ki daketa na kyaleki, kinsan girman darajar ta kuwa, toh wallahi zaki biya abinda kuka aikata, dan sai kunji a jikinku!"

   Yana gama fad'ar haka ya juya tare da barin su a gurin, bai kai bakin kofa ba, su Yaya Rukayyah da Shatu suka fara ihu me mugun shiga jiki.

      "Ba dukar ta ba, kai ko banza kallo kuka mata sai kun biya abinda muka aikata"

         Fita yayi abin shi, sai da suka ci Uban su da duka, sannan aka kyale su.

          Tunda na shiga na kwanta ina kuka, dole na fita a cikin wannan ahalin.

        Dakyar na shiga ban daki nayi wanka da alola nazo na gabatar da sallar. A gurin na kwanta, sabida tsamin da jikina yayi.

          Nayi barci sosai, har kusan sha biyu, ji nayi ana shafa fuskana, a hankali na bude idona murmushi take dokawa kawai kamar an mata babbar kyauta.

"Sarah!" 
Hannun tasaka tare da rufe min baki, tana murmushi tace.
"Toh gaya musu nazo gurinki! Aminiya baki da kirki ko zuwa Bauchi baki yi ba, balle ki san halin da nake ciki ba."

       Ai kuwa halinta magana a gurinta ba sabon abu bane, kawai ta shiga zuba min labari, kawai dake ta san halina ba wani abu bane na iya Gwale ta,

     Cikin karamar murya tace.
"Jannart baki ce min kome ba, kinsan sai da nayi wata biyar, bana zaman gida. Dan Abba ya sanya ni a wani makaranta. Sai kawai ya bijiro min da maganar auren Kawu."

  Nan ta rufe idanunta, tana murmushi. Sannan ta cigaba da cewa.
"Yana gaya min yayi min miji, Yarimar Daura, kuma wanda ake shirin na dashi, sarkin Matasa. Ke shi din hasken matasan daura ne, dan ya tafi da duniyata.

      Ban yi kasa a gwiwa ba, nace na amince. Sai dai Abbana ya gaya mishi zan cigaba da karatu,.  Dan haka da kanshi ya gayawa Abba cewa shima zai tafi.

India karatun, kuma kinsan meye kuma abin farin cikin, har dake zamu tafi tare gani ga ke, ina farin cikin haka, Aminiya ga kayan da na dinka miki wanda zamu yi fitar biki, don Allah kace kawai zamu fita iri daya.

          Aminiya zan koma dan babu wanda yasan nice amaryan, kinyi farin ciki."

Mamakin kawu ne, ya kamani dama yasan zamu bar kasar shine bai gaya min ba, itama Mama bata gaya min ba, sun maidani bita can sakarya wato, babu inda zanje a dinga cika min ciki da  bakin cikin rayuwa shi yayi auren shi Ni kuma na ci-gaba da zaman jiran gawon shanu.
   
   Wato ga mara gata, bari a juyata, yadda yake so bayan yana da matan shi babu inda zani yawwa, kaje duk inda yayi maka da zabiyar shi, amma ba dai Ni yar Innata ba,

     Allah bazan bisu ba, Ni dai na fada,
     

             Mik'ewa tayi ta ajiye min kayan sannan ta rufe fuskarta da sauri suka fita da yarinyar, kamar doluwa, tana tafiya Mama na shigowa ita da Jamlah.

       "Sarah ce ta shigo?!" Mama tambaye ni, gyada mata kai nayi ina ya fuskana. Na nuna mata kayan da ta kawo min, sannan na koma jikin gadon na jingina ina tsaki, ina turo baki na.

       Zazzage jakar Jamlah tayi, tana me zare ido, sabida har da yar karamar akwatin azurfa da yan kunne shi da abun wuyar shi.

        Sai kayan kwalliya, irinta yayi.
"Allah sarki! Da tasan mijinta kike so, Allah bazata  baki kayan ba, dan zata gwammace ta ganshi yana cin wuta, kawai ki rabu da wancan shashan, ki rungume Samim ko dan maidugurin da aka kusan fasa mana don kunne akan shi."

         Tura baki nayi, ina cewa.
"Kawun ne sha-sha-sha, Allah sarki kawuna, aini bana mishi wannan son Mama, sabida Yana auren Aminiya, dan haka na yafe mishi ita. Kuma ni ashe dama zamu tafi India shine ba a gaya min ba, Ni babu inda zanje yawwa gwara ya kwashi matan shi ya tafi, dan nima aure zanyi.

   Wato ga bitalmar, sai yadda yayi da ni. Ni babu inda zani hmm"


      Dariya Jamlah tayi sannan tace.
       "Wannan kuma sai ki gaya mishi babu wanda kika isa kisaka a gaba da fitina.

                 Idan kin so ki zauna abinki, dan nima banga amfanin bin shi da zaki yi ba"

     Harara Mama ta watsa mata, tana fadin.
"Baki da hankali, kin manta abinda yace ne, yafi kowa iko da ita dan haka babu wanda ya isa shiga al'amarin shi da ita Ni dai ki rufa min da fitinar Jaamal"

  Dan dole suka sani naje nayi wanka, sannan Mama ta bani abinci a baki, har sai da na koshi.

         Muna gama shiri ana kiran la'asar, sallah muka gabatar sannan muka fita zuwa kallon yawan dabar daza ayi, wato kilisa.

       Tunda muka fita, nake ganin yadda yan Maraɗi da yan uwan Nana suke min wani irin kallo, bai dame ni, haka na wuce su.

           Har inda aka ajiye mana, inda zamu zauna, daga nesa na hango Sarah. B'ata rai tayi dan kayan mu irin daya ne.

          "Kinga jeki gurinta, tana son ki zauna a kusada ita."

          Inji Mama, a hankali nake tafiya, idanun kowa ya dawo kaina.
Lokaci guda aka shiga min kirari, tare da gwadani. Ban san ya akayi ba sai naji ana kirata.

        Ban wani damu ba, har na isa, inda suke na zauna, riko hannuna tayi cikin nata, sannan ta daura min kanta a kafad'ana, tana faɗin.
"Aminiya! Ina son kawunki! Kuma sai gashi ba ni dayazan rayu dashi ba.

        Aminiyata dubi yadda aka hada shi da mata kamar wasu abin arziki, ni gaskiya ina da kishi, kuma wallahi dole ki tayani nasan yadda zanyi dasu, dan bazan tab'a zama da kishiya ba!"

     Kallonta nayi kamar wawuya bakina a sake, ina kare mata kallo, sabida ta bani mamaki.

   Lumshe idanuna nayi ina jin wani abu na taso min, rike hannunta nayi cikin murmushin karfin hali, nace mata.
"Idan kuma ya kasance dole akwai mutum na biyar bayan ke, da take son shi ya zaki ji? Kinga Maryam kishi halal ce, amma karki cusawa kanki kishin da bazai zame miki abin alfahari ba.

     Namiji yana da ganin dama, idan bai gadama ba, sai ya sake ki ya kuma cika da wata, don Allah karki fara irin kishin da mutanen farko suka yi wato jahilin kishi."

        Kura min ido tayi, sannan ta matse kwallar da take cike a cikin idon tace.
"Yanzun fatar ya sake ni kike min? Anya ba maganar dasu Yagana suka gaya min ba gaskiya bane?"

  Dake sakarya ce, zuba mata ido nayi. Ina me jiran abinda take shakku akai. Aikuwa kamar dai yadda na fadan ne, sakarya ce nan take gaya min abinda ya faru jiya da amsar da ta basu.

       Wallahi ina tausayin kaina, amma yau sai naga matan Jamal sune abin tausayi, domin dukkan su, sun fada son shi, shi kuma baya taso nice a rayuwar shi.

  Mik'ewa nayi zan bar gurin ta rike hannuna, kwalla na zuba a fuskarta tace min.
"Kiyi hakuri bazan kuma ba, nasan faɗa ce kawai da hasashen mutane, ai babu aure a tsakanin ku."

        "Hmm! Tunda baki yarda dani ba, ai shikenan"

#MY ZAHRAH🌹
Gabaki d'aya Labarin sadaukarwa ce gareki😍
ZAHRAN-ADDA RAMLA

Fatan Alkhairi
Gare ku!My Wattpad❤️🤩❤️

4️⃣6️⃣

Lagwabe min kai tayi cikin sakalci tace min.
"Don Allah! Kiyi hakuri bazan kuma ba, wallahi ba wani abu bane kawai dan sun ga yadda Kawu ya nuna damuwarsa akanki ne! Amma Insha Allah bani kuma damuwa akan ko."

         Hmmm! Wannan shine son maso wani koshin wahala, sam Kawu baya ta matan da aka tara mishi.

        Wani irin kallo na mata, Allah sarki sai ta sake ni, tare da yin rau-rau da idanunta.

Toh meye laifinta? Meye nata a cikin al'amarin da nake ji haushin ta.

  Kirkiro murmushin dole nayi, na zauna a sanyayye na fara magana.
"Kinga ita rayuwar duniyar nan, a dunkule take da kalubalen rayuwa.

        Har abada bana jin zan kuma ganin laifin kowa, akan kaddarar rayuwata. 

           Sarah kin yi min abubuwan da bazan manta ba a rayuwata, Sarah ga Kawuna nan ki rike min shi da amana."

           Mik'e nayi na fara tafiya, sannan na sake murmushi nace.
"Fatan Alkhairi! Tare da yan biyu!"

Share kwallar dake sauka daga idanun ta tayi, tana murmushi.
    Wajen su mama na koma, ina zuwa na daura kaina a kafad'arta.
"Mama! Ki sanya ni cikin addu'o'inki domin ji nake kamar zan mutu"

      Make min baƙi Jamlah tayi, cikin fada tace.
"Kar na kuma jin haka! Ki zama me juriya da hakuri, Allah yana tare dake.
Gyada kai nayi cikin jin ba dad'i.

         Wato taron kilisar kafin ayita, masu Kasuwancin gargajiya na ƙasar Hausa ne suka fara Baje kolin su.
           Na taya Yarima Jamal murnan auren da yayi, Mahauta sune suka fara shiga filin, da saniya, inda suka yita wasan hawa kawo, abin burgewa aka shigo da wata mahaukaciyar saniya. Akayi akayi  a haura kanta, amma taki.

   Wata tsohuwa ce ta shigo tace a sanya mata kidan mahauta wato damale(Na gaida Mahauta 👌)
   
Ai kuwa tsohuwar na cire mayafinta, ta sha d'amara dashi, sannan ta kuma cirewa, akayi mata kirari sosai, kafin aka bita da kidan naci balangu naci tsire! Na ci jagwalgwalon mahauta, kawai matar nan ta cire mayafinta ta nade shi kamar yadda ta daura, sannan ta wurga akan Saniyar, kamar yadda ake wurga musu igiya idan za a yanka su. 

   Aikuwa Saniyar nan ta zub'e kasa, tana kad'a jelarta. Aikuwa take gurin ya dauki sowa, da tafi. Sannan yaje bayan Saniyar ta hau suka tashi.

    Wannan abin yayi matuƙar burge ni, domin kuwa al'adar Hausawa ne, kuma yau na ganshi anyi a idanuna.

      Mahauta nafita sai ka manoma, suma sun shigo take makadansu suka shigo aka fara musu nasu kidar da wakar noma tushen arziki ne.
           
   Nasu bajintar itace suka fara noma filin, suna kaftu. Ana kuma kara musu da kidar su.

       Suna gamawa, wanzamai suka shigo, aka kuma saka musu na su kidan, inda suka yita wasa da aska da kuma reza.

Suna fita sai ga yan tauri, suma nan suka gwada tasu hikimar da dabara, bayan fitar su makera suka shiga filin, inda suka yi wasa da wuta, da runshi.

           Suna fita mafarauta suka shigo inda suka Baje nasu baiwar da farauta, suna gamawa Dukawa suka shigo suma suka shiga nuna nasu sana'ar jima.

  Sosai akayi shagalin da raya al'adun Hausawa. 

Suna gamawa aka naɗa shi Shugaban Samarin Daura, sannan akayi wasar zilla da dawakai. 

  Suna gamawa aka dawo cikin gidan domin bud'an kai.

Tun da muka isa gurin bud'an kai yake esuna min harara, musamman da muka koma gefe da Khalil muna hira, wanda duk labarin familyn shi yake bani.

     Dad'i nake ji, yake kuma gaya min yaji dad'in yadda akayi wasan al'adun Hausawa ya burge shi sosai.
   
Ina jin yadda ke kallona amma fir naki kallon shi, ana gamawa naje kusada Sarah muka cigaba da hirar mu, inda nayita dariya tare da sakewa akayi kome dani,

     Da za a maida su, nima babu su. Muka tafi Shashina, inda aka zalinci naira. Domin kome nata kamar ba dan arewa bane, amma duk launin kayan ruwan gwal ce.

          Dake itace amarya, bazai shigo inda take ba, dole sai zuwa  wani lokaci, amma ya tarasu. Dan muna cikin hira yazo har bakin kofarta ya kirani, ina fitowa naga baya nan, bin shi nayi, dan hanyar da zai sadani da  shashin sa muka hadu.

"Gani!"
   Banza yayi dani, dan haka na jingina da bangon ina kallon mulkin mallaka irin na kawuna,

Sai da na shafe awa daya curr, har na fara sauke ajiyar zuciya sabida kafana da suka yi min tsami, sannan ya kalli agogon hannun shi ya tako gabana,

           "Wallahi na kuma ganinki da wani! Fidausih kina kure hakurina, na rantse sai nayi abinda ya dame ki ko? Na rantse kar na kuma ganinki da wani ina ba haka ba zan muzanta miki!" Ya fada a fusace,

      Rau-rau nayi da idanuna, cikin kuka nace.
"Ni ban hanaka yin rayuwar da kake so ba, sai nice ban da iko da rayuwata, gaskiya bazai yi ba, kuma wallahi aure na za.."

Aikuwa ya danne min bakina, tare da tsayawa a gabana, yana me niman abinda zai min dan na daina mishi rashin kunya a cewar shi.
           
            Hannun shi ya kai kutirina, wanda ya samu kallon juna a razane, kamar jan wutar lantarki haka muka ji lokaci guda, cire hannun yayi. Da sauri naja da baya.

      Na bar gurin shi da gudu, na rude baki daya, dan dukar da su Ya Shatu suka min yana nan a raina. Barin shi nayi a gurin, shima mamakin kanshi ne ya kama shi, dan haka kawai yaga kamar ya tab'a min haka, dan har ganin abin yake a zahiri.

    Dafe goshin sa yayi, yana fadin.
"Shikenan! Girma ya fadi"

   Dakyar ya koma Shashina, ashe bawan Allah nan ya bar abokan shi, jiran shi har kusan.
Zuɓewa yayi a kujera  yana sauke ajiyar zuciya.

Murmushi ya sake, cikin wani irin yanayi na farin ciki. Yake wanzuwa a zuciyar shi, yana kuma kallon hannun shi da ya tab'a k'uguna.

"Labarin zuciya..."

       "Kai dalla kun tashi  bamu da lokaci fa, indai wannan dan iskan bane, yana cikin matan shi ne duk inda ya juya mata ne!"

     Shafa wajen shi yayi cikin jin dadi ya mike, dakin Balkisu aka kai shi. Inda suka yi barkwance, sannan suka musu fatan Alkhairi.

         Tare da sayan baki, suna gamawa suka wuce dakin Yahansu, itama suka sayi bakinta. Kafin suka wuce dakin Nanah. Suna gama sayan nata, yaja tunga ya tsaya.

      Kallon baka isa ba yayi mishi sannan ya buga kofar dakin, Kawayenta suka bude, tana tsakiyar su, ana hira.
      Ga kuyanginta sun kewaye ta, tana kishingide, an zuba kayan marmari, lullebe take da alkyaba ruwan madara wanda aka yi sakar shi da zaren ruwan gwal.

           Itama sunyi mata sayan baki sannan suka mata sallama., A hankali suka fito, jikin shi yayi mugun sanyi, sakamakon kamshin turaren ta.

          Rakasu yayi har waje, ya zuba musu ido dan shakiyarnci suke mishi.
"Yau miskilancin zai zama tarihi domin zaka shiga duniyar sama."

      Banza yayi sannan yace musu.
"Bari naje na gaida Abbana."

      Kamar baza su tafi ba, haka dai suka raka shi. Har gurin Abba yayi musu nasiha, tare da bashi wata hadaddiyar shayin da Yasaka Raihanah ta dafa mishi, yana gama sha, ya sallame su Aswad.

"Kaji tsoron Allah! Ka cire son zuciya akan alamarin kowa ka fuskanci rayuwarka, matanka sune sama da duk wata macen da kaso! Ko kuma zaka so a nan  gaba.

        Naso maka adalci daya, na baka yarinyar nan amma.kace baka sonta, dan haka nima nayi amshi tayin abokaina na amshi auren yaransu wa kai.

          Ina mai baka shawara ka sauke hakkin da ya zame maka dole, ka kuma yi adalci a tsakanin su. Bana son naji ko na gani. Allah ya albarkaci rayuwarka baki daya."

         Daga haka ya mishi sallama, ya fita. Cikin sanyin jiki,. murmushi Abba yayi dan ya fahimci halin Jaamal, tsaf zai iya kin bawa matan shi hakkin su, shi yasa ya zuba mishi sinadarin karin kuzari. Dan baya son ace dan shi ragone! Yafi son a gwarzonta Dan shi, bawai a kira shi da lusari ba.

                ---
Tunda na gudo nazo na fada a daki na shiga sauke a jiyar zuciya, kamar wacce tayi gudun fanfalaki. Kawai sai tsintar kaina nayi da fashewa da kuka, sabida ina hango shi a yadda yayi min haka zaiwa Sarah. Me yasa muka yi tarayya akan don abu daya.


     Nayi kuka har na fara jin ciwon kai, sannan na kwanta a gurin.
.....
           Tunda ya shiga gidan, ya wuce shashinsa, inda ya rage kayan jikin shi, ya watsa ruwa. Yana fitowa ya saka wata sabuwar doguwar riga. Ya zauna a hankali yana nazarin wani hali nake ciki.

 Jingina yayi da gadon dakin shi, yana shafa fuskar shi, murmushi yayi yana kuma tuno lokacin da ya samu yayun shi suna dukana, yadda ya hango tsoro a kan fuskana. Murmushi ya kuma sakewa lokacin da yaga kwayar idanuna sunyo waje.

             Girgiza kai yayi, wani irin bakon yanayi, yana shigar shi wanda tunda yake bai tab'a jin haka ba sai yau. 

        A hankali yake jin abin yana karuwa musamman idan ya zurfaffa tunanin yanayin da muka shiga jiya da safe da yau.

    Haka yayi matuƙar tasirin gurin d'ago mishi abinda yasha. Kafin wani lokaci ya fara jin shi wani irin halitta, da sauri ya mike bai tsaya ba, sai dakin Yahansu.

       Dukda da sauran yan uwanta, buga mata kofa yayi, ana buɗewa yace.
"Tazo ina son ganinta!"

       Yana fad'ar haka ya bar kofar d'akin.
              Matar da ta l bud'e mishi ta koma ta gaya musu, take aka shiga gyara mata jiki, tare da bata abubuwan gyaran jiki.

  Sai da suka gama sannan aka rakota zuwa dakin shi sannan suka barta a kofar d'akin. Bayan sun buga kofar dakin.

     Buga kofar wata daga cikin bayinta tayi kafin su tafi.

   Yafi minti goma kafin yazo ya bude mata, a hankali tashiga d'akin. Wuce babban falon yayi tana bin shi a baya har cikin uwar d'aka, sannan taja ta tsaya. Shi kuma ya zauna yana me kurb'an ruwan zuma da aka dafa shi da kayan kamshi.

"Kishiga ban daki kiyi alola,"

           Kamar bata ji shi ba, kafin tayi abinda yace.
       
Tana fitowa ya mike shima, yayi alolan yazo yaja su sallah, yana jima yana addu'a, yana idarwa ya janyo mata tiren kayan ciye ciye.
                Bata ci kome ba, dan ya fahimci, tana dan tsorace dashi.

                Dukda yadda yake ji, bai sanya ya damu da ita ba, har ta gama kewaye-kewayenta ta dan ci kadan, sannan ta tashi ta wanke bakinta.

            Dan yanzun kanshi ya kulle babu ta Yadda zai shiga da Jannart, tunda babu saki a cikin aurensu na masarautar. Kallon yadda take barci cikin salama da nutsuwa, a hankali ya tashi daga kwanciyarshi ya zuro kafarshi kasa, sannan ya janyo rigar shi da ya cire yan mintotin da suka gabata.

    Yasaka, sannan ya nufi ban daki jikin shi a mace, ruwa ya ɗan diba yayi wanka dashi sannan ya fito ya same ta tana barci har lokacin.  

             Komawa yayi kan kujeran ya kwanta, yana me jin Wadatacce nutsuwa na shigar shi, ta ko'ina tabbas aure ni'ima ce, daga kasancewa da ita ya sami nutsuwa da ya jima bai samu ba, a hankali barci me nauyi ya kwashe shi, kirar sallar farko ya tadda shi.

              Yana tashi ya ganta har lokacin barci take, a nutse dan kofar shashin sa yaji ana yi, a hankali ya  tashi ya fita.

      Jakadiya ya gani tsaye tare da wasu bayi guda uku, suna masu zuɓewa, a gaban shi suna gaida shi.

        Rike kofar yayi cikin daurewar fuska yana zubar Jakadiya, fuskar shi babu sassauci yace.
"Hmm!"
                "Allah ya taimaki hasken matasa, dama mun zo duba Gimbiya Yahanasu."

               "Kije babu abinda ya faru." Ya gaya mata kai tsaye,

          Turusss tayi tana kallon shi, ranta na ayyana mata, kawai baya son su shiga ne, suyi mishi bankad'ar da akayi a gidan sarautar.

              Tasan komai akan Jamal, yana da mugun zurfin cikin da ba lallai bane ya nunawa duniya ya samu abinda ake so.

    Dan haka sai ta juya ta bar gurin, sannan shima ya koma ciki yayi alola ya wuce masallaci, yana fita Umma Fulani tana kuma Aiko Shatu da Jakadiyar, dan taso ta saka su Mama da Jamlah suka ce babu ruwan su da fitinar Jaamal.

               Dan haka kasancewar Yahanasu ya nima a daren kuma d'iyar Sarkin Kano, sarki mai daraja na uku, a kasar hausa shi yasa suka damu da son ganin abinda ya tsurewa buzu nadi.

         Sun sameta tana barci suka tasheta, daga nan aka sanyata ta tashi, aka ga yadda gadon ya b'aci, da jinin budurci. Take aka shiga guda a cikin gidan, nad'e zanin gadon aka yi zuwa shashin Fulani, wannan abu yayiwa Jaamal ciwo kamar zai had'iye zuciyarshi.

        Sabida shi mutum ne mai son sirri baya kaunar Bankad'a da terere.

         Daga wajen bai tsaya ba, gidan su Aswad ya wuce, inda yaje ya sauke rabin masifar shi akan Aswad.

    Inda yace mishi.
"Ai nafi zargin duk abinda yake faru dani da saka hannunka! Taya ma za ayi kome ya faru ba tare da sanin ka ba.

    Wallahi Aswad munafuncin da kake min da hada kai da akayi da kai aka ci amanar Ruhina Allah sai ya min sakaya.

   Na dai gaya maka bazan tab'an cin amanar ka ba, kar yadda kai kaci tawa, sai ka cigaba da abinda kaga yayi maka kyau. Wallahi ina tuna maka wutar maciya amanar ruhi daban take."

     Yau kam Aswad ya gaji da danganta shi da ake da gidan wuta, dan haka ya ce Mishi.
"Kai mara mutinci! Wata amanar ruhin naci wanda ban sani ba?! Dan Iskanci sau nawa ina binka akan JANNART? Kace min baka sonta.

     Bamu yi haka da kai ba, wato dan bani da zuciya kai  zuciya, bari ka d'aga min hankali. Toh bari kaji tunda ka sake aka daura mata mata hudu lokaci guda, toh kasaka a ranka Janny tayi maka nisa dan ita mai sabon jini take nima ba irinka da aka zarga maka igiyar mata hudu ba."

                Shiru yayi dan bai da tacewa, dan ya lura yau ya kure Aswad, amma dan bakin taurin kai bai bada hakuri ba, sai ma wani fushin da Ya kuma dauka sosai kamar zai jefa Aswad a cikin wuta.

Ina kwance aka yi ta Shagali sosai dan dama wannan al'adar babba ce cikin masarauta. Kuma ana d'abaka wannan al'adar, kafin wani lokaci an shiga busa algaitu tare da kad'e kad'e.

            Sabon Shagali ne ya kuma barkewa, cusa kaina nayi a tsakanin cinyoyina ina sauke ajiyar zuciya.

      _Tabbas dole na rabu da Kawu! Koda kuwa shine autan maza na hakura dashi! Ya zame min wajibi nayi nesa dashi ba iya shi ba na rabu da ahalin shi baki daya nayi tafiya ta! Amma ina zani? Wa nake dashi? Waye na sani? Koda ban zauna a inda yake ba dole nayi fitar bazata a rayuwar shi!_

Abinda nake ta mai-maitawa kenan a raina, ina cikin damuwa sosai. Sai dai dole nayi nisa da duk inda yake.

    Allah sarki! Mama kamar tasan. Halin da ake ciki, taso ta shiga hada mana kayan mu, tana gamawa, ta je dakin Fulani. Ban san me ya faru ba sai gata ta fito ita da Jamlah suna kuka, sosai.

   ..kayan mu aka fitar dashi ina son zuwa gurin Sarah amma ina tsoron ganin abinda zai tsaya min a raina, motar da zata kai mu Kano na duba nasamu yar takarda da alkalami na rubutawa Sarah sako.

Kamar Yadda kika bani wani abu daga cikin farin cikinki nima na sadaukar miki da abinda nafi so. Na kuma bar miki shi har abada zanyi farin cikin idan kinsan meye na bar miki! Sai dai bazan fada miki ba! In da rai da rabo! Wata rana zaki yi mamakin yadda aka yi haka ta faru! Bana fatan zama mara adalci a amintarmu_

                   ..... Jannart J Jalal

        Tura Wata baiwa nayi takai sannan na koma cikin motar na zauna, kafin Mama ta fito daga Shashin Mai martaba.

      Ba akan shi ake jifarmu da mugun alkaba'i ba, Insha Allah sai duniya ta girgiza da jin wacece ke.

         Jannat Insha Allah, sai kin zame mishi abin alfahari, sai an wayi gari, kece Yaren shi kece nutsuwar shi, kece suturar shi. Sai an wayi gari kece sirrin shi, idan babu ke a rayuwarshi bai cika mutum ba.

         Kiyi hakuri da sannu zaki isa matakin rayuwa! Sai Sarah karki yarda wani mugun abu ya hada ku da ita, dan bata da masaniya, bata kuma san kome ba.

             Koda Jaamal bai wai-waiye kiba, akwai rayuwa akwai mutuwa dole zai nime ki, dole zai nime ki. Ki nutsu ki mai da hankalinki guri guda."

         Gyada mata kai nayi, a hankali take min hirar

Masarautar Borno!

Shirye-shiryen suke babu kama hannun yaro,  zaune ta. Cikin izza da damuwa ta kalli Qoiseh kafin ta tab'e bakinta tace.

"Kina nufin ya tafi baki gaya mishi zaki rakani bane?"
     
Tura baki tayi sannan ta kalli yar matashiyar budurwan da take kwance, kamar ba mutum ba, sannan tace.
"Ni dai idan kika je kice mishi ya kashe Rahil! Na gaji da ganinta haka, ai tun lokacin da abin zai faru nace miki ki bari a isar da aikin akanta kika ce a'a,. A barta da Rahilah ga Rahil din na shirin takurawa Rayuwata, shekaru sha takwas ban tab'a hutawa ba.

     Haka kawai banci buzu ba, a barni da aman gashi, gaskiya da sakel, dan banci nanin ba, bazan biya nanin ba."

Wani mugun kallo Inna ta bita dashi, sannan taja tsaki tace.
"Shi wancan shafaffe da mai dan banza da wuya kamar markin lema, wato tafiyar shi yayi ko ya zauna yaga wucewa ta.

    Dan banza duk abinda zai yi sai yayi, ba a sakewa tuwo suna ba. Kema kinsan darajar da zaki samu a sanadin wannan yarinyar da take kwance.

         Amma dake dama ke din sakarya ce, shine bari ki b'ata rawanki da tsalle.

    Wallahi  kika Kuskura kika janyo min sanadin da na rasa damar juya Kazeem sai na miki abinda bazaki manta da ni ba, ko ce miki akayi ban san me nake yi ba ko?"

  Shiru tayi har ta gama masifar ta sannan tace.
"Toh kiyi hakuri Inna!"

         Tsaki tayi sannan ta nuna mata hanya da sandarta, ita wacce ake magana akanta tana tana jinsu, amma bata da wani ikon abinda zata yi.

    Dan tun tunda Tasan kanta take kwance a gurin, duk wani kulawa a gurin ake bata, ba'a tab'a duba yanayin da zuciyarta ke ciki ba.

     Kawai duk lokacin da suka tashi zasu zakalkale akanta ne. Duk wani masifar Qoiseh akan ta  yake karewa.

           Gyaran murya Inna tayi tana faɗin.
"Ki duba dakyau! Rahil kama take da Kazeem, yayinda da na bar Rahilah ga inda ya dace. kin fini sanin abinda ya dace ne?

              Dan rashin kai shekaranjiya kike gaya min, yana tambayar ki, yar shie. 

     Amma dake baki da lissafi shine kike gaya min magana bazaki kula da ita ba, na fasa tafiyar sai naga yadda kike bawa Rahil kulawa kafin na bar ƙasar nan da sunan nima miki wani taimakon da zai sanya ki samu."

      "Toh inna bana baki hakuri ba ko kuma so kike nayi ta ihu ina baki hakuri"
   
Nuna mata hanyar kofar tayi da sunan ki fitar min a daki, dan dole ta saka kai tafita.

                  Tana fita Inna taja tsaki, saboda jin haushin abinda Qoiseh tayi mata akan Rahil!

               Ko babu kome tana masifar kaunar Rahil, dan tunda ta amshi Haihuwar ta ga kamar da suka yi da Sarki Kazeem ya sanya taki barinta, kawai abinda ta sani shine akwai abinda suka yi mata, dan karya zame musu matsala kamar yadda, Bokanta yace mata.
                    Amma tana matuƙar kaunar yarinyar har cikin zuciyarta. Kuma bata fatan ta Rahil ta zame musu damuwa, dan tana fahimtar haka zata iya aikata kome. Matsalar da takasa fahimta shine Rahil din ce zata kifar da ita ko Kuma Rahilah, a wannan gabbar ce take shiga mugun damuwa.

  Daura....

Kayan Jaamal yazo ya dauka sannan ya shiga cikin gidan domin magana da Raihanah, ya kuma samu damar kira mishi JANNART, kawai yaji labarin ai sun bar daura suna hanyar Kano.

     Sam bai ji dadin abinda Jamlah ta gaya mishi ba, domin abinda ya faru ya masifar kona mishi rai.

      Kallon Jamlah yayi sannan yace.
"Amma! Fulani bata kyautawa, Raihanah ba. Taya zata jife Raihanah da cewa karuwanci take koyawa Jannart, ai wannan abin bai dace ba, sai dai wallahi idan Jaamal yaji tabbas zaku sha mamakin shi, dan ba abu me wuya bane ya kora matan baki daya.

     Keda kuke ciki daya bazai hana shi miki rashin mutunci ba, dan Jaamal akan Jannart ya zama mara tsoro, magana kuwa ko aku. A yanzun haka sunan da ya Makala min shugaban munafukan daura baki daya.

  Jaamal gani na yake nine na hana shi Jannart, bayan iya kokarina, nayi akan shi domin ya fahimci meke damun shi.

     Amma bawan Allah nan ya tsaya kai da fata yace baya kaunarta.

  Toh me kike ganin zan iya mishi, zan daura mishi JANNART akan kafar shi ne? Ko zan goya mishi ita ce a bayan shi yayi ta yawo da ita?

       Na"

        Shiru yayi tare da had'iye maganar da bai fadeta ba, ya zubawa Jaamal din Ido. Wanda yake tsaye a bayan Jamlah.

        Idanun shi sun soma juyewa zuwa kore, jikinshi na wani irin rawa. Watsar da kayan hannun shi yayi yaje ya rungume shi.

      Itama Jamalah hankalinta ya tashi, addu'a suka fara tofa mishi, kafin wani lokaci ya sauke ajiyar zuciya.
     
              Rike shi Aswad yayi zuwa shashin sa, Jamlah na rike da kayan shi.

       Amma yaushe Jaamal ya hadu da wannan matsalar, babu wanda ya sani?  Toh a ina ya hadu da wannan al'amarin, dan bata taɓa dayan biyu mutanen boye ne.

  Kallon Juna suka yi da Aswad lokacin da jikin Jaamal yake wani irin rawa, kafin ya koma ya kwanta. Haka yayi har sau uku kafin ya cigaba da Barci.

"Jaamal da Jannart suna cikin siradin rayuwa, kowanne su zai iya rasa rayuwarshi idan aka matsa dole sai sun rabu da juna. Idan basu mutu ba, toh wallahi duk wanda ya shiga tsakanin su zai iya mutuwa.

          Matsalar da ya faru, a wasu karni ne ya kuma dawowa karnin nan, abinda yake iya zama damuwa shine kar ayi amfani da raunin shi akan Jannart, dan yin haka.

          Dai-dai yake da samun matsalar da zata iya hana masarautar kwanciyar hankali."

           A rude tare da fargaba, ta kalle shi tana me rasa tacewa.
Sai zare ido take, dan Allah yayi mata mugun tsoro.

   Kasa zama tayi ta mike,tare da saka hannunta a habbarta tace.
"Raihanah tasan da halin da yake ciki?"

     Girgiza mata kai yayi, sannan yace.
"Babu wanda ya sani sai ke dani! Domin nima jiya naga haka ya faru dashi, wallahi idan na JANNART ya tashi ita har jini ake saka mata."

Gwalalo idanun waje tayi a birkice, tana me shiga rudani tace..
"Shine ka bar shi baka gaya mai martaba halin da ake ciki ba? Aswad ai wannan babu daɗin jin.

   Tabbas akwai matsala, dole ma mu gayawa Baba Magaji, dan akwai mugun matsala. Kasan burin mu akan shi kuwa?!"

        "Nima naso haka, amma matsalar bata gaggawa bace! Kafin mu gaya mishi dole mu fara gayawa Babanmu! Domin sannan halin da ake ciki."

      "A'ah kawai mubi ta kofar baya mu kaiwa mai martaba shi, in yaso sai a kira Baban."
    Allah sarki Aswad haka ya dauke shi a kafad'arshi, sannan ya fita ta kofar baya, kasancewar gidan sarauta bai rabuwa da kofofin.

      Mai martaba yana karyawa suka shigo da Jaamal, kallon su yayi cikin nutsuwa sannan ya zubawa Aswad idanun yana sauraron karin bayani daga gare shi.

                  Nan Jamlah ta shiga mishi bayani, sannan ta kuma cewa.
"Abba! Ni ina kyautata zaton gamo yayi."
       
Mai da idanun shi yayi kan Aswad, wanda yake gyarawa Jaamal kwanciya.

                   "Ka kira min Magaji!"
A razane Jamlah ta kalli mai Martaba, tare da zare idanu tace..
"Abba! Kaine kayi magana?!"
          Ɗaura yatsar hannunshi yayi saman bakin shi, dole ta had'iye Maganar, tana jin wata irin kuka na zuwa mata, kusada shi taje ta zauna, sannan ta daura kanta a bisa cinyar shi ta sake wata irin kuka me cin rai.
Lumshe idanunshi yayi yana jin kukanta.

          Shafa kanta yayi, sannan yace mata.
"Ki cigaba da tayani yin shirun da bakin ki! Domin kuwa fitowar haka dai dai ne da rasa lafiyata.

        Wannan masarautar cike take da mutanen banza, sanin halin da muke ciki tankar basu lasisin cutar damu ne.

     Idan kika ja bakin ki shiru, zaki sha mamakin yadda Allah zai kawo mana karshen duk wani me mugun nufi a kan mu."

           Gyada mishi kai take kawai.
                 Kwallar ta goge tana kallon Jaamal da yake kwance, sannan tace.
"Abba! Shima Jaamal sharrin masarautar ce?"

    Girgiza kai yayi, cikin damuwa yace.
"Bana tunanin haka, sai dai in Magaji ya iso."
              
     Ko rufe baki Bai yi ba suka shigo da sallama, zama yayi yana kallon Mai martaba kafin ya juya ga Jaamai da jikinshi ke rawa, daga lokaci zuwa lokaci, sai ya bari.

              "Toh Magaji kaga wani tashin hankali ko?! Yau kuma wannan abun ya bayyana mishi, ban san ya zanyi ba." Inji mai Martaba,
            Gyada kai Baba Magaji yayi sannan ya fara da nasiha.
"Ita rayuwar mumini! Dole sai da jarabta! Sannan duk rayuwar da babu jarabta ba rayuwa nace karya ce!

      Duk mutumin da kagani zaka samu yana da nashi kalubalen, na wani yafi nawani, wani zai iya boye nashi wani zai fidda nashi.

     Dan haka karka daga hankalinka kome ka gani dama rubutacciyar kaddara ce.

         Dan haka da Izinin Ubangiji zamu yi abinda ya samu daga cikin Alkur'ani da wasu littattafai musulinci."

         Juyawa yayi kan Jaamal ya shiga tofa mishi Addu'a, kafin ya bukaci ruwa a kofi. Nan ya cigaba da a karanto mishi Addu'a.

       Yana tofawa a kofin, zuwa wani lokaci ya karbi ruwan ya furza mishi a fuska da jini!"

         "Hmmmmmmmmm! Muhammad, Abdullahi, Haruna, Adamu, Sa'idu. Sunanka sunan Babanka sunan kakanka, sunan baban kakanka! Ko ba haka ba?" Aka fada da wata ƙatuwar murya, yana kallon Baba Magaji.

       Juya idanun shi yayi zuwa ga Mai martaba ya fara magana.
"Jalaludeen, dan Habibullah, jikar Aminullah! Dan gidan Shatima korau! Dan uwa ga mati gargara, shi kuma uwar shi yaga kanin Azzalumi Zulum! Wutar fansa ya dawo dani, Kuma ina nan a raye zan dawo.

                Ku kiyayye fushina In ba haka ba zanyi abinda zaku dawwama cikin bakin ciki mara iyaka."

     Ya fada yana zare musu ido, shiru ne ya gifta tsakaninsu,

               "Kaga musami masalaha a tsakanin mu, bani son mu Kai ga na cutar da kai, ba tare da wani jinkiri ba kafita kawai shine masalahar mu da kai"

             Shiru yayi kafin yace.
"Zan tafi amma dole sai na dawo. Kuma ɗaukar fansa zanyi dole na dawo "
        Daga haka Jaamal ya sauke ajiyar zuciya, tuni barci yayi gaba dashi.

          "Alhamdulillah! Ya tafi amma dole a hada mishi magani, wanda bazai bari idan ya dawo ya zauna a jikinshi ba. Sannan a fahimta ta, akwai dalilin farun al'amarin.

         Idan na canka dai,-dai rikicin Zuri'ar Fairo da Zuri'ar Zulum shine ya kuma dawowa, kuma tun ranar da naga yarinyar nan na tabbatar da hakan zai faru.

            Aswad kaine kafi kusanci dasu me ya faru?"

   Duk suka zuba mishi ido, a hankali ya warware musu abinda ya faru, sannan ya kuma da cewa.
" A shekaranjiya ne da na shi ya bayyana. Inda yace matukar wani ya kusanci Jannart sai ya kashe shi.

       Ni abinda nake tsoro akwai Samim da yake son Jannart din, shine nake son a dakatar dashi dan matuƙar suka haɗu da JAMAL toh zai komai zai iya faruwa."

          "Amma bana baka littafin masarautar nan ka kai mishi ba?!" Bana Magaji ya wurga mishi tambaya.

  Had'iye yawu yayi cikin damuwa sosai yace.
"Ina son ku fahimce wani abu, Jaamal yana da wata irin hali, duk duniya mu biyu ne zamu iya jure halayen shi kuma a zauna lafiya, Ni sai Jannart.

   Abinda ya sa na gaya muku haka, sai dan tambayar da kai min, na kai mishi littafin amma bai zama dole ya duba ba.

   Matuƙar abu bai da muhimmanci a gurin shi toh baya tab'a dubawa."

              "Jalaludeen! Tabbas akwai matsala, domin abinda ya faru shekarun mulkin Mallaka na Zulum zai kuma faru.

    
        Tun bayan faduwar Sarakunan masarautar, Jaamal ne kaɗai ya ya tsallake rijiya da baya, ba tare da ya fado dokin tsafi ta taka kirjinshi ba.

          Amma gudun faruwar haka wani abu, zan hada Mishi addu'a da nasha, Insha Allah."

    Wato dai ango yana cikin chakwakiyya, domin kuwa aljanun sun make dan nima.

               Kusan wunin ranar yana kwance ne, ko fita bayi ba. Amma Alhamdulillah, dan bayan addu'o'in da aka yi Mishi sai da aka hada da saukar Alqur'ani.

               A Shashin mai martaba yayi wanka, sannan ya shirya tsaf. Zama yayi yana kallon mai mai martaba, kafin ya sunkuyar da kanshi kasa.

               Yana son yayi magana, amma bai san ta inda zai yi ba, kawai yayi kundunbala yace.
"Hmm! Dama."

     Zuba mishi ido mai martaba yayi yana jiran karin bayani.

  Gyara zama yayi sannan ya shiga rabtab'o bayani kamar haka.
"Dama, akan yaran nan da aka hada ni dasu Ni ban san ya zanyi dasu ba wallahi"

    Dariya ce ta kama mai martaba sosai, yana kallon Jaamal sannan yace.
"Dukkan su matanka ne! Kuma babu yadda zan koya maka kome! Ga takardu can sun baka gida a garin Abuja, me ciki biyu kaga kenan duk wata zaka zo kayi kwana biyu, idan zaka koma sai ka tafi da daya.

        Sannan tunda ka...., Da Yahanasu, idan da hali ka mata kwana biyu. Sauran ma haka zaka musu kwana biyu, kaga kenan kowacce ka mata kwana biyu.

       Idan zaka tafi abujan kuri'a zaka musu, duk wacce ta dauki gida zata zauna a gidan kenan duk wacce ta dauki Abuja sai ku tafi da ita.

        Dukda al'ada ce ta ware mana Uwargida da amarya, amma a addinance Balkisu ce uwargidan ka, kayi ƙoƙarin koyawa kanka jarumta, tare da nuna kulawa ga matanka.

        Duk cikin su Maryam ce me karancin shekaru, dan haka kayi ƙoƙarin gano ainiyin dabi'ar su, dan zaka mori kuruciyarta sosai.

            Sai Balkisu tafi su da kadan, itama kayi ƙoƙarin fahimtar kome akanta, sauran kuma ka bisu a hankali zaka gano yadda dabi'ar kowacce yaƙe.

           Kayi hakuri dama ba koyaushe macen da kaso kake samu ba. Ba dukkan mutane ke samun wannan damar ba.

Ina maka fatan Alkhairi."

  Kamar zai yi kuka haka yake kallon mai martaba, sannan ya gyada kan shi kawai yayi amma kana ganin shi kasan yana cikin damuwa amma shegen katon kan shi bazai tab'a yarda ya fadawa kowa ba.

           Bayan sallar isha da zai tafi Mai martaba yace mishi.
"Yau karka nemi kome a gurin ta, dan tana bukatar a tausaya mata, macen da aka rabata da kimarta ta hanyar aure tana bukatar tattalawa ne.

  Ba wai a kuma sanya zuciyarta cikin fargaba ba. Sannan ina son kafin ka tafi gurin aikinka kwanakin da zaka yi a dakunar su, ina son duk bayan kwana biyu kazo nan.".
       

Nan ma gyada kai yayi, tare da yiwa mai martaba, sallama.

         Wato idan ana niman mara mutunci aka samu Jaamal toh an gama magana kawai a shafa Fatiha a rufe zance, haka ya shige shashinsa ko takan matan bai bi ba.

             Dake yau duk wasu baki da yan kawo amarya duk sun watse, sai amare da bayinsu mata. Dan haka gidan sai ya zama babu hayaniya.

               ......
Takardan da na tura mata tayi tana murmushi, tare da nutsuwa zata karanta kawai sai sallamar Jakadiya ce ta katse mata duba takardan ta zuba mata ido.

       Cikin salon munafunci ta zauna tana murmushi tace.
"Ranki shi dad'e! Gashi inji Fulani wai akawo muku, wannan sarkan wanda zaku yi kwalliya dashi."

             Murmushi tayi wanda ya boye rashin yarda da Jakadiyar, da ya bayyana a zuciyarta ta nuna wata cikin bayinta tace.

"Uwani amshi sakon ki kai cikin sundukin da muka zo dashi na kayayyakin nan, sannan ki buɗe wancan drowe ki dauki dauko mata wancan tiren taskar kayan kawar a kaiwa Uwar mijina, tayi ado dashi."

           Fuskarta dauke da murmushi tace.
"Kinsan mun taso a inda muke fahimtar Yaren mutanen daban-daban, Abinda ke zukatar mutane ne kawai bamu fahimta, dan haka ki fahimtar da ita cewa.

Ko a lokacin zuwan Mujaddadi Shehu Usmanu danfodiyo, sarkin mu ne ke amsa sunar sarkin yakin sarkin Musulmi...........
