4️⃣9️⃣

     Dan haka Akuya bazata saka fatar kura ta razana ni ba, dan Ni nafi karfin kura dan bani da tsoro sai da. Na na bada tsoro!

          Ki isar da sakona! Fuskata ba iya madubin dubawarta bane!  Abinda na sani, ina iya barin shi dan ya zame maka hasken farin wata. 

               Karki manta dabi'ar masarauta tana nan hallo, Babu ja da baya, dan mulki ya haife ni izza ta rene ni. Bana jin zan iya sauya ra'ayina, idan mukan fahimci fuska uku ba iya biyu ba.

               Kicewa surukata ina godiya ga kyautar da ta bani."

       Daga haka ta mai da idanunta, kan wasikar da na tura mata, a sanyayye Jakadiya ta bar d'akin tana mamakin yadda aka yi Sarah ta fahimci Halayyar ta, uwa uba kuwa tuggar da aka gina musu.

       Dan takaiwa sauran matan sarkan sun amsa da hannun su, amma ita taki amsa, kuma tace a saka a cikin sundukin ta.

           "Hmm! Bana cewa Fulani Hasiyah Gimbiya Maryam bata amshi sarkan ba, kawai zan ce ta amsa."
      Da wannan maganar ta isa shashin Fulani, tana zuwa ta ajiye mata tiren tasar da Sarah ta bada a kawo mata. Wanda yake lullube da jar mayafi Mai cud'e da kyallen ruwan gwal.

               Gyara kishingida Fulani Hasiyah tayi, tana kallon Jakadiyar. Tana jiran karin bayani.

        Cikin gulmar da take cinta, ta gyara zama sannan ta fara magana tace.
"Yarinya ce qarama! Wadda bata fi Sa'ar takwaran ki ba, amma tana da shegen yawo, domin kuwa daga ganina ta amshi ni da hannu bibbiyu, ta kuma bani wannan na kawo miki a matsayin tukwaicin kyautar da aka bata."

               Kallon tasar Fulani Hasiyah tayi, tana son a bud'e.

   Yaye mayafin aka yi sarkan gwal ce mai matukar kyau da tsari, sai abin hannun su da zabba.

      Murmushi tayi sannan tace.
"Na bata sarkan gwal din da bai wuce a bawa baiwa ba, ta bani sarkan gwal din da duk inda na shiga sai anyi maganar shi.

          Sannan ta nuna min cewa tafito gidan da basa amsar qarama kyauta, ba tare da sun mai da maka babbar kyauta ba. 

      Su kuma sauran sun saka hannu sun amshi kyautar babu tukwaici balle magana mai daɗin.

               Tabbas Jaamal ya hadu da macen da zata iya tsaya mishi, sannan zata iya bada rayuwarta domin tashi, zata iya watsa dukiyarta domin kare lafiyar shi, sai dai bata san inda gizo ke saka ba.

              Domin nayi da wanda suka fita hangen nesa, ki isar da sakonta ga Shamaki kice mishi an sami Matsalar da zata iya bawa kowa damar juyawa tsarin mu baya."

         Tana fadar hakan ta saka hannu ta dauki sarkan gwal din da yake gaban ta, tana juyawa tare da mamakin yadda aka yi Sarah ta fahimci sakon su.

                ----
Zaune yake gaban Gamjie, yana kallon yadda kome yake wakana, kafin ya kalli Gamji cikin nutsuwa yace..
"Shugabana"
Dakatar dashi Gamji yayi sannan yace.
"Zulum yana bibiyar al'amarin ku! Ka'ida d'anka, yan niman wanda yafi wani shahara a mugunta.

     In ma jininka yayi tasiri ya baibayeka, ko kuma ya koma jinin danka, dan nafi hasashen kai zai bi. Amma ko ya-ya ne kayi ƙoƙarin zubda jini, shima d'anka kayi ƙoƙarin nima mishi mace daga masarautun mun nan.

     Koda kasar Kazaure ce, dan tana da tarihin mai zaman kanta.,

              Idan kayi haka zai iya dakatar da kudirin shi akan d'anka, dan yana niman inda ba a tab'a aure ne."

          Shiru Shamaki yayi sannan yana nazarin maganar Gamji, akan masarautar Kazaure, dan ya tab'a kaftawa Sarki Ahmad AlTahir rashin mutunci..

       Shiru yayi kamar wanda. Ruwa ya cinye shi kafin ya kuma kallon Gamji yace.
"Dakyar Sarkin Kazaure ya bawa D'ana Auren Yar shi, sabida  rashin da na tab'a mishi lokacin da nake ya goya min baya akan Jalal."
           
Gyara zama Gamji yayi sannan yace.
"Lokacin da zaka ka gaya min? Dan haka maza kaje kayi abinda nace, domin shine kadai yarshi zata iya tsayawa Yaronka."

                 Amsawa yayi bawai ya amince da shawarar Gamji bane, dan shi yana ganin daga yau bazai kuma zuwa gurin Gamji ba.

       Tabbas zai sauya guri, domin an gaya mishi akwai wani boka a can abia, ba dan kome zai je ba sai dan kare kujeran mulkin shi.

    Sai batun dan shi da yake ganin tunda ya fita har yanzun bai dawo ba.

     Dan haka yana gama abinda zai yayiwa Gamji bankwana ya bar kogon,  yana fita Gamji ya tintsira da dariya.

            Kafin ya turnike fuska yace..
"Nasan sarki Ahmad AlTahir bazai baka yar shi ba, nayi haka ne dan ka fuskanci wulakancin daga masarautar yadda sai kaji a zubda jin....."

Bai ida maganar ba, aka sare kanshi, ya fadi can jini na tsirtuwa.

                 ---
Zama yayi yana me sunkuyar da kanshi cikin jin matsanancin kunya gaida ita, murmushi tayi tare da tambayar shi.
"Da fatan anyi hidima lafiya? Ya iyalin naka?!""

             Kasa amsawa yayi, yana wasa da taburman kabar da yake shimfid'e a tsakar d'akinta, yayi shiru.
    Dan itama ta rasa me zata ce, sabida jin daɗin ganin shi, yayi haifa. Ba kamar da can baya ba.

        "Yarima yana da abinda yake bukata ne?" Ta tambaye shi tare da kallon fuskar shi.

          Sosa kan shi yayi sannan yace.
"Hmm! Dama ina son ki koma Shashina da zama ne! Sabida yaran nan koda zasu yi shirme akwai babba a tsakanin su.

            Na zab'i nazo gurinki ne, dan ina jinki kamar uwar da tahaife ni, kuma nayi imanin zaki rufa min asiri, bana son wannan al'adar masarautar da ake.

       Ni mutum ne mai son sirri, don Allah Ummana ki taimaka min, dan na hana a bar Jakadiya ta kuma shigowa farfajiya na.

         Idan kika yi min haka, Insha Allah zan miki biyayya a duk lokacin da kika ce min nayi abinda ya dace."

          Hawaye ne ya shiga saukowa daga idanunta, ina ma ace zai san wacece ita! Dan tai imani jininta ne!
    
Ina da zai san daga cikin mahaifarta ya fita, da har abada bazai nemi komi ta hanyar alfarma ba.

      Murmushi tayi wanda ya saukar da hawaye masu zafi tace..
"Iya abinda Kake bukata kenan."

     Gyada mata kai yayi yana kallon yadda take share kwallar da suke sauko mata.

      "Kiyi hakuri idan na takura miki!" Ya fada mata a sanyayye..
"Ko daya, naji dadin yadda ka mutuntani ne, Insha Allah zuwa an jima zan shigo sannan ka gaya musu zan zo gudun kar a samu akasin ra'ayi."

             "Umma kenan! Nine da gidan basu ba, dan haka Ni zance ayi ba su zasu ace ayi ba."

   "Shi kenan!"

       Bayan wani lokaci yayi mata sallama, ya bar dakin.

            ...... Yana komawa cikin gidan shi ya tara su baki daya, duk suna sanye da alkyaba, sai yaki suke mishi, idan ka cire Sarah da take sanye da riga da wanda ta kasar india, sai mayafin da ta lullebe kanta dashi, yayin da sake gashin kanta rabi a waje, fuskarta kawacce da murmushi.

         Dan yau zai shiga dakin Balkisu.

            Zuba mata ido yayi, dan gani yake duk tafisu, haskawa.

          Jannart ce ta fado mishi a  rai kawai ya mika mata hannu dan karya irinta idanun shi juye mishi tayi kamar Jannart.(Maman Khadijah dake da Mommy Sayeed naji kinyi magana 🏌🏽‍♂️)

           A sanyayye take tafiya har ta isa gurin shi, ya mika mishi hannu, a hankali ya zaunar da  ita a jikin shi, kallon shi tayi tana murmushi tace.
"Kawu! Jannart bata zo ba, kuma ina ta zuba idanun ta, ko jiya ban ci abinci ba, ina jiranta har yayi sanyi."

       Murmushi yayi tare da mai da hankalin shi kanta yace.
"Toh me kika ci?"
           Ya tambaye ta, tare da kallon fuskar matan shi a kaikace, yana yana nazarin yadda suke nuna rashin jin dadin su.

     Musamman Yahanasu, wacce fusarta har ja tayi tsabar kishi ya ciyota.
            Dariya Sarah tayi sananne tace.
"Kawu me zanci banda shayi, kasan tun bayan rasuwar Ammihna Hajja ta rike ni, a gurinta na koyi shan shayi, duk abinda aka bani matukar ka ba i shayi ka gama dani."

     Jan hancinta yayi, sannan ya janye ta a jikinshi tare da gyaran murya, ya koro musu jawabi, sannan ya kuma daura musu da bayanin shigowar Umma cikin su.

              Sannan ya kara da cewa.
"Bana son a maida min gida sansanin yaki, bana son amai da gida kamar gidan gandu.

      Ina son kowa ya zauna a inda yake, dan bana son tashin hankali, sannan yau na fita a dakin Yahanasu, zan shiga dakin Balkisu, wanda ya kama ta ace ita na fara shiga.

                 Dukkan ku, Matana ne da mahaifina ya zab'a min, dan zan zauna da ku, sabida kudin zab'in mahaifina ne!

   Ban sani ba ko nan gaba zan samu cikar muradina, amma tabbas babu saki a cikin auren mu daku, dan wannan shine ka'idar auren masarauta.

         Sannan bana son cece-cece, ko wani abinda zai zama kamar na gulma ko tsegumi, bana son Bankad'a ko terere.

             Bana son naji na raina min matar da zata shigo cikin ku, ta zauna, idan na sami matsala daga gare ku, zan hukunta Dai-daiton laifin da kuka aikata.

         Dukkanku a shirya nake na zauna daku har karshen rayuwata.

     Dan haka kuyi kokarin kame kanku, tunda ku ba yara bane.

                   Sannan idan na gama muku kwana bibiyun da ya zama wajibi akaina, zan tafi gurin aikina, dan haka idan zan tafi zan kuma taraku anan. Na muku bayani yadda zan yi tafiyar dan duk karshen wata zan na zuwa ina daukar ko waccen ku.

    Sannan idan na dauke ki muka tafi, idan na dawo bazaki shiga cikin zaben da zan kuma yi ba. Dan haka mun tashi cikin masarauta, mun san al'adun masarautar ƙasar Hausa.

   Yana da kyau muyi koyi da zamewar mu jakadu na gari, kar muyi abinda zai zame mana matsala.

             Idan nazo gari, ka'idar da zan sanya muku na shiga dakunan ku, kwana ɗaya ɗaya, dan haka ku rike magana ta. Idan kuma akwai mai magana zata iya yi."

       Shiru suka yi, Sarah ce ta kwanta a jikinshi tace mishi.
"Kawu! Duk abinda kace shi zamu yi, dan haka na amince. Amma don Allah ina Aminiyata take."

               Kallon sauran matan yayi, sannan ya bata amsar tambayar ta.
          "Sun koma tun jiya da safe."

   Zaro ido tayi cikin damuwa sannan tace.
"Don Allah Ni ka kaini gurinta, kaji kawuna!"

    Bakin ciki ne ya kama Nanah da Balkisu, suka ce.
"Ranka shi dad'e! Mun amince zamu iya tafi."

               "Hmmm!" Yace musu, har zasu tafi Nanah ta dawo tare da cewa.

"Shi namiji tamkar hankaka yake, gaban shi fari bayan shi baki, idan da zamu duba kanmu bamu fi kowa ba, dan haka bazamu nunawa junan mu kome ma ba, baki fini tsuma ba. Ashe kuwa bakya nuna min kwarkwata ba. Dikin-dikirin bikin kawar kawa."

            Murmushi Sarah tayi, wanda ya boye b'acin rainta tace.
"A cikin masarauta aka haife ni, dan haka mun san darajar kanmu, ko banan ba Kakata Hajjah tace min shi zaman gidan miji! Iya ruwa ne fidda kai.

           Ban cika son y'ay'an masu karamin mukami suna min magana, ina ba haka ba zanyi abinda zai dame mutum.

                Tsakanin matan shi  kuwa an sami rashin jituwa sosai, inda suka sako Nana a gaba, dan sun lura Sarah ba ƙanwar lasa ba, dan haka sai suka mai da tunanin su kan Nanah.

             Umma Rumana kuwa, tun lokacin da ya nemi tazo gurin shi da zama Maganar ya faɗa kunnen Jakadiya, nan tayi mata Barazana da cewa matukar ta kuskura taje zata mata sharrin da sai an yanke mata hukuncin kisa baki daya.

                  Dan haka sai ta hakura da yazo tace mishi bata da lafiya ne, bawai dan tsoron mutuwa ba, sai dai bata san meye zasu iya aikatawa ba.

                 ---
Lagos.
Buɗe ido nayi cikin jin dadi, na sauke akan Muhsina da Aliyah da suke jan labulen dakin, mika nayi tare da zuba musu ido..

           "Kuzo nan!"
     Suna zuwa suka fada jikina, murmushi nayi, tare da zuba musu ido.
"Kunga yau Lahadi! Kamata yayi muje bakin ruwa muyi kallo maza ake a gayawa Mama gani nan fitowa."
Da gudu suka fita, nima wanka na shiga, ina fitowa Mama tana shigowa.

   Zama nayi ina kallonta nace.
"Ina kwana?!"
"Lafiya lau!Ya hakurin rayuwa"
        

Murmushi nayi sannan na juya ina kallon abinda take dubawa, nace.
"Alhamdulillah! Mama abincin jiya ne ban ci ba, shi.."

  D'ago kai tayi ta zuba min tare da tab'e baki sannan tace.
"Kiyi maza ki tashi zaki tafi gurin koyan girkin da na biya kudin, idan kin shirya muna jiranki"

   Wato wani lokacin Allah yana musanya maka abinda aka maka, ban samu damar zama da Jaamal ba, amma a yanzun ina farin ciki sosai a rayuwata.

   Gurin koyan girki na wata baturiya tayi baya min ina zuwa gurin, ba iya abincin ba hatta yadda zaka yi rage wasu abubuwa tana koya mana.

    Har yadda zaka amfane da kanka.
   Ina gamawa na fito sanye da riga da skirt na bulas, sai mayafin da na rufe kaina, abin karyawa naci sama sama, sannan nayi musu sallama.

        Daga unguwar mu, taxi zai kai ka zuwa inda matar take, sauri nake tare da saka hannuna cikin aljuhun jakata, ina niman kudin da zan biya.
         Naji ance min.
"Jannart.J.Jaamal" d'agowa nayi da sauri.

      "Humairah!" Na ambaci sunanta, murmushi tayi sannan ta karaso inda nake, tana cewa.
"Ina Sarah!"
Gaba na ne ya fadi, da jin sunan Sarah, kakaro murmushi nayi sannan na gyara tsayuwar ta, nace mata.
"Tana Daura!"

      Murmushi tayi tace.
"Ko dai ta auri wannan Kawun naki ne"

           Wani irin duka kirjina ya kawo, na yi maza na sake murmushi nace mata.
"Eh!"

      "Hmm! Sai nake ganin kamar kwace ta miki?!"
A tsorace na kalleta, jikina ya dauki wani irin rawa, take yanayina ya sauka na b'ata rai muryana yana rawa nace mata.

"Kamar ya? Babu wani abu a tsakanina da shi, kuma ai Kawu..."

   "Kinga renin hankalin ya isa haka, kece dai baki san kome akan gidan sarautar Daura ba, amma Ni nasani.

   Dan haka zan gayawa Sarah ta shiga tsakanin ki da mijinta, munafuka."

        Cikin tashin hankali wanda ban san ina dashi ba, hawaye na zuba na rike hannunta tare da sake wani irin kuka nace.
"Don Allah ki gaya min me kike so? Dan girman Allah karki min haka wallahi Ni babu abinda ya Hadani da mijin Sarah sai taimako.

         Amma idan baki yarda ba."
   "Dalla kiyi min shiru, munafuka sai na gaya mata, me yasa tun a makaranta baki nuna mata kina son shi ba? Sai yanzun. Ai naji kome da yadda kika b'ata musu gurin liyyafan su.

  Nanah Yar Wazirin Daura Kanwar Ummina ta haifeta, sannan itama Sarah zanci Ubanta, dan nasan halinta, zata iya han..."

  Kifa mata Mari nayi, jikina yana tsuma, kallonta nayi na kuma kifa mata mari.
"Kinsan me yasa na mare ki! Ba dan kaina ba, sai dan Sarah idan kika Kuskura kika yi wani mugun abu akanta, sai na shayar dake mamaki, a karan kaina ni matsoraciya ce, amma akan Sarah ban san da wani zuciyar zan fada miki ba.

        Sannan ita yar Wazirin, ta zata gidan Jaamal haka zata shigo shi babu matsala toh i. Dai ina raye, sai na sanya Sarah ta zama tauraruwa a cikin gidan, yadda ke baki isa ko Nanah ba, sai na sauya miki kamani, idan Ni kika tab'a zan baki hakurin dan ita kanta rayuwar bata da yawa.

           Amma Maryam Adamu Yakubu! Toh numfashina fansa ce ga nata, idan kin fasa idda kudirinki ke din Shegiya ce ba yar halak ba. Kuma kinyi na farko kinyi na karshe.".

  Daga haka na barta a gurin, inda ake tarar mota naje na tsaya, ina ji kamar na koma na mata mugun duka

5️⃣2️⃣
Tsaki naja a hankali, ina goge kwalla da take zubo min, na kuma hararar inda take tsaye kamar an dasa gunki, shiga motar nayi ina me kuma gargad'in ta sa ido.

Sake baki tayi tana kallona har muka bar gurin.

....
Har na isa inda nake koyan girkin raina bai huce ba, ba haka dai nayi na gama, karfe biyu na dawo gida na samu anyi baki. Baki a gidan, tunda na shiga naga wani hadaddiyar mota,

Mai tambarin hukumar custom, ina shiga da sallama naji gidan ya kaure da wani kamshin turaren ya daki hancina.

D'ago kai yayi tare da suke idanun shi a kaina, idanun mu cikin juna.

Kauda kai nayi cikin jin kunya, yadda ya tsare ni da ido.
"Ina wuni Abba?!"

Murmushi baban Aliyah yayi sannan yace min.
"Lafiya lau? Janny, ya karatun?"

Karamar murmushi nayi sannan nace.
"Alhamdulillah!" 
A sace na gaida bakon bai kalli inda nake ba ya amsa, yana wasa da hannun Khalid.

Cikin rigima Muhsina ta iso gurina, Ni kuma ina ƙoƙarin cire takalmina.
"Yaya Janny! Kicewa Khalid ya bani abuna."

Dan kallon Khalid nayi idanun na ya sauka akan mutumin shima yana kallona, mikewa nayi na bar falon, jikina na rawa. Dan ina jin kallon shi har cikin kaina.

Ina barin gurin ya kalli Abban Aliyah yace.
"Mr Aryan! Itace wacce Muhsina take cewa sai ta faɗa mata ta zane ku."

Ajiyar zuciya ya sauke, sannan ya kalli Abba yace.
"Yarinyar tayi min, da fatan baku mata miji ba"

Cikin wani irin jin dadi, Abban Aliyah yace Mishi.
"Alhamdulillah! Lallai Addu'ar Umma Allah ya amsa. Kana son Jannart!"

Lumshe idanunshi yayi sannan ya sauke akan hanyar da nabi.
"Jannah! Sunan ya dace da mai sunan. Tayi min amma zanje Sokoto na gayawa Sultan."

Ya fada a hankali, dan muryan shi kamar bazai iya magana ba, sabida sanyin murya.

Mr Aryan Sultan shine da na fari a gurin Muhammad Kabir na uku, sarkin Sokoto. Tun bayan rasuwar matar shi Fhadelah yaki aure kusan shekaru goma sha biyu kenan.

Abinka da gidan sarauta cewa akayi fatalwar matar shi ce take hana shi aure.

An nima Mishi mata daga gidajen sarautu har gidan manyan malamai da attajirai.

Amma fir yaki su, yau ganin Jannart, dan haka wani irin farin ciki ne ya kama Abban Aliyah, ya kasa hakuri ya kira Mama ya gaya mata.

Kirjinta ne yayi mugun bugawa, tayi murna amma idan ta tuna da alo tsiya alo danja, yasa jikinta yayi sanyi.

Dan haka ina sallah ta shigo, zama tayi har na idar ina gamawa ta kalle ni.

Abinci ta mika min tasani a gaba na fara ci, tana min magana jifa jifa.

Ina idarwa tace min.
"Jannart! Shin kina son Samim?!"

Kallon ta nayi sannan na sunkuyar da kaina kasa, ina wasa da kasa.
"Ki buɗe bakinki kiyi min magana,idan kina son shi toh idan kuma bai miki ba, Abokin Abban ku, yace yana sonki."

Zaro idanu nayi waje, ina me tsananin tsoron maslahar da zai Kunno Kai.

"Amma! kuma Kawu."
"Zan sab'a miki! Dan haka ga mr Aryan Sultan, yana da kirki mutumin mun zauna dashi a kaduna, kafin suka bar can, matar shi tana da kirki amma ta rasu, da yarinyar su Aanih Fatimah Khatoon.

Yau shekaru goma sha biyu da wani abu rabon aji yayi maganar mace! Amma yace sunan ya dace da mai sunan! Yana sonki bana wasa ba.

Ki amshi tayin shi zaki ji dadi dan ana kyautata zaton cewa shine magajin Sarkin Sokoto.

Amma idan Samim ne ba damuwa"

Ban iya magana ba, sai dai a gaskiya Ni bana son wannan hadin amma kodan kar na watsa musu kasa a ido, na amince.
"Ya tafi da saka ran amincewarki don Allah karki watsa mana kasa a idanu. Kuma ki fahimce bamu da niyyar cutar da ke ba.

Mutum ne mai addini uwa uba, kamala duk cikin manyan custom babu wanda ya kai shi, ilimin addini.

Bani miki fatan auren inda zaki wulakanta min ke, don Allah ki amshi Mr Aryan domin mata da yawa sun nemi ya dube su amma basu ishe shi ba."

Haka tayi ta lallashina, har na amince amma maganar gaskiya Ni Kawu nake so.

Kuma na gaya mata gaskiya, ita kanta jikinta yayi sanyi.
"Jannart! Ya zaki yi hakuri zaki yi dan gidan Jamal babu inda zaki shiga.

Sannan idan kika yi haka shine zaki cikakkiyar mutum mai cika alqawari, idan kika yi haka Allah zai cika miki burinki.

Jaamal dan uwa ne, amma nafi sonki da Aryan sau dubu, shi wannan yana sonki ne, shi kuma Jamal kece kike son shi.

Yarima Aryan shine yake sonki ba tare da kin ce yayi miki ba. Amma bazan miki dole ba."

Tana gama fad'ar haka tayi ficewar ta, daga cikin ɗakin kifa kaina nayi akan cinyata, ina sake wani irin kuka.

Kasa fita nayi har dare ina nan zaune.

---
Daura.
Dukkan su a zaune, ya dauki takarda da alkalami ya shiga rubutu, musu.
*Gida* Lagos*

Ya rubuta Lagos daya gida guda uku, sannan ya kalle su kamar bazai magana ba, yace.

"Ga kuri'a nan, duk wacce ta dauki Lagos da ita zan tafi idan kuma ta zab'i gida shi kenan."

Shiru suka yi ya gama nad'e takardan sannan ya watsa musu, ya zauna tare da lumshe idanun shi.

Babu abinda yake hasashowa sai Jannart,zai je ya ganta. Ji yaƙe kamar yayi tsuntsu amma babu halin hakan.

Haka suka tai ruwan ido, har dai karshe suka dauka dan tunda ya zuba Sarah dauki daya ta koma gefe tana kallon su.

Bayan sun gama dauka ya umarce su da su bude, jikin su na rawa, kowacce tana fatan ita ta samu.

Murmushi Sarah tayi bayan ta bude nata tace.
"Allah ya dawo daku lafiya Allah ya tsare, Ubangiji ya bada abinda ake je nima."

Tana gama faɗar haka, tabar falon cikin nutsuwa wanda hakan ya jefa sauran abokan zamanta a cikin wannan yanayin na mamaki wato ita bazaka tab'a nuna damuwarta akan su ba.

Binta yayi da ido, ihu Nanah tayi tare da tsalle, tun bata fada ba Balkisu da Yahanasu suka bar gurin.

"Ya Jaamal, baka ce kome ba."

Sai a lokacin ya zuba mata ido, dan duk cikin matan shi yafi su rawan kai, sab'anin Sarah da take da da tsage gaskiya kome girman ka, idan kayi kuskure zata gaya maka.

Balkisu kuwa bata hum balle hmm, Ita dai damuwar ta zuba karance-karance dukda bayinta suna mata hira amma mafiya lokuta zai sameta tana duba irin su ruwan bagaja.

Yahanasu kuwa ita ta dauke su baki daya dan mulki take ji da shi, ga mugun iko, amma gwana ce gurin kwalliya, da son a girmamata, sai dai duk da kwalliyarta idan Sarah tayi duk sai su zama yan kallo.

Kasancewar ita tafisu yarinta, kuma bata da damuwar da zata aza a ranta, dan kishi kan Yahanasu tana nuna musu Sosai.

Abinda bai sani ba, Sarah na kishin sa, kawai bata damu ta nuna a gaban sauran bane, dan ta lura sabida nuna zakewarta, idan suna zaune tanka narke a jikin shi, suna hira. Haka yayi matuƙar sakasu jin haushin ta.

Su gani suke kamar rashin kunya ce, bayan ita kuma yanayin tane, son jiki matukar zata zauna da kai Toh sai ta nukurkusaka.

Mikewa yayi bai tsaya a gurinta ba yace.
"Jibi zamu tafi, ki shirya da wuri dan Ni bana jira."

Dakin Balkisu ya shiga ya sameta zaune da wasu littattafai tana dubawa, a jikin kofar ya tsaya sannan yace.
"Kiyi hkr! Sannan duba abinda zaki bukata na wata guda."

"Hmm!" Tace Mishi, shima bai damu ba, yayi ficewar shi.

Ya shiga dakin Yahanasu, yadda tayi kicin-kicin da fuska koda yayi mata magana bata kalle shi ba, balle ta amsa Mishi. Duk yadda yaso yayi mata magana taki bashi fuska.

Dole ya fita daga d'akin, koda ya shiga dakin Sarah, tana ganin shi ta sake murmushi tana cewa.
"Kawu akwai kayan Aminiyata, sannan akwai nasu Mama idan zaka yarda ka kai musu."

Nan ta shiga fidda kayan, tana ajiye mishi, nuna mishi tare da mishi bayanin yadda zai kaiwa kowa nashi.

Tsabar ta burge shi, bai san lokacin da ya rungume ta, ta baya ba, yana me daura kan shi a kafad'arshi......

5️⃣3️⃣

Kifa kanshi yayi a wuyarta, yana fadin.
"Nagode sosai! Da abinda kika bawa Ruh.... Nagode sosai abinda kika bawa Rahainah duk duniya bani da wacce nake so sama da ita.

   Darajarta daban yaƙe a zuciyata, idan akwai sunan da ya dace na kirata dashi bai wuce abincin ruhina."

     Janye jiki ta tayi da sauri tana kallon fuskar shi, hawayen da suka cika mata idanu ta shanye shi, sannan ta sake murmushi tana me juyawa tana hada kayan gurin.

            Kunyarta ce ta kama shi, na kasa boye son da yakewa Jannart,
      
Rike hannun shi tayi tana murmushin da bata kai ciki ba tace.
"Maman Aliyah ce abincin ruhinka? Hmm kawunmu kana son wata ce kake boye min?!"

              Diriricewa yayi yana niman abinda zai kare kanshi cikin sauri ya janyota jikinshi, tare da matseta a bango, yana kallon fuskarta.

          Sauke kanta tayi tana murmushi tace.
"Wacece mai sa'a haka da tayi dacen ka kirata da abincin ruhinka?

            Lallai na tayata murna, sabon namijin duniya irinka da kake iya kiranta abincin ruhinka a gaban karamar Matarka da kake yar nanjane da ita. 

       Kawu gaya min gaskiya akwai wacce ka fada soyayyarta amma ta subuce maka"

    Tsare shi tayi da ido, jan kumatun yayi sannan yace..
"Babu macen da nake so, sai dai nasan nan gaba zan sameta, ko nace na sameta"

     Cikin raha tace Mishi.
"Haba! Dukda nasan mu din zabin Manyan mu ne, amma ina tayaka murna babu gurinda zaka saka mana wata, dan haka ka ajiye tsohuwar soyayyarka, ka gwada matanka kaga abin mamaki. Ya kace"
     
Ta karshe Maganar tare da d'aga mishi giranta baki daya.

Sake baki yayi yana girmama al'amarin ta.

      So da kauna, na Jannart ce, amma farin cikin rayuwar shi Sarah, zai iya dai-daita kanshi idan yana gaban Sarah, Amma baya jin zai iya dai-daita kanshi a gaban Jannart, domin a duk lokacin da yake tare da ita, toh baya tuna kome sai ita, shi yasa idan yaganta a kusada shi yana son yayita Bata Haushi tana kuka.

            Idan yaga hawayenta yasan ba kukan b'acin rai bane, kukan soyayyar shi da ta samu mazauni a birnin zuciyarta ce.

   Wani irin murmushi ya sauke tare da rungume Sarah Yana dariya, mutumin da magana damun shi take, amma shigowar Jannart ya sanya shi zama.

   Mai yawan Tankiya, shigowar Jannart rayuwar shi ta sanya shi zama mara hakuri,  amma Sarah shigowarta ya Sanya shi fahimtar alkhairorin da suke tare da ita, ya kuma fahimci yadda dimbin kyawawan halayenta.

         Takan iya maida b'acin ranta izuwa farin cikin da ba kowa zai fahimci ainihin b'acin rai ba.

           Dan haka bazai tsallake,ta ba zai wanzan da fahimtar juna a tsakanin su, ta hanyar zama tsuntsiya madaurinki daya.
    Bai yi kasa a gwiwa ba, ya shiga niman zaren sadarwan da zata sadashi da duk wata damar da tasamu akanta, sai dai kash.

   Bai kai ga isa ma ajiyar ba, ta rike hannun shi cikin kuka mai tsima zuciya ta bude bakinta dakyar tace.
"Yarima Jamal! Kayi hakuri karka rabani da kimata! Bayan zuciyarka tana ga wani gurin, don Allah karka tab'a ni.

              Har yau mahaifina yana kukan mutuwar mahaifiyana, sabida irin wannan auren, duk yadda taso ya nuna mata soyayyar shi yaki, nima haka kake buƙatar na rayu da bakin cikin rashin soyayyar gaskiya sai ta soyayyar gangan jiki da gajiyawa.

        Kawu ko sau daya ne, ka dube mu da soyayya kabi damu da soyayya, wallahi ko a ina ka nemi hakkinka, zan baka Kawu kayi hakuri. Bazan iya rayuwar da iya gangan jikina itace muradinka ba.

      Kaji a ranka ko sau daya ne, ka sanyani a Shashin masoyanka toh anan zaka fahimci komai."

      Janye jikin shi yayi yana me sauke ajiyar zuciya cikin wani irin kunyar da ya kasa hada idanu da ita, karshe haka haka ya fice a d'akin.

Ina bazata iya daukar wannan kasadar ba, bazai tab'a kaunar su ba, sabida zuciyarshi yana wani gurin na daban.

.... Amma itama zata wahala idan ta yarda ta bashi damar shimfida rayuwar shi da ita, tunda bata fahimci meye yake bukata akan su ba.

    Kawai an bashi sune ba tare da ya san darajar su ba. Ita kuma bazata iya wannan gangancin ba,  a duk lokacin da yasan meye banbancin dake tare da so da muradin, bawai amsa da sha'awa ba.
      Bawai dan tana don shi ta bashi damar yayi yadda yake so da ita ba.

       Bayan fitar shi, cikin jin kunya ta shiga niman kayanta, tana suturta jikinta, a hankali wani irin murmushi  ya kwace mata, dan koda ta shiga ban daki. Murmushi take sakewa a lokaci zuwa lokaci.

                 Musamman da ta kalli kanta a mudubi, daga wuyarta zuwa kirjinta, jikina bayanta tayi da bango wani irin son shi take mai cike da farin ciki. 

   Tasan bata manta ranar da ta faɗa soyayyar shi ba, domin kuwa tana rike da wannan ranar, tun a ranar ta fahimci zuciyarshi baya tare da shi.

                       Har izuwa lokacin tana jin hannun shi a jikinta, wani irin yanayi ne ya kuma riskanta, wanda bata san meye zata kirashi ba.

     Ta hana shi kanta, ba tana nufin take hakkin shi bane, a'a tayi hakan ne dan niman soyayyarta a idanun shi.

            Amma yadda yake jagorancin zuciyarta bata jin gobe idan ya nime ta zata ki ba.

                  So daya tak take mishi, kuma bata jin zata iya sakacin rashinsa.

     .....
Kwana biyu wasan b'oya suke bata kuma ganin shi ba, sai ranar da zai tafi.
        Da asuba tana kwance bayan ta iddar da sallar asuba, ya shigo ya same ta kwance.

            Janye bargon yayi yana kallon kyakyawan fuskarta wanda take motsa shi a hankali saboda kunyar shi.  Kwanciya yayi yana kallon fusakarta, cikin sauri ta kifa hannuwanta a fuskarta tana sauke murmushi.

"Ina kwana Kawu?!"

       Girgiza kai yayi shima yana jin dadin yadda take jin kunyar shi, janye hannun ta yayi yana me cewa.
"An gama wasan boyar? Janny!"

           Kallon shi tayi da sauri, sai kuma ta sake dariya tana cewa.
"Yau nice Jannyh? Lallai Kawu kayi kewar Jannart, don Allah Kawu kaje dani mana, Ni bazan zauna a gurinka ba.

     Ka kaini gurin Janny! Kaji"

  Yadda tayi mishi fuskar mage sai da tasanya shi dariya, yace.
"Ko me zaki yi bazan tafi dake ko ina ba, kin manta ka'idar gidan sarauta ne? Indai Janny ce Insha Allah zaki ganta, kinji."

     Gyada kai tayi kwalla na zuba mata, janyota yayi jikinshi. Yana jin yadda zuciyarta ke bugawa itama tana jin bugun nashi, kamshin turaren jikin shi ta shaka, ta kuma jin kaunar shi ta kara samun mafakar siyasa a zuciyar ta.

                        (Banda guntun munafunci! Mutumin da yace bazai yi kome da Kawar Yarshi wai shine yake 😜)
   
Sosai yake gaya mata, wani Yaren mutanen, wanda dama tsaf take ya amshi tayin shi, amma me tana tunawa da cewa shi da baya sonta, kamar yadda take son shi, kawai ta sanya mishi kuka.

           Rungume ta yayi yana shafa bayanta.

       "Bazan boye miki ba, koda ban ce ina sonki ba, tabbas farin cikina tare da ganinki, a duk lokacin da zan ganki ban tab'a ganin ki cikin damuwa ko b'acin rai ba.

           Ke karama ce, amma zuciyarki da tunaninki ta manyan ce.

               Kece kawai bana jin nayi amfani da damar da nake dashi na amshi abinda addinina ta wajabta min akai ba.

       Ba zance bana yinki ba, amma kedin wata shashi ce ta rayuwa ta.
      Ke rai ce ga rai biyu, raina da nata da kuma na wasu. Bazan nemi kome daga gare ki ba.

      Sai lokacin da kika gamsu din ina tare dake, amma fada a baki bazai gamsar dake ba.

  ............ Ke Amanata ce, kuma kece soyayyar Jannart, ke din Aminiyar Aminiyata ce, dan haka domin mu bawa zuciyoyin mu hakuri daga abinda muke so, muna iya sadaukar da Rayuwar mu domin amintarmu.

  .. Domin bawa aminan mu damar samun cikar Muradin su, muka ajiye abinda muke so mu bawa aminan mu, dan haka ke din rai uku ne a tare dake, kuma ki zama adila a duk lokacin da aka bukaci sadaukar da abinda yafi kome tsada a gurin ki. Zan tafi da fatan babu abinda kike bukata.

      Idan akwai Toh ki gayawa Aswad Insha Allah zai kawo muku, sannan yanzun zan kawo Umma tazo ta zauna daku, don Allah kar ki manta itama kamar uwa ce a gare ni, idan kinga zasu wulakantata ki tsaya min amadadina ki kare min ita.

               Duk sun ki saurara na amma ke kin bani damar haka, don Allah kiyi abinda nace. Insha Allah zan kasance dake har abada, zan jiyar dake kyakyawan soyayya. Kuma zan ginar da kaunarki a cikin birnin zuciyata. Nagode sosai."

     A hankali ya sumbaci goshinta, lumshe idanun ta tayi, cikin jin sabon kaunar shi yana ratsa zuciyar ta.

               A hankali ya janye ta daga jikin shi ya sauka a gadon juyawa yayi yaga tayi lumo, shafa fuskarta yayi yaji damshin hawaye. Kowa yayi ya kwanta tare da d'agota, kuka ne ya kwace mata ta rike shi sosai. Har jikina na rawa.

      Hura mata iskar bakin shi yayi ta yi a hankali, har ta fara sauke ajiyar zuciya, a hankali ya lallubi bakinta, zare ido tayi (Ja'ira 🏌🏽‍♂️😒 ya gama latsaki baki zare ido ba sai bakinki 😜🏌🏽‍♂️)
    
Hawayen ta ne ya tsananta zuba, lumshe idanun ta tayi, hucin kuka yana fita ta hancinta,(Yarinya Bakin ki ya ciwu🤣😂)

           Matse ta yayi sosai, tare da rike kanta. Ita kuwa tayi lakwas a jikinshi.
          Shi kanshi ya yarda, Sarah tana da wani kaso a zuciyarsa, domin kuwa duk cikin matan shi, itace kad'ai yayi iya wannan danyen aikin da ita. 

        A hankali ya janye bakin shi, sannan ya manna mata wani sumbatar goshi, kafin ya maida ita ya kwantar da ita.

       Tunda ya sauketa take shashekar kuka, a hankali yake shafa bayanta, har barci yayi gaba da Ita. Kallon bakinta yayi wadda suka yi ja, shafa bakin yayi sannan ya mike, ya fita daga dakin.

      Koda ya shiga falon shi, ya same Aswad ya cika fam yana cira, tsabar bakin ciki, kallon agogon hannun shi yayi.

  Tsaki yaja, kafin ya fara magana yana cewa.
"Tsabar Iskanci, kasan dai dole zan dawo daura an jima, shine ka shige dakin Sarah ka zauna, ka manta da kabar Nanah tana jiranka."

        D'aga mishi kafada yayi, tare da shigewa, yayi wanka tare da sauya kayan shi sannan ya fito.

   Da Manyan akwatinan shiga, Aswad baya shiga fita da masa dashi, haka duk suka fito. Har su Yahanasu, juyawa yayi bai ganta ba, ya nufi cikin gidan. Yayi sallama da Abba da Umma Fulani, sannan yaje shashin bayi ya dauko Umma da kanshi ya kawo ta dakin da Bayin Sarah suke.
"Umma zan tafi! Ga amanar gidana a hannunki keda Aswad."

Murmushi tayi tare da rusunawa tace.
"Insha Allah! Ranka shi dad'e. Allah ya dawo da kai lafiya, Ubangiji ya kaika lafiya."

    "Amin Ya Allah!" Ya fada sannan ya fita daga d'akin, ya nufi dakin Sarah wacce take barci cikin salama da nutsuwa, kyau tayi mishi sosai ya tsaya yana kallon yadda take barci.

            Nutsuwata da Yarintar ta yake hangowa, tare da tsantsan hakurinta. Sai dai yasan duk da haka bata kai Jannart ba, dan Allah yayi mata baiwar hakuri da tawwakali.

      Shi kansa yasan da Allah ya nufa Jannart tana cikin matan shi tabbas da yayi sa'a, domin yayi imani zasu zauna cikin salama da aminci. Ba tare da nuna zafin kishin ta ba...

5️⃣4️⃣
      Sam ya manta abinda yake gaban shi, ya sanyata a gaba yana jin arba'in shi bai kyauta mata ba, amma ya zai yi baccinta shine mafi alkhairi.

   Da sauri ya fita ya sami Nanah ta cika fam, kamar fulawa bai damu ba ya shiga motar shi matan shi babu wacce tayi mishi a dawo lafiya.

    Tunda suka bar Masarautar ya soma tunowa da Sarah da take barci, yarinyar tana da halin dattako.

       Cire hular kanshi yayi ya shafa sumar shi a hankali, sannan ya kurawa gefen hanya idanu.
                 ---
Karfe biyu da rabi suka isa Kano, a hankali ya juya ya kalli Nanah da take ta barci, bai tasheta ba. Har suka isa tashar jirgin kasa.

  Dan shima yana masifar son jirgin kasa, kamar wani renon Ingila (England)
             Tunda suka isa Shi da Aswad suka gama kome, sannan aka dauki kayan su zuwa cikin jirgin. Kallon shi Nanah tayi riko hannunta yayi, har suka shiga cikin jirgin ya zaunar da ita, a kujeran da ke kusada shi....

           ---
Lagos.
     Tunda Mama tayi min maganar Yarima Aryan na kasa sukuni, kome nake zaka ga rashin kuzarina, dama sanyin haline dani, sai gashi ya kuma haduwa da rashin sukuni.

               Muna aiki da ita dan tace min wai zai zo cin abincin dare a gidan, miyar kuka nake kad'awa ita kuma tana kwashe tuwon Shinkafar.
  
Munyi nisa, cikin aikin na tsinkayo muryan ta tana faɗin.
"Ba muyi miki dole ba, idan baki son Aryan babu wanda ya takura miki.

   Sai a yi hakuri da gaban naki ai gwara gaban na hana. Ai da ace ba ayi da kai gwara anayi dakai koda baza a kasa da kai ba.

    Dan haka tunda bai miki ba, zan bawa Abban ku hakuri, ya mishi magana ta fahimtar juna.

      Insha Allah zai hakura dan sai fahimce shi."

      Shiru tayi taga yadda na tsaya da aikin, na d'ago kaina a sannu sannan na sake mata murmushi nace mata..
"Insha Allah! Bazaki same ni a me bijirewa iyayena ba, ki tayani da addu'a Allah ya bani hakuri da dangan..."

       Dawowa tayi kusa dani, ta rungume ni.
"Allah yayi miki albarka, sai dai mun ce mishi zamu yi magana da Uban goyan ki, dan haka  baki da damuwa Jaamal kuwa ki rabu dashi. Zamu ji dashi."

     Ajiyar zuciya na sauke tare da rike hannunta, ina son yin kuka amma bana jin

      Nayi hakan dan tamkar butulci ne daga gare ni, murmushi tayi sannan tace.
"Insha Allah! Zaki yi alfahari da auren shi bazaki yi kaico da zab'in mu ba."

      Haka muka gama abincin muna hira sama sama, amma bawai ina jin dadin hiran bane, kawai ina biye mata ne dan kar su ce bani jin magana.

   Muna gamawa, muka fito da abincin aka shirya sannan naje nayi wanka, Mama ta shirya ni cikin wani amfar wax mai yakuwa, tare da yafa min babban mayafi. A hankali na isa falon dan har sun gama cin abincin su.

           Tunda na shiga da sallama, na zauna tare da takura kaina, na saka kaina a saman gwewata, tare da lumshe idanuna.

       Shiru yayi yana kallon yadda na takura kaina.
"Yan mata!"
     D'ago kaina nayi muka kalli juna sannan na sunkuyar da kaina, ina wasa da mayafin kaina.
"Ina da shekaru talatin da shida aka aura min Fareeda! Dukda auren iyayen mu, dai dai naji dadin zama da ita.

     Tana da matuƙar hakuri da juriya, dan Ni kaina nasan tayi hakuri da ni, kasancewar Ni ba mai yawan shige mata ba.

       A hankali soyayya da shakuwa ya shiga cikin alamarin mu, kinsan abin tausayi."

   D'ago kai nayi ina kallon shi, murmushi yayi sannan ya kurbi ruwan zobon dake hannun shi, ya cigaba da cewa.
"Sai da muka so juna, yadda duniya babu wanda zai iya ganewa haɗa mu aka yi, sannan Allah ya amshi Fareeda.

    Ranar da ta haifa min Anih Fatimah Khatoon, tun daga ranar na koma Maraya, iyayena suna raye amma ni, rayuwata ta tafi."

        Ya karshe maganar yana goge kwallar dake cikin idanun shi.
         Shiru ne ya gifta sosai, kafin ya katse shirun, da tambaya na.
"Shekarunki nawa?"

         Duk ya sanya min tausayin shi, a hankali nace.
"Sha takwas!"
        
"Zaki iya auren tsoho mai shekaru arba'in da shida."
      
A karon farko da na d'ago na kalle shi cikin mamaki, sannan na maida kaina kasaka ina wasa da mayafina.
  
Bance kome ba, sai murmushin da ta kwace min ya bani dariya, murmushi nayi. Na kasa magana.

    "Kinga fa duba har furfura na fara tarawa. Zaki zauna da Baba tsoho"

               Dariya ce ta kama ni, dan cikin lokaci ƙalilan ya samu shiga bawai dan ina son shi ba, sai dai yadda yake abin sai ya bani dariya, kuma ita zuciya tana da wanna fahimtar, dukda akwai Saba'in ra'ayi. Dan Kawuna ne a a manne cikin zuciyata.

   Sai dai samun Yarima Aryan Sultan zan iya maneji dashi sabida Gidan Kawuna babu mafakar siyasa, koda kuwa zan samu, toh zan amshi tayi Yarima Aryan sabida wanda yafito ya nuna maka  so da kulawa, yafi wanda kake nunawa so da kulawa.

    Tunda yaga yadda nake ta dariya da yake ce min naga furfuran shi, girma yazo mishi gashi ya tsufa a fage, ga gwauronta.

          Yaji dad'i sosai, dan bai yi tsammanin zan sake nayita dariya haka ba, sai gashi ina dariya har da satar kallon shi.

"Toh Gimbiya Jannah shin kina sona a tsofana?!"
Ya tambaye ni, juyawa kalmar so nayi a hankali na tashi, sai da na kai bakin kofa, nace.
"Na amince!"

      "Son fa"
     Cak na tsaya bayan na d'aga labulen dakin, a hankali na juya tare da nad'e hannuna a kirjina nace.
"Zan zauna da kai! Ina ga iya haka ma ya wadatar, domin ita soyayya ta mai rabo ce, wanda ya fara ya dauke abinshi amma zuwan ka ya sani fahimtar mutane sosai, da kuma yadda suka dauki duniyoyin su.

            Idan nace ina sonka na yaudareka ne, amma zan iya kare rayuwata da kai, domin kai ne farin cikin da na rasa.

       Idan kayi hakuri zan soka wata rana,.kamar yadda kaso Fareeda, zan kaunace ka wata rana kamar yadda ka kaunaci Fareeda.

            Ita so bata bada damar fahimtar sakon Zuci, amma farin cikin da aka ginata tun ranar farko itace mafi girman Soyayyar, Abban Anih zan zauna da kai ko dan Anih, mu taru mu rufawa tsohon Abbanta asiri dan tsofa ya kawo masa farmaki."

Ina fadar haka nayi maza na fita ina dariya shima kuma yana murmushi, ina fitowa na hango Mamah a kicin, da sauri Naje na rungume bayanta.

  Ina murmusa mata, tare da ce mata.
"Na amince, mutumin yana da kirki da abin dariya!"
Juyowa tayi tare da rike hannuna.
"Alhamdulillah! Abinda nake son ji kenan, Insha Allah bazaki yi danasanin akan shi ba."

      Muna cikin tattaunawa Abban Aliyah yayi gyaran murya, fita tayi suka yi magana can ta dawo cikin wani farin ciki tace min.
"Kije Zai tafi!"

   Kunya ce ta hanani tafiya ina makale a jikin bango. Turo ni tayi har bakin kofa, 

     Yana bakin kofar fita daga gidan, kafar shi daya a ciki daya a waje.

           A hankali nake ta kowa har inda yake, na dan ja tunga na tsaya, ina murmushi kaina a sunkuye.

      "Ranki shi dad'e! Zan samu raki'a kuwa?!"
     Nuna mishi hanya nayi, ya fita nima na fito, muka jera dashi har gurin motar shi, dake ya kware abin dariya, muna zuwa gurin motar shi ya kama jan dan gashin dake gashin bakin shi yana fadin.
"Taya ni duba furfuran dan ina son zama matashi! Wanda zai jiyar da zuciyarki nutsuwa"

     Rufe fuskana nayi ina dariya, sosai, wato bayan Samim ashe akwai wanda zai ce yana soni ya kuma mai da kanshi irina.

  Bayan motar ya bude, sannan ya fito min da wata jaka, ya mika min.

   Girgiza kai Nayi cikin murmushi, sannan nace.

"A'ah karka fara bani, nagode bana amsar abin hannun wani sabida."
      Har gabana ya tako, kamar zai shige jikina yace.
"Kalle ni, ban tab'a kyauta anki k'arba ba, dan haka amshe shi, domin Ni bani da abinda zanyi dashi, koda bazaki amfani dashi ba, ki bawa wanda yayi miki, amma ki sani haka da kika yi ya kuma kara miki mukami a zuciyata.

    Ina son mace mara kwaɗayi da hangen nesa, ina son macen da abin hannun namiji bai ishe ta kallo ba, ina son macen da tasan darajar kanta. Wacce bazata dauki reni ba. Kuma wannan dabi'ar bata samuwa sai mace ta kame kanta, ta nunawa namiji ita bata ra'ayin abinda yake hannun shi.

           A cikin yan mintoti kin gama da zuciyata, tabbas zan zauna dashi, koda kuwa bazan tab'a son shi ba.

  Wani irin kwalla ne suke cika idanuna, yadda yake ta min abin dariya, kuma bai damu ba, sai dai yadda yake ganin dariya yafi mishi kome dad'i.

    Ya rasa masoyar shi a lokacin da yake bukatar ta, yau ya same ni dan haka bai yi kwauron baki ba ya faɗa min abinda yake zuciyar shi.

     Ya kuma nuna min ya shirya tsaf domin ni.

"Nagode sosai! Ka sanyani farin ciki, a cikin yan mintuna, ka sanyani jin Ni wata ce, ka sanyani fahimtar ba kowa keda Sab'anin Ra'ayin so ba.

        Naji irin farin cikin da kowa yake ji, naji dadin da kowa yake ji, nutsuwa tashigo min cikin rayuwata,

     
     Nagode Abban Anih! Ban san me zan biya ka dashi ba, amma ina mai yarda da zuciyata zan zauna da kai na bauta maka har karshen rayuwata."

      Ina fadar haka nayi maza na bar gurin, bawa mai gadin kayar yayi, shi ma ya tafi. Zuciyar shi a wanke fesss..

         ..... Kayan Mama take dubawa, duk kusan kayan kwalliya ce, tare da kayan ciye ciye, irin na gwangwani. (Wato cookie biscuit, sai chocolat) dukkan su a cikin gwangwani, 

     Murmushi Mama tayi tace.
"Ban son niman ya fara da kyauta haka ba, naso sai anyi nisa ya samu soyayyar ki, amma Alhamdulillah, yanzun kema zuciyarki zata sami nutsuwa da sallama.

    Ki kuma nunawa Kawunki ba shi daya bane iya kai makura, akwai dubun shi, Ai dama na gaya miki idan kika yi hakuri Allah zai shiga al'amarin ki, gashi nan ya tabbata.

                   Dan haka karki ga dan ya ajiye shekarun shi kice zaki rena shi, bazan lamunta ba, dan zamu iya rikitowa daga sama mu diro kasa, yana da kirki kuma da kike ganin shi. Ogan Abbanku ne, amma kingan shi ya mai da kanshi kamar Abbanku ne gaba dashi a Office. Gashi duk wanda ya zauna dashi bazai tab'a dana sani ba.

              Wannan halayyar shi yasan ya, Inyamurar nan da wasu kabilun suke shakkar shi, duk abinda ya zama kashe mu raba baya yarda ya saka kanshi ciki, idan arziki ne Allah ya bashi domin mahaifin su kafin ya zama sarkin Sokoto sai da ya rike ministan man fetur, daga baya aka bashi jakadancin najeriya a kasar Saudiyya.

            Yaso da ilimi addini da na boko, yana da kanne maza da mata, kasancewar gidan su mata hudu ne da kwarkwarori.

       Mahaifiyarshi itace matar Sultan na farko kuma auren zumunta aka musu, shi yasa matar shi Fareeda aka musu auren zumunta.

     Idan kika ga zaman su lokacin suna kaduna, sai kin ji sun burge ki. Shi yasa data rasu ta ya.......

5️⃣5️⃣

Yadda take bani labarin, rayuwar shi da matar shi sai ya bani tausayi, musamman yadda matar ta bada rayuwarta da kome nata akan shi, amma bai sanya shi ji soyayyarta.

    Shi yasa data rasu sai da yayi jinya sosai, ya zama ko mace ya gani yana matuƙar girmama al'amarin ta.

     Ya kuma dauki soyayyar duniyar nan ya daurawa Anih Fatimah khatoon. Yana matukar son Yarinyar kamar me.

            Haka kawai naji a raina, zan iya kare rayuwata dashi, a hankali na isa kafad'arta na sauke kaina ina ina murmushi na rufe fuskana nace.

      "Mama! Zan zauna dashi a haka."

     Dariya tayi sosai sannan tace.
"Allah ya shirya min ke! Nima zanyi farin cikin kasancewar ki dashi.

         Aryan Sultan, kiyi hakuri da abinda bakina zai fada amma iya gaskiya zan fada miki, Aryan karshe ne. Domin kuwa Mutum ne da yasan ciwon kan shi, ya kuma san darajar Mutane.

       Kozan kwana na wuni bazaki fahimci kome ba, kiyi ƙoƙarin gano abinda yake da ra'ayi akai."

Kusan har muka gama aikin kome sannan na wuce d'akinmu.

        Tunda na zauna nake nazarin abinda Mama ta gaya min. Shiru nayi ina nazarin fuskar shi. Wanda banga kome akai na sai nagartan shi. Da kamala.

            Na hango abubuwa da lokacin bazai bani damar nayi dogon magana akai ba, amma gaskiya ya hadu ba karya Aryan Fari ne, dogo mai matsakaicin jiki.
            Fuskar shi dogo ce, wacce ta kawatu da dogon hanci, tare da wani saje wanda ya haɗa da yar gashin bakin shi.

              Ajiyar zuciya na sauke, tare da jin wani irin abu ya tsaya min, da na tuna yanayin shi da Kawu na.

                       ---
Karfe goma na safe, su Kawu suka iso, daga tashar jirgin motar ma'aikatar su tazo daukar su.

   Tunda ya shiga, ya zauna kamar wanda aka mishi wani laifi, ya fara cin magani. 

        Karfe sha daya da rabi suka isa gidan su wanda yake, Agege.

      Gidan ya haɗu sosai, domin kuwa akwai kome a cikin gidan, dan haka abinda ya fara, zuwa yayi ya nima musu abinda zasu ci.

"Dukkan su Ahaline daga masarautun mun, kiyayyewa da kulawa ya tabbata akan su.

              Kulawa da lafiyar su itace abu na gaba da ya dace su fara yi, Kodan na na fito gidan da babu Uwata ce, ya sani na fahimci gidan sarautar mu, duk wata kyautar da za a baka matukar ka kere darajar abin.

       Abinda Bahaushe yace.
Ka duba rigar wuyar mutum kafin ka amshi kyautar shi.

               Bazan iya ce maka nasan mafarin al'amarin ba, amma zan iya ce maka kwaɗayin daukaka da niman sunan ya sanya su fadawa halin da suke ciki, Ka gafarce ni"

         Shiru yayi yana kallon ta, yarinya karama tayi maganar da hankalin kowa zai ɗauka, lumshe idanunshi yayi sannan ya dauke akanta, kafin yace.
"Kenan! Akwai abinda kika fahimta dangane halin da...."

    "Ina ban fahimci kome ba, asalima ban san kome ba, kawai nayi wannan yar maganar ne sabida a gano bakin zaren, kasancewar na cika katsalandan."

         Ta faɗa mishi, tare da kallon kasa, saukowa yayi daga gadon ya kishingida a matashin, suna fuskanta juna, kauda fuskarta tayi  tana sake murmushi.

         Juyo da fuskar ta yayi, lumshe idanun ta tayi. Cizon bakin ta yayi, a razane ta bude ido tana me kumbura bakinta.

          Dariya yayi yana wuyarta zuwa bayanta.

              Tsigar jikin tane ya shiga tashi, lokaci guda ta shiga ƙoƙarin kwace kanta, riketa gam yayi yana me cigaba da abinda yake ganin zai dai-daita musu rayuwa.

           Da tafiya yayi nisa, nan ya sauya musu. Gurin daga kasa zuwa saman gadon....

    (😜😜😜 Alqur'an bani dauko rahoto sai kome ya kammala, zan leka Ohh dai)

          Bayan wasu lokuta sannan ya janye daga jikinta, yana me sauke ajiyar zuciya, kallon yadda ta koma yayi tausayi ta bashi, dan taso mishi gardama, shi kuma ya sakar mata karfi, ya dagargaji yar nima.

                A daren ya taimaka mata, sai da ta kimtsa kanta, amma kana ganinta a yadda take kasan ta gurju. Sabida rigima da tasanya mishi, a zaune ya kwana.  Ga shegen shagwaɓa da ta tattaro dukkan ta daura mishi,(🤭😜 Haka Aunty Sa'a tace wai na cika sakalci 🙄😔)

             Dakyar ya lallabata, suka yi barci. Washi gari da kansa ya kaita ban daki ya taimaka mata dan tafiyar safiya zasu yi.

          Dama ya-ya lafiyar giwa?! Balle tayi hauka, Sarah da bata kaunar wani Abu ya tab'a lafiyarta!

  .......
A daddafe suka shirya, inda ya sami Aswad yana jiran su, tunda ya fito rungume da ita, sauran matan suke kallon shi. A gurguje yayi musu sallama,

       
       Bayan motar ya sakata,  cikin gidan ya nufa, yayi sallama da Abba tare da Fulani, yana fitowa ya tura aka kira Mishi Umma dan baya jin zai iya barinta a gidan ba.

    Dan haka ya sanyata a mota, suka nufi kano.

          Gurin Jamlah ya ajiye ta, sannan suka nufi Lagos...

                  Gefe yaja Aswad ya gaya mishi abinda yake faruwa, da irin halayyar da nake mishi, sosai Amininshin yaji zafin abinda muka mishi dan haka yayi mishi alƙawarin zai zo.

Lagos..
"Me Kyau! Ki gaya min meke damunki? Me kyau bana son ganin ki cikin damuwa?"

Share kwallar idanuna nayi, sannAn nace Mishi.
"Kawai! A dakatar da wannan al'amari ne"

Kura min ido yayi, cikin damuwa sannan ya dawo kusadani ya zauna.
"Kinsan duk wanda ya fito cikin babban gida ba a sanshi da  magana biyu ba, Janny idan kina ganin dole zasu miki gaya min gaskiya zan miki adalci."

     Yadda yayi maganar a tausashe ya sani jin matukar Kunyan shi.
   
Sake mai-maita min abinda yace yayi, a sanyayye na girgiza mishi kaina.

    Murmushi yayi sannan ya gyara zama inda ya daura kafa daya akan daya, yana murmushi yace min.

"D'ago ki kalle ni, Jannart"

     Duk sai na rasa yadda zan kalle shi.
"Wato zaki iya fadan abu amma bazaki iya kallona ba, wannan aka yi auren ai zaki cuci rayuwata da wannan kunyar, gashi Ni ban iya boye sona ba, idan ina son abu nunawa duniya nake ina son abu. Haka idan bana so kowa ma ya sani.

              
Koda naji Sokoto ban boyewa Ummina ke ba, tayi farin ciki sosai. Jannart tun baki shigo gidan mu ba, Kowa na kusadani yasan darajar ki, dan haka karki ce zaki juya min baya, dan ina tsananin sonki da kaunarki.

          Idan na same ki, zan iya gaya miki gaskiya amma bazan boye miki gaskiya ba, banyi miki Karyan bazaki samu kome ba amma nayi miki alƙawarin zan miki adalci a zaman mu, bazan tab'a wulakantanki ba.

     Zan kare kimarki a ko ina, zan kuma nunawa kowa girmanki.

Jannart ki yarda dani Insha Allah bazan kasance miki Namiji buhun k'aya, ba zan kasance me nazari da adalci a duk lokacin da bukatar haka ya taso.

Ban san lokacin da bakin ciki ya sani kifa mishi mari, ba, wanda ya sanya shi sake ni da sauri, kallona yayi cikin nutsuwa sannan ya shafa gefen bakin shi da ya fashe, jini ya gani murmushi yayi cikin jin dadi yace.

"Tunda na ganki a Daura nasan zaki yi wuta! Sai dai ban tab'a niman abu na rasa ba, kuma ba a tab'a dukana ba. Amma ke kin mare ni!"

         Murmushi yayi a karo na biyu, ni kan takaici ya hanani magana,.wuce shi nayi abuna ban kuma kula shi ba dan ban ga yayi min kama da mutanen kirki ba.

           Ganin nayi tafiyata ban kuma kula Iskancin shi ba, kawai ya d'aga murya yana fad'in.

"Yadda kika mare ni! Sai na zame miki k'ayar karfe! Sai na hanaki sukuni, sai na dakatar da duk wani wanda ya dube ki da fuskar Soyayya!"

               Burina a yanzun bai wuce na isa gidan Mrs William ba. Dan ruwan ya kara yawa, kuma ta inda nake mutane kowa ya samu mafakar inda zai tsaya.

            Ina jin jahili yana ihu da surutun shi. Kirana da yayi wanda da wata ƙatuwar murya, wadda yayi dai-dai da karar tsawa.

"Ziryaaaaaa! Do na wa! Ne ni na Lum zu! Kud Ji na mi, ais she nake, na tse ran ad  ra bu tsu bur!"

        Dafe kaina nayi, sakamakon wani irin k'uwa da kaina yake min,.
          Lokaci guda  naga wani duhu ya lullube ni ban kuma motsawa daga inda nake ba, na zube a gurin.  

Murmushi yayi sannan ya tako inda nake, cikin izza da jin kai. Tare da jin zai iya daukar duk wata kasada indai dan ya sami Ziryaaaaaa ce a karo na biyu.

           Daukata yayi ya nufi gurin da motar shi me kirar kunkuru, ya ajiye Ni a gefen hanya sannan ya bude ya sakani a cikin motar,

              Wasu mutane ne suka yo, kanshi da sauri, d'aya ya dafa shi yana fadin.
"Ina zaka kai musu yar mutane?"

Juyawa yayi ya kalli mutumin cikin wani irin kallo ya juyar da kanshi, tare mutumin ya fara aman jini, faduwa yayi a gurin yana shure shure, da gudu sauran suka bar gurin.

    Shiga motar yayi tare da bata wuta.
               Tafiya yayi me nisa, sannan ya tsaya a wani gida me mugun kyau.

     Juyawa yayi yana kallon yadda nake sume, dariya yayi sannan ya sanya fito daga cikin motar ya shiga cikin gidan ya buɗe kafin ya dawo.

   Yana dawowa ya nime ni, ya rasa takarkarewa yayi ya fasa ihu, kamar wanda aka gaya mishi Baban shi da Mamar shi sun mutu.

     Hanyar da yabi dani ya koma babu Ni babu alamata. Bakin ciki kamar ya kashe kan shi da b'acin rai.
          ---
        Abinda ya faru kuwa, ina kwance kamar  wacce aka watsa min ruwan sanyi, na bude idanuna.

         Kofar motar ne ya bude da kanshi, na fito  daga nan kuma bazan iya cewa ga abinda ya faru ba.
               A hankali nake bude ido na, yana zaune ya saka takardu a gaban shi, hasken ya cika dakin.

       Zaune na tashi ina kallon kayan jikina, koda naga sune dan har zuwa lokacin akwai damshi.

                 "Gidan wani d'an iska kike je a wannan unguwar?!"
Ya jefa min tambaya a zafaffe.

         A hankali na sauka a gadon, na dauki jakata da take ya she a kasa, na rufe kaina da mayafina.

          "Fidausih kika sake na zo inda kike sai na baki mamaki." Ya daka min tsawa.

Juyawa nayi a tsiwa ce, na murguda mishi baki sannan nace.
"Tafiya gida zanyi abuna!"
     
Ranshi ne yayi mugun b'aci, ya tako inda nake ya fincikoni tare da wurgani bakin gadon ya tsaya yana huci.

"Wallahi ni gida zanje! Kuma ka kyale ni ko na fita da k....."
  Buge min bakina yayi, na fashe mishi da kuka, dan Ni yanzun bana tsoron shi.

             Mik'ewa nayi zan bar bakin gadon, yayi maza ya rike hannuna tare da mai dani na zauna.

Takaici ya kama shi kamar ya rufe ni da duka, musamman da nake kiran Aryan.
"Wayyo Allah na! Wayyo Aryan kazo."

                Komawa yayi ya zauna kusadani, kamar zai saka kuka yace.
"Jannart! Me nayi miki da zafi haka?!"

         "Bana sonka ne? Wallahi na tsane ka! Ni bana son ganinka ne? Don Allah Kawu ka rabu dani dan Ni Aryan nake so."

        Idanu shine suka cika da kwalla, a hankali ya tako gabana, ya tsaya cikin tashin hankali ya bude bakin shi dakyar yace.
"Jannart!  Kin daina sona?!"

     Girgiza kai nayi, cikin wani irin kuka nace.
"Kawu bana sonka ne? Wallahi baka cikin zuciyata! Ni a matsayin Kawuna na dauke  ka, don Allah ka barni na rayu da Aryan mana, kaji idan kuma ka k'i toh na tabbatar akwai abinda kake bukata ne ba ni ba."

              "A'a Jannart! Ke nake bukata, kece nake so. Don Allah ki hakura da Aryan mu...."

       Kurawa Kawu ido nayi kamar na rufe shi da mugun duka, 

    "Ba zan iya rabuwa da Aryan ba! Sabida shine ya soma nuna min hanyar so, dan haka zaka iya bani guri na wuce ko na falla maka rashin mutunci."

             Rigima sosai ya b'arke a tsakanin mu, da Kawu fir naki kula shi, ya kuma dage sai na rabu da Aryan, Ni kuma na kafe ina son Aryan.

           "Wallahi Ni bana sonka, kawai ka rabu dani naje nayi rayuwa ta da wanda nake so"

"Don Allah ki fahimce ni mana, Ni"
  "Kai meye?! Ka tuna lokacin da nake binka, Kawu don Allah ka gaya min jikina kake bukata ko me? Dan naga gidan ka bani da muhallin zama, idan kuma dan jik....."

          Tasss ya kifa min mari, tare da ture ni a gaban shi, yana huci zuciyar shi kamar zata fado daga kirjin shi, jikin shi rawa yaƙe kamar wanda aka jona mishi wutar lantarki.

       "Gaya min me kake bukata indai jikina ne! Gani dauka ka bar sauran, Aryan zai hakura da yadda ya same ni, Kawu kayi yadda kake bukata in dai zaka barni na auri wanda nake so! Dan bani da Gata sai ka wulakantani.  Kayi yadda kake so dani."

                       Riko hannuna yayi tare da fitar dani daga gidan, ya nufi waje dani, a karo na farko da naga Yarima Jamal yana zubda kwalla sosai, nima kukan nake Yi. Dan ban ga ta inda zai saka ni a cikin gidan shi ba.

   Sannan ai ana barin halas dan kunya, Mijin Sarah me zan yi dashi.
      Tunda na shiga motar nake kuka, bude motar akayi da sauri na d'ago dan yadda nake jin huci nasan bashi bane, Nanah naga rike da muciya, kafin na ankara ta shiga buga min.

   Kamar mahaukaciya, tana faɗin.
"Yar iska, Karuwa sai na kashe ki tunda Kwartancin ki ya kawo ki gidan mijina,"

  Da wani irin sauri ya zo ya tureta, tare da kwace muciyar, ya wurga a gefe, duk ya rud'e fito da ni yayi yaga yadda hannun yake rito wato k'ashin hannun ya karye.

                Mai dani cikin motar yayi,  sannan ya rufe motar ya koma kan Nanah, kamar mara hankali haka ya rufeta da duka,  ganin zai iya kashe musu yar mutane yasani fitowa na shiga jan shi amma yaki sauraro na, ga hannuna da bazan iya amfani da shi ba.

              Bayan shi na runguma tare da sake wata irin kuka,  ina murza kaina a bayan shi ina faɗin.

"Karka ilata musu yar mutane!  Karka ji mata ciwo, karka manta mace ce kamar ni."

             Ture ni yayi na fadi a kan hannun, sake murmukushe hannun nayi, na fasa ihun da ya shi dole ya dakatar da dukar da yakewa Nanah.
   Dawowa yayi kaina,  tare da d'aga ni ya mai dani motar shi, muka fita a gidan.

               Dukar tsiya yayi mata, dan dakyar ta mike, tana kuka tare da daura hannunta a kai. Akan karuwa Jaamal ya daketa.

                 ---
Tunda muka fita yake tuki, bai tsaya ba sai a wata asibiti na wasu turawan biritania, anan ya kaini.
       Aka fara duba hannun, suka ce mishi karaya ce, da targade.

          Rike hannun suka yi aka fara duba min,  kafin suka fara cire min targad'en, kuka da majina tare da zufa nake lokaci guda.
     Da sauri ya tawo kaina, na rike rigarshi ina sauke ajiyar zuciya, tare da juya kaina a kirjinshi.

          Duk baya ganin laifin kowa laifin kanshi yake gani domin da bai furta baya kaunata ba, da babu abinda zai sauya mana kaddarar mu, sai kashi kalmomi guda biyu sun hanamu zama a duniya guda daya, mun rasa junar sabida subul da bakan shi.

          Nike da ciwo shi yake jin zafin abin a ranshi, ana gama min gyara, suka min allura barci tare da na rage zogi.

                   Tashin hankalin da yake ciki bazai iya kwatantawa ba, ina kwance a gabanshi har asuba, yana zaune kallona yake jikin shi na kara sanyi, da Alamarin da yake faruwa damu.

            Kiran sallah ya sanya shi fita daga dakin, can ya dawo bayan yayi sallah.

   Tunda ya shigo yake kallona, a hankali ya iso gabana, ya ɗan bubuga matashi.......

             Gashi ya cusa mata, wani irin tsoron shi, wanda ko muryan shi taji zata shiga duba kuskuren ta.

"Kinsan Kawu shine akan kome nawa, toh idan aka yi aurena sh.."

      Rufe min baƙi tayi idanun ta na cika da kwalla, sauka suka yi sannan ta fara bani labarin da ya kusan kashe ni da raina.

"Jannart! Don Allah karki yarda ki auri mutumin da kike son shi sosai, ki auri wanda yake mutuwar son!"

"Shine zan aura, Aryan Sultan, dan sarkin musulmai"

,"Uban me ya hadaki da Jaamal? Wato baki da zuciya, bayan zubda kimarki da kika yi na cewar kina son shi, shine bari ki kuma bin shi har kina barin yana  sanya hannunsa a jikinki.

         Wai Jannart me kike son Zama ne? Kin zama mace mara kima da sanin ciwon kai.

          Toh wallahi ana barin halas dan kunya mara hankali kawai ko a kafa aka daura min shi ai na cire shi na huta."

          Fitowar Sarah ya sanyata bata kuma kiran sunan shi ba, amma tabbas nasha masifa, na kuma yi alkawarin bazan kuma kula shi ba,(🤔🤫)
  
Daki muka koma, anan muka yi kukan mu, muka share hawayen mu. Sarah tana kewar gida, dan daga yadda take gaya min damuwar ta na fahimci babu wani a kusa da ita, sai ni. Kwantar da kanta nayi a cinyana.

          Janny ji nake kamar na mutu na huta da abinda yake min, don Allah karki so mutumin da baya sonki"

         Nan tayi ta bani labarin irin wulakancin da yake mata, da kuma cin zarafin ta. Wanda banda tana tsoron shi ba, ba makawa wata rana zata iya ramawa.
      Wani abun da ta gaya min ji nayi kamar kasa ta tsaye na burma cikin ta na boye kaina.

Amma iya gaskiyar kenan, ta gaya min abin tausayi ko mu'alamar aure sai ya cutar da ita.
        Ta gaya min damuwar ta sosai, na kuka muyi kuka, karshe shawaran da naga zan iya bata shi na gaya mata nace.
"Karki cuce kanki! Idan kika ga yana miki haka ajiye so a gefe, ki zabga mishi tijara, wallahi zaki kwaci kanki, sannan ki hada da rokon ALLAH, Ubangiji zai baki nasara akan shi. Kiyi hakuri Allah yana tare dake."


   Sosai na bata shawara yadda zata yi da mara kirkin can.

Har dare muna tare a daki, dan ko da zan shiga kitchen ce mata nayi ta zauna naje na fito.

    Mafi Alkhairi, muyi abinda ya shafe mu."

"Nima ina sonk!"

          Kauda kai nayi, wani irin yanayi nake ji mai dadin. Gashi ina kasancewa da wanda yake fallasa min sirrin zuciyarshi, wanda yake gaya min abinda ke ranshi.

   Kifa kaina nayi a saman cinyata, ina murmusa mishi. Dan na san ya gana min kome, bakin shi bai daina furta min kalamai masu dad'i da taushi ba.

              Har muka gama yawon ya dawo damu gida, iya gaskiyar shi kawai yake amayar min.

           Tsakanina da Aryan yanzun komai ya tabbata domin kuwa Manyan shi sun je har daura niman aurena, tare da jagorancin Kawu da Abban Aliyah.

    Sai dai akwai abinda yake damuna, domin kuwa tun ranar da suka zo Lagos Kawu da kanshi ya turo aka kirani. Nazo inda yaƙe.
  
Banza yayi dani, kafin ya kuma cigaba da kallon jaridar da yake, d'ago fararen idanun shi yayi ya kafa min su.

          "Zan musu jagora har zuwa Daura! Amma bazan b'oye miki ba duk abinda ya faru." Murmushi yayi min. Sannan ya tattara abinda yake nashi a falon Abban Aliyah.

         Har ya kai bakin kofa, kamar wanda aka ce tsaya bai juyo ba, amma tabbas naji abinda yace.
"Kiyi kuka da abinda zai je ya dawo! Ki dauki kome akan wuyarki, dakatar da niman auren shine mafi Alkhairin, amma idan Kinji kin gani toh kuwa rai zai b'aci. 

    Kuma za ayi dana sani, wacce dama ita keya ce, karku zargi kowa kanki zaki zarga."

    Daga haka ya saka kai ya fito. Tun daga lokacin wancan mara mutuncin shima ya kuma shigo rayuwata.

    Suka gigita ni, ban isa nayi kome ba. Sai ya gaya min ina aikata kaza ina aikata kaza.

           Yau ma ina sauri zan koma gida, muka hadu dashi tare min hanya yayi, bakin ciki da yake kunsa min ba iyaka.

      "An tafi nima  aurenki!  Kuma zasu bada ke din akan sadaki mai tsada, amma kuma sai maso hakan zai faru, bari kiji inga miki.

    "Wallahi duk wanda ya Kuskura ya rab'e ki, sai kunyi dana sani mara amfani, ki rike a ranki sai na dakatar da kome."

   Dariya nayi sannan na rab'a gefenshi na wuce, dan kusan abinda Jaamal ya gaya min kenan.
                   
        Duk na shiga damuwa, karshe ajiye batun su nayi gefe, na shiga rawa rayuwar, aure ne dai babu wanda ya isa hanani.

Borno...

_Karnin da zai kuma mai-maita kanshi domin kuwa b'ata yi ba, sai akansu_

       Ita din  Sarauniya ce, kuma an haife ta a larda gabashin. Amma zata yi rayuwa tsakanin yammacin da'irar lardin kafin kaddarata ta sauya zuwa yanki mafi albarka da manyan magudanar tafki.


            Zata kasance zinariya a kirninta! Domin zata hadu da manya kuma sadaukai! 

  D'ago kai yayi yana me amsa sallamar Khalil.

       Ya fadi haka tare da ajiye Littafin hannun shi.
Gyara zama Khalil yayi sannan yace.

 Dake baya son a katse mishi karatun da yake, saka hannun shi yayi a gefen inda yake zaune ya dauko wata takarda a kunshe, sannan ya mika mishi.

          "Zaka iya tafiya! Amma kasami Babanka yayi maka bayani"

Duk da hankalin shi yana kan litaffin hannunshi.

"Na gaji! Idan bazaka iya zama dani ba kawai ka gaya min, akan me zaka sani a gaba da fitina, Kawu meye laifina?!"

     Jikin shine yayi mugun sanyi, ya taso har inda take ya zauna, tare da rike hannun ta, yana kallon yadda take kuka.

   Janyota yayi jikinshi yana shafa Bayan ta, dake Kanwar mage ce, tuni ta narke a jikinshi.

              D'an banza dake ya kwana biyu bai tab'ata ba, kuma ya rigada ya saba da tab'e-tab'e.

    Aikuwa ya shiga lallabata har sai da ya murzata don ranshi, kafin ya kyaleta, rungume ta yayi, yana fad'in.

"Ba zan kuma ba! Insha Allah bazaki kuma kamani da wani laifi ba. Na hakura da kome zan rungume ku Matana na zauna da ku cikin adalci da so, Musamman ke!"

        K'amk'ame shi tayi tana murmushi.
        ... Wannan ita ce karshe rikicin su da Sarah. Haka suka shimfida rayuwar su cikin tsarin so ba laifi. duk da ita take haukar shi kam sai ido da murmushi.

         Bayan ko sati biyu bata yi ba, haushin da yaji ne ya saka aka mai da ita.

   Koda wata wata ta zagoyo yaje ya tawo da Yahanasu, ita kam zamansu ya dai-daita, kuma suna mutunta juna.

     Yan uwan shi mata da suka zo Lagos sun zo har gidan mu, inda suke gaya min ai banga so ba, tunda kowa yana maraba dani.

Kyaututtukan da suka kawo min daga Magajiya wata Mahaifiyarshi, ya masifar sanyani jin dad'i, idan kaga fuskar Mamah wani irin farin ciki take, domin ita mutum ce mai Alkhairi, kuma tana son taga anyi Alkhairi.

             Dan haka muma muka shiga hidima dasu, sai da muka musu abinda suka ce anya ba biyan su muka yi ba?

       "A'ah ai bamu isa sayan soyayyar da kuka nunawa Jannart ba, iya haka ma yaci mu bada tukwaicin kaunarku a gareta, dan haka ku dauki wannan a bisa kyakyawa ba bisa maida Alkhairin da kuka mana ba"

              Su kansu sun yarda da irin halayyar da dabi'un mu, kuma sun amince da abinda d'an uwan su yake so! Dan haka suka nuna jin dadin su. Da kuma karfafa min gwiwar da naso Yarshi Anih Fatimah Khatoon, yarinyar da kakaninta basu kaunar tayi nisa dasu.

       Naso ganints, amma suka ce min nayi hakuri zan ganta nan da watani shida.

       Bayan tafiyar su ne ya zo min yayi ta min godiya wai nayi dawainiyya da yan uwansa, ga tarin Alkhairin da nayi musu.

   Da na gaya mishi ni abinda suka min shiru yayi cikin jin dadi, sannan yace.
"Ke din Nurr ce! Ke din Mahnoor ce, hasken farin wata ce.

   Bani ganin wani abu a tare dake sai Alkhairi, bana jin kome akan ki sai Alkhairi. Ki yarda da son da nake miki bazaki dana sani ba."

    Haka yake gaya min magana masu dad'i, gashi ba wani son shi nake ba, amma nayi alkawarin kare rayuwata a tare dashi, koda haka zai hanani samun sukuni da farin cikin,

   Bayan kwana biyu.

Naje gidan su Sarah, tare da kular dankalin turawa, tunda taga kular take had'iye yawu, haka na ajiye mata, ta saka daya daga cikin bayinta suka kawo mata inda zata zuba abincin da ruwan shan ta. Haka taci ta koshi da yamma bayan dawowa Kawu suka kawo ni gida, tun a hanyar da zasu kawo ni ta fara b'ata fuska, idan ya tambaye ta tace babu.

   Har suka kawo ni sun jima suna hira a gidan mu, har zuwa lokacin ciwon na damunta.

A razane ta kalle shi da cewa.
"Kawu! Wani abu na zuba a jikina,"

             A rud'e ya kalleta, hannun shi ya saka a saman motar inda yar fitilar dake jikin motar take ya kunna, kallon inda take yayi cikin tashin hankali.

                   Kamar zararre haka ya finciki motar,. Duk da halin da take ciki bata nuna mishi tana matukar jin ciwon ba, amma tabbas tana jin jiki. 

      Allah ne kaɗai ya kai su asibiti lafiya.

                Ko tsayuwar motar bai gyara ba, ya fita da gudu tare da ɗaukar ta ya nufi cikin Asibitin da ita.

        Likitoci da malaman jinya suka amsheta, yana cikin wannan yanayin ya rasa inda yake masa dad'i, zama yayi a wani kujeran da aka tanadar domin zamar jiran likita ko wani abu makamancin haka.

              Bayan awa daya da rabi, sai ga malamar jinya ta fito, tare da wasu kayan aikin da suka yi sannan ta mika mishi wasu takardun tace mishi.
"Yallabai! Likita yace ana bukatar jini laida biyu. Ko zaka je shashin gwaje-gwaje a duba naka, idan bai yi ba toh sai ka nimo mata wanda za a saka mata"

     Amsa yayi sannan ya wuce, inda ya sanin inda ake gwaje-gwajen, aka kuwa nuna mishi yana zuwa ya musu bayani sannan ya ce musu.
"Rukunin jinina o ce! Dan haka ina bawa duk wanda ya bukata amma zaku iya dubawa."
(Kun gane tun a baya ai na muku bayanin jinin shi yana cikin Group O! Toh dan haka da Hausa meye ake kiran Rukunin jinin shi! Tunda kowa yasa muna da. Rukunin A! B! Sai O kawai ina son ku gaya min da Hausa meye ake kiran group O Blood)

         Sai da suka duba shi, suka tabbatar da gaskiyar shi, sannan suka bashi kome da yadda za a debi jinin.

Washi gari.
       
Yana tashi ya nufi masalaci, daga fitowar shi ya bai tsaya ba sai gidan mu.

      Tunda yazo ya gaya mana, halin da Sarah ke ciki. Mamah tace.
"Toh! Allah ya kyauta, bari na shirya ka ajiye ni ita kuma Janny ta kawo mana abincin mu"

  Haka kuwa aka yi, suka tafi Ni kuma na zauna na yi duk abinda zan kai musu.

           Karfe sha biyu na gama kome, sannan na nufi asibitin, naje na samu tana barci, ajiye musu kayan abincin nayi na koma gefe, Kawu baya nan daga Mamah sai Sarah, a hankali nace.

"Nayi mamakin zuban cikin fa! Domin ko jiya ai"

   Buɗe ido Sarah tayi tana kallona, kafin ta lumshe idanu a sannu ta shafa cikin ta, tace.
"Mamah cikin ya fita ko? Jikina ciwo yake min kamar an daure ni"

          Tausayi ta bamu, haka muka kaita ban daki Mamah ta haɗa mata ruwa zata fita ta riko hannun Mamah, tace.
"Meye ne rayuwar duniya wanda baki sani ba? Jikina kamar ba nawa ba.

     Ni kaina bazan iya kula da kaina dole sai da taimakawan ki."

      Haka Mamah ta koma ta saka wani karamin tsuma ta gasata Sosai, yadda ko da Iyayen tane iyakacin kulawar da zasu mata kenan.

          Kwanan ta biyu aka sallame ta, gidan mu aka dawo da ita, domin can gidan akwai Yahanasu, dan haka sai abin bai ta kura Jaamal ba.

        Dake Mamah tana min gyaran jiki, sai ta haɗa da ita muka cigaba da samun kulawa, batun ciki da ya zuba, Allah sarki Kawuna ya saka rai sosai akan cikin Sarah, haka suka hakura, dan ita dangina na ce, da aka gaya mata cikin ya zube. Murmushi tayi sannan tace.

"Dama wannan wahalar da nasha ai ya dame ni tunda ya fita toh a sauka lafiya." 

Faɗa Mamah tayi mata. Yanzun kawun baya tani, dan ya cire Ni a tsarin shi sai matar shi, haka zai zo da dare ya tura tazo, ban san me suke yi ba, sai dai tana dawowa zata shige ban daki tayi wanka.

       Sam ban fahimci kome ba, sabida ba tasu make ba, ashe mutumin ku, zuwa yake yana more matar shi a mota.

                     Domin tana cika sati sati biyu ta fara sallah, Mamah bata fahimci abinda ke faruwa ba.

                ..... Shima a gidan shi rigima suke da Yahanasu, dan kullum ya koma mata sai yayi wanka aikuwa tace bata san kwanan zancen ba, idan zai dawo da Sarah ya dawo da ita, karshe dai sai da akayi rigima har yazo gaban Mamah. Ta sanya Sarah tattara ta koma gidan shi.

          Zaman Sarah da Yahanasu a farkon zuwa Yahanasu ai ba matsala, a yanzun kuma sai ta sanya Sarah a gaba, sosai da Fitina. Tana mata tsiya, idan Sarah tayi magana tace zata mata duka ita da bayin da suke gidan.

      Haka sai ya ɗan tsorata Sarah, Ni kuma naje gidan, lokacin Yahanasu tana cin mutuncin Sarah.

            Kan Bala'i, ban tab'a sanin cewa nima yar dambe bace sai ranar, domin sun sami damar Sarah har sai da suka nemi rufeta da duka.

Duk bakin Sarah dole ta gudu d'akinta, dan Mamah ta gaya mata ta rage shiga abinda bai shafeta ba, tare da nuna mata tayi takatsantsar da kowa, bata ce kome ba amma ta nuna mata ko da kuwa akan bayin da Umma ta kawo mata ne babu ruwanta da su.

                   Ina shiga na same su sunyi cirko cirko, kofar Sarah kowacce da abin dukarta.

     Al'amarin Sarah daban ce, a zuciyata da ruhina, Yahanasu tana zaune tana jiran Sarah ta fito su daketa.

    Wani irin iska me hade da walkiya ya gifta ta idona,  fita nayi daga gidan, naje na karyo itacen a bishiyar maina. Na shigo gidan, tare da rufe yan banza da duka.

   Dake jahilai ne har da taso min, ganin nafi karfin su yasa suka shiga niman hanyar guduwa.

Ai kuwa jin ihun su ya sanyata fitowa, a tsorace kamar me ta tsaya tana dariya tace..
"Kai Jannart ashe kema yar dambe ce toh zame min su sun dame ni ban san me nayi musu ba.

         Kullum sai sun nime dukana,  kuma ban iya kai kara ba, dama ina son naje na gaya miki sai gashi Allah ya kawo min ke, sun takura min sosai Janny."

    Aikuwa kamar hura min wuta tayi na cigaba da zane yan banza tun suna ihu har suka daina, sannan na kyale su.

         Ina gamawa na tawo gabanta, zan fadi ta tare ni. Jinin da ya fito ta hancina ta shafe, a tsorace.

         Dakinta ta kaini, ta rud'e dan bata san me zata min ba, kawai zata fita na rike hannunta.

        "Karki damu! Aminiya, naji sauki hab'o ne ya saba min haka idan raina yab'aci." A sanyayye ta zauna, tana kallona kafin tace.
"Laaa! Na tuna ai lokacin da muke makaranta ya tab'a miki haka."

    Nan ta shiga zubo min labari, takaici ya sanya ni lumshe idanuna, dan ita dai a gabana ko waziri Ali sai haka.

   Sai yamma na bar gidan, domin hidima da Ni tayi ta yi.

       Tunda daga ranar Sarah ta samu sukuni, a cikin gidan dan an zuba musu kashedi, kuma sun amince. Dan haka sai ta zama  shugaban su. Ina dalili a maida min Kawata bora.

               ---
Tsakanina da Aryan, ba a magana. Dan dan dattijona ya iya shimfida salon shi mai tafiya da zamanin shi, sam babu ruwan shi da yawan shekarun sa, abinda ya sani nice.

                   Kayan da za a zuba min a d'akina wani kamfanin yan kasar kasar sin itace Abban Aliyah yabawa aikin kome.

          Sai suturar da Mamah take yawan saya min, idan na tambaye ta sai tace.
"Ai gidan Sarautar ana bukatar mace da suturar Alfarma, kar ta damu da wanda aka mata, ina so samu ne a miki kayan da babu wacce aka tab'a mata dan su san kema yar gata ce,sannan kayan da suka kawo miki a ajiye shi har sai kin haihu sai a rab'awa dangin shi a matsayin Alkhairi.

    Karki yarda abin hannunki ya zame miki adon tafiyarki, abin hannunki ya zame miki mafarin soyayyarki a zukatan su.

                     Suna da shi, amma kyautatawa, tankar fansa kai da kai ne! Koda kuwa suna sonki toh tabbas kika sake musu abinki sai kiga sun zame miki dakarun kare kimar ki! Shima yaga kin sadaukar da abubuwan ki a kanshi zai zame miki tankar kassaka kina yakince shi yana kara manne miki.

                    Sai kiga lokaci ƙalilan kin saye kowa, da Alkhairi ayita tunanin ai kin gama dasu ne ta hanyar amfani da sihiri na kuwa zallar kyautatawa ce ta haifar da haka.

    Da mutane da yawa zasu an karar da Y'ayansu mata kafin kai su d'akin mazajen su zai yi wuya kiga an sami matsalar dangin mijin ya bar cikin hausawa.

   Ina babu wannan tunanin ke dai kina shiga ki zama ungulu da kan zabi. Ko kuma ramin kura sai y'ayanta.

      Kina gani dai yadda na dauki yan uwan Abba, da kyautata musu da nake yi a haka zagina wasu suke Amma babu yadda suka iya dani, tunda Allah ya hadamu dasu sai hakuri. Dan haka ban tab'a damuwa dab basu sona ba.

    A'a nafi damuwa da na kyautata musu, kuma Alhamdulillah Nagodewa Allah domin ko Itama babar shi sona take ba iya Ni ba hatta yaran mu kamar su cinye su suke dan so! Dan haka ki yi kokarin kin kyautata musu."

   Mama tayi min bayani sosai yadda zan samu yan uwan Aryan tunda suna sona, toh nayi kokarin ganin nima na musu Alkhairi yadda soyayyar zata dore.

                  --- 
Bikina saura wata uku sai ga wani cikin ya kuma bayyana a jikin Sarah, murna kamar me, kowa ya gama saka burin shi akan cikin, kulawa ake bata tun daga Bauchi aka kawo mata bayi manyan mata, domin Mahaifinta da kakanta sun zo sun dubata.

     Dan haka wani irin kulawa Kawu yake bata, tare da kaf-kaf akan cikin. Bata aikin fari balle baki.

          A wannan cikin taga abinda ake kira soyayya, domin kome har wanka Kawu yake mata, kome yayi daidai fa, amma idan ya tuna cewa ya rasa Janny sai wani irin kunci da damuwa ya aure shi na wani lokaci. Da zaran ya kalli cikin Sarah sai yaji nutsuwa ta sauko miki.

     A haka muke ta rayuwa, shi da farin cikin sa, nima nawa da farin cikin.

          ---
Dake tagar mu tana ta baya ne, haka kawai cikin dare zan ga mutum a tsaye a jikin tagar, sau biyu ina ihu koda an zo babu shi babu kamar shi.

    Wannan abun ya dame ni har Mamah dan haka kawai barci ya fara kaura ce min, idan lokacin da nake ganin mutumin yayi.

   Nan su Mamah suka fara min addu'o'in, har na fara samun Barci, tunda muka dage da Addu'ar sai na daina ganin mutumin.

       Sai dai kuma ina fita zan yi karo da wannan mara mutuncin,...

Raina b'aci yake amma babu yadda na iya, sai na share batun shi na mai da hankali na guri guda.

  .     A wani tsautsayin da ya kuma faruwa wanda na shiga cikin al'amarin dumu dumu,  dan faruwan yaje min tashin hankali da Kawu.

"Gaya min meye na tsare miki da kike sanadin zubda min da cikin mata na"

Wallahi ji nayi kamar saukar aradu ce, domin kuwa ban san kome ba, yake tuhumata.

"Eh ai dole ki fadi haka, tunda da bakin ki aka lalata min cikin mata na wallahi bazan yi kafara ba sai na raba abinda tayi min sai na sanyata zubda kwalla kamar yadda ta sani.

     Alhamdulillah da ban sameta a matsayin matar aurena ba, da ban san abinda zata aikata min ba, ki rubuta ki ajiye kema kin kusan kukan da kika samu muka yi.

Wallahi na kuma ganin ki da mata na sai na miki abinda ko sunan namiji aka kira sai kin rufe kanki a d'aki.

  Sai na lalata miki duniyar da kike tunkawo da shi, dan haka kar na kuma ganin ki a gidana.

In ba haka ba zaki yi na dama mara amfani, jahila wacce ta manta da ikon Allah, kin zubda na farko ban yi magana ba, shine bari ki kuma na biyu, ai zuciyar zinari gare mu.

    Ance ba a sonki mara asali kawai."

Haka ya gama masifar shi ya fita, Ni kam shiru nayi ina kallon Hanyar da yabi, sam kaina ya kulle, taya zan zubda cikin Sarah?

     Yau nake jin kewar gida, sabida gorin da yayi min yau nake jin da zan san dangina dole na koma gare su, na gaji da gorin da Kawu yake min, ina dalili zai sanya ni. A gaba da gori ba.

    Haka nayi kuka har na godewa Allah, sannan na kwanta a gurin barci yayi ba dani, abinda Jaamal yayi min ya janyo min jin tsanar shi a raina,bana son ganin shi a rayuwata.

Sarah kan da jikinta yayi sauki, tattara ta yayi ya sanya su a jirgin kasa zuwa Bauchi. Dan wannan karon yasan Mama bazata amshi riketa ba shi yasa ya hadata da bayin da aka bata suka dawo Bauchi.

Ya fison ta gane Jannart ba kaunarta take ba, ina dalili (kunji ba zai raba aminai biyu 🙄🤔😒🤦)

          Har ta bar Lagos bai muna mata bazai kai takardan ba, kawai abinda yayi yana dawowa gida ya sanyawa takardan wuta ya kona.

           Sai da na gaya miki babu ruwanki Da Fulani Hasiyah, duk yadda kike tsammani ta wuce haka."

Tayu zargin Fulani muke, tunda baku kusa da ita, masu aikata haka suna tare dake."

Kawai tunanin Yahanasu ne yazo mata rai shiru tayi tana kallon yadda Kakarta take dama mata kunun, dan ya huce ta sha.

        "Ni nafi zargin da saka hannun Yahanasu, sabida ita daya ce ta sanya Ni a gaba, Bama ita daya ba duk matan basu kaunata, Kinga kenan babu ruwan Fulani da ni, kawai Matsalar tana hannun abokan zama na ne"

Lagos.

Sauri nake domin ina iya hango wancan jakin yana bin bayana, ina yankar kwana kawai sai gashi a gaba na.
