          Ke kuma Jannart! Gashi kina guduna, Ni kuma na zame miki sassari karfe ga azaba ga k'aya. Shawaran da zan baki "

Tsaki naja sannan nayi wuccewa ta, dan na fahimci bai da lafiya, nan nabar sakarai nata zuba.

    Sha-sha-sha kawai wanda bai san me yake ba.
Ina shiga gida muka kusan karo da Kawu, matsa mishi nayi zan shige, tsaki yayi min ban kula shi ba, nayi shigewa ta cikin gidan.

   Ashe Aryan yana jirana ne, ina shiga yana mik'ewa kamar zai amshi abinda ke hannuna, kallon shi nayi cike da kunya dan mun kwana biyu bamu hadu ba.

  Gaishe shi nayi  sannan na wuce cikin gidan, ina shiga naga Mamah sai dariya take, zama nayi tace min.
"Maza kije idan kun gama kizo na gaya miki abinda yake akwai."

           Da sauri na fito, ina zuwa na same shi a zaune, ya saka abu a gaban shi.

          Hira muka yi sosai, sannan da zai tafi nayi mishi rakiya, anan yake gaya min zasu kawo kaya nan Lagos, ko a kai daura ne?
  Nace.
"Zan tambayi Mamah duk yadda muka yi zan gaya maka idan ka kuma zuwa."

  "Bikin mu saura wata biyu ko?!"
     Ya tambaye ni, gyada mishi kai.
  
Sunkuyar da kanshi yayi kasa yana shafa fuskar shi, bai ce kome ba ya bude motar ya shiga, yana kallona.

            Bai itace komai ba, sai dai har zai yadda motar yace min.
"Ban san me yasa nake bukace da son muzauna a inuwa daya ba"

Da gudu na bar gurin shi, zuwa cikin gida, ina zan iya wannan abar kunyar, tunda na shiga daki nake dariya ni daya.

   Sai kunshe kaina nake, tsabar kunya. Idan na tuna sai dariya ta kani, karshe dai haka na wuni har dare.

   Mamah tayi tayi na gaya mata naki, sai dai ina iya kokarina na shiru da bakina dama ba wai dan kome ba sai dan tana jin haka zata dakatar da zuwan shi gidan.

Sau biyu Abdul yana yunkurin sace ni, tare da gaya min. Shi jikina yake muradi idan ya gama toh idan naso na rayu ko na mutu shi ba damuwar shi bane. Shi dai kawai abinda yake bukata Kenan.

  Tun daga ranar sai na daina fita, baki daya ina dalili za a lalata min yau n.

Na rasa fahimtar matsalar Aryan sosai yake dame da ni, domin yana bukaci dani, tun ina gudun shi har na dawo, ina gaya mishi gaskiya idan haka ya faru nice zan zo nayi dana sani, domin kuwa zai iya shafa min bakin jini ne,

Amma fir yaki fahimta na sai ma fushin da yake yi, ko yaki zuwa na wasu kwanaki idan yazo sai yace min aikin shi ya tsare shi.

Ni kuma bana jin zan iya watsawa Mamah kasa a idanunta, kawai sai na zuba mishi ido.

     Allah ya gani bani cikin yan matan nan marasa kamun kai, balle na dauki kaina sukutun da guda na bawa namijin da dubun al'umma basu shaida mun mallaki juna ba.

  Ba dani ba gyada a hurimi, ai na kowa tara ne nawa kuwa sha biyu ce, idan hakan ta faru ai na shiga uku na lalace.

    Dan haka koda ya dauke kafa nima sai naji nutsuwa yazo min ya cigaba da yaji, nan da wata biyu masu zuwa Insha Allah mun zama tsuntsiya madaurinki daya.

      Dan jin haushi yaji haushi na, amma babu yadda ya iya dani,shi kuma ba mazinace bane balle yaje yayi ta juye abin a layi, da ya zauna tsam yayi dogon nazari a kaina sai ya fahimci na fishi gaskiya, kawai sai ya fara azumi sosai.

        Karshe a gidan mun ya dawo yake shan ruwa,har yana me bani hakuri.

              Sai nayi kokarin kare kaina ta hanyar shigar da zata rufe min jikina, sannan na guje amfani da turare mai karfin, dan turare yana daga cikin abubuwan da suke jan hankalin namiji.

         Daga nan na daina yarda ina zama Ni daya a gurin shi dole na hada da dan rakiya,haka ya taimakawa rayuwata sosai.

Dan shaidanun basu rena mafaka ko min kashinsa, sai sun zauna domin yad'a barna, Ni kuma sai na gujewa faruwar haka.

       Allah da ikon shi sai ya daina min abinda yake min, nima kuma na kuma kame kaina sosai, 

          Toh gaskiya naga iya wannan lamari dai na yan kwanaki ne idan anyi hakuri.

          Dauke kafar Aryan bai bani mamaki ba, domin ya gaya min haka zai iya faruwa.
  
Shirya shiryan da muka yi a nan Lagos, yayi yawa domin kuwa su Mamah da wasu Kawayenta na nan Lagos sunje sunyi min jere,

    Bikin saura kwana goma, muka nufi daura, amma mun yada zango a kano, domin Jamlah tasa anyi min gyaran jiki sosai, tare da gyaran amare ana jibi bikin muka nufi daura.

  Bakin ciki da takaici ga mugun kishin da yake cin ruhinshi, haka yayi ta hadiyewa.

   Da dare.

"Saura awowi ƙalilan ki tashi daga ikona! Jannart ina sonki kamar na dauke ki na gudu, muje wani gurin mu rayu da juna.

    Jannart! Ban san me zan baki ki fahimci irin son da nake miki ba, don Allah Karki mallaka mishi kanki, ki barshi a matsayin kaddarar mu, Jannart Karki bashi zuciyarki dan Ni nafi dacewa dake!

        Jannart! Me zai hana mu shimfid'a alkawarin mu a wannan daren."
Da sauri na kwace jikina, ina mishi wani irin duba,nace.
"Amma Kawu baka da kunya! Nice zan baza habbar zanina! Kayi abinda ranka yake so dani.

Sabida gani nan mara gata, bayan zagin da kayi min bai isheka ba sai na nemi na baka, abu me matukar kankaro kimar mace.

    Lallai kuwa, rungume ni da kayi ma kaje kai da Allah, amma ko zan tafi tsirara bazan tab'a kaunar ka ba, tunda har ka iya zargina zan kashe maka abin cikin matar ka.

  Allah na duba, da ana taya wani laulayi da na taya Sarah da ana taya wani jin azabar fitar ciki toh wallahi Sarah kadai zan taya.

    Haka bai maka ba sai da zage ni har da min gori, sannan yanzun ka tawo kace kana sona kamar ka ɗauke ni mu gudu, tab'di jam, wallahi koda ace. Dole za a min da Aryan wallahi sai na aure shi, kuma na bashi kaina a matsayin fansar soyayyar da ya nuna min.

    Balle kuma auren soyayya ce, ina so yana so, kaga kuwa dan na bashi kaina kamar fansar sone,

      Insha Allah nan da wani lokaci kaji ana shirin zan haihu da abinda nake so!

      Hahhhh, gwara tun wuri ka rungume matan ka, nima zanje na rungume mijina Ni daya, wanda ake mishi tsammaci zan  sarki bayan bikin mu.

       Sai da safe mijin mata hudu, dan asali mai tushe mai kyakyawan nasaba, kaga Aryan bai san kome a kaina ba, amma ya gama tafiya a kaina, sai kai da ka san kome ka nuna min tabbatacciyar Ni din tsintarciyar mage ce bata mage.

   Kace zan zauna da kai yo Allah na tuba, ko maza sun kare ai Kawu bazan iya cin amanar Sarah ba."

Daga haka nayi Wucewa ta ban kuma kula shi ba, sannan na ji a raina Insha Allah na rabu da Kawu har abada.

             Lokacin da na bar gurin shi zan koma cikin gidan, na gamu da Aryan yana tsaye, murmushi nayi naje na tsaya kusa da shi.

            "Dama ba a cikin masarautar nan aka haife ki ba?!"
    Banyi mamakin tambayar shi ba, kai tsaye na bashi labarin abinda na sani.

"Bai min gata ba! Cutarwa kawai na sani daga gare shi."

       "Dama shine muradinki?!".
Ta kuma jefa min tambaya,
"Eh a da can baya shine mafarkina, duniyata, tunanina,  abinda nake so, daga ranar da ya auri Aminiyata naji na ajiye shi a cikin wani waje na daban.

    Na tattara kome nashi na binne, shigowar ka rayuwata, na sami nutsuwa, na fahimci girman Soyayyar da kayi min, kayi hakuri da rashin gaya maka gaskiya da banyi ba."

     Sunkuyar da kanshi yayi, abinda ban tsammaci daga bakin shi naji, sai da kuka ya kwace min.

         "Ko a hanya na ganki, kai ko a gidan karuwai na ganki nan ne guri mafi kazanta, Jannart zan zauna dake, balle nayi kuma ina jin a jikina kin fito daga gida masu nasaba.

     Insha Allah ina tare dake koda bazan yi nisan kwana ba, zan baki farin ciki wanda zai sanyaki jin a ranki ni miki so tsakani da Allah, bazan tab'a barinki ki zubda kwalla ba.

           Fidausi kiji a ranki zan rayu sabida ke! Idan na mutu toh zan mutu ina mai kaunarki."

Maganar da yake gaya min ya kashe min jiki, dan haka juyawa nayi na barshi a gurin dan ba fahimci zai iya sanya ni kuka.

      Tunda na shiga, cikin gida na kwanta, nake juye juye. Gobe Insha Allah zan zama matar Aryan.

     Mugun Fad'uwar gaba naji, wanda ya sanyani tashi zaune, ina nazarin fuskar shi, zuwa maganar da yayi min.

Wasa-wasa sai da na kai asuba, ina nazari dakyar na kwanta kasancewar ina fashin sallah.

             Barci Mai nauyi ne yayi gaba dani, kamshin turaren matan Arewa tare da amarya suka tashe ni, ana gud'a tare da bisa algaita da ka'ka'ki, sai kade kade da ake da kirari.

Shagalin da ake sosai ya sani fahimtar an kusan daura auren kenan, jikina ya mutu sosai asalima nauyi yayi min, kwalla na zuba min, ina zan ga dangina nima naji a raina ina da gata,.  Duk dad'in ka da namiji wata rana sai ya goranta maka domin naga Misali a gurin kawu, duk abinda yayi min akan abinda ban aikata ba ya zauna yayi ta min gori har dace min mara asali, kuka ne ya kwabce min, na maida kaina kan matashin na sake wata irin kuka mai cin rai da ruhi, rabu da Jaamal kenan, koda yake dole ce tasa na rabu dashi tunda ga alamun ana dab da aura auren, toshe kunne na nayi, tare da sake wata gigice kuka ina wancakalar da duk wani abinda yake kusa dani, kuka nake kamar raina zai fita, waye zai fahimci yadda nake ji, sai da nayi kuka na koshi sannan nayi limo a gadon na lafe, kamar wata mage.

    Shigowar Mamah tare da Jamlah, fuskarsu dauke da farin ciki ya sani fahimtar na zama mallakin Aryan Muhammad Muhammad Sultan. 

Na gane hakan ne ta hanyar jin kirarin da marokin yake tare da shelartawa wata kukan ne ta kwace min.....

#MY ZAHRAH🌹

ZAHRAN-ADDA RAMLA

6️⃣4️⃣

Wani irin rawa jikina ya ɗauka, lokaci guda, na fita hayyacina,. Tare da sake wani marayan kuka, duk abinda yake faruwa Kawu ne sanadi, meye amfanin bad'i ba rai. Meye ma'anar wannan al'amarin gashi sakamakon furucin shi na rasashi har abada,

   Duk halin da muke ciki, shi ya bada gudummawar wannan tashin hankalin duk yadda suka so hanani kuka sun kasa dole suka zuba mini idanu nayi kuka har na godewa Allah.

      Sannan na koma inda gadon na kwanta tare da dunkunkunewa, ina ajiyar zuciya. Fita suka yi dan abinda ya kawo su na farin ciki bazasu iya gaya min ba tunda gashi na gigice musu.

      ---
Kawu kuwa! Rufe kan shi yayi a cikin dakin yayi ta sauke ajiyar zuciya, kamar wanda yayi mugun gudu, zubur ya mike tare da fita daga dakin, a falon shi ya samu Sarah tayi wani iri kamar mareniya, yana fitowa ta mike da sauri zuwa gare shi.

      Kauda kai yayi bai kalleta ba, yayi saurin fita a gidan. Zuɓewa tayi kamar danyen nama, dan tun jiya ya gabata fita, zuwa gurin bikin, ta tambaye shi me yasa, yace kar yaga kafarta a wajen bikin.

                 Duk ta damu kamar tayi hauka, amma babu yadda ta iya tunda shi ya hanata, dama zaman da tayi akan idan ya fito ta roke shi ya barta ne, amma fir mara mutuncin ya hanata zuwa.

            Wannan abinda yayi ya mugun damun Mamah amma bata nuna mishi ba, dan ana tsaka da hidima ya shigo, yana gaida mutane. Sai da ya shiga gurin Fulani sannan ya fara Niman ta yadda zai ganni, abu yaci tura.

     Karshe hikima yayi ya fita can hankalin mutanen baya kan shi ya shigo ya wuce dakin da nake, ina kwance, zazzaɓi yayi min lugub. Yana zuwa ba tare da wani damuwa ba, dan bai tsammaci zai iya ganina haka ba, kawai sai d'aga bargon yayi, ai kuwa idanun shi suka hango mishi abinda ya firgita shi.

                    Da wani irin sauri ya lullebe ki, ya juya zai fita suka yi kicibis da Mamah.
"Hankalinka ya kwanta?! Ka ga abinda yake ranka?!"

  "Meye haka Raihana?! Ina ruwana da wani abinda yake jikinta, ni Ita nake bukata ba jikinta ba. Da zanyi wani abu da ita toh da nayi tun a jinyar da tayi min.

     Don Allah ki daina tunanin jiki na ta yana burge ni, ko daya bana jin kome bayan kaunarta.

        Idan akwai abinda nayi mishi fargan daji toh ba makawa! Fidausih ce.

Don Allah ba dan na isa ba, ki bar danganta ni da mutanen banza."

            "Toh meye kazo yi bayan kasan?!"

Cikin dishashiyar murya nace mata.
"Kyale shi Mamah ban san meye Kawu yake nima dani ba, jiya ya bani mamaki yau kuma yazo ya idda nufin shi a kaina, ban san meye matsalar shi dani ba da yake niman lalata min gobe na ba, amma koma meye Allah ya fishi dan yana ganin halin da nake ciki. Don haka ya cigaba da niman ketare iyakar Allah. 

    Aure ne dai ko mutum yana mutuwa ya dawo sai nayi sai da kayi duk abinda kaga yayi maka, Aure ne an daura da Sarkin Sokoto mai jiran gado."

  Cikin takaici ya kalle ni, kad'a kan shi yayi, cikin damuwa sannan ya juya zai fita, murmushi yayi sannan yace min.
"Allah ya bada zaman lafiya! Da zuri'a na gari!"

  Yana gama fadar haka Yasaka kai zai fita. Aukuwa na amsa da cewa..
"Amin Ya Allah, Allah yasa har zuci maganar ta kai."

     Tunda ya fita, bakin ciki yake cin shi, tare da wani irin yanayi kamar ya bar garin daura baki daya.
         Bayan fitar shi Mamah tace min.
"Maza tashi kiyi wanka, domin zuwa anjima kadan za a tafi dake, gidan ki."

           Mik'ewa nayi ina b'ata rai, kamar zan yi kuka, ban daki na shiga inda na samu an hada min Sabulun wanka ruwan ma an zuba turaren wanka.

       Kamshi yake sosai, haka nayi wanka na fita tass, sannan na fito na shiga shiryawa, cikin wani irin mutuwa jiki, dan ji nake kamar wani mugun abu zai faru dani.

             Dakyar na shirya, sannan na zauna ina gyara zaman dan kwalina, jini ne naga yana diga daga idanuna,  koda na kalli madubin ban gani ba, gogewa nayi dan yana shirin b'ata min kayan jikina.

                          Ina gogewa, wasu na sake zuba, a hankali jikina ya kara mutuwa, ban san ya akayi ba barci yayi gaba dani, ban kuma sanin me yake faruwa ba.
       
          ---
Shirya daurin aure kamar yadda aka tsara,  sai dai wani tashin hankalin da ya faru, ana gama  daurin kawai aka nime Ango aka rasa.
   Duk yadda ake tsammani abin yaci tura domin kuwa, abokan shi Kowa yaga lokacin da ya gaisa da Jamal, har suka koma gefe suna ta magana har da dariya.

      Daga nan ba a kuma ganin shi ba, an shiga niman Jaamal din shima babu shi babu dalilin shi.

       Lamarin da yayi mugun tashin hankalin jama'a kenan, yayinda dayawa suka fara alakanta Jaamal shi ya sace shi.

       A lokacin wani sabon tashin hankali ya kuma bayyana dan Shamaki ya fito ya tabbatar da cewa.
  Ai tsakaninmu da Jaamal akwai soyayya dan haka ba wani abu bane, dan an nime Ango an rasa. Wannan abin ya d'aga hankalin Raihanah da Jamlah, domin sune a tsakanin Al'amarin.

                     Duk inda ake saka ran za a ga Jaamal babu shi babu dalilin shi, gidan Biki ya koma tankar gidan makoki. Kafin wani lokaci zancen b'atar Aryan ya karaɗe ko ina, har inda ba ayi tsammanin ba, gani kwance kamar fawa.

      Anyi anyi na tashi, ban tashi ba sai da kwashi awa biyu ina barci, Kafin na farka cikin wani irin yanayi, wanda ya hanani magana, kallon kowa nake, da ido kamar sabin halitta.

      Ana cikin wannan halin, aka shigo gidan da mugun gudu,  wanda ya kawo sakon Kasancewar sunan shi Hudu, kasa magana yayi, yana haki. Nima ganin ana cewa ga shi yazo, kawai na tashi da sauri na fita wajen, ganin su nayi zasu fita na bi bayan su, Turuss suka yi sakamakon hango Jaamal rungume da gawan mutum a gaban shi, yana zaune yayi shiru. Daga shi har gawan basu da kyan gani, ji nayi jikina yayi mugun sanyi, a hankali na ratsa mutanen Mama na rike ni na kwace daga riƙonta, dan naga yadda take kuka, ji nayi kamar na saukar min da wani irin kasala, dakyar nake iya d'aga kafana, idanuna akan hannun gawar.

     Wanda yake sanye da zoben da bazan tab'a mantawa ba. Shiru nayi ina kara nazarin waye a jikin Kawu da irin zoben Aryan Dina, Jan kafana nayi har gaban Kawu, na durkusa.

                   Hannun gawar na rike, ina kallon tafin hannun da ya tab'a ni, sati Uku da suka wuce, hannun ne ya had'a fuskana da nashi,  jikina ne ya kama rawa, kamar wacce aka jona min wuta, nayi maza na janye fuskar shi da take boye a jikin Kawu, wani irin ihu na sake a razane. Nayi baya tare da zaman yan bori, na fashe da wani irin kuka, me shegen ciwo, da cin rai.

         Ban tab'a ganin abinda ya firgita ni irin gawan....
Kuyi hakuri. Ba yawa

6️⃣1️⃣

A razane ta kalle shi da cewa.
"Kawu! Wani abu na zuba a jikina,"

             A rud'e ya kalleta, hannun shi ya saka a saman motar inda yar fitilar dake jikin motar take ya kunna, kallon inda take yayi cikin tashin hankali.

                   Kamar zararre haka ya finciki motar,. Duk da halin da take ciki bata nuna mishi tana matukar jin ciwon ba, amma tabbas tana jin jiki. 

      Allah ne kaɗai ya kai su asibiti lafiya.

                Ko tsayuwar motar bai gyara ba, ya fita da gudu tare da ɗaukar ta ya nufi cikin Asibitin da ita.

        Likitoci da malaman jinya suka amsheta, yana cikin wannan yanayin ya rasa inda yake masa dad'i, zama yayi a wani kujeran da aka tanadar domin zamar jiran likita ko wani abu makamancin haka.

              Bayan awa daya da rabi, sai ga malamar jinya ta fito, tare da wasu kayan aikin da suka yi sannan ta mika mishi wasu takardun tace mishi.
"Yallabai! Likita yace ana bukatar jini laida biyu. Ko zaka je shashin gwaje-gwaje a duba naka, idan bai yi ba toh sai ka nimo mata wanda za a saka mata"

     Amsa yayi sannan ya wuce, inda ya sanin inda ake gwaje-gwajen, aka kuwa nuna mishi yana zuwa ya musu bayani sannan ya ce musu.
"Rukunin jinina o ce! Dan haka ina bawa duk wanda ya bukata amma zaku iya dubawa."
(Kun gane tun a baya ai na muku bayanin jinin shi yana cikin Group O! Toh dan haka da Hausa meye ake kiran Rukunin jinin shi! Tunda kowa yasa muna da. Rukunin A! B! Sai O kawai ina son ku gaya min da Hausa meye ake kiran group O Blood)

         Sai da suka duba shi, suka tabbatar da gaskiyar shi, sannan suka bashi kome da yadda za a debi jinin.

         Tunda aka debi jinin suka kai shi dakin hutu, tare da mishi allurar barci.

            ---
Washi gari.
       
Yana tashi ya nufi masalaci, daga fitowar shi ya bai tsaya ba sai gidan mu.

      Tunda yazo ya gaya mana, halin da Sarah ke ciki. Mamah tace.
"Toh! Allah ya kyauta, bari na shirya ka ajiye ni ita kuma Janny ta kawo mana abincin mu"

  Haka kuwa aka yi, suka tafi Ni kuma na zauna na yi duk abinda zan kai musu.

           Karfe sha biyu na gama kome, sannan na nufi asibitin, naje na samu tana barci, ajiye musu kayan abincin nayi na koma gefe, Kawu baya nan daga Mamah sai Sarah, a hankali nace.

"Nayi mamakin zuban cikin fa! Domin ko jiya ai"

   Buɗe ido Sarah tayi tana kallona, kafin ta lumshe idanu a sannu ta shafa cikin ta, tace.
"Mamah cikin ya fita ko? Jikina ciwo yake min kamar an daure ni"

          Tausayi ta bamu, haka muka kaita ban daki Mamah ta haɗa mata ruwa zata fita ta riko hannun Mamah, tace.
"Meye ne rayuwar duniya wanda baki sani ba? Jikina kamar ba nawa ba.

     Ni kaina bazan iya kula da kaina dole sai da taimakawan ki."

      Haka Mamah ta koma ta saka wani karamin tsuma ta gasata Sosai, yadda ko da Iyayen tane iyakacin kulawar da zasu mata kenan.

          Kwanan ta biyu aka sallame ta, gidan mu aka dawo da ita, domin can gidan akwai Yahanasu, dan haka sai abin bai ta kura Jaamal ba.

        Dake Mamah tana min gyaran jiki, sai ta haɗa da ita muka cigaba da samun kulawa, batun ciki da ya zuba, Allah sarki Kawuna ya saka rai sosai akan cikin Sarah, haka suka hakura, dan ita dangina na ce, da aka gaya mata cikin ya zube. Murmushi tayi sannan tace.

"Dama wannan wahalar da nasha ai ya dame ni tunda ya fita toh a sauka lafiya." 

Faɗa Mamah tayi mata. Yanzun kawun baya tani, dan ya cire Ni a tsarin shi sai matar shi, haka zai zo da dare ya tura tazo, ban san me suke yi ba, sai dai tana dawowa zata shige ban daki tayi wanka.

       Sam ban fahimci kome ba, sabida ba tasu make ba, ashe mutumin ku, zuwa yake yana more matar shi a mota.

                     Domin tana cika sati sati biyu ta fara sallah, Mamah bata fahimci abinda ke faruwa ba.

                ..... Shima a gidan shi rigima suke da Yahanasu, dan kullum ya koma mata sai yayi wanka aikuwa tace bata san kwanan zancen ba, idan zai dawo da Sarah ya dawo da ita, karshe dai sai da akayi rigima har yazo gaban Mamah. Ta sanya Sarah tattara ta koma gidan shi.

          Zaman Sarah da Yahanasu a farkon zuwa Yahanasu ai ba matsala, a yanzun kuma sai ta sanya Sarah a gaba, sosai da Fitina. Tana mata tsiya, idan Sarah tayi magana tace zata mata duka ita da bayin da suke gidan.

      Haka sai ya ɗan tsorata Sarah, Ni kuma naje gidan, lokacin Yahanasu tana cin mutuncin Sarah.

            Kan Bala'i, ban tab'a sanin cewa nima yar dambe bace sai ranar, domin sun sami damar Sarah har sai da suka nemi rufeta da duka.

Duk bakin Sarah dole ta gudu d'akinta, dan Mamah ta gaya mata ta rage shiga abinda bai shafeta ba, tare da nuna mata tayi takatsantsar da kowa, bata ce kome ba amma ta nuna mata ko da kuwa akan bayin da Umma ta kawo mata ne babu ruwanta da su.

                   Ina shiga na same su sunyi cirko cirko, kofar Sarah kowacce da abin dukarta.

     Al'amarin Sarah daban ce, a zuciyata da ruhina, Yahanasu tana zaune tana jiran Sarah ta fito su daketa.

    Wani irin iska me hade da walkiya ya gifta ta idona,  fita nayi daga gidan, naje na karyo itacen a bishiyar maina. Na shigo gidan, tare da rufe yan banza da duka.

   Dake jahilai ne har da taso min, ganin nafi karfin su yasa suka shiga niman hanyar guduwa.

Ai kuwa jin ihun su ya sanyata fitowa, a tsorace kamar me ta tsaya tana dariya tace..
"Kai Jannart ashe kema yar dambe ce toh zame min su sun dame ni ban san me nayi musu ba.

         Kullum sai sun nime dukana,  kuma ban iya kai kara ba, dama ina son naje na gaya miki sai gashi Allah ya kawo min ke, sun takura min sosai Janny."

    Aikuwa kamar hura min wuta tayi na cigaba da zane yan banza tun suna ihu har suka daina, sannan na kyale su.

         Ina gamawa na tawo gabanta, zan fadi ta tare ni. Jinin da ya fito ta hancina ta shafe, a tsorace.

         Dakinta ta kaini, ta rud'e dan bata san me zata min ba, kawai zata fita na rike hannunta.

        "Karki damu! Aminiya, naji sauki hab'o ne ya saba min haka idan raina yab'aci." A sanyayye ta zauna, tana kallona kafin tace.
"Laaa! Na tuna ai lokacin da muke makaranta ya tab'a miki haka."

    Nan ta shiga zubo min labari, takaici ya sanya ni lumshe idanuna, dan ita dai a gabana ko waziri Ali sai haka.

   Sai yamma na bar gidan, domin hidima da Ni tayi ta yi.

       Tunda daga ranar Sarah ta samu sukuni, a cikin gidan dan an zuba musu kashedi, kuma sun amince. Dan haka sai ta zama  shugaban su. Ina dalili a maida min Kawata bora.

               ---
Tsakanina da Aryan, ba a magana. Dan dan dattijona ya iya shimfida salon shi mai tafiya da zamanin shi, sam babu ruwan shi da yawan shekarun sa, abinda ya sani nice.

                   Kayan da za a zuba min a d'akina wani kamfanin yan kasar kasar sin itace Abban Aliyah yabawa aikin kome.

          Sai suturar da Mamah take yawan saya min, idan na tambaye ta sai tace.
"Ai gidan Sarautar ana bukatar mace da suturar Alfarma, kar ta damu da wanda aka mata, ina so samu ne a miki kayan da babu wacce aka tab'a mata dan su san kema yar gata ce,sannan kayan da suka kawo miki a ajiye shi har sai kin haihu sai a rab'awa dangin shi a matsayin Alkhairi.

    Karki yarda abin hannunki ya zame miki adon tafiyarki, abin hannunki ya zame miki mafarin soyayyarki a zukatan su.

                     Suna da shi, amma kyautatawa, tankar fansa kai da kai ne! Koda kuwa suna sonki toh tabbas kika sake musu abinki sai kiga sun zame miki dakarun kare kimar ki! Shima yaga kin sadaukar da abubuwan ki a kanshi zai zame miki tankar kassaka kina yakince shi yana kara manne miki.

                    Sai kiga lokaci ƙalilan kin saye kowa, da Alkhairi ayita tunanin ai kin gama dasu ne ta hanyar amfani da sihiri na kuwa zallar kyautatawa ce ta haifar da haka.

    Da mutane da yawa zasu an karar da Y'ayansu mata kafin kai su d'akin mazajen su zai yi wuya kiga an sami matsalar dangin mijin ya bar cikin hausawa.

   Ina babu wannan tunanin ke dai kina shiga ki zama ungulu da kan zabi. Ko kuma ramin kura sai y'ayanta.

      Kina gani dai yadda na dauki yan uwan Abba, da kyautata musu da nake yi a haka zagina wasu suke Amma babu yadda suka iya dani, tunda Allah ya hadamu dasu sai hakuri. Dan haka ban tab'a damuwa dab basu sona ba.

    A'a nafi damuwa da na kyautata musu, kuma Alhamdulillah Nagodewa Allah domin ko Itama babar shi sona take ba iya Ni ba hatta yaran mu kamar su cinye su suke dan so! Dan haka ki yi kokarin kin kyautata musu."

   Mama tayi min bayani sosai yadda zan samu yan uwan Aryan tunda suna sona, toh nayi kokarin ganin nima na musu Alkhairi yadda soyayyar zata dore.

                  --- 
Bikina saura wata uku sai ga wani cikin ya kuma bayyana a jikin Sarah, murna kamar me, kowa ya gama saka burin shi akan cikin, kulawa ake bata tun daga Bauchi aka kawo mata bayi manyan mata, domin Mahaifinta da kakanta sun zo sun dubata.

     Dan haka wani irin kulawa Kawu yake bata, tare da kaf-kaf akan cikin. Bata aikin fari balle baki.

          A wannan cikin taga abinda ake kira soyayya, domin kome har wanka Kawu yake mata, kome yayi daidai fa, amma idan ya tuna cewa ya rasa Janny sai wani irin kunci da damuwa ya aure shi na wani lokaci. Da zaran ya kalli cikin Sarah sai yaji nutsuwa ta sauko miki.

     A haka muke ta rayuwa, shi da farin cikin sa, nima nawa da farin cikin.

          ---
Dake tagar mu tana ta baya ne, haka kawai cikin dare zan ga mutum a tsaye a jikin tagar, sau biyu ina ihu koda an zo babu shi babu kamar shi.

    Wannan abun ya dame ni har Mamah dan haka kawai barci ya fara kaura ce min, idan lokacin da nake ganin mutumin yayi.

   Nan su Mamah suka fara min addu'o'in, har na fara samun Barci, tunda muka dage da Addu'ar sai na daina ganin mutumin.

       Sai dai kuma ina fita zan yi karo da wannan mara mutuncin,...

Komawa nayi gefe na zube akan gwiwata, ina kallon gawar Aryan, tab'a fuskana nayi ina son Jin ko mafarki nake, amma da na tab'a fuskana sai na fahimci cewa

      Amma na gaza fahimtar kome! Ban iya magana ba saboda kukan yaki zuwa min, sam bana jin kome bana ma fahimtar abinda ake yi.

      Ina kallo suka dauke shi, lumshe idanuna nayi a hankali na soma ganin wani lokacin da ban san yaushe ba an tab'a wucewa da gawar wani a gaba na.

             Kamar yadda jinin yake diga daga yawan Aryan haka na wancan lokacin.

             "Hamar!!!"
Na fada da mugun karfi, tare da rarrafawa a guje, nayi kan masu dauke da gawar.  Rike ni Jaamal yayi na fasa wata irin ihu, wanda yayi sanadin toshewar kunnen kowa.

           Sannan na juya kan shi nayita tsinka mishi mari,  bana wasa ba.
             Kafin na yanki jini zan fad'i, yayi maza ya tare ni.
"Bani da zab'i ne! Kece ya dace ki fahimce ni! Ki k'a k'i fahimtar nauyin dake zuciyata amma kika ki amsar gyaran da nayi."

               "Hankalin ka ya kwanta ka cika maganar da kayi ko Jaamal? Ka kashe musu D'a ka raba ta da wanda yake sanya murmushi a fuskarta ka rabata da mafarkinta, gaskiya kayi nasara babba domin yadda kayi kome ya dace a jinjina maka, amma nayi maka alkawarin karasa Jannart har abada.

  
       Don Allah ka nesance mu, hatta Fidausih ka rabu da ita, idan kuma ka k'i, toh tabbas abinda ya biyo baya kai......"

Bata kai karshen maganarta ba yan sanda suka shigo masarautar, tare da niman a tafi da shi, wannan itace tozarci mafi girma da ya faru a cikin masarautar, tun bayan kashe dan sarkin lardin Kazaure wanda ya tab'a zuwa ya auri mace mafi kyau a cikin al'ummar maguzawa.

Karni na sha bakwai...

"Turuku! Kana tunanin sadaukar da Zirya itace mafita! Ai kasan ma haka bazai faru ba, taya za'a ce anyi gamin gambiza, tsakanin jinsin d'an adam da mutanen boye?

     Na rantse da tsille ban tab'a jin haka ba, domin abun da daure kai!"

    Murmushi yayi sannan yace mishi.
"Ana yin haka mana! Ka dube Zirya a cikin wannan katafaren zuri'anmu ta ƙabilar maguzawan dutse, waye bai san wannan al'amarin ba!

     Sannan ina hangowa Zirya na wata rayuwa ce me cike da daula tare da jin daɗin t, ka duba yadda ta kasance kyakyawa wani sarki ne zai amince ya aureta, sannan dabi'un mu sun banbanta taya zan nima mata wanda zai bata kulawa tsayon rayuwar su!

    Ka duba da kyau! Har yau bata da kawa ko aboki sai d'an Sarkin busa, Hamar. Shine kad'ai take yawo dashi, koda na tambayeta shin tana son shine, ce min tayi a'a tana da wanda take so!

   Kenan Fairo ya bayyana mata kanshi, kuma kasan idan nace bazata aure shi ba, karshe zai zame mana tsinuwa ce mara iyaka na roke ka da kayi hakuri kaje dan Ni ban iya dogon magana ba."

      Dole Mutumin ya fita, daga cikin kogon ramin.

    ......
  A can kuwa bayan dutsen kwatarkwashi, kwance take sanye da bakin kayan sak'i(🤔😒🙄🤦 kusan kayan sakin kuwa ma? Toh kayan da ake Fulani day ko? Toh shine kayan saki kar wata ta min magana)

             Kyakyawa ce amma ba can ba, kasancewar ta mai duhun fata, yanayinta ba wani yanayi bane da za a zuzzuta shi, amma kyanta mai sanyi ne!  Wani sharb'eben macijine, ke yawo a saman jikinta.

    Fari tass dashi, a hankali ya rikide zuwa wani kyakkyawan matashin saurayi, ma abocin miskilancin, da daure fuska.

   Juya mata baya yayi, cikin damuwa ta isa gaban shi, ta zube tana sauke ajiyar zuciya, tare da faɗin.
"Rayuwata! Farin cikina! Duniyata kome nawa Fairo na kane! Don girman gemun Babanka ka daina wahalar da ruhina. Ina sonka"

     Niman janyeta ya fara tayi maza ta shige jikinshi, tare da manne mishi kamar dango. D'ago kanta yayi yana kallon fuskarta me cike da kwalliya na musamman, lashe bakin shi yayi sannan yace.
"Ki gaya min meye hadinki da Hamar!?"

    Rike hannun shi tayi sannan ta fashe da kuka tana faɗin.

"Na rantse maka  da tsalle babu abinda ya haɗa ni dashi sai zumunci, kuma daga yau kace min kar na kuma kula shi bazan kuma ba.

          Kaji Fairo! Bazan kuma ba kayi hakuri karka cuta dashi. Iya shi iyayen shi suka mallaka, idan ka zubda jinin, idan kayi haka bazan yafewa kaunar mu ba."

       Riko hannun ta yayi, cikin damuwa sannan yace.
"Ni dai karki ci amanar ruhina, domin yin haka dai-dai yake da kashe duk wanda ya shiga tsakanin mu!"

        Kwantar da kanta tayi a kirjin shi.
"Alkawarin mu! Bazai gushe ba. Sabida zuciyoyin mu suna tare da juna, kaunar mu tana baibaye da junarmu, bazan tab'a barin ka ba zan iya sadaukar da Rayuwata domin ka.

      Kuma zan iya bada rayuwata domin ka, karka juyawa alamarina baya, dan ina dai dai lokacin da nake bukatar ka, kaji yadda zuciyar mu take bugawa.

     Karka min haka, ina tare da kai karka manta ina son ka Dukda bamu kasance jinsi daya ba."

    D'ago kanta yayi cikin murmushi da abinda ta gama cewa akan shi, a hankali yake zare rigar sak'in jikinta, tare da ajiyewa a gefe, sannan yayi wata magana cikin wata harshe.
"Curka fira furka durka durma!"

Take wata irin gadon Alfarmar ta bayana a gurin, mai dauke da wasu labulayen ruwan gwal, ya d'aga can a iska suka shige cikin gadon, alqawari suka ajiye a wannan lokacin, suka raya soyayyarsu.

          Mai cike da faranta ran juna, soyayyar da suka gina tsakanin su. Mai dauke da wata irin yarda da juna, sunyi musayan jini da ruwa, sunyi musayar kalamai masu daraja, sun kuma kara da wata irin yarda wanda Ziryana ta bawa Fairo kyautar budurcinta a wannan lokaci.

      Shi kuma ya amsa yayinda ya k'arba cikin wata irin salon so da kaunarta, ya kuma bata wata kambu wanda ta sanya a hannunta na dama, sarauniya Ziryana mace mafi alkhairi a duniyar Fairo.

   Buɗe idanun ta tayi ta ganta, a cikin wata alkarya na daban, yayinda mutanen suka zuba gwiwar su, suna masu dunkule hannun su, suka kai gefen kirjinsu.

   Alamun girmamawa, da jinjina mata.
       Kallonta yayi tana kallon mutanen alkaryan, murmushi yayi mata sannan yace.
"Kin gansu! Duk suna cikin al'ummar ki, masu miki mubaya'a, kuma sun sadaukar da Rayuwarsu domin ki lokaci zuwa lokaci.

              Me kika shirya musu, kasancewar na sarauniyar su?!"

         Murmushi ne ya kwace mata, ta d'an ciji bakinta sannan ta zuba mishi ido, kafin ta mika mishi hannu tana kallon yadda yake murmushi shima.

"Kalamai na bazu tab'a kaiwa inda suka dace ba, sai dai soyayyar da mukewa juna tabbatacciya ce! Su kuma al'ummar da ka bar min su, wannan lokaci ina so....."

Shiru tayi sakamakon kallon tafin hannunta da tayi, wani irin haske yana bayyana, da sauri ta juya zata bar gurin, ya riko hannunta.

   "Lafiya!?"
"Hamar! Wani abun kasa a mishie, dan ka kawar da hankali na, ga ceton shi. Shine kasa aka kashe shi."

      Zuba mata ido yayi cikin tashin hankali.
"Bani da zab'i ne! Kuma kece ya dace ki fahimci kome, tunda muna tare da juna, na fada ba yana nufin na aikata bane, amma duk yadda kika dauka duk daya ne."

  Cikin tashin hankali tace mishi.
"Kai ne ka kashe shi kenan?! Fairo meye yayi maka?!"

Ta faɗa cikin wani irin kuka, tare da zuɓewa kasa, tana kallon aka wuce da gawar.....

6️⃣6️⃣

   Borno...
_Duk rayuwar da zaka gina! Yana bukatar so da kauna! Sannan yana bukatar k'iyayya da bakin ciki! Sannan dole jigon shi ya zame hassada da kyashi..... Ya shugabana. Zan kasance maka tankar raƙumi da akala! Ya shugabana ni baiwa ce amma nasan a cikin bayin Ni mai daraja ce kamar yadda ka zab'e Ni Kwarkwarah a cikin sauran bayinka, Umarni ka ya isa biyan buƙatar ka koda kuwa haka yana nufin musayar rayuwa da mutuwa ce._

Matuƙa firgice ya farka yana haki yau ma bai ga fuskar ta ba, kuma yana tsaka da nutsuwa da ita take gaya Mishi wannan kalaman.
Wacece ita? Meye ma'anar haka, duk sai yake jin ya damu da ya san meke faruwa dashi.
Dan haka sauka yayi daga gadon.

Ya dauki littafin tarihin masarautar yana dubawa.

            *Shafin Sarauniya ziryana*
      Mace mai kyau da kyalkyali!
Macen da aka sadaukar da ita domin kasancewar ta....
A cikin karni na sha uku aka binne shi, duk da yayi yaki dan wanke kanshi amma hakar shi bata cimma nasara ba.

                      Kuskuren da zai sanya a kuma mai-maita kanshi, shine na kin sauraran Fairo akaro na biyu, haka zai bawa tarihin daman mai-maita kanshi, zai kuma bada damar sake fahimtar aininin gaskiyar labarin.

     Soyayyar mutum da Aljanan ba sabon abu bane, amma kyautar rayuwa da mutuwa itace sadaukarwa soyayya!

    In dai har zai iya sadaukar da Rayuwar shi toh Tabbas dole Ziryana ta bada nata domin ceto na wanda take kauna..

      Tsaki yayi cikin gajiya da karanta wannan labarin, me kama da taatsuniya.

   Ajiye Littafin yayi tare da kwanciyar shi.

             ---
Washi gari...
Bai fita fada ba, Qoiseh ta shigo mishi cikin shigar Alfarma,ta zauna a gaban shi.

             Kallonta yayi cikin nutsuwa, dan ya fahimci akwai damuwa a tare da ita.

        "Hmm!"
Gyara zama tayi sosai, kafin ta fara mishi bayanin kamar yadda zai fahimce ta.

    Allah sarki da sauri ya mike zuwa shashin Innar daga nesa ya hango ta kwance.

              Yanayin ta, yaso firgita shi, amma bai damu ba. Zama yayi yana kallonta, cikin wani irin SO da Kaunar gudar Yar shi daya Tal a duniya.

      "Rahil! Sannu, duk tsawon shekaru ban san kina nan ba, sabida lalurar da ta same ni! Insha Allah koda zan rasa kome sai na jinya ce ki"

     Lumshe idanu tayi ta bude akan shi. Sannan ta kuma rufewa a hankali.

      Shafa kanta yayi, cikin damuwa ya mik'e,. Kafe shi tayi da ido, dan bata son ya bar dakin .
              "Kina son na zauna ko? Kwantar da hankalinki zan dawo yanzun."

       Haka ya fita da sauri, can sai gashi tare da yan uwansa, aka zo aka fitar da ita. Zuwa shashin sa.

      Nan ya fito yaga wani kofa wanda yake kallon kofar shiga shashin sa, haka kawai a yaji a ran shi kamar yasan d'akin.

Wasu abubuwa ne ya shiga zagaye kan shi, kafin wani lokaci ya nemi faduwa dole ya koma cikin katon, zauren shi ya zube akan gwiwar shi, tare da kifa kan shi a jikin kujerun da aka Kawata shi da wasu irin yadin silke.

                ---
Daura...
Da sauri na sauka zan fita Mamah ta riko hannuna, kuka na saka cikin tashin hankali nace..
"Ba yau ya fara ba! 

Shi ya kashe Hamar! Ya kuma amince da bakin shi. Me yasa ya Kashe Aryan, watacce irin rayuwa ya zab'a min.

  Me yasa zai kashe shi, da ya fahimtar dani, wallahi zan iya dakatar da Aryan, tunda babu dole!
         Sai da ya gama bari na sake da abinda nake so zai kashe min shi."

         Haka nayi ta surutu da kuma, tare da dukar jikin Mamah, dakyar aka  dawo dani daki kuka nake kamar na cire zuciyata na huta.

             ---
"Jamal! Taya haka ya faru?! Ga baki daya kan mu ya kulle! Domin abin ya zame mana sabon abu! Taya."

           "Ni ne na kashe shi ba karya bane! Kawai a yanke min hukuncin da ya dace dani."

    "Innalillahi wa'inna ilaihi rajioun! Jaamal me kake shirin aikatawa!?" Abban Aliyah ya tambaye shi.

"Rabu da mahaukaci! Wallahi bazai iya kisan kai ba, kawai akwai abinda bamu sani ba!"

   Banza yayi dasu, yana jin suna jajjanta al'amarin yaki cewa kome!

        Fita suka yi daga wajen da aka rufe shi suka fito. 
"Aswad! jaamal nya haukace ne? Taya zai amince da wannan abin? Taya zai amince da laifin da bana shi ba?! Sannan ina da ja!"
"Jamlah! Baki san halin wancan bane, Bashi yayi kisan ba, amma kafirin taurin kan shi ba zai sanya ya amsa ba."

              Shiru suka yi dukkan su Hudu, 

.....
Koda suka shigo masarautar har anyi jana'azar gawar Aryan, a kai shi gidan shi na gaskiya.
    
   Masarautar kamar anyi share baka jin kome, babu duk wata Shagali, a cikin awowi ya koma gidan makoki.

                  ....
Manyan fada wanda suka yi alawadaran abinda Shamaki yayi suke ta shiga da fecce, yayinda aka tura Galadima da Waziri, an tura su domin bada hakuri, tare da niman abin ya zama na cikin gida.

            Haka ya bawa Shamaki haushi da shida Fulani, akan me za a goyi bayan Jaamal, bayan ansan cewa shi yayi kisar?

        ---
Sokoto..

            Bayan sun gama jawabi tare da ban hakuri, murmushi Mahaifin Aryan yayi sannan yace.
"Ana iya goge kome! Amma banda zanen kaddara! Idan yau Jaamal shi ya kashe Aryan zan iya daukar wannan abisa kuskuren, dan nasan haka kawai bazai kashe shi dan baya kaunar shi ba.

   Duk da naji ance Abu daya suke so? Amma Ni nafi imanin da cewa, bashi yayi kisar ba. Na fadi haka ne dan irin sanin da nayi mishi, Jaamal din.

           Shima kamar dana ne, dukkan mu sarakunan Arewa babu wanda zai bada goyan bayan  A hukunta shi ba, domin nayi imanin shi din d'an na gari ne!

         Duk d'an da ya amshi mata hudu abisa biyayya da kuma sanin darajar iyayen shi bazai tab'a  kusantar abinda zai bata  suna a idanun duniya ba.

   Ina magana ne a matsayin shugaban sarakunan baki daya, sai a matsayin mahaifin Aryan Na yafe abinda ya faru,  sannan ga wasika akai can gurin hukumar da ta rufe shi ta bude shi.

      Insha Allah Ubangiji zai nuna ikon shi akan al'amarin nan."

              Wannan halin girman da Mai Martaba sarkin sokoto ya nuna ya tab'a zuciyar kowa, dan haka kwanan su Waziri daya suka dawo, abin mamaki suna zuwa ana sauyawa Jaamal inda aka ajiye shi.

     Abinda ya mugun d'aga hankalin kowa, domin har an shigar da karar ya aikata kisan kai, ma'ana an kai kotu.

           A ranar kowa a cikin masarautar sai da ya zubda kwalla, sabida tausayin Mahaifin shi.
           Sannan aka shiga niman inda yake babu shi babu labarin shi.
         Aswad yafi kowa shiga damuwa, domin idan ka ganshi ya fita hayacin sa.

6️⃣9️⃣
Sosai ake shiga da fita akan Jamal, amma yaki magana, karshe haka suka je kotu nan ma yaki cewa kome, har ran Alkali ya b'aci sosai, yayi mishi magana akan banda kimar mahaifin shi da yau sai ya yanke mishi zaman gidan wakafi dan ya koyi magana, mara mutunci kin magana yayi, haka suka tashi daga zaman yau.

              Cikin matukar mamaki, suka kalle shi, sannan suka ce.

7️⃣1️⃣ *A gurguje*
A yadda Sarah ta ture Ni na fahimci akwai abin a ranta, kallon mamaki na mata, sannan na bud'i bakin zan mata magana ta d'aga min hannu. Cikin tararradin abinda zai fito bakin ta na zuba mata ido.

                  "Meye tsakaninku da mijina? Nasan ba iya dangantaka ke tsakanin ku ba, har wani B'oyayyen sirri, gaya min meye alakarki da mijina?!"

     Wani irin yawu na had'iye, tare da in'ina na fara magana da cewa.
"Sarah ki nutsu na miki bayani."

     Kifa min mari tayi, sai da hancina ya fidda jini, tana huci ta hadani da jikin bangon.
"Cin amanar aminta! Na yarda dake ashe ke mijina kike so!"

         "Wallahi babu komai a tsakanina dashi kinyi min muguwar fahimta ne, Sarah billahil azim babu kome, kawai ana son shiga tsakanin mu ne, don Allah karki juya min baya."

Yadda nayi maganar cikin Kuka da tashin hankali, ya sata sake ni, tare da bina da mugun kallo ta bar gurin, zuɓewa nayi a kasar na sake wani irin kuka. Dukana Sarah tayi sabida mijinta.
"Innalillahi wa'inna ilaihi rajioun! Dole na nimo abokin rayuwata."

"Me yasa bazaki bari a kashe ta ba?!"
Da sauri na d'ago kai.
"Ziryana! Don Allah karki tab'a ta, barta karki cutar min da ita, wata rana zata fahimci girman kuskuren da ta aikata min, lokacin babu damar niman yafiya na dan kome ya kure mata."

               Tashi nayi na bar ta a gurin, na nufi inda suke jirana, kafin na isa na goge fuskana kamar ban yi kuka ba, tunda na isa gurin kawu yake ta kallona, yana son gano meye ya b'ata ran matar shi ni kuma me ya sani kuka.

       Har muka shiga, inda za a tantance mu, harara ta take kamar idanunta zai fado, kuma yana lura damu.
.har aka gama mana kome muka shiga, abin mamaki kujeran mu da shi nambarmu daya ce a tsakanin mu, ita kuma tana bayan mu, ai kuwa ta tadda wani irin Fitina da rigima, amsar nata nayi na koma baya, ita kuma ta zauna da mijinta, tare da daura mishi kai a kafad'arshi.

   Sunkuyar da kaina nayi, tare da fashewa da kuka, amma kasa kasa, sabida haka bai tab'a shiga tsakanin mu da ita.

Dake babu me fahimtar kuka nake, haka muka bar garin Ikko.

A can kuwa Daura Abdul na buga Iskanci son ran shi, domin an bashi mata biyu  ya amsa amma sai da ya kartawa kowa rashin mutunci, sannan ya faɗa cewa shi aje nimo duk inda ba shiga.

Wannan malamin yaso Kawu ya saurare shi amma fir yaki.
Ana saura sati zamu dawo ne muka kuma haduwa da Abdul, abun mamaki.

     Na tsinci kaina da amince mishi, Kawu bai sani ba. Amma bawai da aure ba, kawai yayi shawagi dani ne kawai, nan ne kuma na nuna mishi bana ra'ayin shi.

   Har yazo gidan Kawu yana nan ranar, suka yi fada ya fadawa Kawu cewa.
Har da cin alwashin koda tsiya koda arziki sai ya kwanta dani ko da kuwa zan mutu na dawo sai ya cika kudirin shi a kaina.

  Haka ya masifar b'atawa Kawu rai dan dole muka dawo kan dalilin Abdul!

    Tunda aka fara haka Sarah ta kuma dauke min wuta, domin kiri-kiri mijinta ya nuna cewa baya ga Umman shi da Raihanah, sai nice macen da yake matukar kauna. Sai Hasiyah da Jannah tare da UmRumanah cikin da ta haifa. Dan shi ya tsayar damu.

         Ai kuwa muna isowa, ta dauke min wuta, na girgiza kai tare da hawa taxi ya kawo ni gidan Mamah.

          A can daura kuwa, dan dole aka shiga yiwa Abdul bore, dan haka yaje gurin shaidanun irin shi suka ce mishi yaje yazo dani da karfin tsiya, ya kwanta dani duk wani karfin da yake bukata na magana  na iko zai samu, amma sai yayi hakan dani.

  Shine ya hadawa Aswad makirci, shi kuma ya faɗa.

Kasancewar garin iko yana cikin garuruwan da suke samun ruwa sosai, dan haka yau na fito sayan magani, tare da lema na. Kawai naga wasu mutane sun biyo ni zasu kamani, garin gudu na fadi.
 Aikuwa suna zuwa suka rufa akaina tare da yaga min rigar jikina.

 .har kasa, suna ƙoƙarin ta bani motar Aswad ta haska mu, da sauri suka gudu.

         Dukda dare ne, a cikin motar shi ya cire kayan jikin shi yazo ya mika min, kasa tashi nayi ina kuka, Kawai ya durkusa zai saka min, sai ga Kawu nan.

      "Innalillahi wa'inna ilaihi rajioun!" Abinda yake fada kenan, ya fito a fusace. Ba tare da bin ba'asi ba ya rufe Aswad da duka, kamar mahaukaci, ina mishi bayani ya ture ni, da ya daki Aswad din, ya kuma je ya sanya a bayan motar shi, duk yadda nazo na kwaci Aswad abin ya faskara.
     Haka yaja ya tafi dashi, ban san inda ya kai shi ba.

 Ina tsaye sai ga wani yazo ya dauki motar Aswad.

   ......
Wasa wasa, Kawu ya boye Aswad. Nayi sintiri gidan shi yafi sau goma, ina ƙoƙarin fahimtar dashi gaskiya.

           Duk yadda naso abin yaci tura, sabida Sarah duniya tayi dad'i ta saka an hanani shiga gidan, dakyar na tafi har ofishin Kawu, tunda na shiga na zuba gwiwata a kasa, ina mishi bayani.
Ko kallona bai yi ba, karshe sai da na cire kunyata na Ƴa mace nace.
"Kawu! Babu wani abinda zai mallaka maka ni ce koda kuwa ban shiga jerin matan ka ba?!"
 Da mamaki ya d'ago ajiye biron hannun shi yayi cikin daurewar fuska yace.
" KWARKWARAH!! Zaki zo! Toh akan me?!"

 "Sabida ka sake Aswad! Idan ban zo maka da kimata ba!" Na fadi haka cikin kuka.

    "Toh tashi muje!"
Kallon shi nayi cikin mamaki nace.
"Amma abisa sharadin kana tabbatarwa zaka rabu damu daga ni harshin din."
 
"Toh sarauniyar Jannart! umarni ce ko Shawara?!"
     Kamar zan ce mishi dolenka , nasan bazai taba kyale Aswad, sunkuyar da kai nayi kamar mareniya nace.
"Afuwa!"
 Gaba yayi nabi bayan shi, muka fita dukda ruwan da ake yi, bai tsayawa sai acan agege.

    .anan ya tsaya gidan wani malami, nan yayiwa malamin bayanin abinda muke bukata, tare da daya mishi ni din wacece, dukda malamin yaso yaki, amma sai da Jamal ya nuna mishi fa'idar da muje mu aikata zina, dan haka malamin ya amince, bayan an bani rabin sadakin da ake bawa, Cikakkiyar Ƴa me yancin.

      Sannan aka sani na shiga cikin gidan, dukda nasan naci amanar Sarah. Sai da nayi kuka.
Har na fara tari, jini na fita.

        Bayan an daura auren ne, Malamin ya shigo tare da min nasiha, sannan ya kuma gaya min abubuwan da suka zama dole akaina.

    Bayan na isa gurin kawun kaina a sunkuye, ya bude min kofa, haka muka dawo, ya kaini gidan da ya ajiye Aswad, ya fita sannan ya je ya kawo abinda ake bukatar a shiga dakin amarya dashi har da sanin tufafi ya kawo min, nayi wanka na saka.

Ina jin ciwon rashin yarda damu da Kawu ya nuna min, haka ya shigo min.

    Bayan ya gabatar min da kome, tare da bani abinci ya kuma jamu sallar,  ya jagorance ni zuwa ga shimfid'ar shi.

     Duk da ina son Kauce mishi abubuwan da yake min zuciyata tayi nisan ga amsar bayanin shi.

    A hankali yake buɗe wasu shafukan rayuwata tare da sabunta kanshi da kuma ni kaina, a sannu ya  isa inda ban  isa dakatar da shi ba, sai da ya nuna min kwazon shi da kuma jarumta shi.

          A lokacin ko saurarona baya yi.
      .wasu irin mutane nake gani, tare da wata duniya na daban, a hankali kome ya fara bayyana min a kwarya idanuna.

      Duk yadda na so, kwatar kaina na kasa, sakamakon karfina da ya kare, jinin da yake fita ta hancina da kunne na, tare da bakina, sunyi yawa.
Shi kanshi bai fahimci haka ba.

    A hankali jini ya kwanta a saman Idanuna. Abinda ya faru karni na sha bakwai...
